Chapter 101 Lin Zhenjiang, the crowd kowtows to the mountain god!

Inside the county government building.

Upon hearing the shout, Qiu Zhenhe's expression immediately brightened.

"A Yin God Realm expert has arrived!" he exclaimed excitedly.

Old Jin, standing nearby, breathed a sigh of relief, then his expression turned strange.

"Lord Qiu, the person who has arrived... seems to be one of the General's men," Old Jin said slowly.

Qiu Zhenhe was about to go out to greet him when he heard Old Jin's words and instantly realized who it was.

That shout was very familiar; it sounded like someone else!?

Qiu Zhenhe's expression changed immediately, but he quickly regained his composure and said calmly, "Whoever it is, if a Yin God Realm expert arrives, we must go to greet them."

Old Jin nodded. Although they were adversaries, proper etiquette still had to be observed.

The two left the county government hall and immediately leaped into the air, stopping two meters below the man.

"Qiu Zhenhe greets General Lin." Qiu Zhenhe bowed respectfully.

Although Elder Jin was older than the man, in the world of cultivators, strength was respected, so he too had to bow.

"He Sanguan, Jin Buhuan, greets General Lin."

Lin Zhenjiang's sharp gaze swept over the two men, finally settling on Qiu Zhenhe. He sneered, "This general has seen you. Are you working for the prefecture?"

"Yes," Qiu Zhenhe admitted.

"Haha, very good!" Lin Zhenjiang laughed, but his laughter carried a chill.

He coldly scrutinized Qiu Zhenhe, saying in a low voice, "Normally, if you interfered in Liaoyun County, this general would certainly teach you a lesson. But today I have important business to attend to, so I'll spare you this time!"

"Thank you for your leniency, General Lin." Threatened like this, Qiu Zhenhe could only bow his head and thank him.

"I have no time for idle chatter. Tell me immediately where that fifth-tier demon general went!" Lin Jianghe said coldly.

Upon receiving news from Liaoyun County that a new fifth-tier demon general had appeared, he was ordered by the General to come immediately.

After all, Liaoyun County was the General's territory; if it were to be ravaged by the Ink Pig, it would be a huge loss!

He traveled without stopping, determined to arrive as soon as possible, confront the Ink Pig, and try to mitigate the damage.

However, upon arriving at Tianyue County, he only sensed the Ink Pig's aura. Following the aura, he found the trail had ended.

"Reporting to the General, the demon general went to Peach Blossom County, more than seventy li from here!" Qiu Zhenhe hurriedly reported.

"Seventy li!?" Lin Jianghe frowned slightly, but said nothing more. He turned and strode away, his figure disappearing on the spot.

According to his calculations, by the time he arrived, Peach Blossom County should already be completely slaughtered.

He could only hope that the black boar would eat its blood feast slowly and not already headed for the next county.

On his way to Peach Blossom County, he suddenly noticed something unusual.

"Huh, it looks like there was just a battle over there?"

Lin Zhenjiang gazed into the distance; there seemed to be a village there, with quite a few people.

However, the signs of battle were faint, clearly indicating that the fight lasted less than a round and wasn't a fierce battle.

Seeing this, Lin Zhenjiang didn't approach to investigate further, assuming it was likely a superior cultivator crushing a weaker cultivator or demon.

This couldn't possibly be related to the Ink Pig. After all, the Ink Pig was a fifth-tier demon general; only the capital city possessed a cultivator capable of instantly killing a demon general.

Furthermore, it was impossible for the Ink Pig to have overwhelmed anyone else. If that were the case, the people of that village wouldn't still be alive; they would have long since become its prey.

Without further thought, Lin Zhenjiang sped towards Peach Blossom County.

A short while later, he arrived in Peach Blossom County, but the scene before him left him utterly bewildered.

Peach Blossom County was peaceful and serene. The streets were bustling, vendors hawked their wares, completely unaffected by the demonic attack.

He immediately frowned, a growing suspicion creeping into his mind. Had the demon general not come here, or had Qiu Zhenhe deceived him?

Despite his doubts, he didn't stop, directly arriving above the county government building.

"Where is the county magistrate? Come out and see me!" he shouted.

Soon, Qian Ke was alerted and looked up at Lin Zhenjiang in shock.

He reacted quickly, bowing respectfully, "Greetings, Senior. The magistrate is away from the county town these past few days, so I'm handling affairs on his behalf. Please feel free to ask me anything."

"Hmm?" Lin Zhenjiang, being who he was, immediately sensed something was amiss upon hearing Qian Ke's words. However, he was too lazy to concern himself with such trivial matters and coldly inquired, "Have you seen any demons approaching?"

Qian Ke looked suspicious and shook his head, saying, "I haven't seen any. Everything has been fine in Peach Blossom County lately."

"Nothing?"

Lin Zhenjiang frowned, his doubts deepening.

He was promised a fifth-tier demon general, and after all the trouble he'd gone, he hadn't even seen a shadow of one.

But there were still several counties nearby, so he didn't let his guard down and immediately left Peach Blossom County for the next one.

As he left, a sense of unease lingered in his heart.

For some reason, as he approached Peach Blossom County, he felt as if something was secretly watching him.

It made him feel uneasy all over, but the feeling was fleeting and quickly disappeared.

"What was that? Could it be that the Ink Pig gave me a bad feeling?"

Lin Zhenjiang's expression shifted uncertainly. At their level, intuition was never wrong.

Thinking this, his face darkened.

Could it be that the Ink Pig had already slaughtered the other three counties?

Startled, Lin Zhenjiang increased his speed!

Secretly, Li Yue watched Lin Zhenjiang's departing figure, understanding dawning on him.

"This should be the Yin God Realm cultivator sent by the imperial court to deal with the fifth-tier demon general?"

He muttered to himself.

Putting aside everything else, a Yin God Realm cultivator was indeed stronger than a fifth-tier demon general.

After Lin Zhenjiang entered his divine sense range, he had preemptively withdrawn his divine sense aura, but even so, Lin Zhenjiang's intuition still sensed something was wrong.

This person meant no harm; he was there to deal with the Ink Pig. Li Yue naturally made no move, quietly observing him.

Unfortunately, the Mind Mirror couldn't see into a cultivator's mind; otherwise, it could have listened to what he was thinking.

Li Yue withdrew his attention and focused it back on Thousand Mountain Manor.

After the recent manifestation of divine power that slayed the Ink Pig, his merit points experienced a surge.

The villagers flocked to the temple, their expressions excited as they offered incense and kowtowed to the Mountain God.

The laborers from Peach Blossom County, who had come to work, all put down their tasks and cautiously approached Thousand Mountain Manor, eagerly asking if they could also go in and worship the Mountain God.

Du Tiandao, who was responsible for maintaining order, smiled upon hearing the laborers' requests, saying, "The Mountain God protects all living beings; anyone can come to worship. However, there are too many people inside; you'll have to queue."

After thinking for a moment, he suggested, "If you queue, you might have to wait until tonight. Why don't you just kowtow directly to the sacred mountain? Sincerity is key; it doesn't matter where you are. Besides, the sacred mountain is the very embodiment of the Mountain God."

Upon hearing this, everyone's hearts stirred, and they all followed Du Tiandao's advice, devoutly kowtowing towards the sacred mountain.

Meanwhile, Li Yue silently watched all of this, observing his merit points rapidly increasing!


Chapter 102 Ascending the Sacred Mountain, the Vanished Demon General!

The more than four thousand laborers, almost every one of them devoutly worshipping the sacred mountain.

This instantly increased Li Yue's merit points by nearly twenty thousand.

His merit points now totaled over one hundred and fifty thousand.

[Realm: Third-Tier Spirit Mountain]

[Merit: 156420/1000000]

[Supernatural Powers: Mountain God's Blessing, Dharma Form of Heaven and Earth, Resuscitation of Incense]

[Spell: Manifestation]

[Magic Weapons: Mountain-Suppressing Seal, Heart Mirror, Divine Weapon Forging Furnace]

This single manifestation brought him over four thousand believers; this move was incredibly worthwhile.

Moreover, according to his estimation, the gains should be far more than this.

In Thousand Mountain Manor.

The dragged-back Ink Pig had become the focus of all the villagers' attention.

A group of people stared in shock and lingering fear at the mountain-sized monster, all gasping in astonishment.

Such a gigantic monster! How powerful must it be?

Some people vaguely remembered the third-tier rat demon that had appeared when Feng County fell; it wasn't even a tenth the size of this wild boar.

If it weren't for the Mountain God's intervention, they would all be dead!

"Why did the two village heads drag this thing back? It looks terrifying!"

A villager spoke timidly, his tone fearful.

"What do you know? Monsters are terrifying, but they're also incredibly nourishing. Their flesh and blood contain countless amounts of spiritual energy; for cultivators, that's a treasure!"

Shangguan Qiuyu's eyes burned with desire as he looked at the monster's corpse.

He thought to himself, "Such a huge boar, surely I can get a piece of it, right?"

This was definitely the flesh and blood of a monster at the level of a monster general—the ultimate nourishment. Even if eating it didn't make him a cultivator, it could still prolong his life and strengthen his body!

Many others shared the same thought.

Young and strong men like Erqing, Du Tiandao, and Erniu looked on with even greater longing.

Even the little kids like Xiaohu came running over excitedly, drooling over the black pig.

They hadn't had their fill of the demon tiger last time!

Meanwhile, Wu Ze and the others were discussing what to do with the demon general's corpse.

Kong Wang smeared a little blood on his hand, carefully examined it, and then slowly said, "This pig demon's flesh and blood contain no toxins; it's edible."

Upon hearing this, Bai Liang exclaimed with delight, "Excellent! A fifth-tier demon general! The spiritual energy contained in its flesh and blood is incredibly concentrated; it's truly a great tonic!"

Wu Ze also showed joy; he hadn't expected such a nourishing food to be delivered right to his doorstep.

This was completely unexpected for everyone.

However, Kong Wang then added, "The flesh and blood of a demon general possesses a domineering and intense power. Ordinary people cannot consume it, otherwise the spiritual energy contained within the flesh and blood will impact their meridians, causing damage to their foundation. Even we few must undergo processing before we can consume and absorb it."

The flesh and blood of a fifth-tier demon general is not something ordinary people can directly consume.

Ordinary people's meridians are fragile and cannot withstand the impact of a demon general's power. Consuming their flesh and blood could very well lead to death.

"There's such a saying? That's truly a pity," Bai Liang said, feeling regretful upon hearing this.

He also considered distributing the demon general's corpse to the villagers so that everyone could benefit.

Furthermore, he had another idea: to use the demon general's flesh and blood to nurture Du Tiandao and a few others, to see if they could also become cultivators.

Otherwise, there were still too few cultivators in Thousand Mountain Manor.

"Is there any way to make it so that ordinary people can also eat it?"

Wu Ze frowned, feeling that it wasn't right to let just a few people enjoy the demon general's flesh and blood all to themselves.

"There's a method we can try, but it will take some time, and it might not even work."

Kong Wang thought for a moment and spoke slowly.

"A method is good enough! Whether it works or not, we should at least try!"

Bai Liang spoke up, quickly asking what the method was.

Kong Wang replied, “Atop the sacred mountain, there lies a blessed land, shrouded in spiritual energy and imbued with divine power. If the corpse of a demon general were placed there, its contained power should be purified, transforming it into gentle spiritual energy.”

He was referring to the cave space where he was born, near the spiritual veins within the sacred mountain, possessing extraordinary spiritual energy and benefits unimaginable to outsiders.

However, these words stunned Wu Ze and Bai Liang. They exchanged bewildered glances, unsure what to say.

Finally, Wu Ze spoke, frowning, “Ascending the sacred mountain is a taboo of Thousand Mountain Manor. Kong Wang, your method will not work.”

Up until now, they had never considered ascending the sacred mountain to glimpse its secrets.

Bai Liang added, “Kong Wang, you were allowed to ascend the sacred mountain by the Mountain God. But we cannot. The Mountain God has not expressed any decree that we can ascend the sacred mountain.”

The Mountain God had neither forbade nor granted permission.

Therefore, without the Mountain God's permission, they could not violate the rules of Thousand Mountain Manor and ascend the sacred mountain.

Kong Wang thought for a moment and said, "How about we directly ask the Mountain God for his instructions?"

Bai Liang and the other man pondered, then nodded in agreement.

The three then went to the temple, bowed together, and fervently inquired of the Mountain God whether they could temporarily ascend the sacred mountain.

Li Yue sensed their worship and quickly responded, naturally granting their permission.

The cultivation ground was originally created to provide opportunities for his subordinates to cultivate and improve their strength; if they weren't allowed to go up, wouldn't that be a waste of creation?

Having received the Mountain God's approval, the three rose, Bai Liang and Wu Ze both feeling somewhat excited.

Having been at the foot of the sacred mountain for so long, they could finally ascend it and see it for themselves.

Although it was only halfway up the mountain, it was still a true ascent of the sacred mountain, bringing them closer to the Mountain God!

Next, led by Kong Wang, the three of them, working together and witnessed by many villagers, carried the demon general's corpse up the sacred mountain.

...............

Bahe County.

Lin Zhenjiang arrived at the last county town of Liaoyun Prefecture, but the county town below was just like the previous ones, peaceful and undisturbed!

At this point, his expression had become somewhat grim.

He had exerted all his strength, pushing himself to his maximum speed, traveling from the prefectural capital thousands of miles away to Liaoyun Prefecture, all to stop the fifth-tier demon general.

And now he had arrived, but where was the fifth-tier demon general?

Where had that enormous black boar gone?

For a fleeting moment, Lin Zhenjiang wondered if Ye Shanhe had deceived him.

But he quickly dismissed the idea. Ye Shanhe was a veteran of the Great Chu, though only a county garrison commander, he was considered a senior figure in the Qingzhou army.

Such a person wouldn't report false information.

But if the news was true, where was the Ink Pig?

Lin Zhenjiang couldn't figure it out, and finally could only change course and return to Tianyue County, intending to find Qiu Zhenhe and get some answers.

Because of these five counties, only Qiu Zhenhe had personally witnessed a fifth-tier demon general!


Chapter 103 Speculation, Yin God Realm Visit!

Tianyue County.

Qiu Zhenhe and Jin Buhuan were discussing how to deal with Lin Zhenjiang if he returned to settle scores after dealing with the demon general.

“Lord Qiu,” Jin Buhuan said slowly, “in my opinion, we don’t need to panic. Although General Lin is strong, he’s not the commander-in-chief in person. He has to give face to the prefect, so he won’t touch us.”

Old Jin spoke slowly, though his tone lacked confidence.

Qiu Zhenhe’s expression was grim. He had come to Tianyue County hoping to take advantage of the situation.

He had thought it was a great opportunity, but so many things had happened one after another.

First, the Taoist and Buddhist sects came to him demanding explanations, which he had resolved.

But now, facing the demon general and Lin Zhenjiang, he felt a great sense of crisis.

He was about to say something when he suddenly sensed something, and his expression changed.

Just then, a cold voice rang out.

“Qiu Zhenhe, come out and see me!”

He's here!

Qiu Zhenhe and Elder Jin exchanged a glance, then both rose and went out.

He had expected Lin Zhenjiang to return, but he hadn't expected it to be so soon.

Has Lin Zhenjiang's strength improved again? He actually managed to defeat a fifth-tier demon general so easily!

The two of them came out and flew into the air, arriving before Lin Zhenjiang.

“Greetings, General Lin! Congratulations on your return from suppressing the demon and for your great achievement!” Qiu Zhenhe quickly offered his flattery.

However, Lin Zhenjiang snorted coldly after hearing this, his tone icy: "Qiu Zhenhe! You say you saw that fifth-tier demon general! Then I ask you, why, after searching the entire Liaoyun County, did you not even find a trace of that demon general? Are you lying, deceiving me?"

Qiu Zhenhe was taken aback, not expecting Lin Zhenjiang to interrogate him.

He quickly waved his hand, replying, "General Lin, I swear not a single lie. That fifth-tier demon general did indeed come to Tianyue County, stayed for a while, and then headed towards Taohua County. There's no mistake!"

Having confidently stated this, Qiu Zhenhe added, "I am willing to swear a military oath: if I have uttered even a single lie, may I be cut into a thousand pieces!"

Old Jin, standing nearby, also stepped forward to guarantee that everything Qiu Zhenhe said was true. If he was lying, he was willing to suffer the same fate as Qiu Zhenhe—a thousand cuts!

Lin Zhenhe frowned upon hearing this.

The two dared to make such a assertion, so it couldn't be a lie.

But if they weren't lying, where was the fifth-tier demon general?

Qiu Zhenhe noticed the problem and asked, "General Lin, has that demon general really disappeared? We've searched the entire Liaoyun Prefecture and can't find him?"

Elder Jin also looked at Lin Zhenjiang; this matter sounded rather strange.

Such a large demon general, especially one that had just broken through, freed from control, and not yet locked away by the Yin God Realm's power.

How could there be absolutely no movement from him?

In the past, whenever a demon general had just broken through, they would invariably unleash monstrous crimes, slaughtering several counties before being hastily suppressed by the arriving Yin God Realm experts.

But this time, everything was calm and still, without a trace.

This was truly bizarre.

"Could it be that I, your general, am joking with you?" Lin Zhenjiang coldly glanced at Qiu Zhenhe, his tone indifferent.

Qiu Zhen was also very puzzled, and suddenly said, "Could it be that he got into a conflict with other demon generals, fought each other, and was killed?"

“Impossible. All the demon generals within Qingzhou, except for the newly-breakthrough Ink Pig, have their potential locked. If they were to make a move, they would have already triggered their potential, attracting the Yin God Realm experts.”

Elder Jin shook his head, directly rejecting Qiu Zhenhe's guess.

The number of demon generals is finite, and each demon general has a Yin God Realm expert of corresponding strength to suppress them.

A demon general whose potential is locked will be detected by the Yin God Realm experts if they unleash their power or leave their area.

Having ruled out all possibilities, what else could cause the Ink Pig to remain completely silent?

“Did other Yin God Realm experts make a move beforehand?” Elder Jin asked, realizing something.

Lin Zhenjiang glanced at him indifferently and said, “When Yin God Realm experts make a move, there will inevitably be a fierce battle, leaving traces of a great battle, their auras will be visible. However, this general has searched the entire Liaoyun County and found no traces of battle.”

Except for a few traces of battle found near a mountain villa.

But that was clearly not a fierce battle; it was a complete rout, so he didn't take it to heart.

At this moment, Lin Zhenjiang noticed that Qiu Zhenhe seemed to have something to say, but was clearly hesitant.

"Speak plainly!" he frowned and said coldly.

Qiu Zhenhe quickly said, "General Lin, I only have some guesses, but I'm not very sure."

"Just speak," Lin Zhenjiang said calmly.

"Then I'll speak frankly." Qiu Zhenhe considered his words carefully, then slowly said, "General Lin, are you aware that besides the prefectural city and five county towns, Liaoyun County also has a Thousand Mountain Manor?"

"Although Thousand Mountain Manor only has less than a thousand people, even I dare not underestimate them."

"Thousand Mountain Manor?"

Lin Zhenjiang thought of the manor he had encountered on his way to Peach Blossom County.

He had also found some traces of a battle there.

"Go on! Why don't you dare underestimate them!" he said.

Qiu Zhenhe continued, "However, the reason we underestimate Thousand Mountain Manor is because they have cultivators behind them! And their strength is considerable! I suspect they are at the fourth level of the Enlightenment Realm."

He then recounted all the information he knew about Thousand Mountain Manor.

He also mentioned Golden Dragon Temple and Xuanqing Temple, concluding, "Peach Blossom County is now under the control of Thousand Mountain Manor. They've even built a mountain god temple in the county town, intending to collect offerings."

"I see."

Upon hearing this, Lin Zhenjiang recalled why, when he arrived in Peach Blossom County, he was greeted not by the county magistrate, but by an unrelated person.

To be unafraid of the two giants, Golden Dragon Temple and Xuanqing Temple, the person behind Thousand Mountain Manor must be more than just an Enlightenment Realm cultivator.

Thinking back to the battle traces he found near Thousand Mountain Manor, and connecting the dots, Lin Zhenjiang seemed to understand something.

"It seems the disappearance of the Ink Pig is related to Thousand Mountain Manor."

Lin Zhenjiang spoke slowly, his mind already deep in thought.

When did such a powerful rogue cultivator arrive in Qingzhou?

How could he have kept it from all of them?!

There's something more to this than meets the eye.

He didn't impulsively go directly to Thousand Mountain Manor to question the Ink Pig's whereabouts, but instead looked at Qiu Zhenhe and said:

"Didn't you just say that someone in Tianyue County has a close relationship with Qianshan Manor? Summon them all; I want to inquire about everything related to Qianshan Manor!"

Qiu Zhenhe nodded quickly, understanding that Lin Zhenjiang was referring to He Liyuan and Chen Jianchi of Liyuan Escort Agency.

He had already investigated; Liyuan Escort Agency and Qianshan Manor were close, and He Liyuan had even visited Qianshan Manor with generous gifts recently.

Qiu Zhenhe had seen all of this, but he had no intention of getting involved.

Now that Lin Zhenjiang was interested in Qianshan Manor, it was a good opportunity to probe its secrets.


Chapter 104 The Uninvited Guest?

Not long after, Elder Jin went to Liyuan Escort Agency and found He Liyuan and Chen Jianchi.

Having been summoned, He Liyuan looked surprised and asked what was wrong before realizing it was about Qianshan Manor.

"A Yin God Realm expert wants to see us!?" Chen Jianchi's surprise quickly turned serious.

This was probably not good news.

Why would a Yin God Realm expert want to know about Qianshan Manor?

And should they tell us or not?

It seemed they had no other choice but to face a Yin God Realm expert.

"I can go alone. Let Xiao Chen stay behind," He Liyuan said.

"No, General Lin said, "Both of you must go!"

Elder Jin said coldly, his aura pressing down on the two.

He was subservient before a Yin God Realm expert, but at this moment, he would strike hard!

"Boss, I'll go with you," Chen Jianchi said from the side.

He Liyuan frowned, but given the situation, there was no point in arguing. So the two could only follow Elder Jin to the county government.

Upon arriving at the county government hall, Lin Zhenjiang was already seated in the main seat, sipping tea served by Qiu Zhenhe.

"General Lin, He Liyuan and Chen Jianchi have been brought here," Elder Jin said, bowing slightly.

"Greetings, General Lin!" Elder Jin had already told them the identities of those who wanted to see them on the way here.

"Hmm." Lin Zhenjiang nodded indifferently, stood up, and scrutinized the two men below, cutting to the chase: "From the moment you became allies with Qianshan Manor, tell us everything you know. Don't hide anything!"

His tone was cold and unyielding.

He Liyuan's lips twitched a few times, enduring the pressure from the Yin God Realm expert, but he finally gritted his teeth and said, "General Lin, although I am not talented, I believe in righteousness and honor when I roam the martial world. May I ask, General Lin, what is your purpose in inquiring about Thousand Mountain Manor?"

"Hmph!" Lin Zhenjiang snorted coldly upon hearing this, and his Yin God Realm aura erupted, rushing towards He Liyuan.

He Liyuan was struck by this aura and staggered back several steps, a dull pain in his chest, and a trickle of blood escaped from the corner of his mouth.

"Who do you think you are? How dare you question this general?" Lin Zhenjiang spoke coldly, then added, "Even if you tell this general whether your intentions towards Qianshan Manor are good or bad, what can you do about it?"

He Liyuan swallowed hard, the metallic taste of blood shooting straight to his head. Enduring the pain in his body, he said, "Qianshan Manor saved my life. If General Lin harbors ill intentions towards them, then even if I die here, I will never reveal a single word about Qianshan Manor!"

Chen Jianchi, standing nearby, witnessed his leader's injury, his eyes bloodshot.

"Heh, at least you have some backbone."

Lin Zhenjiang's expression softened slightly, and his gaze towards He Liyuan held a hint of appreciation.

Qiu Zhenhe coldly rebuked He Liyuan, "He, Chief Escort, discretion is the better part of valor. General Lin only asked you to reveal information about Qianshan Manor, not to betray them. There's no need for such resistance!"

He Liyuan, however, did not reply.

He remained steadfast in his conviction: if these people harbored ill intentions towards Qianshan Manor, he would rather die than utter a single word!

In the martial world, loyalty is paramount. If one loses loyalty, can one still be considered a member of the martial world?

Lin Zhenjiang waved his hand, stopping Qiu Zhenhe from continuing his reprimand, then said calmly, "Don't worry, this general wouldn't harbor ill will towards a village of fellow humans."

"This general traveled a long way from the state capital to deal with a fifth-tier demon general. But upon arriving in Liaoyun County, the fifth-tier demon general vanished, and the last direction it disappeared was Qianshan Manor."

He rarely offered such an explanation, mainly because He Liyuan's personality suited his tastes.

Such a loyal and righteous person is truly rare in this world.

If it were anyone else, he wouldn't bother explaining; he would have already used his methods to interrogate them.

"Is that really true?"

He Liyuan was half-convinced, but judging from Lin Zhenjiang's tone, it seemed to be true.

"Why would this general lie?" Lin Zhenjiang said coldly. His goodwill had its limits; he had already given He Liyuan considerable face.

He Liyuan looked at Lin Zhenjiang, lost in thought for a long time.

He had witnessed the power of the mountain god of Qianshan Manor firsthand; the deity's strength was unimaginable. Lin Zhenjiang posed no real threat to Qianshan Manor.

His main reason for not speaking was that he couldn't overcome his inner hurdle.

At this moment, Qiu Zhenhe stepped forward and said, "Head镖师 He, you should know that even if you don't speak, we can still find out everything about Qianshan Manor, understand?"

He Liyuan sighed, then said, "I don't know much about Qianshan Manor, and I won't say. If the general wants to know, I can take you to visit Qianshan Manor."

In the end, he still couldn't overcome his inner hurdle.

Who knew if Lin Zhenjiang was telling the truth? If he truly harbored ill intentions, wouldn't speaking out be betraying his 'savior'?

Even if he spoke, it wouldn't affect Thousand Mountain Manor. After all, the Mountain God's power was unimaginable.

But if he spoke, how could he face his brothers in Thousand Mountain Manor? How could he face the Mountain God who had saved his life?

Even if he died here on the spot, he would accept it.

Qiu Zhenhe's face darkened; he felt He Liyuan was truly ungrateful.

Just as he was about to reprimand and interrogate him, Lin Zhenjiang spoke up.

"Very well, since that's the case, this general will personally pay a visit to Thousand Mountain Manor! I want to see just how sacred that 'Mountain God' behind them really is!"

Lin Zhenjiang's tone was calm, revealing neither joy nor anger, making his meaning unfathomable.

"You two will come along too!" Lin Zhenjiang then looked at Qiu Zhenhe and the other man coldly.

Qiu Zhenhe nodded quickly.

Having made his decision, Lin Zhenjiang prepared to set off for Thousand Mountain Manor.

Supported by Chen Jianchi, He Liyuan, blood still trickling from the corner of his mouth, ignored it and whispered to Chen Jianchi, "Quick! Go to Qianshan Manor first and tell them what happened!"

Chen Jianchi nodded, knowing the urgency of the situation. He told He Liyuan to take care of himself, then turned and left the county government, mounting a fast horse and galloping out of the city.

All of this naturally didn't escape Lin Zhenjiang's notice, but he didn't stop him, for he disdained to do so!

After a while, He Liyuan finished recuperating, his injuries temporarily subsiding.

"General Lin, we can go now," he said, standing up and bowing his head.

Lin Zhenjiang simply nodded. Without another word, He Liyuan leaped into the air and headed towards Qianshan Manor.

Lin Zhenjiang, Qiu Zhen, and Elder Jin followed closely behind.

Meanwhile, at Qianshan Manor.

Bai Liang, Wu Ze, and Kong Wang were descending from the sacred mountain.

They had already placed the demon general's corpse in a cave halfway up the mountain.


Chapter 105: Scared Them Away!

The cave, adjacent to a spiritual vein, possessed incredibly rich spiritual energy. Combined with the divine aura of the sacred mountain itself, this allowed it to purify the violent power within the demon general's flesh and blood, making it accessible even to ordinary people.

This was Bai Liang and Wu Ze's first time climbing the sacred mountain.

Everything on the mountain was unfamiliar to them. Along the way, they discovered many spiritual herbs slowly growing.

Everything was so vibrant and full of life!

What shocked them most was the cave halfway up the mountain. Before entering, they hadn't thought much of it.

But as they ventured deeper into the cave, it opened up dramatically, and they were immediately enveloped by a spiritual energy so dense it felt almost viscous.

This was practically a blessed land for cultivation. A single breath brought an incomparably pure and abundant spiritual energy into their mouths and noses.

It wouldn't be an exaggeration to say that one could cultivate simply by breathing!

The two found it hard to believe that such a place with such abundant spiritual energy existed in the world.

But considering they were on a sacred mountain, it made perfect sense.

Bai Liang exclaimed, "Only on the sacred mountain could such a wondrous place exist!"

Wu Ze wholeheartedly agreed, only regretting that they hadn't dared linger.

After placing the demon's corpse in the cave, they turned and descended the mountain.

Kong Wang said, "Actually, we could have stayed a little longer. Cultivating on the sacred mountain is ten times faster than down below."

Since descending, he hadn't climbed the sacred mountain again, and now he even missed the incredibly rich spiritual energy in the cave.

Wu Ze shook his head, "The Mountain God's permission to ascend the sacred mountain is already a great honor; how could we dare be greedy?"

Bai Liang nodded in agreement.

In truth, they too longed to cultivate on the sacred mountain.

But the Mountain God always held the highest place in their hearts.

Kong Wang chanted a Buddhist prayer and said nothing more.

After descending the mountain, the villagers crowded around, eagerly inquiring about the situation on the sacred mountain.

"Master Bai, did you see the Mountain God on the sacred mountain?"

"Is the Mountain God a kind and benevolent old immortal?"

"Master Bai, is there a celestial court standing tall on the sacred mountain, with divine pavilions scattered among them, filled with ethereal energy?"

A jumble of questions followed, all reflecting the villagers' imaginations of the sacred mountain.

Bai Liang smiled and explained to the villagers that they hadn't seen the Mountain God, and he didn't know if there was a celestial court or divine pavilions on the mountain, as they had only reached the halfway point.

As for the difference between the upper and lower parts of the mountain, the difference was enormous. The spiritual energy was incredibly dense, and there was a previously unheard-of blessed land where even breathing was comparable to cultivation!

Upon hearing this, everyone felt an overwhelming longing.

A blessed land where breathing was cultivation—they had never dared to dream of such a thing before!

Truly worthy of being called a sacred mountain, possessing such a wondrous blessed land!

At this moment, Du Tiandao approached Bai Liang with a grave expression and whispered a few words in his ear.

Bai Liang's brows furrowed immediately. He then waved for the villagers to leave and led Du Tiandao and the others to the temple.

Upon arriving at the temple, they found Chen Jianchi anxiously waiting there.

"Brother Chen, what exactly is going on?" Bai Liang asked, his brow furrowed.

Upon seeing the arrival of the people, Chen Jianchi breathed a sigh of relief and hurriedly said, "A Yin God Realm expert has arrived in the state city. He's got his eye on your Thousand Mountain Manor!"

He recounted the whole story, concluding with apologetic words, "Manor Lord Bai, Manor Lord Wu, our leader had no choice. He did his best against a Yin God Realm expert."

The three of them exchanged glances after hearing this.

Wu Ze patted Chen Jianchi on the shoulder and said, "It's alright. We can easily find out about Thousand Mountain Manor's situation. Even if Chief Escort He tells us everything, it won't matter."

After comforting him, he turned to Bai Liang and asked, "Is it because of that fifth-tier demon general?"

Bai Liang nodded, "That's right. The Yin God Realm expert sent by the imperial court is here to confront the fifth-tier demon general. I was wondering why a fifth-tier demon general could break free of his restraints; it turns out he just broke through."

Completely understanding, Bai Liang was no longer in a hurry.

With the imperial court sending someone, everything would be easier to manage; at least they weren't the kind who came with malicious hatred.

It's estimated that this Yin God Realm cultivator is curious about the whereabouts of the fifth-tier demon general and the power behind Thousand Mountain Manor.

"Let him come," Bai Liang said calmly.

However, just as he finished speaking, someone rushed in to report that cultivators had come to visit outside the manor!

The group exchanged glances, and Wu Ze said, "Let's go!"

They quickly arrived outside Thousand Mountain Manor and saw four people in mid-air.

He Liyuan, who was at the front, landed and walked up with a somewhat ashamed expression, cupping his fists and saying, "Sect Masters, I... did my best!"

"He Liyuan, the镖头 (bodyguard/escort), don't blame yourself," Bai Liang comforted him, but didn't say anything more.

There were more pressing matters to deal with.

Wu Ze looked at the three who hadn't landed yet and cupped his hands, saying, "I am Wu Ze, the Master of Thousand Mountain Manor. May I ask what brings you fellow cultivators here?"

"General Lin has arrived; why don't you pay your respects?" Qiu Zhenhe said calmly.

Wu Ze feigned surprise, then hurriedly bowed and asked, "May I ask which esteemed person this is?"

"Lin Zhenjiang, Deputy General of the Xuanlong Army of Qingzhou!"

Qiu Zhenhe revealed Lin Zhenjiang's identity.

"Greetings, General Lin!" Wu Ze and Bai Liang exchanged a glance, then both bowed respectfully.

As for Kong Wang, who had followed, he remained motionless.

In the current Great Chu Dynasty, Buddhist and Taoist sects enjoyed special privileges and were exempt from bowing to court officials.

Lin Zhenjiang glanced at Kong Wang a few times, releasing his Yin Shen realm divine sense. He felt something was off about this monk, but couldn't quite put his finger on it.

However, Kong Wang's realm was only the Tongfa realm, so he only glanced at him a few more times and didn't pay much attention.

These people surprised him, but they weren't surprised after Qiu Zhenhe revealed his identity.

This was understandable, since Chen Jianchi had already come to inform him beforehand.

But these three, facing him, a Yin God Realm expert, remained neither servile nor arrogant. They appeared to show ample courtesy, yet none displayed any fear of someone in a higher position.

This surprised him greatly. It seemed Thousand Mountain Manor was not as simple as he had imagined. The person behind it was undoubtedly a Yin God Realm expert!

“General Lin, what brings you here?” Bai Liang stepped forward and asked.

Lin Zhenjiang remained calm and said indifferently, “To see the person behind Thousand Mountain Manor and inquire about the Ink Pig!”

His tone was extremely arrogant, as if he looked down on Thousand Mountain Manor.

His Yin Spirit Realm aura slowly spread out, pressing down on the three of them.

This was something high-level cultivators loved to do.

Bai Liang and the others felt the oppressive aura, their breathing momentarily halting, but they quickly regained their composure.

They thought to themselves, "This man really is here for a fifth-tier demon general."

Bai Liang was about to reply when Li Yue, who had been watching the whole scene, couldn't hold back any longer.

This Lin Zhenjiang is putting on airs like this on his turf?

Releasing his aura to intimidate his subordinates, and even specifically asking to see him by name—does he really think his sacred mountain is so indifferent?


Chapter 106 The physical body remains, the primordial spirit departs!

In Li Yue's heart, he didn't care about the imperial court at all.

In his eyes, Lin Zhenjiang was nothing more than a Yin God Realm cultivator; imperial status meant nothing to him!

Lin Zhenjiang looked down on him arrogantly. Even if the backer of Thousand Mountain Manor truly was a Yin God Realm cultivator, he wouldn't be afraid in the slightest.

As for more powerful beings, he had never even considered them.

Because Yang Gods couldn't leave the capital, and demons above the sixth rank couldn't enter the territory.

Therefore, the current ceiling of combat power within the Great Chu was locked at the fifth rank.

As fellow fifth-rank cultivators, what did he have to fear?

But this thought only lasted for a moment. The next second, his expression suddenly changed!

The usually unmoving divine mountain trembled slightly at this moment, as if it had been angered.

Following this, an overwhelming divine might pressed down on everyone's hearts, causing everyone to hold their breath.

Even Lin Zhenjiang, a Yin God Realm cultivator, was no exception.

"What...what is this?!" Qiu Zhenhe's expression changed drastically, his heart pounding, feeling a crisis he had never experienced before!

This was the prelude to impending doom!

Lin Zhenjiang's expression had long since lost its previous cold arrogance, replaced by horror!

Among those present, he possessed the highest cultivation level and was the most capable of sensing the terrifying power of this divine might.

He had only experienced this oppressive aura when he went to the capital to meet the Emperor.

But this was merely a remote border region of Liaoyun County in Qingzhou. How could such terrifying divine might exist here?!

This overwhelming sense of oppression was even more suffocating than that of the imperial palace in the capital!

Lin Zhenjiang's heart sank. He knew he had kicked a hornet's nest, and this hornet's nest was forged from Nine Heavens Meteorite Iron.

Without hesitation, he suddenly unleashed all his strength, blasting Qiu Zhenhe and the others away, and tried to escape.

"Trying to leave after all that?"

Li Yue thought to himself, unleashing his Dharma Body technique. A golden giant, bathed in divine light, descended from the sky, like a deity descending to earth.

This scene stunned everyone!

Qiu Zhen and Elder Jin stared wide-eyed at the sky, completely terrified.

"Boundless... Boundless Heavenly Venerable! What kind of being is this? It possesses such terrifying power!"

Elder Jin exclaimed, speechless. He had seen countless wonders in his life, but he had never seen such a wondrous golden giant.

He Liyuan swallowed hard, speechless with shock.

"This... is this the mountain god worshipped by Thousand Mountain Manor? A true deity!"

His eyes gleamed as he murmured to himself.

Meanwhile, Bai Liang and the other two, seeing the golden giant descend from the sky, looked astonished, but were also incredibly excited.

The mountain god had manifested his power; this trouble could be easily resolved!

Lin Zhenjiang, who had fled more than three miles away, paled drastically. Looking back, he nearly stumbled in shock.

What the hell was that?

A golden giant, dozens of feet tall, radiating an incredibly terrifying aura; a single glance instilled fear!

"Senior! I meant no offense, please spare my life!"

Lin Zhenjiang cried out, gritting his teeth and fleeing with all his might.

Li Yue acted as if he hadn't heard him. Controlling the golden giant, he swung his paw from a distance of over a thousand meters and struck.

Lin Zhenjiang breathed a sigh of relief upon seeing this.

An attack from this distance wouldn't be very powerful, even for a Yang Shen Realm expert.

It seems this senior was holding back.

But soon, Lin Zhenjiang realized how ignorant he had been!

The golden giant's palm print appeared behind him in the blink of an eye, its power undiminished, even stronger!

"Not good!" Lin Zhenjiang shouted!

He mustered all his strength, desperately trying to block the attack, while frantically pulling out various magical artifacts.

There were spears, golden armor, ironwood shields, large seals, and more.

These artifacts were at least fourth-tier, but they were shattered into pieces by the golden palm print in less than a second.

Lin Zhenjiang was in excruciating pain, but with his life hanging in the balance, he couldn't care less about these things now.

After all the artifacts were destroyed, the palm print's power had only been reduced by twenty percent.

He then crossed his arms, protecting his chest, simultaneously unleashing all his life-saving trump cards, hoping to save his life.

"Bang—"

The golden palm print struck Lin Zhenjiang. His trump cards, on which he had placed his hopes, were utterly useless, instantly dissolved by the golden light.

He himself didn't even have time to scream before his body was shattered on the spot. Before his flesh and blood could even fall to the ground, they were melted into ashes.

With a single strike, a Yin God Realm expert was reduced to ashes!

Qiu Zhen and the others, who witnessed the entire process, were petrified, standing rooted to the spot, too afraid to move.

A Yin God Realm expert who looked down upon everything... just like that, he died?

So many magical artifacts and life-saving trump cards, and he couldn't even withstand a single blow?

Instantly, cold sweat poured down their bodies, their hearts filled with immense fear. They looked at the distant golden giant, trembling, terrified that the golden giant would turn around and attack them at any moment.

Fortunately, the golden giant slowly dissipated after striking out with its palm.

This brought a sigh of relief to the two men.

He Liyuan never imagined that the Yin God Realm expert, who had rendered him speechless with just a few words, couldn't even withstand a single move from the Mountain God.

This was a true deity!

Cultivators and mortals were like ants in His eyes!

Li Yue withdrew his Dharma Body and Heaven, and dealt with Lin Zhenjiang simply as punishment.

Lin Zhenjiang didn't die; his physical body was shattered.

Cultivators who reach the Yin God Realm condense a primordial spirit. As long as the primordial spirit survives, they can live.

Therefore, at the moment his physical body shattered, Lin Zhenjiang's primordial spirit seized the opportunity to escape, but at a terrible cost.

Li Yue naturally saw this, but didn't finish him off.

This time, his action was merely to punish someone and make an example of them.

The sacred mountain is not a place where just anyone can dare to offend!

Bai Liang and Wu Ze hadn't expected the Mountain God to directly intervene and kill the court's Yin God Realm expert.

Kong Wang's eyes flashed with golden light; he had realized that Lin Zhenjiang wasn't dead, but his primordial spirit had already departed.

Qiu Zhen and the other two were also present, standing rooted to the spot, trembling with fear, cold sweat pouring down their bodies, completely unable to move.

Bai Liang stepped forward and smiled, "Gentlemen, General Lin has left. Is there anything else you need?"

"I...can we be alright?" Qiu Zhen took a deep breath and forced a smile.

"Of course," Bai Liang said.

Upon hearing the word "allow," Qiu Zhenhe immediately breathed a sigh of relief.

But then Bai Liang added, "However..."

Qiu Zhenhe's heart leaped into his throat again.

"Our Qianshan Manor doesn't have many people. We might go to Tianyue County to recruit talent. What does Lord Qiu think?"

Bai Liang said slowly.

Just now, He Liyuan had come over and explained Qiu Zhenhe's and Bai Liang's identities to him.


Chapter 107 is still above the Yang Shen level!

Knowing that the man before him was the de facto ruler of Tianyue County, Bai Liang naturally wouldn't let this opportunity slip by.

Using the Mountain God's remaining influence, he directly pressured Qiu Zhenhe, outlining plans for the future development of Qianshan Manor.

Qiu Zhenhe dared not refuse, quickly replying, "Reclaiming wasteland and expanding the borders has always been a policy supported by the imperial court; how could I dare not support it? If you need men, just come to Tianyue County!"

"Then I'll have to trouble you from now on, Lord Qiu," Bai Liang said calmly.

After giving them another warning, he let the two leave.

Qiu Zhenhe and Boss Jin breathed a sigh of relief, not daring to linger for a moment, and turned to leave swiftly.

At that moment, they swore in their hearts that whoever wanted to come to Qianshan Manor could come, but they would never return!

The psychological trauma inflicted by that golden giant was truly terrifying.

The two didn't dare speak along the way, until they were only ten miles from Tianyue County when Qiu Zhenhe finally let out a sigh of relief.

"What...what powerful being is behind Thousand Mountain Manor?"

Lin Zhenjiang, a Yin God Realm cultivator, couldn't even withstand a single blow; no one would believe it if word got out.

Old Jin sighed, his eyes filled with deep fear, and said, "Now I believe the rumors about Thousand Mountain Manor. The mountain god they worship...is probably real!"

The divine might emanating from the golden giant pressed directly on the mind, impossible to resist.

Cultivators simply couldn't do that!

"Mountain...god!?" Qiu Zhenhe murmured to himself, repeating the word, then shuddered.

The two fell silent.

Whether that powerful being was a deity or not, it was not someone they could afford to provoke.

That terrifying aura had already surpassed the Yin God Realm; within Qingzhou, who could contend with it?

Meanwhile, at this moment, more than a hundred miles away from the sacred mountain, in a remote mountain valley...

A phantom floated in a dark corner.

He could vaguely make out that the illusory figure resembled Lin Zhenjiang.

However, Lin Zhenjiang was currently in a Yin Spirit state, and his primordial spirit was unstable, showing signs of dissipating at any moment.

His face was incredibly pale, his previous arrogance gone, replaced by fear.

"Impossible! Impossible! How could Qingzhou possibly have such a powerful being?" Lin Zhenjiang muttered to himself.

Even now, he still couldn't believe it.

After the Tianshui Pact, within the Great Chu territory, Yang Spirits were not allowed to leave the capital, and sixth-rank experts were not allowed to enter.

This resulted in the entire Great Chu having a fifth-rank expert as the ceiling of combat power; no expert above the sixth rank had ever appeared.

After all, the Tianshui Pact had only been in effect for two years; it was impossible for both sides to break the pact so quickly.

"Is it a demon? Or a monster? Did they break the pact!?" Lin Zhenjiang wondered, but ultimately dismissed all possibilities.

Whether it was a demon or a monster, their aura and power couldn't possibly be like that.

The golden giant's unique divine might was too extraordinary!

He had only sensed a similar aura in one place: the altar of the gods.

Considering the rumors of Thousand Mountain Manor worshipping the mountain god, Lin Zhenjiang's heart surged with a tremendous wave of emotion!

"Hiss! Could it be... a god?!"

He gasped sharply, somewhat horrified by his own audacious thought.

But whether it was a god or not, he was definitely not going to seek revenge.

The golden giant's displayed strength was not only at the Yang God Realm, but even surpassed it!

The transition from the sixth to the seventh rank is a huge hurdle; cultivators above the seventh rank are already high-level combatants!

Furthermore, he understood that his opponent had already shown mercy.

They only used one move, not finishing him off.

Lin Zhenjiang took a deep breath. This trip to Liaoyun County had been a complete disaster.

As for the black pig, without a doubt, it had already been annihilated by this seemingly divine being.

The traces he initially discovered were probably where the black pig had died.

"My Yin God is unstable; I can only wait until nightfall to continue my journey."

Looking at the blazing sun outside, Lin Zhenjiang sighed.

The Yin God Realm can only patrol at night; to separate one's primordial spirit during the day, one must be at the Yang God Realm.

He forcibly forced his primordial spirit to flee a hundred miles during the day, exhausting his life force, hoping for only a sliver of survival.

His physical body was destroyed, and his primordial spirit was severely injured.

It would take several years for him to fully recover.

But he had ultimately survived.

He needed to return to Qianshan Manor as soon as possible to inform the General.

Whether the General should report to the Imperial Court was up to him.

However, before returning to the prefecture, he needed to go to Liaoyun County.

Otherwise, in his current state, he wouldn't be able to return to the prefecture; his primordial spirit would disintegrate.

..........

Qianshan Manor.

That day, the Mountain God manifested twice, and everyone was thrilled, especially the villagers of Qianshan Manor.

Everyone was incredibly excited; the threshold of the temple was worn down by a centimeter.

"See? I told you our Mountain God is invincible! No demons can come; they all die!"

A villager boasted proudly to the laborer, his tone full of pride.

Several laborers surrounded him, their faces full of flattery, nodding repeatedly.

"Yes, yes! The Mountain God truly lives up to his divine status. Slaying demons is like crushing an ant; he's incredibly powerful!"

"I really envy Brother Zhang for being able to live in Thousand Mountain Manor. He has no worries about any danger!"

"Indeed, with the Mountain God's protection, what demons in this world can possibly harm Thousand Mountain Manor?"

The villagers of Thousand Mountain Manor were flattered, making them feel quite pleased.

"Brother Zhang, could you perhaps let us stay in Thousand Mountain Manor as well?" The laborers finally revealed their purpose, their tone full of anticipation.

The villagers, who had been flattered, suddenly sobered up, coughed, and said awkwardly, "That depends on the two Manor Lords' wishes; I can't make that decision."

"Then could you please go and speak to the Manor Lord, Brother Zhang?" one of the laborers said, stuffing a small bag into the villager's hand.

The villager was startled and quickly threw the small bag on the ground, glancing around to make sure no one was watching before letting out a sigh of relief.

"You're trying to get me killed!" the villager said angrily, turning and running away without daring to linger.

The rules of Qianshan Manor are very strict!

If the patrol team saw him accepting money from outsiders, he'd be in big trouble.

While it wouldn't be a death sentence, being expelled from Qianshan Manor would be the same as being dead.

So he simply left, avoiding contact with these people with impure intentions!

The laborers exchanged bewildered glances. They hadn't expected the villager to refuse their bribe, even cursing them for it, and then leave.

"What do we do now?" one of them asked.

Seeing the mountain god's intervention, they genuinely wanted to stay at Thousand Mountain Manor.

With divine protection, it was infinitely safer than the county town!

"Since the villagers won't accept it, let's go directly to their manor lord!" one of the laborers said decisively.

He had already decided that in a place protected by the gods, even at a great cost, he would try to stay.


Chapter 108 Thousand Mountain Town, Mountain Tower!

Bai Liang, who was handling some business, was approached by a group of laborers.

Seeing such a large group, he initially thought something had happened again.

However, after the lead laborer explained the reason, he smiled knowingly and said, "Of course! Thousand Mountain Manor welcomes everyone, as long as their character is decent!"

He was just worried about how to expand the population of Thousand Mountain Manor after its expansion was complete.

And here they were, coming to him of their own accord!

The laborers' faces lit up with joy.

"Thank you for taking us in, Master!" they thanked excitedly.

Bai Liang waved his hand, saying, "No need to thank me. You should thank the Mountain God. Thousand Mountain Manor has been able to stand firm in this chaotic world thanks to the Mountain God's protection."

"Yes, yes! We should thank the Mountain God!"

Upon hearing "Mountain God," the laborers' gazes immediately became respectful.

The divine intervention left a deep impression on everyone.

“Well, you can all focus on your work. Once everything outside is built and Qianshan Manor officially becomes Qianshan Town, you can live there!” Bai Liang said.

Hearing this, everyone was immediately motivated.

Working for others and working for your own future home are two completely different things.

After they relayed the news back, the laborers erupted in cheers.

Everyone worked with renewed vigor, putting in tremendous effort to build Qianshan Town as soon as possible!

Moreover, Bai Liang promised not only that they could live in Qianshan Manor, but also that their families and relatives could come along.

Although there were only four thousand laborers, once they brought their entire families, it would likely drain half of Peach Blossom County.

“It seems the population problem can be solved for now,” Wu Ze said with a smile to Bai Liang after learning all this.

Bai Liang nodded in agreement, saying, "This is just the first wave. Qian Ke has also started working in Peach Blossom County. My initial estimate is that they'll drain 80% of Peach Blossom County's population!"

He had previously estimated that while the expansion of Qianshan Town wouldn't be huge, it could still accommodate 80,000 people.

The main problem is that you can't build it up first and then attract people.

That would be too slow.

The best method is to attract people directly and let them participate in the construction. This way, Qianshan Town can develop rapidly.

Wu Ze nodded, then added, "There's another problem: should we establish official positions? With so many people, it'll be difficult to manage."

"That's indeed a problem," Bai Liang nodded.

If Qianshan Manor becomes Qianshan Town, then the two of them will become town mayors.

The positions of those below will also change.

After much deliberation, Bai Liang finally decided to adopt the official position system of the Great Chu Dynasty.

Using it directly would be convenient and quick, and the villagers would adapt more easily.

If the population explodes later and the town is upgraded to a county, the imperial system can still be followed.

Wu Ze couldn't help but wonder, what if this development continues to improve and reaches the scale of a prefecture, or even a state?

Wouldn't that constitute a rebellion?

Right now, the scale is small enough that the imperial court can turn a blind eye. But once it reaches the prefecture level, even if the officials in the court are muddle-headed, they should take notice.

By then, confrontation with the imperial court would be inevitable.

It seemed that the only path left for them was to rebel against the court?

Wu Ze found the idea absurd and quickly shook his head, banishing the thought from his mind.

That was still a long way off. Thousand Mountain Manor hadn't even reached the size of a town yet, let alone a prefecture-level city.

At this moment, He Liyuan brought Chen Jianchi over to say goodbye.

"My two masters, I will not stay any longer and will return now."

He Liyuan cupped his hands in greeting.

They had been brought to Qianshan Manor; there were still a lot of matters to attend to in Tianyue County.

However, witnessing the Mountain God's two miraculous manifestations on this trip made it worthwhile!

"Take care, Chief He, come again when you have time!"

Bai Liang saw the two off.

Before leaving, He Liyuan turned back and said, "Master Bai, I've made up my mind. This time when I return, I will disband Liyuan Escort Agency and then bring my wife and children to join Qianshan Manor."

His tone was solemn, clearly indicating that he had made up his mind.

Chen Jianchi stood to the side, stunned.

He Liyuan had decided to join Qianshan Manor? How come he didn't know?

"Then I'm coming too!" he quickly chimed in.

Actually, Chen Jianchi had had this idea before, but loyalty to the martial world kept him following He Liyuan.

Now that He Liyuan had decided to join Qianshan Manor, he was naturally happy to go with him.

Bai Liang's eyes lit up, and he smiled, "Brother He, then we're on the same side from now on!"

Cultivators at the Profound Realm are exactly what Thousand Mountain Manor desperately needs right now.

After He Liyuan and his companion left, Bai Liang informed Wu Ze of this news.

Wu Ze was delighted upon hearing this.

Since Thousand Mountain Manor was renamed Thousand Mountain Town, it was precisely at a time of manpower shortage. He Liyuan's willingness to join was a good thing.

Over the next ten days or so, thanks to the hard work of more than four thousand laborers, the initial construction of Thousand Mountain Town was completed.

During these days, everyone's enthusiasm far exceeded Bai Liang's expectations.

Sometimes, he asked them to rest, but they refused, saying that the sooner they finished, the sooner they could move in and bring their families over.

Bai Liang couldn't do anything about it. All he could do was take some of the 'spiritual rice' grown by Uncle Wu, mix it with the other ordinary rice, cook it, and give it to the laborers as rations.

With the addition of the 'spiritual rice,' the laborers' nutrition was naturally adequate, and they even gained some strength than usual.

This is one of the reasons why the initial construction of Qianshan Town was completed within half a month.

Bai Liang and Wu Ze came to inspect the site. As for Kong Wang, he had been cultivating indoors ever since the day the Mountain God manifested twice.

Bai Liang naturally didn't disturb him.

The current Qianshan Manor—no, it should be called Qianshan Town—is now.

Its area is more than ten times that of the original Qianshan Manor.

Several main roads are clearly visible, the ground compacted with huge stones, while the main road is paved with blue bricks.

Houses are situated throughout, most of them wooden houses, simple but quite sturdy.

There are also some brick and tile houses, most of which are shops along the main roads.

With Qianshan Manor expanded into Qianshan Town, commerce is naturally essential.

The largest new building is the core office of Qianshan Town.

It is located near the original Qianshan Manor, less than 500 meters from the temple.

After discussing it, Bai Liang and Wu Ze named it Shanyue Tower.

From then on, all affairs, large and small, of Qianshan Town would be handled from Shanyue Tower.

Six stories high, Shanyue Tower occupied more than an acre of land, a truly magnificent building.

It could easily accommodate several hundred people.

At this moment, Bai Liang and Wu Ze were on the top floor of Shanyue Tower, observing the newly established Qianshan Town.


Chapter 109 Uprising, External Threat!

"It's finally finished! It wasn't easy!" Wu Ze exclaimed, looking at the brand-new scene below with a somewhat dazed expression.

Not long ago, Qianshan Manor was just a small village with only a dozen or so houses.

Now, it's a large town with over a thousand buildings.

"It really wasn't easy!" Bai Liang echoed with the same sentiment.

After so many days of hard work and countless sums of money flowing out, Qianshan Manor has now officially become Qianshan Town!

"Brother He has also contacted many merchants in Tianyue County, and quite a few have agreed to open shops here."

"Wu Ze spoke up.

Two days ago, He Liyuan had already brought his wife and children to Qianshan Town."

They naturally welcomed He Liyuan and his family warmly and made sure they were settled in.

Then Bai Liang entrusted He Liyuan with the task of returning to Tianyue County to see if he could persuade some merchants to do business in Qianshan Manor.

Liyuan Escort Agency had many connections in Tianyue County, and with He Liyuan's involvement, merchants would naturally give him face.

"Well, once everyone has brought the people from Taohua County over, then Qianshan Town can truly be called a town!" Bai Liang said with a smile.

The more than four thousand laborers had already chosen houses in Qianshan Town, waiting only to return and bring their families.

"Once we finish all these things, we can focus on our cultivation."

Wu Ze spoke up, then added, "These mundane matters have consumed too much of our energy. Kong Wang is right; our priority is to improve our strength, not to waste time on mundane affairs."

Kong Wang never interfered in the affairs of Qianshan Town; in his eyes, it seemed there was only cultivation.

Even the young girl, Li Xue, had been diligently cultivating, and hadn't been seen for a long time.

"I had the same idea," Bai Liang said, "but we also need to cultivate talent capable of managing the affairs of Qianshan Town, so that we can cultivate in peace."

Wu Ze nodded; that would take time.

At this moment, someone came up to the sixth floor and approached the two.

"Brother Wu, Brother Bai, things have been arranged," Chen Jianchi said as he approached.

"What did Qiu Zhenhe say?" Bai Liang asked.

Chen Jianchi replied, "He didn't say anything, but the two newly arrived county garrison commanders asked me many questions, but in the end, they didn't say anything either. Qiu Zhenhe said that these two are Lin Zhenjiang's men."

He was referring to the Lin brothers. After arriving in Tianyue County, these two, following Ximen Longyu's instructions, directly took control of the county garrison to oppose Qiu Zhenhe.

But Qiu Zhen, having experienced the Qianshan Manor incident, was already preoccupied and couldn't be bothered with the Lin brothers.

He was even already planning how to leave Liaoyun County, this godforsaken place; whoever wanted Tianyue County could have it.

However, the prefectural government had issued a strict order, and leaving to return to the prefectural city wouldn't be so easy.

"Good that you have no objections. As for the Lin brothers, ignore them," Bai Liang said indifferently.

Lin Zhenjiang was slapped to pieces by the Mountain God; what were these Lin brothers?

They posed no threat to Qianshan Town whatsoever.

"Since everything is arranged, let's prepare and have them all come," Bai Liang said to Chen Jianchi.

Chen Jianchi nodded: "Okay, I'll go now."

With that, he turned and left.

"Won't letting people from Tianyue County do business in Qianshan Town be like inviting wolves into the house?" Wu Ze frowned.

"Not at all. Merchants are profit-driven; all they know is how to make money. Besides, under the watchful eye of the Mountain God, who can cause any trouble?"

Bai Liang said confidently.

Everyone has their own expertise. Since there's no one skilled in business in Qianshan Town, we'll have to find them from outside.

These were all minor matters, and Bai Liang didn't pay much attention to them.

What did concern him was a recent piece of news!

Something had happened in Baijiang Prefecture!

Three days ago, He Liyuan hurriedly brought news from a friend he'd met while working as a镖师 (bodyguard/escort).

Demons had rampaged through Baijiang Prefecture, sweeping across half the prefecture overnight, with three county towns falling in succession.

It was said that the moonlight that night was stained crimson.

The land of Baijiang Prefecture was covered in blood; every river was dyed red—truly rivers of blood.

The population of the three prefectures reached a staggering ten million!

Ten million people, slaughtered by the demons in just three days and three nights!

Upon hearing this news, Bai Liang and Wu Ze were both shocked!

This could be considered the greatest casualty tragedy in the Great Chu Dynasty since the Tianshui Pact.

Ten million people, forty percent of Baijiang Prefecture's population, all gone just like that?

Moreover, along with them perished six or seven Yin God Realm cultivators, and countless other low-level cultivators!

Bai Liang couldn't understand why this had happened. Weren't both sides only fifth-tier experts?

With everyone at the same level, why was there such a one-sided situation?

Later, He Liyuan told him that two sixth-tier demonic creatures had appeared on the demon side.

These two sixth-tier demonic creatures had broken through to the sixth tier within Baijiang Prefecture, so it wasn't considered a violation of the Tianshui Pact.

Faced with sixth-tier demonic creatures, even the most powerful Yin God Realm cultivators were powerless to resist.

Overnight, half the prefecture fell!

In the end, it was only through the direct command of the Baijiang Prefecture government, bearing the prefectural seal and personally leading the charge, that countless amounts of good fortune were expended to stop the surging demons.

Otherwise, Baijiang Prefecture would have become the first major prefecture to fall to demons after the Tianshui Pact!

The news sent shockwaves through the imperial court!

The first reaction of the court officials was not to immediately send powerful figures to support the fight against the demons.

Instead, they seized upon the officials who had supported the Tianshui Pact and began to attack and purge them.

In an instant, the capital's court was in chaos, countless officials were implicated, and heads rolled at the Meridian Gate.

After hearing this, Bai Liang sighed heavily.

"With such a corrupt court, can the people still have any hope of survival?"

These were Bai Liang's words at the time.

Wu Ze listened in silence, a surge of anger welling up inside him.

"Just take care of yourselves," Bai Liang finally said, patting Wu Ze on the shoulder.

The Baijiang Prefecture incident has a huge impact!

Because Qingzhou and Baijiang Prefecture are adjacent, those two sixth-tier demons might turn around and attack Qingzhou tomorrow.

And what's most alarming is that if the demons of Baijiang Prefecture can break through to the sixth tier, what about the demons in other prefectures?

That's the most terrifying thing!

Two years have passed since the Tianshui Pact. The initial batch of fifth-tier demons who entered the Great Chu territory, after dormancy, have likely already seen many break through to the sixth tier.

On the other hand, the Great Chu is tightly bound by the treaty that Yang Shen cannot leave the capital!

Even if someone breaks through from the Yin God Realm to the Yang God Realm, they must return to the capital and cannot linger outside.

To put it bluntly, the strength of Great Chu is inferior to that of the demons. If Great Chu had a Saint, would the situation be like this?

The Demon Saints and Monster Saints are eyeing us covetously from the outside. If Great Chu dares to violate the Heavenly Water Agreement, they dare to lead a massive army and completely conquer the Great Chu Dynasty!


Chapter 110 Population Surge

For the past few days, Bai Liang had been troubled by this matter.

Next to Liaoyun County was Baijiang Prefecture.

If the demons of Baijiang Prefecture were to change their targets, Liaoyun County would be the first to suffer.

Qianshan Town had finally managed to develop; it couldn't afford any more trouble.

Bai Liang could only pray that the disaster in Baijiang Prefecture wouldn't spread to Qingzhou.

He wondered what the imperial court would do about the sixth-tier demon.

Sending a Yang Shen realm cultivator away from the capital was definitely out of the question.

Given the current weakness of the court officials and the emperor, it was absolutely impossible for them to completely turn against the demons.

The two major powers, Jinlong Temple and Xuanqing Temple, would also not agree to the Great Chu Dynasty violating the Tianshui Agreement.

After all, the original intention of signing the Tianshui Agreement was to give humanity some time to wait for several half-saints to break through and become saints, thus changing the situation.

"Now that the Sage has not yet appeared, how can we break the agreement with Tianshui?"

Therefore, the capital will likely continue to exercise restraint and will not take any major action.

Bai Liang shook his head, feeling incredibly frustrated.

...

Three more days passed.

Many people from Taohua County had begun to migrate to Qianshan Town and settle there.

The laborers' efforts to spread the word were far more effective than Qian Ke's preaching there.

People trusted their relatives and friends and remained wary of strangers.

Therefore, no matter how hard Qian Ke tried to preach, the effect was not significant.

In just a few days, the population of Qianshan Town had already surged to over five thousand, with many more on their way.

With such a large population, security forces needed to be doubled.

The patrol team, led by Du Tiandao, expanded from the original twenty-odd men to over a hundred.

They were all strong and capable men, carefully selected by Du Tiandao.

With a larger population, the number of merit points also increases.

In the past month, Li Yue's merit points have grown to over 300,000.

[Realm: Third-Tier Spirit Mountain]

[Merit: 350,520/1,000,000]

[Supernatural Powers: Mountain God's Blessing, Dharma Body of Heaven and Earth, Incense Resumption]

[Spell: Manifestation]

[Magic Weapons: Mountain-Suppressing Seal, Heart Mirror, Divine Weapon Forging Furnace]

At an average daily increase of seven to eight thousand merit points, it won't be long before he advances to the fourth tier.

After learning about the events in Baijiang Prefecture, Li Yue became eager to improve his strength as quickly as possible.

Although, currently, the strongest beings within the Great Chu territory are only sixth-tier demons, this is due to the restrictions of the Heavenly Water Pact.

But who knows when the demons might break the pact and launch a large-scale attack on the Great Chu Dynasty?

Li Yue's understanding of his own strength is rather vague.

With his current strength at the third tier of the Spirit Mountain realm, he could easily handle fifth and sixth tier cultivators and demons.

But there were still seventh and eighth tiers to go!

And among the demons, there was even one peak ninth-tier Saint Realm warrior.

He wasn't yet confident in facing peak ninth-tier Saint Realm warriors.

Therefore, he needed to improve his cultivation level as quickly as possible, at least reaching the fourth tier, to cope with the various variables to come.

Fortunately, Thousand Mountain Manor had now become Thousand Mountain Town.

Li Yue estimated that the population would exceed 20,000 within a month.

With so many people offering incense for him, he'll soon advance to the fourth rank.

Sending his divine sense down to Thousand Mountain Town, Li Xue's personality had changed drastically since the Zhao Yihe incident.

She was no longer as lively and cheerful as before, becoming very calm, spending all her time cultivating indoors.

It could be said that the combined cultivation time of Wu Ze and Bai Liang didn't even equal that of Li Xue alone.

Five days ago, Li Xue successfully broke through to the Tongfa Realm.

This cultivation speed was truly exceptional.

However, this was normal. After receiving the divine talisman, Li Xue's talent was already top-tier, and with significantly more cultivation time than others, her progress was naturally faster.

Thinking about the divine talisman Li Xue received, Li Yue didn't know whether it was a good thing or a bad thing.

Compared to Bai Liang and Wu Ze, Li Xue's divine talisman was very special.

Even he was somewhat stunned after bestowing the blessing upon her.

This was also the reason why he later stopped easily bestowing blessings upon others.

Divine talismans aren't always beneficial.

However, judging from the current situation, the effects on Li Xue after receiving the talisman are quite good.

A mere ten-year-old at the Tongfa Realm—there are only a handful in the capital.

Kong Wang's cultivation speed is also astonishing. After breaking through to the Tongfa Realm, he didn't stop there, quickly advancing to the late Tongfa Realm.

This speed led Bai Liang to believe that Kong Wang had also received a blessing from the Mountain God.

But in reality, he hadn't.

Kong Wang's rapid cultivation was due to his own unique characteristics.

Li Yue also planned to bestow a blessing upon Kong Wang, but he would wait until he broke through to the Ruxuan Realm.

He remembered Kong Wang saying that upon reaching the Ruxuan Realm, he would travel to the outside world.

Peach Blossom County.

The county town was much busier than usual during this period.

Many people were moving, carrying large and small bags; long lines of horse-drawn carriages and oxcarts stretched along the streets.

All were preparing to head to Qianshan Town.

Some villagers, not understanding, sneered, "Isn't that stupid? Did a donkey kick your head? Why not stay in the proper county seat and run off to some random town? Aren't you afraid of being attacked by demons?"

This was overheard by the laborer returning from Qianshan Town. He turned around and scoffed, "A frog in a well, only seeing the sky from the bottom. You should stay in Peach Blossom County then. Don't ever come to Qianshan Town again. If I see you, I'll give you a good beating!"

"Heh, what a load of crap, Qianshan Town! I wouldn't go even if you paid me!" the man sneered, his face full of disdain.

The laborer, too lazy to waste words on such people, turned and left the town with his wife and children.

There were many other villagers like this who didn't understand.

Meanwhile, news about Qianshan Manor and the mountain god's miraculous appearance also spread throughout Peach Blossom County.

This stirred up many people whose resolve was wavering.

Some wealthy farmers, seeing that nearly half the populace had fled, realized something was amiss and immediately went to the county government to report the situation, demanding that Master Zhang close the city gates and prevent the people from leaving.

But Master Zhang had no intention of paying them any attention and simply ordered his constables to drive them away.

Inside the Mountain God Temple in Peach Blossom County.

Qian Ke went to the back hall and found Qing Quan, who had been under house arrest there.

Qing Quan's complexion was somewhat better than before, but she was still extremely pale.

Moreover, due to the damage to her foundation, her current cultivation level was much lower than before.

She was almost back to the Refining Technique Realm.

"Master Qing Quan, how have you been?" Qian Ke greeted her politely.

But all he received in response was Qingquan's cold gaze.

"What is it? Speak plainly!" Qingquan said indifferently, his tone unfriendly.

Indeed, anyone who had been under house arrest for so long wouldn't be in a good mood.

"I've come to inform you of some news. Something has happened in Baijiang Prefecture. Overnight, three prefectures fell, rivers of blood flowed, and millions of people perished in the jaws of demons!"

Qian Ke said calmly.


Chapter 111 Qingzhou City

"What did you say?! Impossible! Baijiang Prefecture has dozens of Yin God Realm experts, stronger than your Qing Prefecture. How could it have fallen?"

Qingquan's calm expression finally changed, her tone becoming somewhat agitated.

Qianke had anticipated this reaction.

They already knew that Qingquan and her two siblings were from Baijiang Prefecture.

He was here on Bai Liang's orders to inquire about information regarding Baijiang Prefecture from Qingquan.

"Nothing is impossible. What use are more Yin God Realm experts if two sixth-tier demonic creatures appear in Baijiang Prefecture?" Qianke said slowly.

"Sixth-tier demonic creatures? That's even more impossible!" Qingquan still refused to believe it.
