Seeing this, Qianke could only explain the situation in detail.

Qingquan listened patiently, her expression shifting, finally turning somewhat grim.

"Do you know who those Yin God Realm experts who died were?" she asked.

Qian Ke shook his head: "I don't know."

"Let me go back! I want to go back to Baijiang Prefecture!" Suddenly, Qingquan raised his head, his tone pleading.

"Impossible. Unless the Master agrees, you can forget about leaving for now," Qian Ke said, shaking his head.

"How can I get him to agree?" Qingquan asked directly.

"Tell me what you know about Baijiang Prefecture first."

Qian Ke didn't answer, but instead asked.

"Okay, I'll tell you." Qingquan didn't hesitate and gave the general information about Baijiang Prefecture.

Baijiang Prefecture is smaller in area than Qing Prefecture, but its population is larger.

There are six prefectures in total.

Baijiang Prefecture, Meijiang Prefecture, Rongjiang Prefecture, Wushan Prefecture, Heyun Prefecture, and Zhijiang Prefecture.

Baijiang Prefecture is the prefecture where the state capital is located, and it is the core area of ​​Baijiang Prefecture.

Closest to Baijiang Prefecture are Wushan Prefecture and Rongjiang Prefecture.

The prefectures that were conquered by demons overnight are Meijiang Prefecture, Heyun Prefecture, and Zhijiang Prefecture.

Baijiang Prefecture is stronger than Qingzhou Prefecture, with dozens of Yin God Realm experts, including seven or eight at the peak of the Yin God Realm.

These individuals could break through to the sixth-tier Yang God Realm in an instant.

However, due to the restrictions of the Heavenly Water Pact, they could only suppress their cultivation levels and dare not break through.

This is why Qingquan initially disbelieved Qianke when he said Baijiang Prefecture had fallen.

With such formidable strength, demons could not shake it without a sixth-tier expert.

Qingquan and her companions hailed from the state capital, where the Xuanqing Temple's branches were also located.

She had a master and a senior uncle, both at the peak of the Yin God Realm!

Baijiang State differed from Qingzhou, where the state government and the general were evenly matched.

In Baijiang State, the Long family controlled both the state government and the general.

"That's all I know," Qingquan said.

Qianke noted everything Qingquan said, intending to write a letter back to Qianshan Town.

"By the way, I've been curious. Why did you travel all the way from Baijiang State to Liaoyun County in Qingzhou, this remote border region? Is it really for what the monk Sangui is plotting?" Qianke suddenly asked, remembering something.

Qingquan thought for a moment; there was no need to hide it now. She revealed the reason: "The world is in chaos, divine artifacts are changing hands, and a sage is being born! This is the divination my senior uncle Qian Kun performed. The divination indicates that the opportunity lies in Liaoyun County, Qingzhou!"

“Originally, we thought the monk Sangui was also after the opportunity, but now, looking back, it seems Senior Sister misunderstood.”

Qingquan said, her expression darkening.

If they hadn't misunderstood the divination, Senior Sister and the others wouldn't have died at the hands of the monk Sangui.

After this period of confinement, Qingquan had roughly understood what was happening.

If Uncle Qian Kun's divination was correct, and a sage truly was being born in Qingzhou, then it was very likely within Thousand Mountain Manor.

During her confinement, through observation, she increasingly felt that the 'Mountain God' behind Thousand Mountain Manor was far too mysterious and unpredictable.

She had initially thought it was just a rogue cultivator gathering offerings, who would soon suffer a backlash and die a violent death.

But now, so much time had passed, and the 'Mountain God' was not only alive, but even more powerful.

Adding to this various other strange occurrences, Qingquan was beginning to believe that this 'Mountain God' might truly be a mountain god!

"Divine artifacts changed, a sage reborn?" Qian Ke looked puzzled upon hearing this.

He thought to himself, these Taoist priests just love spouting nonsense.

What sage reborn? A deity descending to earth is a different story!

"I will convey your request to the two manor lords." After gathering information about Baijiang Prefecture, Qian Ke said this and turned to leave.

Back in the county government, he immediately wrote down everything Qingquan had said and had it sent to Qianshan Town.

........

About two thousand miles from the sacred mountain.

Here stood a massive city.

The city walls, tens of meters high, were all built of blue bricks, sturdy and durable, with many dark purple bloodstains still visible on them.

Proof that these walls had witnessed a bloody battle!

The city gate was over twenty meters high, with three large, elegant characters carved on it.

Qingzhou City!

This was the heart of Qingzhou, the capital city of Qingzhou!

More than ten Yin God Realm experts had gathered, along with several peak Yin God Realm powerhouses.

With them present, the demons within Qingzhou dared not act recklessly.

Qingzhou City was quite large, with sprawling city walls and countless pavilions and towers within.

On the streets, vendors hawked their wares, crowding both sides of the road, and people thronged the streets.

The taverns on both sides were filled with all sorts of patrons. Scholars in their robes, full of spirit, discussed poetry.

Men dressed in a江湖 (jianghu, a term referring to the martial arts world) style drank heartily, boldly recounting tales of chivalry and brotherhood.

In the brothels, women in revealing dresses danced gracefully, their figures alluring. The melodious sound of zithers added a touch of romance to the patrons.

Qingzhou City was a scene of unparalleled prosperity.

It seemed as if everything outside was irrelevant to those within the city.

Everyone drank and enjoyed themselves to the fullest, having a wonderful time.

Inside the General's residence.

The atmosphere here was the complete opposite of outside, filled with a chilling, murderous aura!

Rows of soldiers stood in perfect formation, their gazes all fixed on an elderly man with gray hair standing in the center.

The old man's face was wrinkled, but his eyes were bright and piercing, and he was full of vigor!

His gaze swept across the crowd, and not a single soldier dared to meet his eyes.

Finally, his gaze settled on a phantom figure directly below him in the center.

The phantom figure was none other than Lin Zhenjiang, who had returned in a disheveled state.

After escaping from the sacred mountain, his spirit was extremely weak, and he could only temporarily recuperate in the city of Liaoyun County.

Ximen Longyu provided him with a large quantity of nourishing elixirs to replenish his primordial spirit, which finally allowed him to recover some of his injuries.

After recuperating for over a month and regaining the ability for his primordial spirit to travel, he returned to the prefectural city and the general's mansion.

At this moment, his primordial spirit lowered its head in utter shame, unable to meet the general's gaze.

"Alright, the opponent's strength far surpasses ours. It's already an honor to spare your life!" the old man said, his voice somewhat hoarse but full of power.

"I have disgraced you, sir," Lin Zhenjiang said, raising his head in shame.


Chapter 112 Could there really be gods in this world?

"What does losing face matter? As long as you don't lose your life!" the old man said calmly.

He then took out a small wooden box from his sleeve and used his spiritual power to present it to Lin Zhenjiang.

"This is a Qi-stabilizing pill from Xuanqing Temple. Take it and use it to stabilize your primordial spirit. As for your physical body, we'll find the bald monk at Jinlong Temple when we have the chance, and have them make you a golden body."

However, Lin Zhenjiang hesitated after hearing this.

Everyone knew how filthy the golden bodies from Jinlong Temple were; normal people would avoid them like the plague.

Xiang Tiandao immediately saw through Lin Zhenjiang's thoughts and said calmly, "Don't worry, I will have them use demons to nurture the golden body. You don't need to worry."

"Thank you, General!"

Lin Zhenjiang immediately knelt down to express his gratitude.

"General Lin, why did you suffer such a humiliating defeat in Liaoyun County? Was the one who attacked you a demon or a monster?"

At this moment, a soldier of similar age to Lin Zhenjiang stepped forward and asked, staring at him.

The others also turned their gazes towards him.

They had just been summoned by the General and were unaware of what had happened to Lin Zhenjiang in Liaoyun County.

They only knew that Lin Zhenjiang had been ordered by the General to go to Liaoyun County to deal with the demon general.

Lin Zhenjiang did not answer immediately. Instead, he looked at Xiang Tiandao and, after receiving Xiang Tiandao's approval, replied, "Neither a demon nor a monster."

Upon hearing this, everyone frowned.

"Not a demon or monster, then it must be a human cultivator!? Who dares to attack an official of the imperial court? Are they courting death!?"

"Even the Golden Dragon Temple and Xuanqing Temple wouldn't dare to do this. Could it be a rogue cultivator?"

Everyone was puzzled.

The Great Chu Dynasty, though not as powerful as it once was, was not one to be trifled with.

Otherwise, how could it have ruled this land for so long?

To dare attack a court official, especially one at the Yin God Realm, one can only imagine the audacity of the attacker.

Aside from fearless, unbridled rogue cultivators, no one could think of anyone else who would dare to attack a court official.

Lin Zhenjiang paused for a moment, then replied, "It shouldn't be human either."

"What are you saying!?"

This made the others frown, wondering if they had misheard.

"Neither demon, nor monster, nor human, nor some kind of evil spirit," Lin Zhenjiang said, stating his guess: "It's very likely a deity!"

Silence fell over the room.

The crowd exchanged bewildered glances, their eyes filled with confusion. After a long silence, someone finally spoke up: "General Lin, could it be that your physical body has been destroyed, and your consciousness has been damaged as well?"

"My primordial spirit is injured; I need to recuperate. What deity? How could there possibly be a deity in this world?"

The group simply didn't believe Lin Zhenjiang's words.

They looked at the general, expecting him to reprimand Lin Zhenjiang, but to their surprise, the general simply closed his eyes, seemingly lost in thought.

What does this mean?

Does the General believe Lin Zhenjiang's words?!

"My primordial spirit is fine, and I'm not talking nonsense."

Lin Zhenjiang spoke, looking at everyone, "What kind of strength do you all think could destroy my physical body in one strike?"

The group thought for a moment, then answered, "To shatter the physical body in one strike, one would need at least the mid-stage of the Yang God realm."

"What if it's a thousand meters apart?" Lin Zhenjiang asked again.

Everyone was taken aback, then replied, "To destroy a Yin God Realm physical body with a single blow from a thousand meters away, that requires at least a seventh-tier expert."

After saying that, they realized what he meant and looked at Lin Zhenjiang in shock, saying incredulously, "You mean, in Liaoyun County, your physical body was destroyed by someone a thousand meters away?"

Lin Zhenjiang smiled wryly and slowly nodded.

"Hiss!"

A collective gasp filled the air.

To destroy a Yin God Realm physical body with a single blow from a thousand meters away—this was absolutely impossible for a Yang God Realm expert, even a peak Yang God Realm expert!

Only experts of a higher realm could do it!

Seventh tier!

That's already considered a true expert.

Severing a river with a single finger, comprehending divine abilities, possessing profound skills, one person could be worth a thousand troops!

How could such a powerful expert appear in that wretched place, Liaoyun County? This is very abnormal!

Lin Zhenjiang then recounted all the information he had gathered in Liaoyun County.

This included news he had asked Ximen Longyu to gather while he was recuperating in Liaoyun County.

It was all related to Qianshan Manor, and he had even investigated the backgrounds of Bai Liang and Wu Ze thoroughly.

One was a direct descendant of the Bai family in the capital, and the other had served in the military.

Finally, Lin Zhenjiang displayed a wound on his primordial spirit, where a faint golden light still lingered.

This golden light was very weak, but they felt its overwhelming divine might; the extreme pressure made them feel suffocated!

This aura was unlike anything they had ever encountered before; it was something no cultivator could possess.

For a moment, everyone present fell silent.

The Thousand Mountain Manor, which had appeared out of nowhere, had ceased its incense offerings, manifested its divine power several times, and now possessed this golden aura.

Could there truly be deities in this world?

After a moment, Xiang Tiandao broke the silence, saying, "Whether this 'Mountain God' is a god or a ghost, it's none of our business now. Since he wants to cease his incense offerings in Liaoyun County, then let him have it."

"But... Liaoyun County is our territory. Losing this county will put us at a disadvantage against the prefectural government," someone said.

Qingzhou has five counties; the General controls three, and the Prefectural Government controls two.

The General has always held the upper hand. Losing Liaoyun County would mean both sides control the same number of counties, which would significantly reduce their advantage.

"It doesn't matter. Counties are just unimportant bargaining chips. In the end, we still have to rely on strength to fight that Qiu thief." Xiang Tiandao's tone was calm, but he gave people a domineering aura.

Someone else spoke up: "If we hand over Liaoyun County to that 'Mountain God,' what if he becomes too powerful?"

"Isn't he powerful enough now?" Xiang Tiandao's words silenced the questioner.

Indeed, he's currently at the peak of the Yang God Realm, enough to dominate the entire Qingzhou.

His current presence in Liaoyun County is already a great favor.

"Actually, Liaoyun County is a hot potato right now. Giving it to that 'Mountain God' might not be a bad thing for us," Lin Zhenjiang suddenly said.

The crowd looked puzzled.

Someone seemed to have realized something, their expression subtly changing.

"Baijiang Prefecture,"

Xiang Tiandao said calmly.

Upon hearing these three words, everyone present, being intelligent, immediately understood, their eyes lighting up!

"I see, General, your insight is brilliant!"

"Liaoyun County borders Baijiang Prefecture. Now that Baijiang Prefecture is facing a major upheaval, Liaoyun County is also a potential threat. Rather than getting involved, it's better to let them clash and watch them fight it out!"

Everyone suddenly understood.

Liaoyun County is indeed a hot potato right now. The events in Baijiang Prefecture have already spread throughout the prefectures.

The only prefecture closest to Baijiang Prefecture is Qingzhou.

If the sixth-tier demon over there changes its target, then Liaoyun County will be the first to stand in its way!


Chapter 113 Establishing a School

Thus, Liaoyun County was given to the Mountain God, whose strength was at least at the peak of the sixth rank.

Facing the two sixth-rank demons of Baijiang Prefecture, it was difficult to predict the outcome.

However, everyone believed the Mountain God had a better chance of winning.

After all, he was very likely a deity, and his strength far surpassed theirs.

Xiang Tiandao immediately decided to relinquish control of Liaoyun County.

As for their people in Liaoyun County, they didn't need to withdraw; they could remain there as spies.

They should try to avoid conflict with the Mountain God's forces.

Xiang Tiandao didn't want to be enemies with this 'Mountain God'; it would be best to show him goodwill.

At least based on the current information, this 'Mountain God' seemed to be of the righteous path.

After the discussion, everyone left. Lin Zhenjiang returned to the recuperation room arranged for him by the general to take a Qi-strengthening pill to repair his primordial spirit.

Xiang Tiandao, on the other hand, went to his study.

He sat at his desk, took out paper and pen, and began to write a letter.

Several hundred words were quickly written.

He addressed the shadows, “Black Hawk, send this to the capital and personally deliver it to my father. Remember, keep your whereabouts a secret, and never reveal it!”

“Yes!” Black Hawk replied in a low voice, then picked up the letter and disappeared into the shadows.

...

In the prefectural government residence.

A scholar sat in the courtyard, drinking tea and reading, sometimes frowning, sometimes chuckling.

Across from him was a handsome monk, meditating with his eyes closed.

“Prefectural Governor, a scout has reported that Xiang Tiandao has abandoned Liaoyun County,” a servant announced.

“Oh?” The scholar put down his book and chuckled. “Just as I expected, that old fox Xiang Tiandao is trying to use that suddenly appearing ‘mountain god’ as a shield?”

Qiu He stood up, gazing in the direction of the general's residence, his eyes shifting, lost in thought.

He had known about Liaoyun County for a month.

It was Qiu Zhenhe who reported it.

“Prefect Qiu, what scheme are you plotting now?” The young monk opened his eyes and looked at Qiu He with a smile.

“Haha, what scheme? All perfectly legitimate plans!”

Qiu He smiled, picked up his tea, and drank it all in one gulp.

“Since Xiang Tiandao wants to abandon Liaoyun County to curry favor with that ‘Mountain God,’ then I’ll have someone inform the ‘Mountain God’ of Xiang Tiandao’s intentions. Let Xiang Tiandao’s attempt to curry favor backfire and alienate the other party!”

He sneered, easily devising a countermeasure.

“That’s a good plan,” the monk uttered, agreeing with Qiu He’s method.

Qiu He quickly gave the order, instructing someone to arrange the matter.

He was more than willing to do anything to thwart Xiang Tiandao’s intentions.

As for this ‘Mountain God,’ why would he believe him?

He was probably just a powerful rogue cultivator.

"That old codger Xiang Tiandao is really getting worse with age; he's even backing down because of this."

Qiu He thought with utter disdain.

"By the way, I had a junior brother who apparently died in Liaoyun County," the handsome monk suddenly said.

"Hmm? Brother Sanwu, are you talking about that Sangui who suffered a damaged foundation and was forced to retreat from the initial stage of cultivation?" Qiu He thought for a moment and asked.

"Exactly," Sanwu nodded.

"Brother Sanwu, are you perhaps seeking revenge for your junior brother?"

Qiu He frowned as he spoke.

Sanwu chuckled softly, chanted a Buddhist prayer, and shook his head: "No, my junior brother is nothing to me. It's just that when Junior Brother Sangui left, he took a scripture with him. This scripture is so profound that even our Grandmaster couldn't fathom it."

"Now that Junior Brother is dead, I wonder where this scripture has gone."

Upon hearing this, Qiu He stroked his beard and frowned, saying, "This is troublesome. That 'Mountain God' is at least at the Yang God realm. If the scripture falls into his hands, it will be difficult to retrieve it."

"Therefore, I informed our sect last month that an elder from our sect would be coming to Qingzhou soon," Sanwu said calmly.

Qiu He was shocked!

"An elder from our sect? That's above the Yang God realm! How can they leave the capital?"

The Tianshui Pact was like a long chain, binding the entire Great Chu Dynasty.

Cultivators above the Yang God realm could not possibly leave the capital.

"This shows that Lord Qiu doesn't understand the mysteries of Buddhism. Since ancient times, Buddhism has had the concept of reincarnation. If a senior disciple leaves the capital, it's because they've been reincarnated," Sanwu said with a faint smile.

Qiu He's brows twitched upon hearing this.

Reincarnation!?

The Golden Dragon Temple actually has a way to allow a Yang Shen realm cultivator to leave the capital without violating the Heavenly Water Agreement!

However, this method is opportunistic, exploiting a loophole. If the demons find out, it will be extremely troublesome.

"Then we'll await the arrival of the senior from the Golden Dragon Temple," Qiu He said with a smile.

........

Qianshan Town.

After several days of migration, the entire town is now full of people.

The new residents all have a sense of novelty on their faces, because everything in Qianshan Town is different from Peach Blossom County.

Merchant guilds from Tianyue County have also moved in.

A dazzling array of goods has begun to appear in Qianshan Town.

The villagers of Qianshan Village now had their own affairs to attend to.

Long Qianqian gathered the former girls and opened a restaurant in the new town.

The girls were all entertainers; they didn't engage in any other sordid activities.

This first restaurant in Qianshan Town, featuring talent performances, immediately attracted many people.

The restaurant quickly became bustling, with many residents coming to relax after work.

Just order a bowl of wine and you could watch the girls perform various talents.

Of course, the most important thing was the opening of the new mountain god temple.

Several new mountain god temples had been planned during the initial establishment of Qianshan Town.

Because with so many people, it was impossible for everyone to crowd into one temple; it simply couldn't accommodate so many people.

The original temple remained unchanged, as it was the main temple.

The other newly built mountain god temples, like those in Peach Blossom County, displayed images of the sacred mountain.

The residents of Qianshan Town could then go to the nearest mountain god temple to offer incense and prayers.

After the mountain god temple opened, incense burned continuously, and a large number of residents rushed in to offer incense and prayers, their eyes filled with reverence.

Bai Liang watched all this and nodded with great satisfaction.

With the most important matter of the mountain god temple settled, the next step was to prepare to open a school.

Education could not be neglected; this was for long-term development.

There was already a school in Taohua County, and Bai Liang directly instructed Zhang Shiye to bring all the teachers from there over.

A new school was opened in Qianshan Town, open to all the residents' children.

Tuition was reduced to one-tenth of the previous rate, used to pay the teachers' salaries.

This was just an ordinary school, and the arrangements were quickly made.

However, there was also a school for cultivators, which was more difficult.

For Qianshan Town to have a future, it needed to cultivate more cultivators, so an academy for cultivating cultivators had to be established as soon as possible.

But talented people were few and far between.

Older people were too late to cultivate, not very suitable, unless they had exceptional talent.

Therefore, they could only start with the children.


Chapter 114 Hundred Refinement Hall

When Bai Liang announced that he would select children with cultivation talent from Qianshan Town for training,

the newly arrived residents became excited.

Families with children readily sent them over.

The age requirement was sixteen, but many concealed their ages, hoping to get away with it.

Bai Liang didn't care too much about it; he accepted anyone who looked even slightly immature.

Soon, over a thousand teenagers were sent to the newly built school.

The school was named Hundred Refinement Hall, specifically for teaching children cultivation.

At this moment, in Hundred Refinement Hall, a group of teenagers crowded together, their eyes filled with anticipation as they looked towards Bai Liang and Wu Ze.

"Everyone, you are the younger generation of Qianshan Town, the hope and the lifeblood of the future! The future of Qianshan Town depends on you!"

Bai Liang spoke, then added solemnly, "Now is a time of chaos caused by demons and monsters; strength is the foundation of survival. As the saying goes, all other professions are inferior, only cultivation is superior. Only by becoming cultivators can you have the strength to protect Qianshan Town and your families against the cruel demons."

The youths listened, their hearts surging with fervor, wishing they could become cultivators right now and slay demons!

"But..." Bai Liang's tone shifted, "Not everyone can become a cultivator. Those with cultivation talent are, to say the least, one in a thousand."

These words immediately caused the youths to pause.

One in a thousand? Did that mean that among them, there might only be one person with cultivation talent?

The odds were too low!

Bai Liang then offered some words of encouragement, urging the youths to cultivate diligently.

Afterwards, he had the Fire Heart Sutra distributed.

The Great Chu Dynasty's method for testing cultivation talent was simple and brutal: provide a cultivation technique, and if you can cultivate it effectively, you have talent.

Otherwise, you don't. The level of talent is naturally judged by cultivation speed.

For example, before receiving her ordination, Li Xue's cultivation speed was nearly ten times that of Wu Ze, placing her in the upper-middle tier of talent.

Other methods existed for testing cultivation talent.

In the capital city, there were spirit pillars. Ordinary people could touch these pillars to resonate with them, thus determining whether they possessed cultivation talent and the strength of that talent.

Qianshan Town lacked spirit pillars, so this simple and brutal method was the only option.

Generally, it took about a month of cultivation to determine if someone possessed talent.

However, Qianshan Town was located at the foot of a sacred mountain, where the spiritual energy was several times denser than elsewhere, so results could be seen in about seven days.

The young people who received the cultivation techniques were eager to try them out, wishing they could sit down and cultivate immediately.

The Hundred Refinement Hall provided a cultivation space, but it couldn't accommodate over a thousand people at once.

Therefore, some youths went home to cultivate, but the results were the same.

These past few days had just arrived, the time to climb the mountain and collect the flesh and blood of the demon generals.

Wu Ze summoned Kong Wang, and the three of them first consulted the mountain god at the temple. After receiving permission, they ascended the sacred mountain.

On their second ascent, Bai Liang observed the mountain more closely.

They discovered that the number of spiritual herbs was significantly greater than on their first ascent.

Furthermore, they found several high-quality spiritual herbs.

He couldn't help but sigh inwardly, truly worthy of being called a sacred mountain.

If such a spiritual herb exists halfway down the mountain, then surely the legendary sacred medicine resides at the mountain's peak?

The three ventured deep into the cave and found the long-existing corpse of a demon general.

Previously, the spiritual energy contained within the flesh and blood carried a violent aura.

But now, it was incredibly gentle, showing absolutely no adverse effects.

Bai Liang stepped forward and used a small knife to cut off a small piece of flesh. As the skin separated from the flesh, blood flowed out.

This demon general's corpse hadn't decayed after so long, and the blood was still vibrant—no wonder it was a fifth-tier demon general!

"Try it," Bai Liang said, looking at the raw flesh and blood, finding it somewhat unpalatable, and handed it to Wu Ze.

Wu Ze had no reservations; having served in the military, he wasn't unfamiliar with raw meat.

He took the small piece of flesh and blood and immediately popped it into his mouth to chew.

With one bite, the blood burst in his mouth like juice, a little salty, the metallic taste shooting straight to the top of his head.

The flesh was like tree sap, impossible to chew thoroughly, and in the end, it could only be swallowed whole.

"Not tasty, the flavor is awful," Wu Ze shook his head, offering his assessment of the demon general's flesh.

Bai Liang asked, "And the effects?"

That was the most important thing.

"Very powerful!"

Wu Ze felt the changes within his body. The spiritual energy contained within the demon general's flesh surged forth wildly, flowing to his limbs and bones, continuously nourishing him.

Upon consuming the demon general's flesh for the first time, his cultivation level visibly increased slightly.

There were no side effects, nor any other discomfort.

Bai Liang smiled: "Not bad, now you can rest assured."

The three of them then worked together to carry the black boar's carcass down the mountain.

Demon general-level flesh was a highly nourishing substance.

Ordinary people could consume it to prolong life and strengthen their bodies.

Wu Ze instructed Du Tiandao to arrange for a large piece of meat to be cut off and distributed to the villagers of the original Thousand Mountain Manor.

These villagers, though they hadn't accomplished anything great, had still worked hard.

So everyone received a small share.

Of course, the majority was divided among Bai Liang and his group.

Bai Liang, Wu Ze, Li Xue, Kong Wang, Qian Ke, He Liyuan, and Chen Jianchi.

Also, two cultivators who had come from Peach Blossom County to seek refuge.

These people were the only cultivators in Qianshan Town.

Most of the demon general's flesh and blood naturally went to them.

The remaining portion was distributed among Du Tiandao, Er Qing, Shangguan Qiuyu, and others.

“Kong Wang, aren’t you a monk? Don’t you break your vows?”

Wu Ze looked at Kong Wang and asked curiously.

Kong Wang calmly shook his head and said, “Monks don’t eat meat; that’s a shackle imposed by the secular world. This humble monk does not follow that vow.”

Bai Liang smiled but said nothing.

He had long noticed that Kong Wang was different from the other monks; he had a deep understanding of Buddhist teachings but never spoke of it.

"That's good, otherwise you would have missed out on this demon general's flesh and blood." Wu Ze laughed heartily.

There was a lot of demon general's flesh and blood; everyone received a large amount.

Absorbing it all would probably take a very long time.

Wu Ze estimated that once he absorbed all the demon general's flesh and blood, he would be able to break through to the Enlightenment Realm.

The others would be in the same boat, at least breaking through one realm.

Those with deeper accumulation, like Qian Ke, might even be able to touch the threshold of the Profound Realm.

Seven days passed quickly.

It was time to test the cultivation results of the youths in the Hundred Refinements Hall.

Bai Liang arrived with Du Tiandao. Over a thousand people had already gathered there, chattering amongst themselves.

Most of the youths looked dejected, sighing and complaining.

A portion were nervous, clearly having made some progress in their cultivation but unsure if it was satisfactory.

A dozen or so, however, were spirited and beaming with joy, suggesting their cultivation had been highly effective.


Chapter 115 Wild cultivator sneaks in

"Master Bai is here!"

A young man shouted, and the scene immediately quieted down as everyone turned to look at Bai Liang and his group as they entered.

Bai Liang calmly walked to the front of the group, his gaze sweeping over the youths.

He activated his Divine Sense Talisman, spreading his divine sense to encompass everyone in the Hundred Refinement Hall, scanning them one by one to examine the youths' cultivation progress.

After the examination, his expression showed slight disappointment.

As expected, most of them possessed extremely mediocre talent, utterly incapable of cultivation.

Even worse than his and Wu Ze's original talents.

Some were slightly better, but it was useless; they couldn't become cultivators.

Suddenly, his expression shifted; he had finally spotted a youth with cultivation talent.

Although his talent was low-grade, it was still quite good.

Continuing his examination, Bai Liang's eyes lit up as he discovered three more seeds of cultivation talent.

One of them was even of average ability.

After reviewing all the candidates, there were six youths with cultivation talent.

Two girls and four boys.

"Not bad!" Bai Liang couldn't help but exclaim.

"You lot, step forward." He pointed to the six, and their faces lit up with excitement.

They quickly squeezed through the crowd to the front.

Those who weren't chosen looked disappointed. Several who had been brimming with confidence, believing they possessed cultivation talent, blushed and lowered their heads in embarrassment.

Looking at the six youths, Bai Liang showed satisfaction and said,

"You all possess cultivation talent. With diligent practice, you can all become cultivators."

"Don't worry, Master Bai, we will definitely cultivate diligently!" one of the youths replied.

He was the one with the highest talent among the six, the only one with average ability.

Bai Liang had high hopes for him.

"Good. From now on, you will cultivate in the Hundred Refinement Hall. I or Master Wu will personally instruct you," Bai Liang said.

The six youths nodded excitedly, their eyes filled with longing for the future.

As for the remaining youths without talent, Bai Liang offered a few words of encouragement and then sent them back.

Ultimately, only the best can become cultivators.

Li Yue was also observing the Hundred Refinement Hall.

Watching these youths cultivate desperately, hoping to be chosen and become cultivators,

but ultimately, out of more than a thousand, only a handful possessed cultivation talent.

He suddenly realized that, strictly speaking, everyone in this world possessed cultivation talent.

Because they could all cultivate through techniques, only the effects differed.

Some people cultivated their entire lives, yet never reached the Refinement Realm before dying of old age.

These were considered to lack talent.

Bai Liang and Wu Ze before receiving their ordination were among these people.

Some people cultivate for decades and can barely break through to the Refining Technique Realm, becoming cultivators.

This is considered possessing the lowest level of cultivation talent.

Qian Ke is such a person.

The better the cultivation effect, the better the talent!

Then, if the spiritual energy were concentrated to a certain degree, could people with poor talent also become cultivators?

Li Yue thought of this question.

For example, judging whether someone possesses talent requires a month in the outside world.

However, in Qianshan Town, the spiritual energy is abundant, so only seven days are needed.

If these youths were to cultivate for a month, would the outcome be different?

Li Yue was very curious about this.

But after another seven days, he realized he had oversimplified the situation.

Those eliminated youths returned and continued to cultivate diligently, but the results were still not significant.

He observed them carefully with his divine sense and discovered that it wasn't that these youths had poor cultivation progress.

It was that after reaching a certain level, they found it difficult to continue absorbing spiritual energy and improving their cultivation.

Initially, they could digest half of a portion of spiritual energy they absorbed.

After absorbing a certain amount, they could only digest less than half, making no further progress.

"It seems I was too simplistic. Indeed, if it were that simple, other cultivators would have already figured it out," Li Yue thought to himself.

It's like a fate lock; what you are born with is your fate, and that is it—unchangeable.

Even if you were thrown into a blessed land with spiritual energy so dense it was almost tangible, and fed elixirs and miraculous medicines, your potential is still limited; you can't break free of that shackle.

"The Great Chu Dynasty has yet to produce a saint. Could it be that its path is also blocked?"

Li Yue pondered this, a sense of doubt creeping into his heart.

If it truly is a shackle of destiny, then having one's potential locked means being destined to never become a saint.

If that's the case, then what are the semi-saints in the capital still persisting in?

Trying to break free of the shackles?

And who placed these shackles of destiny on humanity?

Do demons possess them?

Li Yue considered many things, but none provided answers.

Perhaps there's some hidden secret involved.

He stopped thinking about it. As long as those he blessed weren't bound by shackles of destiny, that was enough.

This also meant that the divine talisman he bestowed could break those so-called shackles of destiny.

He turned his attention to the panel.

The population of Qianshan Town has exploded, bringing him a significant increase in merit points over this period.

It's now approaching 800,000 points.

[Realm: Third-Tier Spirit Mountain]

[Merit Points: 781,345/1,000,000]

[Supernatural Powers: Mountain God's Blessing, Dharma Body of Heaven and Earth, Incense Resurrection]

[Spell: Manifestation]

[Magic Weapons: Mountain-Suppressing Seal, Heart Mirror, Divine Weapon Forging Furnace]

780,000 merit points—only a little over 200,000 short of 1,000,000.

Qianshan Town now has a population of over 10,000, providing him with 30,000 to 40,000 merit points daily.

That means he'll be able to advance to the fourth tier in six or seven days.

Thinking of this, Li Yue felt a surge of anticipation.

........

Qianshan Town is developing remarkably rapidly.

Last month, it was an empty town; now it's bustling with activity.

Especially along the main street, shops on both sides had already opened, and the threshold of Long Qianqian's restaurant was practically worn out from the crowds.

Of course, the most frequent destination for residents was the mountain god temple in town.

Every day, the mountain god temple was packed with a constant stream of people.

Regarding the situation in Qianshan Town, Bai Liang didn't mandate that everyone go to offer incense and prayers every day.

There were too many people; it was impossible for everyone to go every day.

Therefore, the requirements were relaxed considerably.

Du Tiandao led his patrol team, patrolling back and forth near the several mountain god temples.

He was in charge of maintaining order, with the Mountain God Temple being a key area of ​​focus.

Not far from the Mountain God Temple, in a house...

A man and a woman got out of bed. The man straightened his clothes, while the woman wrapped herself tightly in the blankets.

"It's still early, let's do it again!" the woman said, still somewhat unsatisfied.

"Once isn't enough? If we do it again, and it interferes with the sect leader's important business, can you bear the responsibility?"

The man's tone was cold, his face full of impatience.

The woman dared not argue, and could only get out of bed naked and begin to put on her clothes.


Chapter 116 The Dragon Slayer Cult

"Chu Lang, why did the leader insist on bringing us to this wretched place? It doesn't look special at all."

The woman finished dressing and said with some dissatisfaction.

Whether it was dissatisfaction with the task assigned to them by the cult leader, or something else, only she knew the answer.

"A woman's view!" Chu Sha said coldly, glancing at the woman with displeasure. "The sect leader's skills are unparalleled. How dare you question his orders? If you dare to say such things again, don't even think about me chiseling you."

"No...no, I won't say anything," the woman said hurriedly.

"Hmph!" Chu Sha snorted coldly, flicked her sleeves, and went out the door.

As soon as he stepped out, he bumped into the burly Du Tiandao.

Du Tiandao happened to be patrolling when he saw Chu Sha, and suddenly narrowed his eyes.

He sensed a dangerous aura emanating from this person.

If someone can make an ordinary person like him, who is born with superhuman strength, feel threatened, then this person must be a cultivator!

Chu Sha remained calm and said indifferently, "Make way, you're blocking the way."

"I am the captain of the patrol team in Qianshan Town. Who are you? Show me your identification!"

Du Tiandao would never agree to that. He took a step forward and blocked Chu Sha's path, staring at him warily.

Chu Sha felt a chill in his heart; this man in front of him was courting death!

In any other place, given his style of doing things, he would have already killed someone.

"The Moonlit Merchant Guild, here to do business in Qianshan Town." Chu Sha finally calmed down and took out an identity token, handing it over.

Du Tiandao took it and confirmed that there was no problem with the identity card; he was indeed a member of the Moonlit Merchant Guild.

"Are you a cultivator?" Du Tiandao suddenly asked.

Chu Sha was slightly startled, but quickly replied, "Of course, who would dare to trade in such a chaotic world if they weren't a cultivator?"

Du Tiandao asked a few more questions, and only after seeing that there were no major problems did he allow passage.

Chu Sha then left with the woman.

Du Tiandao watched the two figures leave from behind, his brows furrowed.

He called over a patrolman and asked him to inform Bai Liang about the matter, while he himself followed.

His intuition told him that the two men were highly suspicious.

Sure enough, after following them for a while, Du Tiandao saw the two men lingering around the newly built temples in Qianshan Town.

He also pulled several residents aside and asked them about something.

"There's definitely a problem!" Du Tiandao thought to himself.

He didn't act rashly. These two people should both be cultivators. Although he was born with superhuman strength, there was still a huge gap between him and cultivators.

We must wait until Bai Liang or other cultivators from Qianshan Town arrive.

Chu Sha and his companion wandered among several mountain temples, gathering intelligence before boldly heading towards the main temple.

"Chu Lang, that's their core area. Aren't we afraid of the danger if we just go there like this?" the woman asked with some concern.

"Heh, what's there to be afraid of? I've already observed that the strongest person here is only at the Profound Realm, while I am at the Fourth-Order Divine Enlightenment Realm. If I'm afraid of even that, how can I have the face to be a member of the Dragon Slaying Cult?"

Chu Sha spoke calmly, showing no signs of worry.

"But didn't the leader say that the person behind this place is not to be underestimated?" the woman added.

"He's not to be underestimated, but does that mean our leader can be underestimated?" Chu Sha said disdainfully. "Besides, Protector Yue is outside to meet us, so what's there to worry about?"

Thinking of Protector Yue, the woman gradually felt relieved.

Chu Sha glanced at a spot behind him out of the corner of his eye and sneered inwardly.

Without any cultivation level, you dare to follow him? That's ridiculous!

The two walked slowly, stopping occasionally, and gradually approached the main temple.

Du Tiandao was unaware that he had been exposed and continued to follow.

Meanwhile, the Protector Yue, whom Chu Sha had mentioned, was sweating profusely and hurriedly fleeing.

As he approached Qianshan Town, he suddenly felt a strong sense of crisis.

It's the kind of situation where even a moment's delay could mean instant death.

Without any hesitation, he used all his skills to escape to a distant place, leaving Chu Sha in Qianshan Town unattended.

"The sect leader has harmed me! This is far from a mere suspected Yin God Realm cultivator; it's at least a Yang God Realm cultivator!"

Protector Yue was terrified. Although they had no ill intentions on their trip, the other party's cultivation level was too high. Who knew if he might kill him on a whim?

With the limited resources provided by the church, why risk your life?

Li Yue silently observed the man and realized that although he had escaped, he had not escaped completely.

It was still within fifty miles of the sacred mountain.

He has now restrained his power and can no longer be detected, which led Protector Yue to believe that he is safe.

"The Dragon-Slaying Cult?" Li Yue thought to himself, as if he vaguely remembered the name.

I heard He Liyuan mention it before; it seems to be a rebellious sect within the Great Chu territory.

The Dragon Slayer Sect is incredibly powerful. It is said that its leader is an eighth-level cultivator who lives outside the Great Chu Dynasty and manipulates his members to cause trouble.

He has countless experts under his command.

However, they were unable to contend with the two major powers of Xuanqing Temple and Jinlong Temple, so they were driven to the outskirts of the Great Chu.

Those who mingled within the territory of the Great Chu were frequently hunted down by the imperial forces, like rats crossing the street, hated by everyone!

How did a rebellious force end up in Qianshan Town?

Li Yue was somewhat surprised, but he had just used the Mind Mirror to examine them, and these people didn't seem to have any ill intentions.

At this moment, Chu Sha and his companion were already close to the main temple.

But by this time, Bai Liang had already received the news and rushed over, accompanied by He Liyuan, Wu Zekong, and several others.

"Master!" Du Tiandao breathed a sigh of relief when he saw that everyone had arrived. He quickly stepped forward and explained the situation.

"They've reached the main temple now," Du Tiandao said, pointing in the direction.

Kong Wang looked up at the location of the temple, a golden light flashing in his eyes, then frowned and said, "One is at the Enlightenment Realm, and the other is at the Profound Realm."

Wu Ze and the others exchanged glances and frowned.

"Let's not worry about that for now, let's go straight there!" Bai Liang said.

Everyone nodded and headed straight for Chu Sha and the other person.

Seemingly sensing the approach of Bai Liang and his group, Chu Sha stopped and stood still to wait.

A moment later, Bai Liang and his group arrived.

Chu Sha first smiled and said, "Chu Sha of the Dragon Slayer Sect greets fellow Daoists."

The Dragon Slayer Cult!?

Upon hearing these three words, the expressions on their faces changed slightly.

They all knew this was a heretical religion, a rebellion against the Great Chu Dynasty.

Bai Liang spoke directly, "The Dragon Slayer Sect? May I ask what brings you to our Thousand Mountains Town? It seems that our Thousand Mountains Town has no connection with the Dragon Slayer Sect, does it?"

"That's because we weren't in contact before; who knows what will happen in the future?" Chu Sha said calmly.

Wu Ze said, "I don't think we'll have any contact in the future!"

"What do you mean?" Chu Sha's tone turned cold.

Bai Liang stared at Chu Sha and said coldly, "Fellow Daoist, you infiltrated my Thousand Mountain Town and lingered around the Mountain God Temple. Do you have anything more to say about what you're up to?"


Chapter 117 The Feud Begins, Li Xue's Divine Talisman

“That was just curiosity; I wanted to learn about the situation in your Thousand Mountains Town,” Chu Sha said, his tone somewhat dismissive.

“And how much have you learned, sir?” Bai Liang asked calmly.

Chu Sha replied, “Not much, just that I can tell you're suppressing your offerings.”

He didn't say anything more, lest he offend the people behind Thousand Mountains Town.

He was here to discuss an alliance, not to make enemies.

“Investigating without permission—is this the style of your Dragon-Slaying Sect?” Wu Ze's tone was full of dissatisfaction.

“Knowing the ins and outs is essential for discussing an alliance.”

Chu Sha remained unperturbed, believing that even if Wu Ze and his companions were angry, they would ultimately choose to cooperate with their Dragon-Slaying Sect.

He spoke directly, saying, "Your Qianshan Town is independent of the Great Chu Dynasty and unrestrained. Am I right?"

"This reveals your ambition, at least your dissatisfaction with the current court. Coincidentally, our Dragon-Slaying Sect also dislikes the current Great Chu Dynasty, so we've chosen to rebel!"

"Since you're also dissatisfied with the current court, then ally with our Dragon-Slaying Sect and rebel together. We'll provide you with assistance later. If the court sends troops to suppress us, the Dragon-Slaying Sect will protect you."

"If you can overthrow the Great Chu Dynasty in the future, you'll be rewarded for your service, and titles and official positions will be within your grasp!"

After hearing Chu Sha's words, Bai Liang and the others exchanged bewildered glances.

Where did this man get his confidence? What made him think that Qianshan Town would agree to an alliance with the Dragon-Slaying Sect?

Rewards for service, titles and official positions?

What kind of outrageous idea is this?

"What kind of world is this? Demons are harming humanity, and they actually dare to rebel, fight amongst themselves, overthrow the Great Chu Dynasty, and become emperors themselves?"

Instantly, Bai Liang and the others felt even less favorably disposed towards the Dragon-Slaying Sect.

"What rebellion? Our Thousand Mountains Town would never participate in such a thing!" Wu Ze said coldly.

He Liyuan said, "The Dragon-Slaying Sect is a rebellious sect. They shout that they are fighting for humanity, that the Great Chu is corrupt, treating its people like straw dogs, compromising with demons, and selling out its people for survival. The Dragon-Slaying Sect claims to be acting on behalf of Heaven to slay the evil dragon, eliminate the scourge for the people, drive out the demons, and restore peace to the world."

"I originally thought you were a group of people of noble ideals, genuinely rebelling for humanity. Now it seems, how are you any different from the imperial court?"

"You...you! You don't know what's good for you, you don't understand fate!"

Chu Sha's face darkened, and he said in a deep voice.

"Since that's the case, then you'd better see what happens. Having offended the Dragon-Slaying Sect, let's see if you can still stand peacefully."

Chu Sha said this and then tried to leave with the woman.

He wanted to return to the sect and summon the experts of the Dragon-Slaying Sect to teach the ungrateful Thousand Mountains Town a lesson.

Currently, he was wary of the people behind Thousand Mountains Town, so he could only endure it for the time being.

“Leave? Thousand Mountains Town is not a place you can just come and go as you please?”

At this moment, Wu Ze and He Liyuan stepped forward, blocking Chu Sha and He Liyuan's path.

“You two? Two Profound Realm cultivators dare to block my way?” Chu Sha sneered, not taking them seriously at all.

“Try it, and you'll find out,” Wu Ze said calmly.

Since consuming the flesh and blood of the Demon General, his strength had skyrocketed, and he was now in the late stage of the Profound Realm.

He Liyuan had even directly reached the peak of the Profound Realm.

He had been accumulating experience at the Profound Realm for many years, expecting another seven or eight years to advance further. Unexpectedly, the demon general's flesh and blood directly provided him with a tremendous boost.

“Since you want to die, don’t blame me for showing no mercy!” Chu Sha flipped his hand, drawing a curved blade and striking in an instant.

He Liyuan countered, also drawing his broadsword and unleashing the Great River Blade Technique to withstand Chu Sha’s attack.

He grunted, retreating step by step; the difference in their cultivation levels made it difficult for him to defend.

“I’ll take your place!” Wu Ze shouted, taking He Liyuan’s broadsword and slashing down with a backhand.

His strength was slightly weaker than He Liyuan’s, but his speed was several times faster.

Chu Sha initially paid no attention, but his expression changed when he noticed Wu Ze’s unusual speed.

He became serious, his curved blade constantly changing in the air, creating a series of dazzling sword flowers.

But Wu Ze, using the power of the Divine Speed ​​Talisman, was so fast that it was impossible to detect him.

Even Chu Sha, at the Enlightenment Realm, was helpless.

"Damn it, something's wrong!" Chu Sha sensed something was wrong.

Suddenly, Wu Ze appeared above him, slashing down heavily with his blade.

Fortunately, he reacted quickly; the strength of the Enlightenment Realm was not to be underestimated. He dodged by tilting his head, and simultaneously slashed back at Wu Ze, leaving a gash on his arm.

But his hair was cut off.

"Amitabha." In the distance, Kong Wang chanted a Buddhist prayer.

His eyes gradually filled with golden light, and tiny Sanskrit patterns appeared within them.

Chu Sha immediately felt his speed restricted, his movements becoming sluggish.

"Bald monk, what kind of sorcery is this?!" He was terrified, feeling as if he were sinking into mud, making even a single step extremely difficult.

"Chu Sha, I'm here to help you!" The woman moved.

But Bai Liang quickly stopped her: "Your opponent is me!"

"You're courting death!" The woman struck with a palm, which Bai Liang easily dodged.

Controlling the Divine Sense Talisman, his divine sense could capture every detail of his opponent.

Wu Ze and Chu Sha clashed. Even though Chu Sha's movements were restricted by Kong Wang, the two were evenly matched.

Chu Sha grew increasingly alarmed as the fight progressed!

He, a dignified Enlightenment Realm cultivator, was struggling so much against even a Profound Realm cultivator.

What was wrong with these people? Their methods were all so strange.

Wu Ze, on the other hand, found it troublesome. The Enlightenment Realm was ultimately the Enlightenment Realm, far superior to the Profound Realm.

With the power of the Divine Sense Talisman and Kong Wang's pupil technique, he could still restrain his opponent.

But as time went on, he would inevitably fall into a disadvantageous position.

"Uncle Wu, I'm here to help you!"

Just then, a somewhat childish voice rang out.

Everyone looked in the direction of the voice and saw a little girl step forward—it was Li Xue.

Li Xue's current cultivation level was at the late stage of the Tongfa Realm, even surpassing Bai Liang's.

"Little Xue, you can't help, go back!"

Wu Ze shouted. The Tongfa Realm was two major realms weaker than the Wushen Realm, offering no chance to even make a move.

"I can do it!"

Li Xue stepped forward, her small face filled with determination.

She activated the divine talisman within her, dormant for a long time, using this 'double-edged sword' for the first time.

Li Xue's divine talisman was somewhat different from others.

Wu Ze and Bai Liang's divine talismans were both golden, radiating a sacred aura.

Her talisman was entirely blood-red, its aura both sacred and ominous, quite eerie.

Seeing Li Xue's actions, Bai Liang and Wu Ze were both surprised.

Ever since Li Xue received the mountain god's blessing, they had been curious about what kind of talisman the young girl had received.

But since she wouldn't say, they hadn't asked further.

Now, this commotion—was she about to use the talisman?

What exactly was the effect of Li Xue's talisman?

Li Yue, who had been watching this scene, sighed inwardly when he saw Li Xue step forward.

"In the end, she still used the talisman. I wonder if it's a blessing or a curse?"


Chapter 118 Divine Destiny Register

Li Xue's appearance surprised not only Wu Ze and the others.

Even Chu Sha and his companion from the Dragon Slayer Sect were immediately puzzled.

Why would a little girl stand up?

Upon seeing Li Xue's cultivation level, Chu Sha was deeply shocked!

This...this is actually late-stage Tongfa Realm!? How is that possible?!

Judging from the little girl's age, she's probably only around ten years old, yet she's already at the second-tier Tongfa Realm!?

This talent is even more extraordinary than that of the Holy Maiden of their Dragon-Slaying Cult!

"This place is extraordinary!"

Chu Sha immediately realized what was going on.

The people he fought were strange in their methods and powerful in their abilities; they were not like ordinary cultivators at all.

On the contrary, they are just as powerful as those who are given special attention and are hailed as the future prodigies of the human race.

I realized the crux of this trip was that I underestimated my opponent.

Chu Sha began to have thoughts of retreating. As long as he left Qianshan Town and had Protector Yue meet him, he could leave safely.

But before that, he wanted to try one more time.

"Fellow Daoists, my Dragon-Slaying Sect sincerely wishes to cooperate with your Thousand Mountains Town. Fighting will not benefit either of us. Why not sit down? We can discuss any terms!" Chu Sha said in a deep voice.

"We can't talk about it!"

But all that responded to him was Wu Ze's greatsword.

"Qianshan Town has no need to form alliances with any forces, and it disdains to form an alliance with an anti-religion!"

Bai Liang spoke coldly, showing no respect whatsoever to Chu Sha.

Chu Sha's face darkened, knowing that this meant the feud was now completely over.

He was furious. It was just a run-down town. Even if there was a Yin God Realm expert behind it, how strong could it be?

The Dragon-Slaying Sect is a major sect in the world, comparable to the Golden Dragon Temple and the Xuanqing Temple.

People from this small place, they don't even give me any face?

"Fine, since you're so stubborn, then you'll have to face the wrath of the Dragon Slayer Sect!" Chu Sha said sinisterly.

He began to change his strategy, avoiding Wu Ze's attacks while simultaneously trying to fight and retreat while holding the woman hostage.

However, he overlooked Li Xue, who had stepped forward. At that moment, Li Xue's eyes were bloodshot and filled with crimson.

At the same time, her face turned deathly pale, and her aura became somewhat terrifying.

He Liyuan, who was standing to the side, was shocked by this scene, but he didn't know what had happened and didn't dare to go forward.

Divine Decree!

Li Xue's emotions were in turmoil and she was extremely unsettled.

From the day she received her ordination, she knew that the ordination she received was not simple.

The divine talisman is extremely powerful, but it comes at a price.

The Divine Life Register, at the cost of one's lifespan, is transformed into divine power, which is applied tenfold to the opponent, sacrificing their life.

By sacrificing ten years of one's own lifespan, one can also sacrifice the lifespan of one's opponent, and it's ten times that, which is one hundred years of lifespan!

Ignoring all living beings and any restrictions, it can transcend three major realms!

The life essence consumed by oneself cannot be replenished.

At this moment, Li Xue was activating the Divine Life Talisman.

This was her first time trying it, and it required immense courage.

The Divine Life Talisman was activated, emitting a blinding blood-red light, and some points of light were burning intensely within her body.

This is the origin of life, which is also her lifespan.

Li Xue burned off fifteen years of her lifespan in one go, because the normal lifespan of a cultivator in the Enlightenment Realm is three Jiazi, which is 180 years old.

If you sacrifice 15 years of your lifespan, the other person will lose 150 years of their lifespan.

Chu Sha was still dealing with Wu Ze's offensive and was completely unaware of the commotion on Li Xue's side.

However, at this moment, he felt a strong sense of unease that made his heart pound.

He was terrified, thinking that the powerful figure behind Qianshan Town might attack him, and he became extremely anxious.

Why hasn't Protector Xindao Yue appeared yet? Could something have happened?

But that's impossible. Protector Yue is a Yin God Realm expert. What could possibly happen to him within the Great Chu territory?

Just as he was thinking about these things, he suddenly felt a strong sense of crisis.

Chu Sha was shocked. As soon as he turned around, he was met with a red light rushing towards him.

He reached out to block it, but the red light was invisible and intangible, and could not be touched at all. It passed directly through his palm and into his body.

"What is this thing!?"

Chu Sha felt the red light spreading through his body, but he did not feel any discomfort, which made him very puzzled.

But soon he understood what the red light was.

His hair was visibly turning white, and the skin on his face was becoming aged and full of wrinkles.

His once straight back gradually became hunched, his eyes became lifeless, his teeth aged and fell out, and when he opened his mouth, he could only make old and hoarse sounds.

"What...what's wrong with me?" Chu Sha was shocked. At the same time, she felt weak, very sleepy, and wanted to sleep.

"Chu Lang, what happened to you?!" The woman's face paled, and she stared at Chu Sha in disbelief, her eyes filled with fear.

“That…that red light.”

Chu Sha realized that it was the red light that was causing the trouble, and quickly turned to look in the direction where the red light had appeared.

The little girl was standing there, much weaker than before. Her face was deathly pale, and she was staggering, almost falling to the ground.

He Liyuan quickly stepped forward to help Li Xue up.

"It's her!?" Chu Sha was shocked and furious, but at this moment he was already emaciated and it was extremely difficult for him to speak.

Overwhelmed with anger, he died on the spot.

"No! My love!" the woman cried out in grief and indignation.

Taking advantage of the opportunity, Wu Ze turned his broadsword around and slashed directly at the woman.

This time, there was no mercy; with a swift stroke, the head fell to the ground.

"Xiaoxue, how are you?!" Bai Liang rushed to Li Xue's side, frowning as he looked at the extremely weak girl.

"I...I'm fine, Uncle Bai." Li Xue forced a smile and stood up with difficulty.

Wu Ze also came over, looked at Li Xue's pale face, and said, "Let's go back to the temple first."

He turned back to Du Tiandao and said, "Tiandao, dispose of these two bodies. Also, increase patrols to see if there are any more members of the Dragon-Slaying Sect in Qianshan Town!"

"Yes!" Du Tiandao nodded emphatically, his face serious.

Then, Li Xue was carried by Bai Liang to the temple.

He Liyuan knew that Bai Liang and the others had questions to ask, and it was inconvenient for him to stay, so he said goodbye and prepared to leave first.

"Brother He, don't leave yet. We're all on the same side now, no need to avoid each other," Bai Liang said, pulling He Liyuan back.

He Liyuan stopped in his tracks, looked at Bai Liang's sincere expression, and nodded, saying, "Thank you for your recognition!"

And so, He Liyuan stayed at the temple and went to the side hall with Kong Wang and the other two.

Li Xue sat in the chair, her face still pale, though a little better than before. Bai Liang poured her a cup of tea.

"Xiaoxue, what exactly happened?" Wu Ze asked, his tone filled with worry.

He noticed that Li Xue's condition was very strange.

"I'm fine, uncles, don't worry, I'm just a little tired," Li Xue said with a smile.

Bai Liang looked at it and suddenly said, "Xiao Xue, did you activate the divine talisman?"


Chapter 119 The Celestial Observatory

Li Xue hesitated for a moment, then finally nodded in agreement.

Bai Liang and Wu Ze exchanged a glance, thinking to themselves, "Just as I thought."

"Xiao Xue, could you tell me what your divine talisman is?" Bai Liang asked earnestly.

Li Xue remained silent, thinking for a long time before finally deciding to speak.

She pursed her lips and said, "Divine Fate Talisman."

"Divine Fate Talisman?" Bai Liang was puzzled upon hearing the name.

Until Li Xue explained the function of the Divine Fate Talisman, everyone present was shocked!

A life for a life, injuring the enemy a thousand, but losing a hundred of your own!?

And capable of crossing three major realms—this Divine Fate Talisman is far too powerful!

But it requires sacrificing one's life essence, which is irreversible. Using too much will harm oneself.

No wonder Xiaoxue became so weak, and those members of the Dragon-Slaying Cult suddenly turned into dying old men and then breathed their last.

Thinking of this, Bai Liang's face darkened. He looked at Li Xue and hurriedly asked, "Xiaoxue, how many years of your lifespan did you burn?"

"Fifteen years," Li Xue replied calmly.

Bai Liang and the others exchanged glances, momentarily speechless.

"Fifteen years, directly exchanging one hundred and fifty years of the other's lifespan—it's hardly a worthwhile trade."

He Liyuan spoke, his expression somewhat complicated.

Wu Ze looked at Li Xue and said, "Xiao Xue, don't use the Divine Talisman unless absolutely necessary. We can handle the enemy ourselves."

"That's right. The Divine Life Talisman is a life-for-life exchange; the life essence cannot be restored. It's best not to use it," Bai Liang said solemnly.

Li Xue has lost fifteen years of her lifespan in one fell swoop; no wonder she is so weak.

“Amitabha,” Kong Wang said, “below the third rank, lifespan is no more than two Jiazi (60 years). From the fourth rank of the Divine Enlightenment Realm onwards, each rank corresponds to one Jiazi, and a Saint enjoys an eight-hundred-year lifespan.”

Kong Wang clasped his hands together and said, “Little Xue, you sacrificed fifteen years of your lifespan today. You can make up for it later through breakthroughs in cultivation. But you must never use the Divine Life Scroll lightly again!”

One Jiazi is sixty years. Below the Profound Realm, the lifespan is 120 years.

Each breakthrough to a higher realm increases lifespan by sixty years.

Up to the eighth rank of Half-Saint, the maximum lifespan is seven Jiazi, or 420 years.

If she can break through to the ninth rank of Saint, her lifespan will increase dramatically, directly enjoying an eight-hundred-year lifespan!

Eight hundred years—that is the limit in the mortal realm.

But how difficult is it to reach the Saint Realm? Li Xue may only have a maximum of seven Jiazi.

If the Divine Blood Scroll were to be activated a few more times, consuming her lifespan, it would be extremely dangerous.

“I understand,” Li Xue nodded.

She naturally understood all this, but she wanted to do something for Thousand Mountain Town.

“Treat this as a last resort, only use it as a last resort,” Bai Liang warned.

This Divine Life Scroll was a trump card, but it would backfire on her.

It could transcend three realms; this was simply defying the heavens.

Consider that a Saint Realm cultivator only has an eight-hundred-year lifespan, while Li Xue, once she breaks through to the Yang God Realm, could directly kill a Saint by burning eighty years of her own lifespan.

This was simply a devastating weapon!

But it couldn't be used unless absolutely necessary. Because the consumed life essence was irrecoverable.

Wu Ze brought over the demon general's flesh and blood, processed it, and gave it to Li Xue to consume, hoping to help her recover quickly.

Having consumed fifteen years of her lifespan, Li Xue's body was very weak.

Fortunately, her body still resembled that of a ten-year-old, without any other abnormalities.

Only her complexion looked somewhat pale.

Li Yue watched Li Xue use the Divine Fate Talisman, without interfering.

Ultimately, she needed to grow; if he always protected her, she would only become a hothouse flower.

The Divine Fate Talisman was a double-edged sword.

Used well, it was a powerful weapon, a trump card that could determine the outcome of a battle.

He had just tried using incense resurrection to restore Li Xue's burning life essence.

As expected, it had no effect.

This was normal; if she could truly restore her life essence, then Li Xue would be invincible.

But unfortunately, the life essence is the beginning of all things and cannot be restored by supernatural powers.

This also confirms that the revival of incense offerings cannot bring back those whose lifespan has run out.

More than forty miles away from Qianshan Town, Protector Yue had been waiting for a long time, but still hadn't received any news of Chu Sha.

He was slightly impatient, mainly because he had just been frightened by the aura that resembled that of a Yang God Realm expert, and now dared not approach Qianshan Town at all.

"Could he have already been crushed to death?" Protector Yue muttered to himself, thinking, "I'll wait another incense stick's time, and if he doesn't appear, I'll leave!"

An incense stick's time quickly passed, but still no sign of him.

Protector Yue frowned and shook his head, sighing, "It seems his chances of survival are slim. Chu Sha, don't blame me for disregarding the bonds of the sect; the opponent was simply too strong."

After a moment of lamentation, Protector Yue turned to leave, intending to return to the Dragon-Slaying Sect to report.

"Traitorous villain, surrender now!"

Suddenly, a furious shout rang out, followed by a large net falling from the sky, directly covering Protector Yue's head.

Protector Yue, ever vigilant, paled the moment the net appeared.

"The Celestial Patrol?! You imperial lackeys!" he roared, his hands bracing himself as his robes flew off, instantly enveloping the net.

The robes and net intertwined, allowing Protector Yue to escape the encirclement.

Looking back, he saw two middle-aged men in uniform, both wearing bird feather headbands—the insignia of the Celestial Patrol.

"Traitors of the Dragon-Slaying Sect, how dare you appear within the borders of Great Chu? You're courting death!" one of them said coldly, his longsword pointed at Protector Yue.

"Hua Wuzei, you dare to challenge me? Tell your colleagues to retreat, and we'll duel. I'll see if I don't kill you!" Protector Yue sneered.

Hua Wuzei's expression remained indifferent. He spoke calmly, "Traitor, what right do you have to fight me? Prepare to die!"

He took a step in the air, appearing beside Protector Yue in the blink of an eye, and swung his sword.

Guardian Yue was prepared. He patted his chest, and a steel shield appeared in his hand. He raised it and hurled it at Hua Wuzei.

With a clang!

The shield and the longsword clashed, both men staggering back several steps, the shockwaves sending up waves of dust.

The two fought to a draw.

But Hua Wuzei had two men.

The other quickly surrounded Guardian Yue, blocking his path.

“Tell me the location of your Dragon-Slaying Sect's Holy Maiden, and I might consider giving you a quick death!”

Hua Wuzei said coldly.

"Don't even think about it!" Protector Yue's face darkened, and he quickly made a decision.

He turned and fled swiftly towards Thousand Mountain Town.

"Attack directly! Be careful, it might be a trap!"

Hua Wuzei gave Protector Yue no chance to escape, drawing his longsword to block him once more.

Another powerful Sky Patrol officer pulled out a large net from his sleeve and threw it down towards Protector Yue, trapping Hua Wuzei as well.


Chapter 120 Acquaintance, Brother-in-law

The Heavenly Net descended, instantly trapping the two men, its opening tightening.

Hua Wuzei's figure dimmed as the net passed right through him, ultimately only capturing Protector Yue.

"Imperial lackeys!"

Protector Yue roared, using all his strength, but the Heavenly Net was a secret weapon of the Celestial Patrol Bureau, specifically designed to capture criminals; how could it be so easily broken?

"Retrieve!" Hua Wuzei reached out and grabbed the Heavenly Net.

He stared at Protector Yue, who was glaring angrily inside the net, and said calmly, "Tell me the whereabouts of the demoness, and I'll give you a quick death!"

"Bah! Imperial lackeys, you dare to ask my Holy Maiden to remove you? Heh, let me tell you frankly, the Holy Maiden is currently near the capital. Guess what she's up to?"

Protector Yue spat out a mouthful of blood, speaking coldly.

Hua Wuzei frowned, reached into the net, grabbed Protector Yue's neck, and spoke coldly.

"Will you talk or not?!"

"What else can I do but talk?" Protector Yue gritted his teeth, his face full of defiance.

He accepted his fate of falling into the hands of the Sky Patrol.

But getting him to reveal the Holy Maiden's whereabouts was absolutely impossible.

This involved the Dragon Slayer Sect's grand plan!

*Thud!*

Hua Wuzei drew his sword, piercing Protector Yue's heart directly.

This sword strike wouldn't kill a Yin God Realm cultivator, but it inflicted physical torment on Protector Yue.

"Wuzei, this person just led us in that direction; he probably has a plan."

Another Sky Patrol expert spoke, frowning as he looked towards Thousand Mountain Manor.

He felt extremely uneasy in this area.

It was as if a gaze was constantly watching them.

But when he used his divine sense to investigate, he found nothing.

This was very strange.

"It's very likely that the demoness is just ahead!" he guessed.

Hua Wuzei's heart skipped a beat.

Just now, Protector Yue said the demoness was near the capital, which might be a diversion.

So close yet so far, the demoness might be nearby.

Having been struck by a sword and severely injured, Protector Yue listened to their conversation with a cold smile.

He didn't speak, thinking to himself, "You'd better believe the Holy Maiden is ahead and go to investigate.

Once you've alerted that suspected Yang God Realm expert, let's see how you two escape."

Thinking this, Protector Yue felt a surge of anticipation.

Perhaps, he still had a chance to escape.

Hua Wuzei had been observing Protector Yue's expression, trying to glean something from it.

But Protector Yue remained expressionless, revealing nothing.

"Let's go, let's go see!"

In the end, Hua Wuzei decided to take the risk.

Since they were already here, the two of them, following the trail of the Dragon-Slaying Sect, traversed several states from the capital to Liaoyun County in Qingzhou.

How could they give up on a journey of tens of thousands of miles?

They were unaware of what had transpired in Liaoyun County. The Celestial Patrol Bureau was personally commanded by the Emperor; everyone there obeyed only the Emperor, making it an independent institution.

Therefore, upon arriving in Qingzhou, the two did not visit local court officials, focusing solely on their assigned tasks.

The other Celestial Patrol Bureau expert, Yang Youshui, nodded, imprisoning Protector Yue's Yin Spirit within the Celestial Net, then tucked it into his sleeve. The two, with utmost concentration, began their approach towards Thousand Mountain Manor.

Li Yue watched the two men's retreating figures, thinking to himself that people from the capital had also arrived.

However, these two were there to hunt down the Dragon-Slaying Sect, and had no connection to Thousand Mountain Town.

Hua Wuzei and his companion approached for about ten miles when they suddenly saw a small town.

The two exchanged a glance, and Yang Youshui spoke first: "The map provided by the intelligence doesn't seem to record a town here."

Hua Wuzei made a hand seal, his Yin Spirit briefly leaving his body, and glanced in the direction of Thousand Mountain Town.

"It's not a mirage, it's a real town!" he said.

Confirming it wasn't the work of demons, both of them breathed a sigh of relief.

As they approached Thousand Mountain Town, they were astonished by the density of spiritual energy there.

"How could this be? The spiritual energy here is no less dense than in the capital! How is this possible!?" Yang Youshui was somewhat surprised.

Hua Wuzei was equally astonished and uncertain.

Why would such a remote place have such dense spiritual energy?

Could it be some rare and precious treasure, or perhaps a spiritual vein here?

It should be known that several spiritual veins have been migrated from various dangerous places beneath the capital, resulting in such a rich spiritual energy for cultivation.

"Something's not right, be careful!" Hua Wuzei felt a sense of unease and warned Yang Youshui.

Yang Youshui nodded solemnly, and the two slowly walked into Qianshan Town.

The scene in the town left them even more bewildered.

Everything here seemed no different from other towns, except that everything was relatively new.

It was clear that the town had just been built.

But they soon discovered something different.

The people here didn't believe in Buddhism or Taoism; instead, they worshipped a deity they had never heard of before.

Yang Youshui wanted to approach to see what was going on, but Hua Wuzei stopped him.

"Everything is unclear; you mustn't offend them," Hua Wuzei shook his head.

The Imperial Astronomical Bureau, acting on behalf of the Emperor, patrolled the realm and had witnessed all sorts of strange and unusual things, so they were extremely wary of anything unfamiliar.

The unusual behavior of the two entering Thousand Mountain Town had already attracted the attention of the patrol team.

Of course, this was also because Hua Wuzei and his companion hadn't concealed themselves; they had entered openly.

They wanted to inquire with the person in charge of the area.

The incident with the Dragon-Slaying Sect had only recently passed, and now suspicious individuals had appeared again.

Upon receiving the news, Bai Liang and his group became quite grave and immediately led their men to the scene.

Seeing Hua Wuzei and his companion standing below the Mountain Tower from afar, Bai Liang suddenly felt that one of them looked somewhat familiar, feeling a sense of unease but not quite certain.

Meanwhile, Hua Wuzei and his companion had already noticed Bai Liang and his group's arrival.

Hua Wuzei seemed to have seen something, his face showing surprise and astonishment.

"Brother-in-law?" Bai Liang asked uncertainly upon approaching and seeing the familiar figure.

"Brother-in-law?"

Hua Wuzei's tone was quite surprised upon seeing Bai Liang.

He even wondered if he had been affected by the demonic illusion; otherwise, how could it be such a coincidence?

But after his Yin Spirit left his body and used his Dharma Eye to look again and again, he saw no trace of any demon, indicating that the person before him was indeed his brother-in-law!

"What are you doing here!?"

Bai Liang and Hua Wuzei spoke almost simultaneously.

Those present, having overheard the conversation, exchanged bewildered glances, unsure what to say.

Hua Wuzei never imagined that he would encounter his wife's long-missed younger brother, Bai Liang, who had run away from home, under such circumstances.

Bai Liang, equally surprised, hadn't expected to see a relative in this remote place, Qingzhou.

At first glance, he assumed his brother-in-law had come specifically to find him.

But Hua Wuzei's question, "What are you doing here?!" revealed that he didn't know Bai Liang was there.


Chapter 121 The Situation in the Capital
