They were all acquaintances, and even family, so Hua Wuzei and Yang Youshui were invited to the Mountain Pavilion.

After they were seated, Bai Liang personally brewed tea for them.

"Come, brother-in-law, have some of our tea," Bai Liang said with a smile.

Hua Wuzei was one of the few close relatives he had in the capital.

He had been ostracized by his family, and only his married elder sister cared for him, as they shared the same mother.

And Hua Wuzei was his elder sister's husband.

Hua Wuzei looked at Bai Liang, who had now become a cultivator, with a complex expression, his mind filled with countless questions.

In his memory, this brother-in-law had absolutely no talent for cultivation and was incapable of becoming a cultivator. That was why he had been ostracized by his family and run away from home.

But now, he was already at the peak of the Tongfa Realm, which made it hard for him not to have many doubts.

“Bai Liang, Wan Jun often thinks of you these past years. If you have time, go back to the capital to see your eldest sister,” Hua Wuze said, his tone much gentler.

No matter what happened, he was still his brother-in-law.

“Eldest sister…” Bai Liang thought of his 'only' close relative, feeling a pang of emotion. He asked, “How is she doing?”

“With your brother-in-law taking care of her, she’s naturally doing very well. She’s plump and healthy,” Hua Wuze smiled faintly.

“Haha, being a little chubby is good! My eldest sister always wants to be thinner to look better, so she only eats half her meals. How can her body handle that in the long run?” Bai Liang laughed, feeling much more relaxed.

The two chatted for a few more minutes, then Hua Wuze changed the subject, his tone serious. “Bai Liang, what are you doing here? And how did you become a cultivator?”

Bai Liang looked at Yang Youshui.

“It’s alright, Yang Youshui is one of us,” Hua Wuze said.

Bai Liang nodded. He trusted his brother-in-law quite a bit.

Of course, his becoming a cultivator wasn't a secret, so there was no harm in telling him.

"This is a sacred mountain. Sacred mountains possess spirits. We are fortunate to be protected by the mountain god and stand here. As for becoming cultivators, it's naturally because of the mountain god's favor and blessings."

Bai Liang explained simply and clearly.

"A sacred mountain?" Hua Wuzei and Yang Youshui exchanged a glance, both looking doubtful.

However, they quickly thought of the mountain god temples they had just seen.

"You mean, this place is protected by deities?" Hua Wuzei was quite knowledgeable and had heard many rumors.

"That's right!" Bai Liang nodded.

"Really?"

Hua Wuzei's tone became serious.

If his brother-in-law was being manipulated by someone, he would do everything in his power to rescue Bai Liang.

"Absolutely true!" Bai Liang said with unwavering certainty.

“Brother-in-law, haven’t you noticed that the spiritual energy in our Thousand Mountains Town is much denser than in other places? It’s because of the sacred mountain that Thousand Mountains Town has become a spiritual land,” Bai Liang said slowly.

Hua Wuzei nodded; the spiritual energy was indeed incredibly dense.

More importantly, the means to transform Bai Liang, who had absolutely no talent for cultivation, into a cultivator at the Tongfa Realm was something he couldn’t even imagine.

Even those semi-saints in the capital couldn’t help someone break free from the shackles of destiny.

If anyone could truly do that, perhaps only a god could.

But the Heavenly Path had long been severed; where did the deities come from?

Could they have always existed?

Hua Wuze couldn't understand it, and it wasn't a question he could answer.

Since Bai Liang was unharmed and had even obtained a great opportunity, he was naturally happy for him.

He turned his head and looked at Wu Ze and the others.

Were these people also like Bai Liang, lacking cultivation talent but blessed by the deities and gaining opportunities to become cultivators?

If so, then this 'Mountain God' was quite terrifying!

His curiosity about this 'Mountain God' grew even stronger.

"Brother-in-law, why did you come to Liaoyun County?" Bai Liang asked, voicing his doubts.

He knew Hua Wuze's position: the Celestial Patrol Bureau, a fearsome institution that only answered the Emperor.

Even the two major powers, the Golden Dragon Temple and the Xuanqing Temple, had to give them some face.

After all, the Celestial Patrol Bureau could be said to represent the current Emperor.

“This…” Hua Wuzei hesitated for a moment, then, deciding there was nothing he couldn't say, began, “We’ve come here following a group of rebels.”

“Rebels? Is it the Dragon-Slaying Sect?” Bai Liang suddenly asked.

“Indeed, the Dragon-Slaying Sect. Have you been in contact with them?” Hua Wuzei looked at Bai Liang.

Bai Liang then recounted Chu Sha's situation.

After listening, Hua Wuzei frowned and said, “So these traitors are trying to persuade you to form an alliance.”

He turned to look at Yang Youshui.

Yang Youshui shook his head and sighed, “Looks like we’ve lost track of the demoness again. We followed the wrong people.”

Their goal was to find the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect; minor figures like Protector Yue were not among their targets.

The group chatted on this topic for a few more minutes, until Bai Liang finally inquired about the current situation in the capital.

Hua Wuzei looked somewhat indignant, then shook his head.

"The capital is in complete chaos. In the court, factional strife is raging, with officials preoccupied only with their own self-interest. The conflict between the Buddhist and Taoist sects is escalating, threatening to become a life-or-death struggle."

"As for His Majesty..." Hua Wuze sighed, "Not only is he neglecting the situation, but he's even somewhat possessed. The entire capital is now a complete mess!"

Yang Youshui nodded in agreement, adding, "And the Dragon-Slaying Sect is secretly stirring up trouble. The events in Baijiang Prefecture have further ignited the situation in the capital, pushing it to utter chaos."

Bai Liang and the others gasped upon hearing this.

Has the situation in the capital really become this dire?

Wu Ze felt this was a harbinger of impending doom. Despite the threat of a demonic army, they were still engaged in internal strife.

Do these people even have brains?

"Humans are practically food for the demons; can't they see the situation?" Bai Liang asked incredulously.

Hua Wuzei spoke calmly, "It's precisely because they see things clearly that they're so reckless."

"Several half-saints, including the current emperor, are now resorting to any means necessary to achieve sainthood. Most below the half-saint level have already given up resistance, living for the moment, enjoying life while they can, until the demons break the Heavenly Water Pact."

Yang Youshui sighed deeply.

The two of them had chosen to go out on this mission precisely because they couldn't bear the squalor and oppressive atmosphere of the capital.

These words left Bai Liang and the others speechless.

“Amitabha, the capital is lost in delusion, its true nature obscured, it has become a den of demons!”

Kong Wang clasped his hands together and chanted a Buddhist prayer.

Hua Wuzei glanced at Kong Wang; he had noticed this monk from the very beginning.

Not for any other reason, but simply because this monk smelled so good!


Chapter 122 The weak have no voice!

Hua Wuzei felt he was hallucinating, finding a monk incredibly fragrant.

It was utterly ridiculous, and the thought sent chills down his spine.

But that instinct from the depths of his soul couldn't be wrong; he genuinely felt the monk before him smelled wonderful.

What was going on?

Hua Wuzei was somewhat confused, only finding the monk strangely sinister.

Yang Youshui, standing nearby, shared the same feeling, frequently turning to look at Kong Wang, his face full of bewilderment.

Bai Liang and the others noticed the two's odd behavior, but they didn't say anything.

In fact, they had sensed Kong Wang's abnormality for some time now.

Kong Wang himself understood what was happening.

He was originally a human elixir nurtured by the monk Sangui using a secret method.

After awakening his spirit and becoming a true living being, his cultivation improved, making this human elixir even purer and more alluring.

Bai Liang invited Hua Wuzei and his companion to stay in Qianshan Town for a few days, but they politely declined.

Hua Wuzei felt that Qianshan Town was somewhat eerie.

The mountain god his brother-in-law spoke of might actually exist. Otherwise, the miracle of someone without cultivation talent becoming a cultivator wouldn't be possible.

Both of them felt uneasy staying there.

In the end, the two didn't go to the mountain god temple, mainly because they had never encountered such a deity before.

Although they were curious, they were afraid of breaking a taboo.

Offending the gods would be a terrible loss.

Before leaving, Hua Wuzei pulled Bai Liang aside, his expression serious. "Bai Liang, you are favored by the gods. From now on, you must be cautious in your actions and not act impulsively. Do you understand?"

Bai Liang nodded solemnly; he understood.

His brother-in-law meant for him to obey the Mountain God's decree, to be single-minded in his duties, and not to have his own ideas.

As a member of the Celestial Patrol Bureau, Hua Wuzei had always been on the side of danger, having witnessed far too many things.

He had seen countless disloyal colleagues meet tragic ends.

"It's good that you understand," Hua Wuzei nodded, then reminded him, "And what happened in Baijiang Prefecture? Do you know?"

"A sixth-tier demon?" Bai Liang asked.

“Yes!” Hua Wuzei’s expression was grave. He said in a deep voice, “The imperial court intends for the two Yin God Realm cultivators from Baijiang Prefecture to break through to the Yang God Realm and then exterminate the demons. However, His Majesty is worried that this would violate the Heavenly Water Pact and anger the demons, so he has hesitated to make a decision. The matter has been shelved.”

“The demons have already violated the Heavenly Water Pact, so why should the imperial court abide by it?” Bai Liang frowned.

“The weak have no voice,” Hua Wuzei sighed.

The Heavenly Water Pact isn’t that important to the demons.

But it is crucial for the Great Chu Dynasty. If they lose the bondage of the Heavenly Water Pact, then the Great Chu Dynasty will face a million-strong army of demons!

Bai Liang remained silent. With weak strength, this is all they can do.

"The imperial court is currently unable to deal with the sixth-tier demons, so you must be careful. The sixth-tier demons cannot break through the protective aura of the prefectures, so they are very likely to shift their target and attack Qingzhou."

Hua Wuze solemnly informed Bai Liang.

Liaoyun County borders Baijiang Prefecture and is the first to bear the brunt. If what he said is true, then Qianshan Town may very well face two sixth-tier demons.

"Brother-in-law, don't worry. Our Qianshan Town fears no threat!"

Bai Liang smiled confidently at this.

With the sacred mountain protecting them, what demons or monsters would dare to come seeking death?

Hua Wuze looked slightly surprised, thinking to himself that his brother-in-law was very confident in the mountain god.

It seemed this mountain god was even more powerful than he had imagined.

Seeing this, he felt relieved.

Afterwards, Hua Wuze and his brother left Qianshan Town and headed towards Qingzhou Prefecture.

The trail to the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect had gone cold; they needed to quickly contact other teams to obtain the latest intelligence.

Watching his brother-in-law and his brother-in-law leave, Bai Liang let out a heavy sigh.

Wu Ze, standing beside him, chuckled, "What's wrong? Missing home?"

Bai Liang didn't deny it, nodding.

"I have been thinking about it. Come to think of it, it's been several years since I've been back to the capital, since I've visited my mother's grave." He sighed softly.

He didn't feel much longing for the Bai family.

The only people he missed were his eldest sister and a few close relatives.

"The situation in the capital is so bad right now, I wonder when it will stabilize."

Wu Ze frowned.

He hoped that the person who killed his wife and children wouldn't die too soon; he wanted to avenge them himself.

"It's hard to stabilize. His Majesty has been on the throne for almost seventeen years, and every year it gets worse. There's a saying circulating in the capital: this year is the best year of the next ten years, and it still applies now."

Bai Liang shook his head, pessimistic about the future of the Great Chu Dynasty.

With such a foolish ruler, it's already a miracle that the dynasty has managed to withstand the oppression of the demon army for eight years.

Indeed, it was due to the blessings of their ancestors.

If it weren't for the depletion of the Great Chu Dynasty's accumulated fortune over hundreds of years, they probably wouldn't have been able to hold on until now.

As long as humanity lacks a saint, they are ultimately lambs to the slaughter.

The Tianshui Pact gave humanity time to buy time, but didn't the demons also have their own calculations?

"Regardless of all that, what happens in the capital is irrelevant to our Qianshan Town," Wu Ze said, shaking his head.

Bai Liang nodded after hearing this.

He suddenly felt motivated and wanted to quickly develop and strengthen Qianshan Town.

If the capital truly collapsed in the future, he could bring his elder sister and the others here.

With the protection of the Mountain God, he believed that in the future, this sacred mountain would become a pilgrimage site for all people.

Tianyue County.

Qiu Zhen and the Lin brothers were still fighting for Tianyue County.

The General had already made the decision to cede the entire Liaoyun Prefecture.

The news reached Ximen Longyu, who then informed the Lin brothers.

The general's message was simple: if Qianshan Town asked for it, they would cede it.

If it didn't ask, their people would remain based in Liaoyun County.

The main strategy was to avoid trouble; if someone came looking for them, they would back down and show goodwill.

The Lin brothers even approached He Liyuan to directly state their intentions.

He Liyuan was initially quite surprised.

The general from the prefectural capital was actually trying to curry favor with Qianshan Town?

But upon reflection, it made sense. This general wasn't currying favor with Qianshan Town, but rather with the powerful figure behind it all.

The general probably already knew what had happened to Lin Zhenjiang.

The general's choice was also quite rational; instead of making an enemy, he instead extended an olive branch.

This indicated that Qianshan Town could do whatever it wanted in Liaoyun County, and the Liaoyun County governor would cooperate.

Upon hearing this news back in Qianshan Town, Bai Liang smiled.

Now, the development of Qianshan Town was finally free of obstacles.


Chapter 123 Advancement to the Fourth-Order Sacred Mountain

With the imperial court's support, many actions no longer needed to be concealed.

Bai Liang directly summoned Zhang, the clerk, instructing him to prepare for the phased relocation of Taohua County's residents to Qianshan Town.

He planned to transform Qianshan Town into a small county town in one fell swoop.

Then, Shenshan would no longer be a desolate wilderness, but a true county town, a prosperous place.

Meanwhile, the name Shenshan gradually spread throughout Liaoyun County.

This was naturally aided by Ximen Longyu's efforts; otherwise, the news wouldn't have spread so quickly.

Many refugees, upon hearing the news, were filled with longing and embarked on the journey to Shenshan with their remaining belongings.

"Have you heard? Near Peach Blossom County, a sacred mountain has appeared. It's said that immortals live on it, and demons dare not approach."

"Hey, you're so out of touch! What immortals living on it? That entire mountain is made up of gods! The sacred mountain is the embodiment of deities!"

"Wow, really? If gods exist, then we'll never have to fear demons again!"

"Of course it's true! It's said even the prefect himself admitted it. Do you think it could be fake?"

"Wow, even the prefect admitted it! We have to believe it."

People who heard of the sacred mountain were buzzing with discussion.

Many were tempted to go, but worried the news was false, and hesitated.

Even so, the number of people heading to the sacred mountain was countless.

On the main roads leading to the sacred mountain, one could constantly see refugees carrying their packs.

Qianshan Town suddenly had a large influx of refugees, and they were struggling to feed them.

The unexpected population growth caught Bai Liang and his companions somewhat off guard.

"How many people are there now?" Bai Liang asked, looking at the young man taking notes beside him.

This was his recently promoted deputy, skilled in arithmetic and reliable, worth training.

Xiao Nian paused, pondered for a moment, and said, "Reporting to the Master, there are now over 27,000 registered residents in Qianshan Town."

"That many already?"

Bai Liang was slightly surprised; this didn't even include the people from Taohua County.

If they were relocated, wouldn't their population be over 100,000?

"Is there enough space?" Bai Liang quickly realized a problem: the expanded Qianshan Town was still too small.

Xiao Nian nodded, "Master Bai, please rest assured, I've already made arrangements to continue expanding the construction."

"Furthermore, the new refugees will only be provided with food; if they want shelter, they'll have to build it themselves. We can't expect Qianshan Town to pay for everything."

Upon hearing this, Bai Liang nodded in satisfaction and praised, "Xiao Nian, you've done a good job. Keep up the good work. The population will continue to grow, thank you for your hard work."

Xiao Nian solemnly replied, "I will work hard!"

After saying this, he continued to lower his head and record his notes.

Bai Liang looked around; there were several other young people like Xiao Nian.

These were all people he had carefully selected and trained; they would form the core of Qianshan Town's management team.

As for him, he would likely spend more time cultivating.

The development of Qianshan Town couldn't be neglected, and the incense offerings to the Mountain God couldn't be neglected either. Therefore, strengthening Qianshan Town was essential!

These days, he would frequently visit the Hundred Refinement Hall.

Those children with cultivation talent now practice daily in the Hundred Refinement Hall.

The spiritual energy in Thousand Mountain Town is exceptionally rich, allowing these youths to cultivate much faster than ordinary people.

Bai Liang is responsible for guiding them and answering their questions about cultivation.

However, most of the time, they cultivate on their own.

After all, Bai Liang doesn't know much about the basics of cultivation; he directly skipped to the Refinement Realm.

An interesting incident occurred during this time.

The boy with the best talent among them actually expressed a desire to learn Buddhism from Kong Wang.

Bai Liang looked surprised upon hearing this and asked him why he had such an idea.

Lin Qian's answer was straightforward: he thought being a monk would be economical; he would eat vegetarian food every day, wouldn't have to eat meat, and wouldn't be ridiculed by others for not having meat to eat.

"I've never eaten meat since I was little. The Liu family's children always mocked me, saying we were too poor to afford meat. My mother cried while holding me."

"That's why I want to become a monk. That way, even if we don't have meat, my mother and I won't be laughed at."

Lin Qian said.

Bai Liang was speechless, unsure what to say.

"Silly boy, we're in Qianshan Town now. You don't need to worry about that. There will be meat, everything will be alright." Bai Liang patted Lin Qian's head.

"Really?" Lin Qian looked up.

"Of course it's true. As long as you cultivate diligently and become a cultivator, those who used to mock you will never dare to laugh at you and your mother again." Bai Liang smiled.

"Then I will definitely cultivate hard!" Lin Qian nodded emphatically, his expression resolute.

Bai Liang had high hopes for Lin Qian.

After all, he was the only one among the six teenagers with average talent.

Based on calculations, average talent could at least reach the Enlightenment Realm, which was already quite impressive.

The other five children, though less talented, could at least cultivate to the Tongfa Realm.

The difference between having talent and not having talent is truly vast.

With the population boom, Li Yue also felt a surge in merit points.

He stared at the panel; his merit points had reached over 990,000, just a few thousand points away from reaching one million.

[Realm: Third-Tier Spirit Mountain]

[Merit: 997,543/1,000,000]

[Divine Abilities: Mountain God's Blessing, Dharma Body of Heaven and Earth, Incense Resurrection]

[Spell: Manifestation]

[Magic Weapons: Mountain-Suppressing Seal, Heart Mirror, Divine Weapon Forge]

Just a little over two thousand points short.

Li Yue felt a slight excitement; those two thousand merit points would likely be reached in just one or two more hours.

He was looking forward to what changes would occur after breaking through to the fourth tier.

What kind of divine abilities would be unlocked?

In Qianshan Town below, residents flocked to the mountain god's temple to offer incense and prayers.

Each person's offering contributed a few merit points to him.

About an hour passed in this manner.

Li Yue's merit points finally reached one million.

【Merit Points Reached 1,000,000, Realm Advancement, Advancing to Fourth-Order Sacred Mountain!】

【Unlocked Divine Ability, Remembering the Source】

In that instant, Li Yue felt his power undergo a qualitative leap.

If his previous power was like a clear, condensed lake, it was now completely different.

But now it had all turned into mercury, not only increasing in weight but also improving in quality.

Li Yue felt that he no longer needed to use his Dharma Body; a mere thought could easily crush cultivators at the Yin God Realm, and even the Yang God Realm.

At the same time, his divine sense rapidly expanded from its original fifty li to a range of one hundred li.

That's a full doubling of its range.

Of course, what surprised him most was that after advancing to the fourth rank, it was no longer Mount Ling, but Mount Sheng!


Chapter 124 Casually Annihilating a Fifth-Order Demon

"Sacred Mountain!"

The change in the realm's name stirred something within Li Yue.

The first three realms were Spiritual Mountains, the fourth becoming Sacred Mountains.

Then the fifth and sixth realms would likely also be Sacred Mountains.

But what about the seventh realm and beyond?

Divine Mountains?

Thinking this, Li Yue's divine sense had already extended fifty miles.

His current divine sense range was one hundred miles, and Tianyue County, seventy miles away, was directly within his range.

Everything in the county was clearly visible.

All the cultivators in Tianyue County felt a sudden, inexplicable unease, looking up at the sky, but seeing nothing.

"Elder Jin, did you feel it?" Qiu Zhenhe asked, his expression slightly changing.

Jin Buhuan nodded, his expression grave.

"Such a powerful pressure, what is this? Is it an illusion?" he murmured to himself.

The aura lingered for only a moment before vanishing.

Qiu Zhenhe turned to look at the location of Qianshan Town.

"Could it be that mountain god?" he guessed.

"Very likely!" Old Jin said, his expression showing lingering fear.

Lin Zhenjiang's fate had left a considerable shadow in his heart.

The two fell silent, daring not to say another word.

Qiu Zhenhe had already reported the information to the prefectural governor, whose attitude was that he couldn't afford to offend him.

The governor had also ordered him to sabotage the general's overtures.

However, he hadn't acted yet, because Lin Zhenjiang's fate was still fresh in his mind.

Who knew what kind of temper that mountain god had? If he uttered even half a harsh word, he might be slapped to death.

Therefore, he wanted to wait a little longer.

Li Yue looked down over Tianyue County, taking in everything within the county town.

Compared to Taohua County, Tianyue County was indeed very large, almost a prefectural city.

The next moment, his divine sense appeared a hundred miles away, his limit.

There happened to be a county town there, about the same size as Peach Blossom County, called Che County.

It was named for the durability of its horse-drawn and ox-drawn carts. Che County had a population of about seventy or eighty thousand, not many.

Li Yue's divine sense swept across the area and found that there were quite a few cultivators in Che County, including one at the third-tier Profound Realm.

However, this Profound Realm cultivator was old and weak, probably not long to live.

Next, Li Yue's divine sense expanded wildly, covering an area of ​​a hundred miles around the divine mountain.

But within this area, he discovered many demonic creatures, though their ranks were relatively low, the highest being a fourth-tier blue fox.

He thought for a moment and decided not to kill them, leaving them for Wu Ze and the others to deal with.

Growth always comes with danger.

Then he suddenly discovered a river at the limit of his divine sense's detection.

A gigantic, blood-red eel lurked beneath the water.

"A fifth-tier demon?" Li Yue mused, his divine sense sweeping over the eel's entire body without restraint.

The eel, resting peacefully in the river, was slumbering.

Suddenly, an unparalleled pressure of divine sense descended, startling it until its blood-red face turned deathly pale.

"Who goes there!?" The eel leaped up, attempting to flee without hesitation.

It was currently at the peak of the fifth tier, just one step away from becoming a sixth-tier demon general, invincible within the Great Chu Realm.

It had never imagined encountering such a terrifying aura.

In that instant, it even wondered if the human race had broken the Heavenly Water Pact, and if a half-saint from the capital had emerged?

The blood eel billowed into a mist, moving with incredible speed, flying several miles in the blink of an eye, escaping Li Yue's divine sense range.

But Li Yue was completely unconcerned. As long as it wasn't too far away, he could still attack from outside the range of his divine sense.

An earthen-yellow seal appeared in mid-air. The Mountain-Suppressing Seal, upon its appearance, continuously grew larger, resembling a small mountain.

Then, under Li Yue's control, it crashed directly down towards the location where the blood eel had fled.

A whooshing sound rang out; its speed was so great that it caught up with the blood eel in no time.

"No!" The blood eel died without ever seeing what had killed it.

The Mountain-Suppressing Seal suppressed the blood eel, then continuously extracted power from it, slowly converting it.

Only after it had all been converted into harmless energy did Li Yue retrieve it.

Sensing the harmless energy within the Mountain-Suppressing Seal, Li Yue thought to himself, "An unexpected gain."

After briefly testing the range of his divine sense over a hundred miles, he then focused his attention on the sacred mountain.

Each time he broke through his cultivation level, changes would occur within the mountain.

This time was no exception, and the changes were even more pronounced.

What was originally only 20% of the rock layers had turned into white crystals; now, that number had doubled to 40%.

Li Yue already knew these were spiritual veins.

These spiritual veins crisscrossed within the mountain, just like his blood vessels, only the blood had been replaced by spiritual energy.

The spiritual energy of the sacred mountain became increasingly dense, even ordinary people could feel it.

Everyone in Thousand Mountain Town felt this change.

An elderly villager immediately declared that this was the manifestation of the Mountain God, and everyone was extremely excited upon hearing this.

And for cultivators, the changes were even more pronounced.

"The spiritual energy has doubled in density!?"

Bai Liang stopped cultivating, his face filled with astonishment.

He immediately realized that the sacred mountain had changed. Although he didn't know what had happened, it was definitely a good thing.

He Liyuan walked out of his room, looking completely bewildered, utterly baffled by this change.

"Hiss, the density of the spiritual energy now is ridiculously high!"

He couldn't help but exclaim.

They were staying in the original location of Thousand Mountain Manor, the closest to the sacred mountain, so they naturally received the most benefits.

"Big brother, with such dense spiritual energy, if I cultivate for a few more days, I can break through to the Tongfa Realm!" Chen Jianchi said joyfully.

The most direct benefit of coming to Thousand Mountain Town was that cultivation became twice as effective with half the effort.

Kong Wang looked up at the sacred mountain. He could feel that the sacred mountain was even more sacred and extraordinary than before.

Li Yue felt a sense of satisfaction as he experienced the changes of becoming a fourth-tier sacred mountain.

Then he looked at the panel.

[Realm: Fourth-tier Sacred Mountain]

[Merit: 4,300/10,000,000]

[Divine Abilities: Mountain God's Blessing, Dharma Form of Heaven and Earth, Resumption of Incense Offerings, Gratitude for the Source of Water]

[Spell: Manifestation]

[Artifacts: Mountain-Suppressing Seal, Heart Mirror, Divine Weapon Forging Furnace]

The required Merit Points have increased from 1 million to 10 million.

"It seems that the number of merit points required increases tenfold after each breakthrough."

Li Yue thought to himself.

"It costs ten million to break through to the fifth rank now, so breaking through to the sixth rank will cost one hundred million."

Seventh tier, one billion; eighth tier, one hundred billion; ninth tier, one hundred billion!

It will become increasingly difficult as you progress.

But Li Yue felt it was still acceptable. As long as the number of believers increased, one hundred billion wouldn't be too much.

You must understand that the population of the Great Chu Dynasty was considerable.

Liaoyun Prefecture alone had three to four million people.

The entire Qingzhou region had tens of millions.

The Great Chu currently had eleven prefectures, with a population of at least seven or eight hundred million.

Not all of them needed to become his believers; half would be enough.


Chapter 125 The Water of Creation

Li Yue wasn't in a hurry for the next level.

After advancing to the fourth-tier Sacred Mountain, he unlocked a new divine ability: "Remembering the Source of Water."

[Remembering the Source of Water: Water is the source of life, the origin of all things. Using this divine ability, one can create a stream of divinely created water. Mortals who drink this water will be cured of illness, improve their health, prolong their lives, and have a chance to gain cultivation talent. Cultivators who drink this water can understand their true nature, purify their spiritual power, and enhance their cultivation.]

[Note: Once one drinks the divinely created water, they will unconsciously feel awe and gratitude towards the creator deity of the water. The more one drinks, the deeper the influence!]

After reading the explanation of "Remembering the Source of Water," Li Yue's heart stirred.

This was a very powerful divine ability!

It was, so far, the best divine ability besides the Mountain God's blessing!

It could benefit mortals, even giving them a chance to gain cultivation talent—this was simply unheard of.

And for cultivators, drinking this water would greatly aid their cultivation path.

This divine power is absolutely perfect!

As for the side effects, what are they?

For him, they are not only not side effects, but even beneficial.

Anyone who drinks the Divine Creation Water will feel reverence and gratitude towards him from the bottom of their heart, and will be utterly loyal.

This directly eliminates the possibility of cultivating ungrateful individuals, ensuring that everyone who drinks the Divine Creation Water is utterly loyal to him.

Li Yue realized the importance of remembering the source of the water he received. He wanted to develop Thousand Mountains Town and continuously gain followers.

That required his own power.

While the Mountain God's blessing is extremely powerful, it cannot be given indiscriminately, because it is too heaven-defying.

Each divine talisman almost represents a future ninth-tier saint.

Therefore, he will only bestow blessings upon those he approves of.

However, this means he cannot cultivate too many cultivators.

Especially as Thousand Mountains Town develops, a shortage of cultivators will make it difficult to continue growing.

Now, with the "Remembering the Source of Water" technique, the problem is perfectly solved.

Although the exact probability is unknown, as long as there's a chance, it's fine.

Li Yue couldn't wait and immediately used his supernatural power, "Remembering the Source of Water."

In an instant, clouds and mist swirled around the sacred mountain, and a ray of light descended from the sky, illuminating its peak.

A clear spring, three zhang in size, slowly condensed at the summit, then grew larger and larger, becoming the size of a pond.

Soon, the clear spring water cascaded down the mountaintop, automatically forming a channel as it passed, stopping only at the foot of the mountain.

At the foot of the mountain, the spring water formed a pool, crystal clear and shimmering.

This was the Water of Divine Creation!

The appearance of the Water of Divine Creation quickly attracted the attention of the villagers.

Because it appeared near the original Thousand Mountain Village, close to the temple.

The villagers, noticing the anomaly, immediately found Du Tiandao of the patrol team and informed him of the situation.

"A spring has appeared?" Du Tiandao was quite surprised upon hearing this.

He quickly asked the villager who had told him to lead the way.

A moment later, Du Tiandao arrived at the place where the Divine Creation Water had appeared.

He saw that what had been a barren land now contained a crystal-clear spring.

The spring was completely devoid of any debris; there were no fish or shrimp.

Nearby, the land nourished by the spring water was faintly sprouting green shoots, teeming with life!

Du Tiandao looked astonished. He had been here before, and this spring hadn't appeared out of nowhere.

"Captain Du, what's going on? This spring water looks so sweet and refreshing; I really want to drink some." The villager who discovered the spring swallowed hard, his eyes filled with longing.

"Don't move yet. I don't know if there's a problem."

Du Tiandao reached out to stop him. He had never seen a spring appear out of thin air before.

He first walked around the spring, checking for anything unusual.

Finally, he went around to the back of the spring to see where it came from.

"It actually flows down from the sacred mountain!?"

Discovering that the spring water came from the sacred mountain, Du Tiandao was astonished.

Water from the sacred mountain, then there was definitely nothing wrong with it.

After thinking for a moment, Du Tiandao bent down, cupped a handful of spring water in his hands, and brought it to his mouth.

Drinking the divine creation water, he felt an incomparably sweet and refreshing sensation, invigorating his spirit and giving him an unparalleled feeling of comfort.

Du Tiandao's eyes lit up, and he was about to praise the water when

suddenly, the spring water flowed into his stomach, transforming from cool to warm. The fatigue of a day's patrol vanished, replaced by a surge of energy.

"This..." Du Tiandao's eyes widened, never expecting the spring water to have such miraculous effects.

Truly worthy of being spring water flowing from the divine mountain, it's practically divine water!

"This is divine water! Quickly, go and inform the Manor Lord!" Du Tiandao exclaimed excitedly.

The others nearby were taken aback, but after Du Tiandao's explanation, they all bent down and drank a mouthful of the spring water.

Then their reactions were the same as Du Tiandao's: utter shock, followed by excitement!

The news reached Bai Liang and the others, immediately alerting them.

"Master Wu, Master Bai, this is the divine water I told you about, flowing down from the sacred mountain."

Du Tiandao led Wu Ze and the others to the spring and said.

Bai Liang and Wu Ze tried drinking some of the spring water, and immediately felt the restlessness of their spiritual energy. The thoughts that had arisen during their cultivation were calmed, and they gained insight into many cultivation issues.

Their cultivation levels actually increased slightly at that moment.

The two exchanged a glance, both seeing shock on each other's faces!

"It really is divine water!"

Bai Liang couldn't help but exclaim, and drank a few more mouthfuls, although the effect wasn't as good as the first time, but it was still incredibly powerful.

Simply drinking water could have such a huge benefit; it was unimaginable.

He Liyuan, who had followed, looked on with a strange expression. Was this spring water really that miraculous?

He stepped forward and also drank some spring water, then his expression changed, and his eyes widened.

"This...this is too amazing!"

He Liyuan was shocked, feeling the changes brought about by the spring water entering his throat.

He quickly sat cross-legged and closed his eyes.

The Water of Creation could enlighten one's true nature. After years of cultivation, countless confusions had accumulated, but at this moment, almost all of them were resolved and understood.

Therefore, his cultivation began to loosen, and naturally, he directly reached the late stage of the Profound Realm, even showing faint signs of breaking through to the peak of the Profound Realm.

Bai Liang and the others glanced at him but remained silent, so as not to disturb Brother He.

About fifteen minutes later, He Liyuan finally opened his eyes, a glint of light flashing within them.

He stood up and laughed heartily, his expression filled with amazement as he looked at the spring: "Truly divine water! Just one sip, and my cultivation level, which hadn't broken through for years, has actually loosened!"

At this moment, he was already at the peak of the Profound Realm, only one step away from the Divine Enlightenment Realm.

"Congratulations, Brother He!" Bai Liang and the others offered their congratulations.

Wu Ze said with delight, "This divine water flows down from the sacred mountain; it must be a gift from the Mountain God!"

Everyone nodded in agreement.

Only the Mountain God could create such miraculous divine water!


Chapter 126 The Prayer of the Clear Spring

News of the divine creation water quickly spread within a small area of ​​Qianshan Town.

However, the divine water was extraordinary; no one was allowed to approach it, and it required guarding.

Bai Liang suggested that water be taken from the divine spring and then distributed to everyone for drinking.

He also had Du Tiandao try it to see if the divine water retained its magical effects after leaving the spring.

The result was surprising.

The divine water retained its effects after leaving the spring, though slightly weaker, but this had no significant impact.

Bai Liang quickly made arrangements, assigning Du Tiandao to guard the divine spring.

Villagers who wanted to drink the divine water could sign up and queue, allowing patrol members to retrieve it.

The divine water was plentiful, so everyone received a share without any restrictions.

Initially, people were curious about the water's effects; after all, it sounded so mystical, and they were somewhat apprehensive.

But after drinking the divine water, everyone was astonished, exclaiming that it truly lived up to its name as divine water!

Everyone's physical fatigue vanished, and their spirits lifted. Most importantly, some old ailments were even slowly healing themselves.

This thrilled everyone, and the divine water instantly became a coveted prize.

The mountain god who bestowed the divine water was filled with immense gratitude and respect, and the incense at the temple burned brighter and more profusely.

The effects were strongest for Bai Liang and the other cultivators the first time they drank the divine water; subsequent drinks were less effective, but it still greatly benefited their cultivation.

After drinking the divine water, Kong Wang successfully broke through to the Profound Realm. His cultivation speed astonished Wu Ze and the others.

Furthermore, as a third-tier cultivator, Kong Wang became even more fragrant.

Smelling the fragrance, Bai Liang hesitated, then finally asked, "Kong Wang, did you use some kind of perfume like a woman?"

Kong Wang remained silent, decisively shaking his head in denial.

After advancing to the Profound Realm, he needed to refine his skills for a period before leaving Thousand Mountain Town to travel.

These past few days, Qian Ke returned to Qianshan Town from Taohua County, where Zhang Shiye took over his work.

Moreover, he brought someone with him this time.

Qingquan from Xuanqing Temple.

Compared to when Qingquan first came to Qianshan Manor, it was just a slightly larger village.

But now, before her stood a town of over 20,000 people.

She couldn't believe it; how much had changed in such a short time.

Was this still the Qianshan Manor she had visited before?

And when she saw Bai Liang, her expression became even more shocked.

“Tong…Tongfa Realm!? And at its peak?” Her mouth gaped open, her pale, bloodless face filled with disbelief.

She had only met Bai Liang four or five months ago.

Back then, he was just a cultivator in the Refining Law Realm, but now he was already at the peak of the Tongfa Realm, only one step away from the Entry-Profound Realm.

Bai Liang smiled faintly, invited Qingquan to sit down, and then asked, "Daoist Qingquan, have you decided to return to Baijiang Prefecture?"

Qingquan remained silent. What did he mean by "decided to return"?

She had been under house arrest until now, and finally, she could go back.

Under the eaves of another's roof, one must bow one's head, so she silently nodded.

"Baijiang Prefecture is not peaceful these days. Since we've met, we can send someone to escort you for a while," Bai Liang said with a smile.

To be honest, he felt a little guilty for keeping this female Taoist under house arrest for so long.

After all, she hadn't had any real conflict with them.

“Will you send me to Baijiang Prefecture?” Qingquan asked.

“No, to the border of Liaoyun County,” Bai Liang replied.

Qingquan was speechless.

Sending her to the border of Liaoyun County—what difference was there between that and not sending her at all?

“I have a request, I wonder if you can grant it?”

After hesitating for a long time, she finally spoke.

“What request?” Bai Liang frowned.

Qingquan took a deep breath, her pale face resolute, and said, “I want to repair my foundation and restore my cultivation talent.”

Bai Liang was taken aback, his expression turning serious. “You know about Qianke?”

“Yes,” Qingquan nodded.

“I can't make that decision. Qianke was able to survive from the brink of death because of the Mountain God's favor, which allowed him to restore his cultivation foundation,” Bai Liang said solemnly.

"May I pray to the Mountain God? I'll accept anything as long as I don't betray my family," Qingquan said.

In this chaotic world, with a damaged foundation and a plummeting cultivation level, it's impossible to survive without strength.

Even if she returned to Baijiang Prefecture, her master and uncles wouldn't despise her, but Xuanqing Temple wouldn't accept a cripple like her.

Therefore, she must have her own strength!

Seeing the woman's determination, Bai Liang hesitated.

He had no right to stop Qingquan from praying to the Mountain God; after all, the sacred mountain didn't belong solely to them.

On the contrary, they were the Mountain God's subordinates, or to put it bluntly, servants. He had always been clear about this and understood his own position.

"I can take you to the temple. As for whether the Mountain God will even acknowledge you, that depends on your sincerity," Bai Liang said, standing up.

Qingquan's expression relaxed, and she followed Bai Liang to the temple.

Entering the temple, she had expected to find an image of the mountain god, but there was none, not even a portrait.

She began to offer incense, kneeling and kowtowing, earnestly praying to the mountain god.

Bai Liang watched silently from the side.

A long time passed, but the temple remained unchanged.

However, Qingquan remained kneeling, unmoved.

Li Yue wasn't unaware; on the contrary, his divine sense remained within the temple.

This female disciple of Xuanqing Temple possessed a resilient spirit.

Using the incense resurrection once consumed several thousand merit points, not a large amount.

After Qingquan knelt for an hour, Bai Liang left, and someone else took over.

Her knees began to tremble slightly; her foundation was damaged, and she was still injured internally, making her extremely weak.

Li Yue observed her, seeing her genuine sincerity, and recalling the side effects of the Divine Creation Water, a bold idea suddenly arose in his mind.

However, this plan needed someone to implement it, and ideally, it shouldn't involve Qianshan Town.

Qingquan was exactly that person!

So Li Yue casually used the Incense Resurrection technique to repair her foundation.

[Consumed 6000 merit points.]

Looking at the notification on the panel, the merit points consumed were 3000 more than last time.

He thought for a moment; it was probably because Qingquan was at the Tongfa Realm, while Qianke was only at the Lianfa Realm.

Inside the temple, just as Qingquan was about to collapse without using her spiritual power, a ray of golden light suddenly enveloped her.

She was first astonished, then overjoyed.

Completely opening her mind, she allowed the golden light to enter her body, nourishing and healing her injuries.

A moment later, the golden light dissipated.

Qingquan's complexion, which had been deathly pale, became rosy, and her eyes regained their luster.

"This humble girl, Qingquan, kowtows in gratitude to the Mountain God!"

Qingquan was extremely excited, kneeling down and kowtowing deeply, remaining motionless for a long time.

Bai Liang, seeing the commotion, came over. Upon seeing the situation, he smiled and said, "It seems that Daoist Qingquan's wish has been fulfilled, and she has received the Mountain God's favor."

Qingquan nodded, deeply moved.

Her damaged foundation had been completely restored, even improved—an unexpected gain!


Chapter 127 Awakening Cultivation Talent

"Master Bai, please let me know what you need me to do, as long as it doesn't betray my family."

After her foundation was restored, Qingquan's mood improved considerably, but she hadn't forgotten the conditions she had just mentioned.

Her meaning was clear: she cared about no one except her master's family, including Xuanqing Temple.

"That's not urgent. Let's discuss it first," Bai Liang said with a smile.

He hadn't yet decided what he wanted Qingquan to do.

Mainly because Baijiang Prefecture was quite a distance from their location, and they didn't have much connection there, so it wasn't necessary for the time being.

"Then I'll stay here for now. Let me know when you've decided," Qingquan said.

Bai Liang nodded and then had someone arrange accommodations for Qingquan.

So Qingquan temporarily settled in Qianshan Town.

About three days later, Bai Liang found Qingquan and spoke to her.

Qingquan frowned at first, but finally nodded in agreement.

Afterwards, Qingquan prepared to return to Baijiang Prefecture. Her foundation had recovered, and her cultivation level had naturally returned to the Tongfa Realm.

Therefore, Bai Liang didn't escort her; she set off alone.

This time, however, she carried a large, heavy bundle on her back, and the sound of water could be faintly heard emanating from it as she moved.

In the past few days, all the residents of Qianshan Town had been drinking the divine water.

The miraculous effects of the divine water deeply impressed everyone, and they wished they could drink it every day.

Unfortunately, there was only one pool of divine water. Although the water flowed continuously, it took time to fill it up, and it couldn't sustain everyone drinking it every day.

Therefore, apart from the first unconditional drink, subsequent requests for divine water were limited to a quota.

The quotas were awarded based on people's performance and contributions.

Those who contributed to the sacred mountain and Qianshan Town, demonstrated merit, and worked diligently, would be rewarded with a quota.

The quotas were permanently reserved and could be used whenever needed.

The moment these rules were announced, everyone's enthusiasm was aroused, and they began thinking about how they could contribute to the sacred mountain and Qianshan Town, and earn merit.

This morning, Du Tiandao woke up feeling a little strange, a warm sensation inside him.

At first, he thought he was ill, so he went to see Doctor Liao.

At Doctor Liao's, he happened to encounter an old madman.

It was the same old man who had gotten into a fight with someone, been pushed to the ground, and shouted that his granddaughter was a concubine and that he himself was a member of the royal family.

This old man had been on the verge of death last time.

Unexpectedly, Doctor Liao had saved him, earning everyone's praise for Doctor Liao's medical skills.

However, Doctor Liao said it wasn't his superior medical skills, but rather the rich spiritual energy of the sacred mountain that indirectly saved the old man's life.

"Captain Du, you're perfectly healthy. You could carry an ox."

After examining Du Tiandao, Doctor Liao said with a smile.

"That's strange. I feel a burning sensation inside my body," Du Tiandao frowned.

After listening, Doctor Liao took his pulse several more times and even felt his abdomen with the back of his hand.

"You're definitely fine. There's nothing wrong with your body," Doctor Liao said confidently this time.

Seemingly realizing something, Doctor Liao asked uncertainly, "Captain Du, could you have cultivated spiritual power? I heard Brother Chen say that when you cultivate spiritual power, it feels like a raging fire is burning inside your body, which is very similar to your current symptoms!"

"Impossible, I don't even have the talent, how could I cultivate spiritual power?" Du Tiandao shook his head decisively.

It wasn't that he hadn't tried cultivating; he had put in tremendous effort, even consuming the flesh and blood of the demon general given to him by the Manor Lord, but he still couldn't cultivate spiritual power and become a cultivator.

While saying this, he harbored a vague doubt in his heart.

"You'd better go see the Manor Lord and have them examine you," Doctor Liao suggested.

Du Tiandao nodded, then got up and headed towards the Mountain Tower.

Upon arriving at the Mountain Tower, he went directly to Wu Ze, while Bai Liang was attempting to break through to the Profound Realm.

Du Tiandao shared his and Doctor Liao's speculations, and Wu Ze looked somewhat surprised upon hearing them.

“Let me see.” He placed his hand directly on Du Tiandao’s shoulder, examining him carefully.

Then, his expression shifted from surprise to a joyful smile.

“Congratulations, Tiandao! You’ve cultivated spiritual power!” Wu Ze congratulated him.

“Really…really spiritual power?”

Du Tiandao was somewhat incredulous. He had completely given up and decided to live as an ordinary person.

Unexpectedly, things had taken a turn for the better, and he had unexpectedly cultivated spiritual power!

“Really!” Wu Ze said with certainty.

Du Tiandao was excited, carefully feeling the warm current within his body, unsure of what to say for a moment.

Wu Ze waited for him to calm down before asking, “Tiandao, have you eaten anything recently?”

Du Tiandao originally had no talent for cultivation; that was certain.

But now he could cultivate again, which was very strange.

Therefore, Wu Ze guessed that it was very likely that Du Tiandao had eaten something, or perhaps encountered some fortuitous opportunity.

Wu Ze had a vague idea of ​​what was going on.

Du Tiandao suppressed his excitement and began to ponder deeply.

“It shouldn’t be the demon general’s flesh and blood; that was a long time ago. Uncle Wu has eaten a lot of the spirit rice he grows, but that shouldn’t be it either. The divine water from the Divine Spring…”

At this point, the two of them immediately had their answer.

“It’s the divine water!” they exclaimed in unison.

“The divine water can give ordinary people cultivation talent!?”

Wu Ze’s tone was somewhat excited. If that were true, then this divine water wasn’t just miraculous, it was heaven-defying!

It wouldn’t be an exaggeration to say that those with cultivation talent are one in a thousand.

If the divine water could allow everyone to cultivate, how terrifying would that be?

“I’ll go ask the others now if they’re like me.” Du Tiandao knew the importance of this matter. Forgetting his own joy at being able to cultivate, he turned to check on the others.

In Qianshan Town, more than 20,000 people had drunk the divine water, so everyone needed to be checked.

When the patrol team arrived, no one knew what was going on. They thought there was a traitor among them and cooperated fully with the questioning.

It took the patrol team of several hundred people a day to barely finish questioning over 20,000 people.

Like Du Tiandao, a total of seven people awakened their cultivation talent.

The number wasn't large, but it was still quite astonishing.

This showed that the divine water truly could grant ordinary people cultivation talent.

Of these seven, those who drank the divine water the most times had done so, indicating that the more they drank, the greater their chance of awakening cultivation talent.

This good news overjoyed Wu Ze!

From now on, Thousand Mountain Town would no longer lack people with cultivation talent.

The divine water was incredibly powerful, and Wu Ze knew its importance, so he decided to raise the level of protection for the divine spring.

Therefore, he found He Liyuan and entrusted him with the protection of the divine spring.

He Liyuan naturally agreed readily.

Du Tiandao awakened his cultivation talent and was overjoyed, beginning to cultivate diligently.

The other seven who awakened cultivation talent were all young people, indicating that young people were more likely to awaken talent by drinking the divine water.

Wu Ze arranged for them to join the Hundred Refinement Hall to cultivate alongside the original six youths.


Chapter 128 Randomly Setting Up a Chessboard

Above the Mountain Tower, the spiritual energy fluctuated violently, resembling an inverted vortex.

Countless spiritual energies were drawn in from the surrounding area, pouring into the Mountain Tower like a black hole.

Bai Liang closed his eyes, absorbing the power from the demon general's flesh and blood. Combined with the effects of the divine water, he had already touched the threshold of the Profound Realm.

At this moment, his mind was operating at full capacity, constantly striving to break through the final barrier.

Outside, Wu Ze and the others waited patiently.

If all went well, Bai Liang would become a third-tier Profound Realm cultivator today.

This would bring the total number of Profound Realm cultivators in Thousand Mountains Town to four, but since Kong Wang would soon be leaving, it would only count as three.

Although not many, Wu Ze believed that with the divine water, cultivators in Thousand Mountains Town would spring up like mushrooms after rain. The number of experts would also increase.

The phenomenon lasted for half an hour, then gradually returned to calm.

Bai Liang, dressed in scholar's robes, stepped out of the door, his demeanor transformed, and leaped downstairs.

Everyone was overjoyed at the sight.

"Congratulations, Master Bai, on becoming a cultivator in the Profound Realm!" Du Tiandao and the others offered their congratulations, genuinely happy for Bai Liang.

Wu Ze stepped forward and smiled, "Brother Bai Liang, it hasn't been long, and you've already caught up with me. In a little while, you might even surpass me."

"Brother Wu Ze, you're already at the peak of the Profound Realm, and the Enlightenment Realm won't be far behind. How could you possibly catch up?"

Bai Liang smiled, in high spirits after his breakthrough.

The group chatted for a few more minutes before entering the Mountain Tower. Wu Ze told Bai Liang about how the Divine Water could grant ordinary people cultivation talent.

Bai Liang was utterly shocked after hearing this.

"The Divine Water has such miraculous effects!?" He gasped, then went to Du Tiandao to verify it himself.

"It really is so!"

Having witnessed the spiritual power cultivated within Du Tiandao's body, Bai Liang's tone was even more surprised.

Then came excitement and joy.

The divine water's miraculous effects were simply too good for Qianshan Town.

He was worried about the lack of cultivators in Qianshan Town, which would slow down its development.

"However, although the divine water is abundant, the daily supply is limited and cannot provide for tens of thousands of people," Wu Ze said.

Bai Liang waved his hand and said, "There's no need for that. We'll only give it to those who have contributed to the Divine Mountain and Qianshan Town."

He Liyuan nodded in agreement.

"That's right. The divine water is a divine substance; we can't let some people reap it without sowing. Those who have made contributions should be given priority."

Bai Liang called Xiao Nian and instructed him to make the arrangements.

They compiled a list of people in Qianshan Town who felt a sense of belonging to the Divine Mountain and had made contributions, merits, and hard work.

They then ranked them according to their level of contribution, and the divine water could be distributed to these people from then on.

Young people are given priority, as younger people have a higher chance of awakening their cultivation talent.

This time, Qian Ke will not return to Peach Blossom County after returning to Qianshan Town.

His cultivation level had previously been at the Refining Law Realm, but recently, after consuming the flesh and blood of a demon general, he successfully broke through to the Understanding Law Realm.

With his original talent, the Entering Profound Realm was his limit.

However, thanks to the Mountain God's revival through incense offerings, Qian Ke now has a chance to attempt to reach the Enlightenment Realm.

Therefore, Bai Liang urged him to return to Divine Mountain. The spiritual energy there was abundant, making cultivation far more efficient than in Peach Blossom County.

Qian Ke's mission in Peach Blossom County was nearly complete, so he obeyed Bai Liang's arrangement and returned to Thousand Mountain Town.

With a portion of the demon general's flesh remaining, coupled with the miraculous effects of the divine water, his cultivation speed would only accelerate.

Furthermore, during this time, several cultivators came to Thousand Mountain Town intending to seek refuge with Divine Mountain.

However, their cultivation levels were mostly below the Tongfa Realm, with only a few above the Ruxuan Realm.

He Liyuan, with his years of experience in the martial world, was tasked with discerning the sincerity of these individuals.

The conclusion was that these people had sensed the abundant spiritual energy of Divine Mountain, their hearts surging with emotion, which was why they decided to come seeking refuge.

They hoped to stay in Thousand Mountain Town and utilize the incredibly rich spiritual energy to aid their cultivation.

After discussion, Wu Ze and the others concluded that these people harbored no ill intentions; they were simply drawn by the abundant spiritual energy.

They could accept it, but it couldn't enter the Mountain Tower, their core territory.

Keeping it in Thousand Mountain Town as a guard force would allow it to handle some tasks occasionally, which wasn't bad.

These past few days, Wu Ze had a question lingering in his mind, and finally couldn't resist going to Bai Liang to ask.

"Brother Bai, I don't understand why Qingquan from Xuanqing Temple was given such a large amount of divine water when she left?" Wu Ze asked, puzzled.

Bai Liang hesitated, then shook his head. "I don't know either. It was the Mountain God who gave me the order to prepare the divine water and have Qingquan take it back to Baijiang Prefecture."

He himself was also puzzled, wondering why the Mountain God would give such miraculous water to Qingquan.

Could it be because he saw potential in her? But that didn't seem likely; if he really saw potential, wouldn't it have been simpler to recruit her?

"So it was the Mountain God's order," Wu Ze said, realizing, and then said no more.

This was beyond their comprehension.

Li Yue had his own calculations in having Qingquan bring the divine water back to Baijiang Prefecture.

The side effect of gratitude for the source of the water could subtly instill a sense of awe and gratitude in others.

The more one drank, the deeper the effect!

This effect was gradual and imperceptible.

This side effect seemed insignificant, but its actual application was terrifying.

If someone at the Yin Spirit Realm drank the divine water, they would unknowingly become his person.

Li Yue could already imagine the commotion Qingquan would cause upon bringing the divine water to Baijiang Prefecture.

However, he felt no guilt.

What Qingquan did had nothing to do with him, a mere mountain.

Besides, it wasn't exactly brainwashing.

The divine water had effects, right? It could awaken one's talents, clarify one's true nature, and purify spiritual energy, right?

Since one had accepted his favor, shouldn't one revere and be grateful to him?

"When you drink water, remember its source," as the sages said, "Don't forget the well-digger when you drink water."

Therefore, this is reasonable and normal.

Of course, this was just a move he casually made; he wasn't too concerned about the magnitude of the consequences.

Anyway, it was just a matter of consuming a little divine water, something he had plenty of.

Putting these thoughts aside, Li Yue's divine sense began to quiet down, attempting to comprehend the laws of heaven and earth.

This was a clue he had discovered after advancing to the fourth-tier Sacred Mountain.

The laws governing this world are incredibly stable, almost unbelievably so.

It's like a flawless square box crafted using advanced technology in my past life.

No gaps, no bumps, every part is perfect.

It's too perfect, which is abnormal.

Li Yue harbored some doubts. Was it deliberately fabricated by some powerful being?


Chapter 129 The Reincarnation of Buddha

The Heavenly Path is Severed!

This is what Li Yue heard from most of the cultivators.

The entire world has been sealed off; it's definitely severed.

It's directly cut off from all the heavens and universes, severing the connection between heaven and earth.

Li Yue is an outsider and doesn't know there are heavens beyond heavens, but he's certain that even if there were, those inside couldn't get out.

The same logic applies to those outside; they can't get in either.

For thousands of years, no one has been able to ascend to immortality, and this is probably the reason.

The doors and windows are welded shut; where can one ascend to?

Li Yue doesn't understand the reason; after all, this happened thousands of years ago, and he's only been in this world for less than two years.

After advancing to the fourth-tier Sacred Mountain, he can use his divine sense to comprehend the laws of heaven and earth and is trying to find the flaws in this world.

But this world is too vast; searching every corner would take an unknown amount of time.

However, he happens to be a mountain himself, with nothing else to do, so this is a good way to pass the time.

Li Yue, full of enthusiasm, continued to immerse himself in comprehending the laws of heaven and earth.

.........

The capital city.

A colossal city sat at the center of the earth.

Here gathered all the elite forces of the human race, as well as the most abundant fortune of the imperial court. From afar, it resembled a black dragon coiled on the ground, slumbering.

Once awakened, it would shake the entire land.

At the massive city gate, tens of meters high, people came and went in an endless stream, half of whom were cultivators!

Among them, a young novice monk and an old monk stood out.

Passersby gave way, their gazes filled with awe.

Almost all the monks in the capital city came from the Golden Dragon Temple; none of them were to be trifled with.

After all, they were the number one Buddhist sect in the world.

“Grandmaster, is this really alright?”

The one who spoke was the old monk, his tone weathered and tinged with worry.

The young novice's voice was childish, but his gaze was incredibly profound, like that of an ancient monster who had lived for countless years.

"It's nothing. This reincarnated body is only at the peak of the fifth-tier Yin God Realm. It hasn't violated the Heavenly Water Pact."

The young novice's tone was calm, his expression utterly unwavering.

The old monk hesitated, wanting to speak but stopping himself. The body was indeed at the fifth-tier Yin God Realm, but his master was an eighth-tier Buddha!

"Master, what exactly is that secret manual? Is it really that important? The situation between humans and demons is delicate right now. Perhaps I should go to Qingzhou alone?" the old monk said, bowing his head.

The young novice glanced at the old monk indifferently.

"I must go on this trip. That secret manual is very likely the key to my senior brother's sainthood. It cannot be lost!"

The old monk's eyes widened in shock. He hadn't expected it to involve sainthood.

So he said no more and silently followed the young novice out of the city gate.

Not long after leaving the capital, seeing that nothing had happened, the old monk breathed a sigh of relief.

It seemed that his master's reincarnation could still deceive the demons.

"Although I left the capital using this reincarnation, I cannot act rashly. If anything happens on the road, you will handle it!" the young novice said calmly.

"Yes, Master," Sanzhi bowed in response.

"Master, this junior has a question," Sanzhi hesitated for a moment before speaking.

"Speak."

"Grandmaster, the manual you mentioned involves the key to Grandmaster's ascension to sainthood. Why was it so easily taken by Junior Brother Sangui?" Sanzhi voiced his doubts.

Manuals related to sainthood are no ordinary objects; they are beyond their reach.

And Sangui, his junior brother, had suffered a damaged foundation, halting his progress to the Profound Realm.

He had long been rejected by the Golden Dragon Temple, yet he could so easily take such a manual—this was unusual.

"That manual wasn't originally important; it was merely a secret method for cultivating the great elixir of the human body. But after your Grandmaster failed to break through to the Saint Realm recently, he suddenly had an epiphany."

"Since the physical body is bound by destiny, preventing any progress, why not create a flawless golden body of Buddha? Then your Grandmaster thought of the secret method in the library, but he never expected it to be taken by a disciple and then lost."

The young novice's usually calm face revealed a hint of murderous intent.

Sanzhi suddenly understood; he hadn't expected it to be like this.

“Those three monks have caused a great disaster!” he sighed.

If the secret manual isn't found, the key to the Grandmaster's ascension to sainthood will be severed; no one can bear such a loss.

No wonder the Second Grandmaster, an eighth-level cultivator, personally left the capital.

“Grandmaster, let's go to Qingzhou Prefecture first; Junior Brother Sanwu is there,” Sanzhi said.

The little novice nodded.

The two figures vanished from their spot.

About an incense stick's time later, a group of men in black appeared, standing where the two monks had disappeared.

“Holy Maiden, this bald monk from Jinlong Temple has left the capital; is there some conspiracy?”

One of the men in black spoke to a cloaked, veiled woman in the middle of them.

The woman was tall and slender, her black dress perfectly accentuating her figure, exuding allure.

The Holy Maiden frowned, sensing the lingering aura, her expression grave.

“Something’s strange. One of those two bald monks has a very odd aura.”

The Holy Maiden spoke, and the others looked surprised.

“What’s strange about him?” the man in black asked.

“This bald monk has strong blood and energy; he should be a young monk. But his aura is very old, more like an old monster.”

The Holy Maiden pondered for a moment before answering.

Everyone was puzzled.

“What should we do? Should we still chase after them? Could it be a conspiracy, a deliberate lure?”

The man in black frowned.

The Holy Maiden thought for a moment, then took out a token and activated it with her spiritual power.

The token glowed brightly, then quickly dimmed.

“Continue to follow them and see what they’re up to!”

Having received a response from the person on the token, the Holy Maiden gave the order.

So, the group of men in black headed towards Qingzhou.

Shortly after they left, an old Taoist priest slowly emerged from behind a tree, an expression of keen interest on his face.

"Interesting, very interesting! The bald monk from Jinlong Temple left the capital and was immediately targeted by the demoness of the Dragon-Slaying Sect. But that little novice monk, why have I never seen him before?"

The old Taoist priest tried to calculate with his fingers, but he couldn't figure out the little novice monk's identity.

"Strange indeed. I'll follow him and see what kind of conspiracy these bald monks are plotting."

The old Taoist priest put his hands behind his back, white smoke rose from his feet, and his figure vanished on the spot.

Just as he disappeared, he glanced at the hill behind him out of the corner of his eye.

An incense stick later.

Two figures emerged from behind the hill, both wearing bird feather headdresses.

"That Taoist priest seems to have discovered us," one of them said calmly.

"It's alright, so what if he discovered us?" the other replied dismissively.

He was more concerned about what these three forces were up to.

Especially the demoness from the Dragon-Slaying Sect—she was their target!

"You return to the capital and tell the Grand Preceptor about this. I'll go alone."

"Alone? That's too dangerous."

"It's alright. I'll contact Hua Wuzei and the others; they're also outside the capital."


Chapter 130 Demon Vanguard

The Sacred Mountain.

After advancing to the fourth-tier Sacred Mountain, the spiritual energy had doubled in density.

During this time, Uncle Wu's carefully cultivated spirit rice was finally ready for harvest.

Compared to ordinary rice, this spirit rice absorbed far more spiritual energy, so it matured much faster, requiring only a few months to harvest.

Because the spiritual energy was even more concentrated than before, this harvest of spirit rice was plump and translucent, emitting an enticing fragrance that soothed the soul.

"The spiritual power contained within this spirit rice has increased significantly! Now it truly deserves to be called spirit rice!" Uncle Wu's wrinkled face beamed with a satisfied smile, like that of a hearty-laughing old farmer.

Then he took the spirit rice to the Mountain Pavilion and found Bai Liang.

"Master Bai, look at this."

Handing the spirit rice to Bai Liang, Uncle Wu looked at it expectantly.

Bai Liang took the rice, picked up a few plump grains, and popped them into his mouth to chew. A fragrant aroma lingered on his tongue, leaving a pleasant aftertaste.

His eyes lit up, and he exclaimed, "The spiritual energy is even richer now! It's almost indistinguishable from real spiritual rice!"

Uncle Wu was overjoyed to hear this reply.

This spiritual rice was carefully cultivated by him. Although it mainly relied on the spiritual energy of the sacred mountain, without his care, the rice wouldn't have reached this quality.

"Uncle Wu, how much spiritual rice is there in total?"

Bai Liang immediately asked.

"A hundred mu in total. The old man doesn't dare to plant more, afraid that planting too much will distribute the spiritual energy and affect the quality," Uncle Wu replied.

Bai Liang nodded and smiled, "Uncle Wu, you're right. The most important thing about spiritual rice is its quality. Keep it at a hundred mu from now on, no need to increase it."

Uncle Wu agreed; he thought the same way.

Planting too much would distribute the spiritual energy, making it difficult to guarantee the quality of the spiritual rice.

He continued, "The spirit rice can yield 800 jin per mu, so 100 mu would be a full 80,000 jin."

"That much?" Bai Liang exclaimed in surprise.

Assuming one person eats five liang a day, that's 183 jin per year.

80,000 jin of spirit rice could feed over 400 people for a year!

Keep in mind, this is spirit rice! Even the imperial family in the capital wouldn't eat it every day.

Spirit rice can be considered a type of spiritual herb; it can be cultivated, but it's difficult to grow on a large scale like regular crops.

Only a small plot can be planted at a time.

Only the vast, rich spiritual energy of the sacred mountain can provide such a superior growing environment for spirit rice.

"This is because the old man didn't dare plant it too densely; otherwise, it could yield a thousand jin per mu. And this spirit rice grows rapidly; three crops can be grown a year," Uncle Wu added.

"Very good!"

Bai Liang said, "Let's stockpile half of this spirit rice for now. Like the divine water, it can be used to reward those who have made contributions."

"The other half can be sold to exchange for other cultivation resources."

There are three harvests a year, totaling 240,000 jin of spirit rice.

They can't consume that much themselves; the surplus can be sold to exchange for other cultivation resources.

For example, spiritual herbs, pills, or even talismans, magic tools, cultivation techniques, and so on.

These are currently in short supply in Qianshan Town.

However, to sell them for cultivation resources, one needs connections.

It's better if He Liyuan is in charge of this, after all, he has been traveling the martial world for many years.

However, since He Liyuan now needed to guard the Divine Spring, they had to find someone else.

Bai Liang quickly thought of Chen Jianchi, who had also traveled the martial world.

A short while later, Chen Jianchi arrived at the Mountain Pavilion.

“Master Bai, what brings you here?” Chen Jianchi asked curiously.

He had recently been working with Du Tiandao, and the two of them were jointly responsible for patrolling Qianshan Town.

He had just been dealing with a conflict between a merchant and a customer when he was hurriedly summoned by the person Bai Liang had called.

“Brother Chen, it’s like this,” Bai Liang said with a smile, explaining his plan to sell spirit rice.

After hearing what Bai Liang said, Chen Jianchi was astonished.

Then he tried the spirit rice that Uncle Wu had brought, and feeling the incredibly rich aroma and spiritual energy, his eyes shone even brighter.

“Good rice! Although it’s not quite as good as real spirit rice, it’s still good enough!” Chen Jianchi said excitedly.

Especially when he heard that there were a full 80,000 jin of spirit rice, his breath caught in his throat, and he could hardly believe it.

“We’ll keep half of this spirit rice for ourselves, and we can exchange the other half for other cultivation resources,” Bai Liang told Chen Jianchi.

Chen Jianchi nodded, then said, “If we want to sell spirit rice, a cultivation resource, we can only go to the county town. However, we have too much spirit rice; the county town simply can’t handle it all. We’ll have to go to the prefectural capital.”

“The prefectural capital is over a thousand li away. That’s a difficult journey,” Bai Liang said, frowning.

Not to mention the possibility of encountering demons along the way, the journey itself will take time.

“I’ve been to the prefectural capital twice. With a fast horse, a round trip can be done in half a month,” Chen Jianchi said.

There are official roads leading to the prefectural capital, allowing carriages to travel quickly.

Currently, Chen Jianchi is only at the Tongfa Realm, so Bai Liang ultimately decided against sending him to the prefectural capital.

The journey itself is too unpredictable, and moreover, there’s too much spirit rice—such a valuable resource—it’s hard to guarantee that the people in the prefectural capital won’t be tempted.

“Forget it, going to the state capital is too dangerous. You should try contacting the old channels of the Liyuan Escort Agency, and ideally have them come to Qianshan Town to buy the spirit rice themselves,” Bai Liang said.

Chen Jianchi thought for a moment and nodded in agreement.

Before leaving, he took a bag of spirit rice with him; this needed to be inspected by someone else.

..............................

The border between Baijiang Prefecture and Qing Prefecture.

This place was unguarded, just desolate mountains and forests.

A large swath of black mist swept across, and a group of demons swaggered across the border, entering Qing Prefecture.

“Second Brother, did Commander Dao send us to Qing Prefecture to prepare for an attack?”

A figure spoke from within the shadows.

It looked human, but had an eye on its forehead, and horns growing from its head, as if a knife had been stuck in its forehead, making it appear extremely sinister.

"Nonsense! If we weren't going down the mountain to Qingzhou, why would we come here? I think your brain's been damaged by those knives, you've become stupid!"

The demon known as Second Brother replied impatiently.

His appearance was the same as the former, with eyes on his forehead and his head riddled with blades.

They were one of the demon race, the Knife Demons.

"Then why don't we just come ourselves? Why did we have to bring those stupid donkeys from the demon race along?"

"Who are you calling a stupid donkey?"

This was overheard by the other demon race, and a hot-tempered donkey demon immediately roared.

"We're all allies, why get angry? I just spoke without thinking, Brother Donkey, calm down," the Blade Demon said, quickly soothing the angry donkey demons.
