"Our target is the human race. Demons and monsters are one family, let's not fight amongst ourselves," he continued.

The donkey demons, hearing this, finally suppressed their anger.

"Alright, Commander Blade has ordered us to scout out Qingzhou. The border between Qingzhou and Baijiangzhou is Liaoyun County, so we'll start there," the Blade Demon said calmly.


Chapter 131 Wu Ze Breaks Through to the Divine Enlightenment Realm

The Blade Demon clan naturally had no objections and obeyed the orders of their second brother, the Blade Demon.

The Donkey Demon hesitated for a moment, but ultimately also obeyed the Blade Demon's commands.

Although demons were allies, the two sixth-tier beings appearing in Baijiang Prefecture were both demons.

As the vanguard of the demons, they were both fourth-tier in strength.

Then, a group of demons crossed the border of Qingzhou and entered Liaoyun County.

To gather intelligence, their first task was to find the local demons of Liaoyun County.

So they began searching the area around Liaoyun County, but the results puzzled them.

Why were there so few demons in Liaoyun County?

Moreover, not a single fifth-tier demon was found; they hadn't found any at all.

"What happened? Could it be that some human expert intervened in Liaoyun County and cleared it out?"

The Blade Demon said coldly.

"Impossible! How could humans have such courage? I'd rather believe the demons committed suicide than that humans would launch a preemptive strike!"

The donkey demon scoffed.

In the eight years of struggle, apart from the initial two years when humans showed some backbone, they had been completely on the defensive.

Even in the largest battle in recent years, the Battle of Tianshui, the Great Chu Dynasty only ever defended, only being forced to fight back when attacked on their own.

"Search again, they might be hiding," the sword demon said calmly.

Then, the demon vanguard began a thorough search of Liaoyun County.

As for the human counties, they would not attack for the time being, lest they alert the Great Chu court.

The direction they headed was precisely towards the Divine Mountain.

.......

The Divine Mountain.

Wu Ze's cultivation had long since reached the peak of the Profound Realm. During this time, he had been continuously consuming the flesh and blood of demon generals, coupled with the miraculous effects of the divine water.

This had allowed him to vaguely touch the threshold of the Enlightened God Realm.

The fourth tier of the Divine Enlightenment Realm is a significant hurdle. The leap from the third to the fourth tier involves the condensation of the Primordial Spirit, a qualitative leap!

At this moment, Wu Ze is attempting to condense his Primordial Spirit and break through to the Divine Enlightenment Realm.

This hurdle is extremely difficult, unlike his previous breakthroughs.

Condensing the Primordial Spirit requires complete focus and tranquility, free from distractions.

Therefore, before his breakthrough, Wu Ze consumed divine water to clear his mind of confusion and clarify his true nature.

Even so, his breakthrough process is incredibly difficult.

Each time his Primordial Spirit is about to condense, it immediately dissipates.

He repeats this process endlessly, his Primordial Spirit condensing and dissipating repeatedly.

This process might require hundreds, even thousands of attempts for an ordinary cultivator to succeed.

But with Wu Ze's talent enhanced by the divine talisman, he only needs a few dozen attempts.

Upon breaking through to the Divine Enlightenment Realm, no unusual phenomena appeared in the outside world, except that the surrounding spiritual energy became slightly denser.

The real anomalies occurred within his body.

His primordial spirit condensed, slowly taking on the shape of Wu Ze, but each time this happened, strong winds, thunderbolts, fire, and floods would surge within his body, constantly impacting and destroying the newly condensed primordial spirit, causing it to dissipate.

Condensing a primordial spirit under these anomalies was extremely difficult.

A day had passed, and Wu Ze had tried more than a dozen times, still without success.

Outside, Bai Liang and the others waited silently.

None of them had experience breaking through to the Divine Enlightenment Realm, so they could only wait idly.

Kong Wang sat cross-legged on the ground, his eyes flashing with golden light, seemingly able to see the extraordinary phenomena within Wu Ze's body.

"His primordial spirit is becoming increasingly refined. In another day, he should be able to successfully break through," Kong Wang said calmly.

"Really? That's good!"

Hearing Kong Wang's words, Bai Liang and the others breathed a sigh of relief.

They were all familiar with Kong Wang's pupil technique; it was quite extraordinary.

He truly deserved to be chosen by the Mountain God.

Among them, He Liyuan watched most intently.

Because he was also about to break through to the Enlightenment Realm, observing Wu Ze's breakthrough process and accumulating experience was very important to him.

Time passed second by second, and Wu Ze was still breaking through.

At this point, he had already condensed his primordial spirit more than twenty times, but each time it dissipated.

The power of the extraordinary phenomena was extremely fierce; if the primordial spirit wasn't perfectly refined, it wouldn't be able to withstand the impact of the phenomena.

After experiencing more than twenty failures, Wu Ze began to slow down his progress.

He slowed down the condensation of his primordial spirit, focusing more intently on it to maximize its strength.

The moment the primordial spirit solidified, extraordinary phenomena descended: raging winds, flames, thunder, and floods relentlessly assaulted it.

This time, the primordial spirit withstood several waves of impact without disintegrating.

Wu Ze's heart stirred; he thought he was about to succeed.

But under the next wave of these phenomena, the primordial spirit crumbled again.

Compared to the previous twenty-odd attempts, this time it lasted much longer.

Seeing hope for success, Wu Ze was invigorated and immediately began attempting to condense his primordial spirit again.

With the experience from before, this time it went incredibly smoothly, quickly raising the strength of his primordial spirit to another level.

However, it still couldn't withstand the impact of the phenomena, but it lasted a little longer.

Continuing to try, on the fourth attempt, the primordial spirit withstood the impact of the phenomena.

Despite the onslaught of all the strange phenomena, his primordial spirit did not dissipate but remained intact within his body.

Overjoyed, Wu Ze immediately seized the opportunity, mobilizing all his power to stabilize his condensed primordial spirit, and then slowly fused it with his physical body.

At this moment, his aura steadily increased, growing stronger and stronger until he broke through to the Enlightenment Realm, unleashing the aura of a fourth-tier cultivator!

Feeling the powerful aura spreading outwards,

Bai Liang and the others waiting outside all wore expressions of delight.

"It's done! Brother Wu Ze has successfully broken through!" Bai Liang laughed.

After waiting a moment longer, a spirited Wu Ze pushed open the door and walked out.

The others hurriedly stepped forward to congratulate him, and Wu Ze laughed heartily, in a very good mood.

Becoming a fourth-tier Enlightenment Realm cultivator meant that his strength had undergone a qualitative change.

The speed provided by the Divine Travel Talisman also experienced a leap.

Wu Ze recalled the day the Mountain God had smashed Lin Zhenjiang's body. Now, if he were to face Lin Zhenjiang, his Swift Travel Talisman would prevent Lin Zhenjiang from touching him.

However, injuring Lin Zhenjiang wouldn't be easy either; the outcome would be roughly even.

"From now on, we won't need to ask the Mountain God for help anymore."

This was what pleased Wu Ze the most.

Before, every time they encountered an enemy, they couldn't handle them, and in the end, the Mountain God had to protect them.

Although he outwardly appeared pleased by the Mountain God's manifestation, he was inwardly ashamed, feeling utterly useless.

He couldn't solve problems on his own, always having to wait for the Mountain God's intervention.

He wasn't the only one feeling this way; Bai Liang and the others felt the same way.

But now things were different. With the power of the Fourth-Order Enlightenment Realm and the Divine Travel Talisman,

within the Great Chu territory bound by the Heavenly Water Pact, they now possessed sufficient strength to handle most problems.


Chapter 132 Wind-Fixing Pearl, Divine Concealment Talisman

Li Yue, who was comprehending the laws of heaven and earth, suddenly received a notification on his interface.

[The recipient has broken through to the Enlightenment Realm for the first time, obtaining the magic artifact, the Wind-Stabilizing Pearl.]

[Wind-Stabilizing Pearl: Feng Shui, the terrain. Using the Wind-Stabilizing Pearl can stabilize the terrain of a region, establish a grand formation, and protect it from natural disasters and man-made calamities.]

Seeing this on the interface, Li Yue reacted and focused his divine sense on Thousand Mountain Town.

Then he learned that Wu Ze had successfully broken through to the Enlightenment Realm.

He had been too engrossed in comprehending the laws of heaven and earth to pay much attention to what was happening in Thousand Mountain Town.

"Pretty fast, about a year, from an ordinary person to a fourth-tier Enlightenment Realm cultivator."

Li Yue was quite satisfied.

He could only say that the talent bestowed by the divine talisman was truly top-tier.

At this rate, given another five years, he might even have a chance to touch the threshold of the Saint Realm.

He then turned his attention to the newly acquired Wind-Stabilizing Pearl, a new magic artifact.

Li Yue glanced at the description and immediately understood that this was a magical artifact related to terrain formations.

After use, it could stabilize the power of a region, protecting it from any natural disasters or man-made calamities.

He took out the Wind-Stabilizing Pearl, an earthen-yellow bead radiating a mysterious aura.

He sensed its power; the area it affected was small, roughly twenty miles in radius.

Just enough to cover the entire divine mountain, including Thousand Mountain Town.

"This is good; it can protect Thousand Mountain Town in my place."

Li Yue was quite satisfied with the Wind-Stabilizing Pearl. Recently, he had been deeply immersed in comprehending the laws of heaven and earth, his divine sense focused and unable to concentrate.

Moreover, during this comprehension, he had no concept of time; if it weren't for the system panel notification, he might not have woken up from his comprehension.

The laws of this world were peculiar; he felt there were many questions surrounding them, so he wanted to thoroughly understand this world through comprehension.

However, he was still worried about Thousand Mountain Town, so the Wind-Stabilizing Pearl was perfect for him.

Then, Li Yue placed the Wind-Stabilizing Pearl atop the Divine Mountain and activated it.

Instantly, a ripple of dark yellow light spread from within the pearl, rapidly expanding outwards from the Divine Mountain.

It was like a pebble dropped into still water, creating ripples.

The dark yellow ripples stopped after spreading for twenty miles, then slowly disappeared into the terrain.

The protective formation was complete!

Li Yue tested the strength of the Wind-Stabilizing Pearl again, finding it could withstand an attack of eighty percent of his strength. Converted, this meant it was at least at the seventh or eighth level of cultivation.

With this strength, even if a demon army launched a full-scale attack on the Great Chu Dynasty, the Wind-Stabilizing Pearl could protect the people in Thousand Mountain Town.

Unless the demons had an expert of the eighth level or higher, they wouldn't be able to break the Wind-Stabilizing Pearl's formation.

Moreover, even if an expert of the eighth level or higher appeared, the Wind-Stabilizing Pearl could hold out until he was awakened from his comprehension.

"That's enough!"

After the experiment, Li Yue wanted to continue comprehending the laws of heaven and earth.

However, after thinking about it, he didn't know how long this comprehension would take.

So he decided to arrange some things to avoid being delayed by too much comprehension.

Then, Li Yue took out the Divine Weapon Furnace and placed it in a cave halfway up the mountain.

Another decree descended upon the temple; as long as Bai Liang and his companions went to offer prayers, they would receive his decree.

Having done this, his divine sense located Kong Wang, who was honing his cultivation in the Mountain Tower.

Kong Wang was born through a secret technique, possessing innate knowledge and an extremely high potential.

Li Yue had examined his talent and found no so-called shackles of destiny.

In other words, Kong Wang had a chance to reach the level of a saint!

However, it was only a chance. Kong Wang was not a normally born being; he was an exception to the rules of heaven and earth, which was why he was not bound by the shackles of destiny.

Li Yue had wanted to bestow a blessing upon Kong Wang before, but it had been delayed until now.

He began to cast the Mountain God's Blessing. Countless golden lights began to gather, and power was drawn from within the divine mountain, converging into a golden talisman.

The moment this talisman appeared, it suddenly vanished, as if it had become invisible.

But Li Yue could clearly see the golden divine talisman floating in the air, and its information also appeared clearly in his mind.

“Divine Concealment Talisman, command!”

With his low shout, the Divine Concealment Talisman transformed into a faint golden light, falling into the Mountain Tower and entering Kong Wang's body.

Kong Wang suddenly opened his eyes at that moment, his pupils flashing with golden light.

He knew that the Mountain God had bestowed something upon him, but there was no time to express gratitude. He quickly closed his eyes, opened his mind, and accepted the Divine Concealment Talisman into his body.

With the talisman inside, the divine power unique to the deity bloomed, filling every part of his body.

Kong Wang's cultivation level continued to rise.

Having only recently broken through to the Profound Realm, his cultivation level was now rising rapidly, finally approaching the Enlightened God Realm, reaching the peak of the Profound Realm.

“Hoo!” After receiving the talisman, Kong Wang exhaled.

He immediately rose and bowed respectfully towards the sacred mountain.

"Disciple Kongwang kowtows to thank the Mountain God for his blessing!"

After blessing Kongwang, Li Yue withdrew his divine sense, carefully considered the situation, and confirmed that all necessary arrangements had been made.

Then he focused his mind and began to concentrate all his divine sense on comprehending the laws of heaven and earth.

The changes that occurred on the sacred mountain were sensed by Bai Liang and the others.

First, ripples of a dark yellow color radiated from the sacred mountain for a radius of twenty miles, eventually disappearing underground.

Then, divine light fell upon the temple.

Finally, golden light transformed into a divine talisman, and the Mountain God blessed Kongwang.

These successive events alarmed everyone.

"What's going on? Is there something unusual about the sacred mountain?"

Wu Ze, who had been consolidating his cultivation, couldn't care less now and immediately sought out Bai Liang to inquire.

Bai Liang nodded: "There is indeed something unusual. Let's go to the Mountain Tower first. Judging from that aura just now, someone else must have received the Mountain God's blessing."

Divine talismans are interconnected, so they could sense the arrival of another recipient.

The two arrived at the Mountain Tower and saw Kong Wang meditating cross-legged.

Sensing that Kong Wang already possessed the aura of a peak Profound Realm cultivator, the two immediately understood that the recipient was Kong Wang.

A moment later, Kong Wang finished meditating, opened his eyes, and stood up.

"Congratulations, Kong Wang, on receiving the Mountain God's blessing," Bai Liang said first with a smile.

Wu Ze also offered his congratulations.

Kong Wang nodded slightly: "I must thank the Mountain God for his favor."

Bai Liang then asked Kong Wang what the name of the divine talisman he had received was.

Kong Wang didn't hide anything and answered directly, "The Divine Concealment Talisman."

"The Divine Concealment Talisman?" Wu Ze asked in surprise, then thought for a moment and asked, "Is it related to concealment?"

Kong Wang nodded, then activated the power of the talisman, and instantly the alluring fragrance of the human elixir emanating from his body vanished.

Not only that, he then demonstrated how to conceal his aura.

Standing before Bai Liang and the other man, there was not a trace of his aura; they couldn't sense him at all.


Chapter 133 A Wandering Journey Leads to Suicide

Besides concealing one's aura, it can also conceal one's form.

Watching Kong Wang's figure slowly dissipate, Bai Liang and the other man both showed surprise.

The effect of this Divine Concealment Talisman is incredibly powerful.

It's a bit unusual for a monk to use it. It's more suitable for assassins.

"With this Divine Concealment Talisman, Kong Wang will be much safer traveling outside."

Having witnessed the powerful concealment effect of the Divine Concealment Talisman, Bai Liang couldn't help but praise it.

The talisman he received was the Divine Sense Talisman, and now, at the Profound Realm, his divine sense was already comparable to the late-stage Enlightenment Realm.

Even so, his divine sense had difficulty sensing Kong Wang's location.

As for Wu Ze, it was even more difficult. After all, he possessed the Divine Speed ​​Talisman, which gave him an advantage in speed.

Kong Wang revealed himself, a smile on his face.

He had always been troubled by the alluring fragrance emanating from his body; it was practically a walking, talking drug, attracting trouble wherever he went.

Now, with the Divine Concealment Talisman, this problem could be solved perfectly.

Wu Ze informed Kong Wang of the changes occurring on the sacred mountain, then asked, "Kong Wang, you have eye techniques, can you tell what's going on?"

Kong Wang opened his eyes, surveying the area around Thousand Mountain Town, and saw the mysterious yellow energy hidden in the terrain.

"It should be a great array protecting the sacred mountain," Kong Wang judged.

Bai Liang and the other man understood immediately.

"And there are changes at the temple, let's go take a look," Bai Liang said, and the three of them went to the temple together.

As is customary, they first offered incense and prayers to the Mountain God.

Just then, a ray of golden light swept past them, and Li Yue's decree entered their minds.

The three of them were dazed for a few seconds, then completely understood.

The Mountain God's decree was extensive and very important.

Bai Liang's expression turned serious, and he kowtowed deeply.

"Mountain God, please rest assured, your disciples will not fail your guidance."

Wu Ze and Kong Wang also kowtowed.

The Mountain God's decree was concise: in the coming days, the Mountain God would be comprehending the Great Dao and would have no time to protect them.

The mountainside was open to them; they could cultivate in the spiritually rich caves there, which also contained a supreme treasure—a divine weapon forging furnace.

The activation incantation for the protective array was also explained to them.

This was a major event, the biggest event Qianshan Town had ever encountered since its founding.

Bai Liang and the others felt a mix of emotions, a sense of heaviness.

They felt like children who had lost their adult's protection, filled with worry.

Leaving the temple, Wu Ze said in a deep voice, "From now on, Qianshan Town will truly have to rely on ourselves."

Bai Liang nodded, "I hope we won't disappoint the Mountain God."

This news was known only to the core members of Qianshan Town.

For example, He Liyuan, Qian Ke, Du Tiandao, etc.

As for Li Xue, the little girl doesn't need to be informed.

Because of the Divine Fate Talisman, Bai Liang and the others don't want Li Xue to get too involved.

Unless absolutely necessary, in a situation they can't handle, then Li Xue will be used.

Therefore, Li Xue is currently Qianshan Town's ultimate weapon, their trump card, and won't be used unless absolutely necessary.

Bai Liang has delegated some tasks, and he's gradually handing over the management of ordinary people in Qianshan Town to Xiao Nian.

"The development of Qianshan Town cannot be neglected," he told Xiao Nian.

Residents from Taohua County continued to migrate, along with refugees from various counties in Liaoyun Prefecture.

The sheer number of people meant that Qianshan Town was constantly under construction, never ceasing.

These days, several more people had developed cultivation talent after drinking the divine water, and they were all sent to the Hundred Refinement Hall.

These people would become Qianshan Town's reserve force.

Du Tiandao had successfully broken through and officially become a cultivator.

He was extremely excited, and even went to Long Qianqian's restaurant to get completely drunk.

In the end, Long Qianqian personally helped him to his room to rest.

That day, Kong Wang decided to leave to travel.

Bai Liang, He Liyuan, and several others came to see him off.

"Gentlemen, see me off here."

Kong Wang chanted a Buddhist prayer and bowed his head slightly.

His current realm was the peak of the Profound Realm, only one step away from the Divine Enlightenment Realm.

Bai Liang and his companions stopped, about to bid farewell.

But suddenly, a fierce wind arose, and a dark, ominous cloud appeared on the distant horizon, accompanied by a violent aura sweeping in.

"Help...help! Demons!!"

"Ah...run!"

Following this were screams and cries for help.

Looking ahead, they saw a group of refugees fleeing frantically from the dark cloud, their faces filled with fear and despair.

Those who were slow were grabbed by a giant hand reaching out from the cloud, dragged into it, and then blood began to fall.

"Bold demon! How dare you act so recklessly near the sacred mountain!"

Wu Ze's face darkened. He took a step forward, activated his Divine Speed ​​Talisman, and in the blink of an eye appeared beside a refugee who was about to be captured. He punched the hand, smashing it off in two.

"Ugh—"

A cry of pain rang out, followed by the dispersal of the dark clouds, revealing a group of demons—the vanguard of demons from Baijiang Prefecture.

The one whose arm Wu Ze had broken was a sword demon. It gritted its teeth, its face contorted with rage, its venomous eyes fixed on Wu Ze, wishing it could devour him alive.

"Where did these human cultivators come from, daring to block our way? Don't they know their own limitations!"

The leader of the sword demons said coldly, glancing briefly at Kong Wang and the others who had rushed over.

They were all at the Profound Realm, except for the one who had just attacked—this kid was at the Enlightened Realm, nothing to worry about!

On their side, there were four or five fourth-tier demons!

"So many demons, where did they come from?"

Bai Liang's expression was grave as he looked at the at least twenty demons, his brows furrowed.

"Immortal...Immortal Master, save me! These demons came from the direction of Baijiang Prefecture; they've been chasing us all the way," a refugee cried, weeping.

"Baijiang Prefecture!?"

Bai Liang and the others immediately understood.

"Haha, now that you know we're from Baijiang Prefecture, why don't you obediently offer your heads?" The donkey demon grinned, its large mouth gaping open in a cold voice.

Wu Ze coldly replied, "I don't care where you're from. Since you dared to appear near the Divine Mountain, today is the death day for you beasts!"

"How dare you! There are really people who aren't afraid of death?"

The Blade Demon's tone was chilling. It had never encountered such a defiant human before, which greatly displeased it.

Wu Ze didn't say anything more. Having broken through to the Enlightenment Realm, he was already itching for a fight.

Today's the perfect opportunity to practice on these demons.

Kong Wang slowly stepped forward, his eyes flashing with golden light. His aura suddenly vanished, and none of the demons sensed his approach.

"Seeking death!" The donkey demon's gaze turned cold, its demonic energy spreading outwards as its massive hoof slammed towards Wu Ze.

But the speed of the Divine Speed ​​Talisman was beyond its comprehension.

In the blink of an eye, the figure disappeared, reappearing behind the donkey demon.


Chapter 134 Annihilation!

"What?!" The donkey demon had never seen such speed before, and was completely stunned.

If it weren't for the aura of the Enlightened God Realm emanating from Wu Ze, it would have thought he was a Yin God Realm cultivator.

Unable to defend itself, the donkey demon could only turn sideways to avoid a fatal blow.

As a result, Wu Ze punched its rump hard, instantly tearing open its flesh and splattering blood everywhere.

The other demons were all horrified at the sight.

"Be careful, this human is strange, his strength should not be underestimated!" The sword demon's face darkened, and its eyes began to turn serious.

The blade stuck upside down on its head began to glow red, radiating a chilling aura.

In an instant, more than a dozen blades were drawn and shot directly at Wu Ze.

Wu Ze glanced at them; the blades that the sword demon carried were quite powerful, it was best not to take them head-on.

After all, there were more than twenty demons here, and he needed to conserve his strength.

He activated the Divine Speed ​​Talisman again, and as the blade slashed towards him, Wu Ze's figure vanished once more.

"Damn it!" The attack missed, and the Blade Demon's face darkened.

"Human rats, are you only good for hiding? If you have the guts, fight us fair and square!" He began to sneer and mock.

The donkey demon, whose rear end had been smashed by a punch, also jumped out, cursing, "Humans are only fit to be cowards, resorting to these sneak attacks. If we face you head-on, you're nothing but food for us demons!"

As soon as he finished speaking, a ripple appeared on his right, and a handsome monk suddenly appeared, then raised his hand and slapped the donkey demon's other rear end.

"Smack—"

A crisp sound echoed; the force of the slap was considerable, directly causing the donkey demon's other rear end to swell.

"Ouch!"

A scream rang out, the donkey demon's eyes bulging. Having been ambushed twice in its rear, it was seething with rage.

Turning around, it saw a handsome monk smiling at it, chanting a Buddhist prayer.

"You bald donkey!" the donkey demon roared in fury!

It was both shocked and enraged, never expecting to be ambushed by a monk.

When did this human monk learn the art of concealment?

"Are you insulting yourself?" Kong Wang said calmly.

The donkey demon's anger was completely ignited, and it launched a desperate attack on Kong Wang.

Wu Ze tried to help, while the Blade Demon stepped in to block him.

Kong Wang dared not take the donkey demon's attack head-on; he was not yet at the Enlightenment Realm, and the difference in strength between them was significant.

Relying on the concealment effect of the Divine Concealment Talisman, he vanished from the spot, causing the donkey demon's attack to miss completely.

The donkey demon, enraged, began frantically attacking the surrounding air, trying to pinpoint Kong Wang's location.

Wu Ze was still fighting the sword demon when another demon and a monster joined the battle, both at the fourth level.

Using the power of the Divine Speed ​​Talisman, Wu Ze easily dealt with the three demons, occasionally even launching counterattacks—a clear case of one against three.

Seeing that he still couldn't find Kong Wang's location, and witnessing that the sword demon's two monsters and one demon couldn't defeat Wu Ze, the donkey demon immediately cursed, "Three against one and you still can't take him down? Do you even know how to fight?"

"If you're so good, you go!" the sword demon coldly replied, taking a dozen steps back and gesturing for the donkey demon to go and take down Wu Ze.

"I was just saying, you actually believed me?"

The donkey demon, naturally, wouldn't advance. Its speed was inherently slow; forget about defeating Wu Ze, it would be a miracle if it didn't get killed.

"Shut up if you're not going to fight!"

The sword demon's tone was annoyed, but in truth, the thought of retreating had already taken root in its heart.

These two humans were truly too strange, their strength ridiculously powerful.

They were both clearly fourth-tier, and that monk was only at the peak of the third tier, yet he could hold his own against them to a draw.

"Ah! No..."

The battlefield changed again. Wu Ze rapidly approached one of the demons, his fists slamming down repeatedly.

Simultaneously, ripples appeared on the other side, and Kong Wang appeared behind him, striking down with a palm.

The demon, bearing the full force of the two attacks, exploded on the spot, turning into wisps of black mist.

Then a crystal fell to the ground.

"Retreat!"

The sword demon didn't hesitate at all, turning and running.

The donkey demon reacted slowly, only realizing what was happening after Wu Ze had snapped the other demon's head off.

"Run!" It turned to flee, but Kong Wang had been waiting, appearing before it, his eyes flashing with golden light as he searched for an opening in the donkey demon's body before attacking.

The donkey demon hastily parried, but with a thought of retreat forming in its mind, it had already lost a third of its fighting strength.

And Kong Wang, empowered by the Divine Concealment Talisman, was simply beyond its ability to withstand.

In less than ten rounds, the donkey demon was struck on the forehead by a palm, dying instantly.

"Kill! Don't let them escape!"

Wu Ze, watching the remaining fleeing demons, had no intention of letting them go.

Activating the Divine Speed ​​Talisman, not a single demon could escape his grasp.

"Leave a way out for others. We came to Qingzhou on the orders of the Blade Commander. If you kill us all, the Blade Commander will personally lead a large army to slaughter the entire Qingzhou!"

The Blade Demon threatened, his words a mixture of bravado and fear, as he fled rapidly.

"Heh, even if we don't kill you, the demon army will still come to Qingzhou. So you might as well die!"

Wu Ze's face was cold, his hands already stained with demon blood.

The next moment, he appeared behind the Blade Demon and snapped its neck.

Kong Wang was also chasing the demons, and the two of them annihilated all twenty-odd demons that had attacked in just a quarter of an hour!

"No one's been missed," Kong Wang said in a low voice, his golden eyes dimming.

Wu Ze wiped the blood from his hands and said in a deep voice, "That's good. Let's clean up first, so as not to frighten the passing villagers."

Afterwards, Bai Liang arranged for the patrol team to come and deal with the demon corpses.

There were also demonic seeds left on the ground after the demons died. These were beneficial to cultivators, but had many side effects; one could easily be affected by the demonic energy within them.

However, Kong Wang was very knowledgeable about demonic seeds. He said, "This thing can be processed like the flesh and blood of demon generals. After processing, it can be absorbed directly."

There was nothing that the divine aura of the sacred mountain couldn't dispel.

After finishing everything here, Kong Wang said to the group, "This humble monk will take his leave now. This journey is for travel, and I may not return for several years."

Bai Liang and the others solemnly bid farewell to Kong Wang.

"Master Kong Wang, have a safe journey."

Kong Wang uttered a final Buddhist chant before turning and leaving.

Only after watching Kong Wang's figure disappear did Bai Liang and the others turn their gaze away.

Now there were more important matters to attend to; these demons all came from Baijiang Prefecture.

This indicates that the two sixth-tier demonic creatures from Baijiang Prefecture have already set their sights on Qingzhou.

They need to prepare a countermeasure as soon as possible, otherwise the consequences will be unimaginable.

The group returned to Qianshan Town, and Bai Liang summoned all the core personnel to the Mountain Pavilion for a meeting.

Even Zhang, the clerk in charge of Peach Blossom County affairs, was summoned.


Chapter 135 Chaos in Baijiang Prefecture

When everyone arrived at the Mountain Tower, there were dozens of people, including Li Xue.

Seeing this, everyone knew something serious had happened, and a solemn expression appeared on their faces.

Wu Ze gestured for Bai Liang to speak, and Bai Liang nodded, looking at the crowd and saying, "Everyone, you should all know what happened in Baijiang Prefecture, right?"

"Now, those two sixth-tier demons have their eyes on Qing Prefecture. And the first target is Liaoyun County, where Qianshan Town is located!"

Bai Liang went on to say that the demon vanguard had arrived outside Qianshan Town and was then killed by Wu Ze and Kong Wang.

Upon hearing this, everyone's hearts tightened.

However, they weren't particularly afraid. After all, they had the Mountain God's protection; what could sixth-tier demons do?

Bai Liang saw the thoughts in their minds and said calmly, "Do you all think that with the Mountain God's protection, you don't need to worry?"

Some hesitated, but finally nodded.

“Yes, Master Bai, the Mountain God protects us, so what are sixth-tier demons?” he said.

“Arrogant! Self-righteous!” Bai Liang’s tone hardened. He glanced at the man and then said,

“Then I’ll tell you now, the Mountain God has gone into hiding and won’t be showing himself to protect us anytime soon!”

Upon hearing this, those unaware of the news were immediately horrified and incredulous.

“Ah? The Mountain God has gone into hiding? What do we do?”

“That’s right, Master Wu has only just broken through to the Enlightenment Realm. Facing a sixth-tier demon, he’s utterly powerless!”

Their expressions shifted, and unease began to creep into their hearts.

Bai Liang and his companions silently observed their reactions.

Some were frightened and retreated, while others hesitated, lost in thought.

“Master Bai, have you thought of a plan?” Zhang Shiye cautiously inquired.

“No,” Bai Liang replied calmly, “So isn’t this all for the purpose of bringing you all here to discuss this together?”

Some people began to murmur.

“We’re just ordinary people, how can we discuss this?”

“Right, why don’t you, Master Bai, awaken the Mountain God and ask Him to manifest His divine protection for us?”

Wu Ze’s gaze swept coldly over them, noting down the names of those who spoke.

Bai Liang’s expression was stern as he looked at these people, then waved his hand.

Du Tiandao, who was standing at the back, led several people forward and immediately apprehended the few who had spoken and those who had acted suspiciously.

“What…what’s going on? Master Bai, Master Wu, are you trying to discard us after we’ve served our purpose?” the apprehended man shouted.

Bai Liang looked at him calmly and then threw an account book at his face.

"You know what you've done. Don't think I'll turn a blind eye just because you're old. I'm giving you this last chance!" Bai Liang said coldly.

Qianshan Town has grown considerably in scale, with its management staff expanding from a dozen to over a hundred.

This growth naturally breeds corruption.

Bai Liang knew this well, so he had Xiao Nian secretly investigate the accounts long ago.

They found over twenty people involved in embezzlement and bribery.

Half of them were original members of Qianshan Manor!

The man's face turned deathly pale upon seeing the ledger.

"Take them out and sentence them according to their crimes. Those who deserve death, kill; those who deserve imprisonment, imprison!" Bai Liang said calmly.

"Yes!" Du Tiandao, upon receiving the order, immediately commanded the patrol team to take the seven or eight people away.

After removing the corrupt elements, those remaining were the true core members of Qianshan Town.

Next, Bai Liang and Wu Ze, along with the others, discussed how Qianshan Town should deal with the threat from Baijiang Prefecture.

In short, with the Mountain God of Qianshan Town currently in hiding, there was no absolute power to defeat a sixth-tier demon.

However, similarly, the sixth-tier demon could not invade Qianshan Town because of the protective array left behind by the Mountain God.

This directly made Qianshan Town invincible.

Of course, if the demons kept their eyes on Qianshan Town and provoked them,

then Li Xue would have to intervene, activating the Divine Fate Talisman to kill the sixth-tier demon and establish their authority.

However, this was her ultimate trump card, requiring Li Xue to expend her lifespan, so it couldn't be used lightly.

At the same time, she needed to contact the imperial court to request that they also send troops to fight the demons.

They couldn't let Qianshan Town fight the demon army of Baijiang Prefecture alone, could they?

.........

Baijiang Prefecture, the capital city.

This capital city was far more prosperous than Qingzhou. The city's pavilions and towers were brightly lit at night, a truly bustling scene.

Although Baijiang Prefecture had just experienced the massacre of three prefectures and millions of its people by demons,

this did not affect the wealthy people of the capital city's carefree lifestyle, indulging in nightly revelry.

Within the capital city stood a Taoist temple called Xuanqing Temple.

It occupied a vast area, spanning over ten acres.

As the foremost Taoist sect in the land, such grandeur was to be expected.

At this moment, in Xuanqing Temple, two Taoist priests sat cross-legged facing each other.

Before them was a huge water bag made of animal hide.

It was filled with crystal-clear spring water, emanating a cool, refreshing scent.

“Junior Sister Qingquan, you’re saying a deity sent you to bring this back?” Qian Kun asked, feeling utterly bewildered.

Qingquan nodded, saying, “Yes, Uncle-Master, this divine water was ordered by the mountain god to be brought back to Baijiang Prefecture by his subordinates.”

She spoke the truth, though she too was filled with doubt.

“Why?” Qian Kun asked, puzzled.

After returning to Baijiang Prefecture, Qingquan breathed a sigh of relief upon seeing her long-lost junior sister unharmed.

But when she asked about her senior sisters and brothers, Qingquan burst into tears.

Qian Kun, after hearing the whole story, sighed softly.

“It was my fault, Uncle-Master. I shouldn’t have told you about the divination.”

He had only casually cast a divination and recited the result.

Qingqiu overheard this, and the three of them volunteered to go to Qingzhou together.

In the end, only the youngest, Qingquan, returned.

Qian Kun felt somewhat guilty, unsure how to face the master of his three junior disciples.

"I don't know either, but it was the condition that the mountain god restored my injuries and foundation, and I agreed," Qingquan replied.

Qian Kun frowned and said, "It was precisely because He restored your foundation and left behind divine aura that your uncle believed what you said."

Qingquan's initial words were too far-fetched, claiming that a god existed in Qingzhou.

At first, he thought Qingquan had lost his mind.

It wasn't until he sensed that incomparably sacred aura emanating from Qingquan, an aura not belonging to a cultivator, that he gradually began to believe it might truly be a deity.

"Then, Uncle-Master, should we listen to that mountain god and send this divine water away?" Qingquan asked hesitantly.

Qian Kun pondered for a moment, then said, "This divine water contains the essence of heaven and earth. I believe that drinking it should allow one to see through their true nature and purify their spiritual power. It's a rare treasure of heaven and earth; even those at the Yin God Realm would be tempted by it!"

"But that mountain god asked you to take the divine water to Baijiang Prefecture to send it away, which means there are other problems with the divine water that I can't see."

"It can be sent away, but it must be done using other methods. Otherwise, if something goes wrong, our nephew can't bear the responsibility."


Chapter 136 Joint Pressure

"How should we deliver it?" Qingquan asked, a hint of curiosity in her voice.

Qian Kun smiled slightly and whispered a few words.

Qingquan listened, her face gradually showing astonishment, finally giving her martial uncle a strange look.

"Martial Uncle, will they believe this?" she asked hesitantly.

"They will. They'll be suspicious of things delivered to their door and won't dare to take them. But if it's stolen, they'll be smug, thinking they've gotten a good deal."

Qian Kun spoke calmly, having long seen through the true colors of those people in the prefecture.

Qingquan was half-convinced, but she still got up, preparing to do as her martial uncle had just said.

Soon, a piece of news began to spread throughout Baijiang Prefecture.

"Hey, have you heard? The junior from Xuanqing Temple returned from Qingzhou, supposedly having obtained some kind of celestial treasure."

"What celestial treasure? You're so out of touch! I heard it's a divine object!"

"Really? A divine object obtained by a junior? Could it be fake news?"

"How could it be fake? Go check Xuanqing Temple yourself. The gates are all locked tight, refusing visitors and not accepting anyone."

"It's definitely true. Several Yin God Realm experts in the city wanted to visit Master Qian Kun, but they were all politely refused."

"Hiss, if that's the case, then it might really be true! Otherwise, why wouldn't they dare to see anyone if they had nothing to hide?"

"What kind of divine object is it? They're making it all so mysterious."

In the city, countless cultivators were discussing it.

From ordinary people who had just begun their cultivation to the top Yin God Realm experts in the city.

Their eyes were all focused on Xuanqing Temple.

Soon, the divination result Qian Kun had made six months prior was unearthed by those with ulterior motives.

"The world will be in chaos, divine artifacts will change hands, and a sage will be born!"

This divination, once unearthed, caused quite a stir.

It actually involved a sage!?

Moreover, Qian Kun's divination abilities were renowned throughout Baijiang Prefecture. A disciple of the Purple Moon Immortal, he was considered the pinnacle of divination in the world.

With such skill, the divination result was naturally quite accurate.

More importantly, after Qian Kun divinated this result, he immediately dispatched three of his junior disciples to Qingzhou, their whereabouts shrouded in mystery, keeping it from everyone.

And Qingquan was one of these three junior disciples.

Now that she had returned, and news had spread that she had brought back an extremely extraordinary divine artifact, it was hard not to speculate.

In the prefectural government residence.

Several powerful figures gathered there. A burly man spoke first, "Prefect of Chu, that fellow Qian Kun might really have obtained the divine artifact. If we don't act quickly, we might miss out on it!"

The burly man's tone was urgent, seemingly genuinely worried that Qian Kun had already monopolized the artifact.

"This matter is too suspicious. Qian Kun is cunning and treacherous. If he truly possessed the divine artifact, why would he allow the news to leak out? I suspect there's some kind of scheme involved!"

A scholar, however, frowned, indicating disbelief in the news.

"Old Chen Cu, your words sound nice, but if it's true, and we miss out on the divine artifact, can you bear the guilt?" The burly man glared, his face full of indignation.

Seated in the middle was the Prefect of Chu, an elderly man with white hair and beard.

Chu Wuliu stroked his beard, frowned, and said in a deep voice, "Whether this information is bait or not, we have to take it. If we succeed, we'll have profited. If not, it doesn't matter; Qian Kun doesn't dare drag us ashore."

"The Prefectural Governor is absolutely right. Qian Kun wants to fish, so let him fish! Let's see how much power he has to pull us down."

The burly man laughed heartily, showing no concern about falling for Qian Kun's scheme.

This was, after all, Baijiang Prefecture, not the capital.

Xuanqing Temple didn't have many Yin God Realm experts here to begin with. During the recent large-scale demonic attack, Xuanqing Temple lost a Yin God Realm expert, leaving only Qian Kun to single-handedly support the temple.

"Of course, it's best to be cautious. Go contact the General's Office, Jinlong Temple, and the other Yin God Realm experts, and ask them to join in."

Chu Wuliu said with a faint smile.

The scholar accepted the order, stood up, and went to deliver the message.

Inside the General's Mansion.

Several Yin Spirit Realm cultivators, after hearing the words of the Confucian scholar who had come to deliver the message, exchanged glances before looking at the bearded man seated at the head of the table.

"Chu Wuliu wants to drag us into this mess, to put pressure on Xuanqing Temple? What a clever tactic. If he were this proactive in dealing with demons, the people of Baijiang Prefecture wouldn't have to suffer."

The bearded man chuckled coldly, his tone dripping with sarcasm.

The scholar was speechless, unsure how to respond.

After a moment's thought, he decided to avoid the topic altogether and instead inquired, "I wonder if the General agrees to the Prefect's invitation?"

"Agreed? Why wouldn't I?"

Hu Zhengqiang said calmly, "Qian Kun obtained a divine artifact. Whether it's true or not, we must investigate thoroughly. Baijiang Prefecture is currently in grave danger, with two sixth-tier demons eyeing it covetously. If it truly is a divine artifact, it should be shared so that everyone can benefit. This way, we'll have more confidence in dealing with the demons!"

The General's Mansion agreed to Chu Wuliu's proposal and prepared to unite to put pressure on Qian Kun.

Meanwhile, at Jinlong Temple.

"That Taoist priest Qian Kun, he can also obtain divine objects?"

The kind-faced old monk, hearing his junior brother's report, slowly opened his eyes, his gaze filled with doubt.

"Senior brother, regardless of whether it's true or not, since it's a matter for Xuanqing Temple, our Jinlong Temple naturally has to get involved. We can't let Xuanqing Temple get away with it."

"Hmm, you're right. Then let's do as Chu Wuliu said, put pressure on Qian Kun together, and see what tricks he's up to!"

At the same time, a few other Yin God Realm cultivators from other forces also received invitations.

Unsurprisingly, they all chose to agree to Chu Wuliu's proposal.

Suddenly, Xuanqing Temple was 'on the verge of collapse'.

Soon, the group of Yin God Realm cultivators gathered. They greeted each other first, and then headed to Xuanqing Temple together.

At the entrance of Xuanqing Temple, a dozen or so Yin God Realm cultivators arrived. Chu Wuliu and his companions, leading the group, stepped forward and called out to those inside.

"Fellow Daoist Qian Kun, we have come to pay our respects at Xuanqing Temple."

Their voices were clear and resonant, carrying into the temple.

"Uncle-Master, they've arrived!" Qingquan exclaimed excitedly. Just as his uncle had predicted, once the news spread, these people would come knocking.

Qian Kun smiled faintly and stood up.

"Don't let your guard down yet. This is the most crucial step. If we're exposed, all our previous efforts will be in vain!"

Qingquan nodded emphatically, quickly composing himself.

Then Qian Kun walked out of Xuanqing Temple. Upon seeing the dozen or so Yin God Realm cultivators, his expression turned unnatural. He forced a smile and said,

"What brings you all here today? May I ask what brings you to my humble Xuanqing Temple?"


Chapter 137 This is the divine water!?

When Qian Kun emerged, everyone observed his expression.

Seeing his unnatural look and forced smile, their suspicions deepened.

"Haha, Fellow Daoist Qian Kun, you know why we're here, don't play dumb."

Chu Wuliu went straight to the point, his gaze fixed intently on Qian Kun, trying to glean something from his face.

"What reason?" Qian Kun looked bewildered.

"Heh, Qian Kun, stop pretending. Didn't you deliberately spread the news about the divine artifact? Do you really think everyone else is a fool?"

Hu Zhengqiang's expression was volatile; he directly exposed him on the spot.

Upon hearing this, Qian Kun forced a smile and said, "Fellow Daoists, even if I deliberately spread this, it seems to have nothing to do with you."

"Amitabha, Fellow Daoist Qian Kun, we are thinking of you. Who doesn't know that demons are adept at bewitching people's hearts, and sixth-tier demons are even more so? Your spreading such news without reason makes it hard not to suspect demons."

The old monk from Jinlong Temple said calmly, his tone devoid of emotion.

These words were echoed by the others.

"Yes, Fellow Daoist Qian, we're worried you've been bewitched by demons, that's why we've come to investigate."

"Quickly open the gate and let us in to investigate and see if there are any traces of demonic influence!"

Hearing the urging from the Yin Spirit Realm cultivators, Qian Kun's expression gradually darkened. He said in a deep voice:

"Gentlemen, my Xuanqing Temple is the number one Daoist sect in the world. Although I, Qian Kun, am the only one here, not just anyone can arbitrarily intrude and search. As for demons? Although I am only at the Yin Spirit Realm, my cultivation is profound, and I am not easily bewitched by demons."

"That's a misconception," Chu Wuliu said calmly:

"Fellow Daoist Qian Kun may have a strong mind and be unaffected by demons, but what about your nephew? As far as this prefecture knows, your nephew is only at the Tongfa Realm. If he is targeted by demons, he has no way to escape!"

Upon hearing this, the others immediately seized on this point.

They said that this junior, Qingquan, had brought back some kind of 'divine object,' which was likely a conspiracy by demons.

Therefore, it must be handed over for everyone to examine.

Qian Kun's face was extremely grim. Faced with the pressure from everyone, he finally had no choice but to give in.

"Fine, I'll let you in, but if you don't find the source of the demonic calamity, you'll all get out immediately!" he said angrily.

"Don't worry, Fellow Daoist Qian Kun, if we make a mistake, we will apologize to you."

Chu Wuliu said calmly, then entered Xuanqing Temple with the others.

Once inside Xuanqing Temple, their divine senses searched the temple without restraint.

The array outside had blocked their way, but now that they were inside, there were no restrictions, so they searched freely.

Soon, they arrived at the main hall, where Qingquan was.

At this moment, she looked nervous, trembling slightly in front of the group of Yin God Realm experts.

Chu Wuliu stepped forward to inquire, and Qingquan gave a somewhat hesitant answer, basically answering whatever was asked truthfully.

"Qingzhou? Mountain God? Divine Water?"

Having received these answers, the group exchanged glances, filled with skepticism.

But when they saw the divine water, their eyes widened in astonishment.

"This...this is the divine water?"

Sensing the spiritual energy and a hint of divine aura contained within the divine water, the group of Yin God Realm cultivators were deeply shocked.

"The legendary divine object circulating outside, is this the divine water?"

Chu Wuliu and the others spoke with fluctuating tones, their expressions dark and uncertain as they stared at the divine water.

Qian Kun approached, his face grim. "Everyone, you've all seen it, haven't you? Xuanqing Temple has no trace of demonic influence left behind. You may leave!"

His tone was forceful, and he stood in front of the divine water, intending to drive them away.

"No rush, Fellow Daoist Qian Kun," Hu Zhengqiang laughed, glancing at the divine water with a burning gaze, then said, "I think this divine water is very suspicious. It might be a trap set by demons."

"Hu Zhengqiang, don't push your luck! This divine water is a divine object that my nephew obtained with great difficulty. If you want to steal it, don't make up such excuses!"

Qian Kun's face darkened with rage, his aura exploding, as if he was about to attack.

The old monk stepped forward, his body radiating golden light, blocking Qian Kun's imposing aura. He then spoke, "Fellow Daoist Qian Kun, you are mistaken. General Hu's words are correct. Demons possess extraordinary abilities; who can know that this divine water isn't a trap set by them?"

"Furthermore, if it truly is a divine object, it should be shared with all of you. This way, you will all feel more confident facing the threat of demons."

"Why should the things of my Xuanqing Temple be shared with outsiders?!" Qian Kun roared.

The others ignored him, exchanging glances.

"Make your move!" Chu Wuliu said calmly.

Then, two Yin God Realm experts blocked the enraged Qian Kun, while the others stepped forward. After confirming that the divine water had no other problems, they took it away, leaving only a pitiful amount.

"Fellow Daoist Qian, we'll take this thing, suspected to be the root of demonic calamity, for your appraisal."

Leaving these words, Chu Wuliu and his group hurriedly departed.

"Uncle-Master!"

Qingquan, her eyes red-rimmed, rushed forward to support Qian Kun, who was so angry he could barely stand.

"Shameless! Despicable! These bastards are worse than pigs and dogs!"

Qian Kun cursed, clearly enraged.

Qingquan's eyes lit up with joy, about to turn her sorrow into celebration of the plan's success.

But then she saw a hint in Qian Kun's eyes, and she immediately snapped back to reality, continuing to feign pain.

Outside Xuanqing Temple, the departing group didn't leave but secretly observed from the shadows.

"Nothing unusual," a Yin Shen Realm expert said.

Hu Zhengqiang focused his gaze on the divine water, his eyes burning. "Then it seems the news was true. Qian Kun really did get a divine object from Qingzhou. It's a pity it ultimately belongs to us."

The others smiled upon hearing this.

Although they still had some doubts, the divine water was right before their eyes. They weren't blind; they could naturally see that this thing was truly valuable.

“This humble monk has examined it several times with my divine sense. This divine water is flawless, even better than other spiritual treasures of heaven and earth; it truly deserves to be called a divine object,” the old monk said, also explaining the water's effects.

One effect concerns one's state of mind, enabling enlightenment and the exorcism of inner demons. The other is the purification of spiritual energy, making it more refined.

“Hiss! Enlightenment, purification of spiritual energy? Truly a divine object!”

Upon hearing the water's effects, everyone's gazes burned with fervor.

"Alright, everyone gets a share of the divine water," Chu Wuliu said with a faint smile.

Then, the group began to divide the divine water.

The old monk, Chu Wuliu, and Hu Zhengqiang, the three strongest among them, received the largest portions.

The remaining people were also divided according to their strength.

Those with stronger cultivation received more, and those with weaker cultivation received less—it was reasonable.

As for Qian Kun, they had long since forgotten about him.

They were already being generous by leaving him a tiny bit, barely the size of a fingernail.


Chapter 138: Mind Demon

While Chu Wuliu and his group were dividing up the divine water,

on the other half of Baijiang Prefecture, a place that had once been prosperous and thriving,

had gradually become a hellish landscape after the appearance of two sixth-tier demonic creatures.

Ruins and broken walls marked the once-prosperous county towns, now reduced to ruins, stained crimson.

The rivers had dried up, and it was clear that what flowed last was not water, but blood.

Not a blade of grass grew; all life was gone. The few remaining people struggled desperately, hiding and fleeing.

But ultimately, they could not escape the clutches of the demons, slowly being found and becoming their prey.

Now, this place was a living hell!

In a relatively well-preserved county town, a bustling scene unfolded.

However, those present were not humans, but demons.

On the city walls hung seven or eight human heads, all severed Yin God Realm experts.

In the heart of the county town, demonic energy swirled, and the stench of blood was overwhelming.

The culprits, two sixth-tier demonic beings, resided here.

“What’s going on? The vanguard sent to Qingzhou—why haven’t we heard anything yet?”

A deep, cold voice rang out, sounding like a blade being sharpened.

“Commander, I don’t know either. I’ve sent people to try and contact them, but we still can’t reach them.”

Below, a demonic swordsman bowed his head and replied.

“Useless! If you can’t contact them, keep trying, or send another vanguard. Where’s your brain?!”

The commander’s tone was full of displeasure, and a murderous intent surfaced.

“Yes, sir, I’ll do it right away!” The Yin God Realm demonic swordsman, drenched in cold sweat, hurriedly replied, then turned and hastily retreated.

Fearing that the commander would kill him in a moment of displeasure.

“Sixth Blade, your temper is still as foul as ever. You’re already a sixth-tier demonic commander; you should have some dignity.” A very calm female voice rang out.

The Sword Commander looked in the direction of the voice and sneered, "What are you pretending for? Before you broke through to the sixth rank, your temper was even worse than mine. Now that you've broken through and become a Demon Commander, you've put on airs. You're quite the actor."

"I'm just reverting to my true nature," the voice replied.

"Setting your sights on Qingzhou was your suggestion!" the Sword Commander said coldly.

"That's why I'm not in a hurry. I'll let the demons below get a good grasp of the situation first, so there won't be any unexpected incidents."

"What are you afraid of? A sixth-rank being is invincible within the Great Chu territory. Why are you so timid?" The Sword Commander's tone was icy, clearly showing his dissatisfaction.

His plan was to directly lead the demon army to attack Qingzhou and seize half of its territory in one fell swoop.

That way, they would each control half of Baijiang Prefecture and Qingzhou, which would be equivalent to taking control of an entire prefecture.

When the news got back, they would have performed a great service!

"Better safe than sorry. My intuition tells me that a hasty attack on Qingzhou would be a grave mistake!"

An extremely beautiful woman emerged from the shadows. Her figure was slender and graceful, even more stunning than that of the humans; a single glance would be enough to make one lose control.

"Heh, a grave mistake? You mean the humans dare to send a Yang God Realm expert to attack us?" The Sword Commander scoffed, completely looking down on the humans.

"If you don't believe me, you can go to Qingzhou yourself and try it."

The female demon commander licked her bright red lips and said calmly.

"Hmph! You really think I wouldn't dare?" The Blade Commander sneered, his eyes filled with murderous intent.

"Since you're so cowardly, then I'll personally go to Qingzhou and storm a county city to show you!"

With that, the Blade Commander transformed into black mist and vanished.

"That fool, fine, let him scout ahead."

The Female Demon Commander murmured, then casually sat down in her chair, legs crossed, robes draped down, revealing a large expanse of fair skin.

She frowned slightly, muttering to herself, "What's going on? Why is that feeling of unease getting stronger and stronger?"

She was a Mind Demon, born from various negative emotions.

Therefore, she possessed an innate, powerful intuition, able to vaguely foresee danger.

Previously, she could sense despair, unease, panic, and other negative emotions pervading Qingzhou.

This pleased her greatly, allowing her to constantly receive these emotions and strengthen herself.

But starting half a year ago, these negative emotions had miraculously decreased.

It even began to transform, transforming into the very positive emotions she loathed: positivity, exhilaration, and hope.

These positive emotions, in her eyes, were as disgusting as excrement, utterly repulsive.

While feeling disgusted, she was also deeply puzzled as to what had happened in Qingzhou to cause this unprecedented situation.

She hadn't felt these positive emotions from humans for countless years.

This made her uneasy, preventing her from taking any rash action.

"Hopefully, it's not some unexpected turn of events," the female demon general muttered to herself.

........

Divine Mountain.

Since Kong Wang left, Qianshan Town conducted a self-examination.

All management personnel underwent investigation by the patrol team.

Anyone found guilty of embezzlement, bribery, or other violations of Qianshan Town's regulations was punished.

The patrol team went door-to-door with the list of offenders, arresting them every time.

The villagers cheered, spitting out contempt for these lawbreakers.

Of course, this was just a minor incident.

At this moment, Bai Liang, Wu Ze, and Li Xue, the three of them, ascended the sacred mountain.

The decree left to them by the Mountain God granted them, the ordained, the freedom to ascend the sacred mountain in the future.

The cave halfway up the mountain was an excellent blessed land, extremely helpful for cultivation.

Entering the cave, even though Bai Liang and Wu Ze had been there before, they were still amazed by the incredibly dense spiritual energy, almost tangible.

Li Xue, who was visiting for the first time, was speechless with astonishment.

“This must be the treasure the Mountain God spoke of?” Wu Ze saw a huge bronze furnace in the center of the cave.

The furnace was engraved with extremely intricate inscriptions, exuding a sacred aura.

On one side of the bronze furnace, four magical artifacts were casually placed.

These were the magical artifacts that Li Yue had obtained from the three people from Xuanqing Temple and the monk Sangui.

It was originally intended for Bai Liang and his companion, but a suitable opportunity hadn't arisen, so it was kept until now.

"This should be it," Bai Liang said, stepping forward.

The two studied the Divine Weapon Forging Furnace, spending a quarter of an hour figuring out its function.

It could synthesize magical artifacts!

However, the requirements were quite demanding, requiring the input of ten artifacts at once.

Even so, the Divine Weapon Forging Furnace still deeply impressed the two.

Because it has no upper limit, this means that if they have enough magical artifacts, it's not impossible to combine them into a sacred artifact!

A sacred artifact! If used properly, it's equivalent to the combat power of a saint!

However, right now they only have four magical artifacts, and they're only at the second-tier Transcendent Realm. They're still six artifacts short of combining them into a third-tier artifact.

Sacred artifacts are just a dream for them.


Chapter 139 Qiu Zhen and the Message

However, another function of the Divine Weapon Forging Furnace could now be used.

That was changing the shape of magical artifacts.

Bai Liang picked up the alms bowl that once belonged to the monk Sangui and placed it into the Divine Weapon Forging Furnace.

Then he released his spiritual power to activate the furnace.

Instantly, the furnace emitted a dazzling light, frantically absorbing the spiritual energy in the cave, filling it to the brim in the blink of an eye.

The next moment, the inscriptions on the furnace walls flickered, then gradually returned to calm.

Within the furnace, the alms bowl had transformed into a longsword, hovering silently.

Bai Liang reached out and grasped the longsword, immediately feeling the power of a second-tier magical artifact, and a look of joy appeared on his face.

Now he had a magical artifact!

Before, without a magical artifact, he could only fight bare-handed, making him far less powerful than cultivators with magical artifacts.

"This Divine Weapon Forging Furnace truly lives up to its reputation as the Mountain God's treasure!" Bai Liang exclaimed.

To reforge a completed magical artifact into another, one must be a high-level weapon-refining master.

Furthermore, this only applies to artifacts below the sixth rank; those above the sixth rank cannot be reforged.

However, the Divine Weapon Forging Furnace can reforge artifacts of any rank.

Wu Ze, seeing this, was tempted. He picked up a whisk and tossed it into the Divine Weapon Forging Furnace, activating it.

The Divine Weapon Forging Furnace shimmered, and the whisk transformed into a large sword in an instant.

Wu Ze picked it up and immediately twirled it.

Li Xue followed suit, picking up a peach wood sword and throwing it into the Divine Weapon Forging Furnace. With a faint light, it transformed into a short sword.

Now all three had their own magical artifacts, and smiles appeared on their faces.

Then, Bai Liang turned to Li Xue and said,

“Xiao Xue, you can cultivate here from now on. The spiritual energy here is abundant, and there's even a trace of divine power from the sacred mountain, which will greatly benefit your cultivation!”

Li Xue nodded emphatically, “Yes, Uncle Bai.”

Next, the three of them worked together to carve out several small cave dwellings in the mountain cave, where they could cultivate without disturbing each other.

After the cave dwellings were completed, the three of them entered to try cultivating.

Their cultivation speed astonished them. Their top-tier talent was already fast enough, but unexpectedly, cultivating here made them more than twice as fast.

Wu Ze speculated that if he continued cultivating on the sacred mountain, he could probably break through again and become a Yin God Realm expert in less than half a year.

This was something he wouldn't have dared to dream of before.

After cultivating for several hours, Bai Liang and Wu Ze chose to descend the mountain.

They still had some matters to attend to in Qianshan Town.

As for Li Xue, she had nothing to do, so she stayed on the sacred mountain to cultivate.

Before descending the mountain, Bai Liang glanced at Li Xue. The young girl would likely break through to the Profound Realm in about half a month.

It's safe to say that those who cultivate every day progress incredibly quickly.

Upon returning to Qianshan Town, someone came to report news.

"Master Bai, I've found a buyer. It's a distributor from the state capital. They were very interested after seeing our spirit rice."

Chen Jianchi, upon arriving at Shanyue Tower, didn't even have time for a sip of tea before informing Bai Liang of the news.

"Not bad, I didn't expect you to find a buyer so quickly."

Bai Liang was somewhat surprised, then asked what cultivation resources the other party wanted to exchange, and how much.

“They plan to exchange it for Crimson Spirit Grass, but they can't consume that much; they can only buy a maximum of 50,000 jin of spirit rice,” Chen Jianchi replied.

“Crimson Spirit Grass?” Bai Liang was surprised to hear this.

Crimson Spirit Grass is a special spiritual herb of Crimson Province. After consuming it, one experiences a burning sensation in the heart and it can enhance cultivation; it's a very good spiritual herb.

Of course, the most important use of Crimson Spirit Grass is in refining it into pills, Crimson Spirit Pills.

However, there are no alchemists in Thousand Mountains Town, so even if they had Crimson Spirit Grass, they could only consume it directly and couldn't refine it into pills.

“How do they plan to exchange it?” Bai Liang asked again.

Chen Jianchi paused, then said, “I talked to them several times, and the final price they offered was one Crimson Spirit Grass for twenty jin of spirit rice. They said our spirit rice isn't considered true spirit rice.”

Bai Liang nodded; this price was acceptable.

Their spirit rice was indeed inferior to genuine spirit rice, so it was normal for them to be pressured to lower the price.

That's alright, they have plenty of spirit rice anyway.

"Fifty thousand catties of spirit rice, so they're offering two thousand five hundred Crimson Spirit Grasses in exchange?" Bai Liang pondered.

Chen Jianchi nodded, then said, "They said if the spirit rice we offer is of the same quality as the one they showed us, they can give us an extra hundred Crimson Spirit Grasses."

"No need. How about this? You go and tell them we only want two thousand Crimson Spirit Grasses, but they need to find us an alchemist. They don't need to be top-notch, just someone who can refine Crimson Spirit Pills," Bai Liang said.

Chen Jianchi nodded after hearing this.

"Alright, I'll go tell them now."

Then he turned and left to negotiate with them.

After Chen Jianchi left, Xiao Nian pushed open the door and came in.

"Master, this is the situation outside these past few days." He handed a booklet to Bai Liang.

Bai Liang took the document and examined it, his expression somewhat subtle.

“The Prefect of Liaoyun, is he trying to curry favor with us?”

The booklet stated that recently, refugees from all over Liaoyun Prefecture had been arriving in Qianshan Town.

It turned out that Prefect Ximen Longyu of Liaoyun Prefecture was involved in this, even spreading the fame of the Divine Mountain within Liaoyun Prefecture.

Bai Liang was somewhat puzzled, but after seeing that Ximen Longyu was a member of the General's Office, he understood.

He was from the General's Office when Lin Zhenjiang's physical body was shattered by the Mountain God.

He probably informed the General upon returning, and the General, knowing the Mountain God's power, was thus showing goodwill.

“This means he intends to cede half of Liaoyun Prefecture, or even the entire Liaoyun Prefecture to us,” Bai Liang remarked with some emotion.

Previously, he had been worried that Qianshan Town's expansion was too rapid, and the imperial court might struggle to handle it.

Now, it seemed to have been resolved directly.

As long as it's within Liaoyun County, they can do whatever they want with Qianshan Town.

"Master, take another look," Xiao Nian suddenly said.

Bai Liang listened and continued reading.

After finishing, his brows furrowed slightly.

"Qiu Zhenhe, the people from the prefecture?" he murmured.

Qiu Zhenhe had sent a message to Qianshan Town, 'kindly' reminding them that the General's Office planned to push Qianshan Town ahead of Qingzhou as a shield against the demon army of Baijiang Prefecture.

Qiu Zhenhe also mentioned the General's Mansion's sinister intentions; they feigned goodwill but secretly planned to use them as vanguards against the demon army.

"Yes, Master, he not only brought these words but also expressed his willingness to help us fight against the demons and the General's Mansion," Xiao Nian replied.

Bai Liang put the booklet aside and said, "Don't worry about it. Neither the General nor the Prefectural Government is relevant to us. Qianshan Town just needs to focus on its own affairs!"


Chapter 140 The Flaw in Heaven and Earth

Bai Liang could naturally see that Qiu Zhenhe was trying to sow discord.

Regardless of the motives of the prefectural government or the general, Qianshan Town didn't need to concern itself with them.

"Should we respond?" Xiao Nian hesitated.

Bai Liang thought for a moment and said, "Sure. Since they want to curry favor with Qianshan Town, let's ask them to send people to help us deal with the demons."

Although Qianshan Town wasn't afraid of the demons from Baijiang Prefecture, if they were to stand in front and block the demons for the court, they could reap the benefits without lifting a finger.

That was somewhat unsettling.

Therefore, Bai Liang decided to have the court send people. Otherwise, if the demons entered Qingzhou, Qianshan Town wouldn't care; they would simply close their doors and take care of themselves.

Xiao Nian nodded and arranged for someone to reply to Qiu Zhenhe and the others.

Next, they reported on the current situation in Qianshan Town.

Because of Ximen Longyu's instigation, more and more people were coming to Qianshan Town.

The population has reached 50,000 and is still growing at a frenzied rate.

Xiao Nian chuckled, "This town isn't settled yet; it might be renamed a county town soon."

A larger population brings many benefits.

These newcomers, guided by the management of Qianshan Town, have all gone to the outskirts to establish their own residences.

Therefore, Qianshan Town is expanding very quickly; Bai Liang doesn't need to supervise much, they've built it themselves.

"Excellent, this is the effect we wanted." Bai Liang was very satisfied.

As the scale grows larger, the corresponding mountain god temple cannot be neglected.

This is of paramount importance!

Xiao Nian and the others understand this, so they are in charge of arranging and supervising the construction of the mountain god temple.

With so many believers, the increase in merit points is naturally substantial.

However, Li Yue is immersed in his enlightenment and doesn't notice the changes on the panel.

At this moment, his divine sense is completely absorbed, already integrated with the laws of heaven and earth.

The laws of heaven and earth were flawless, without a single loophole, utterly closed off.

This reminded Li Yue of the Qing Dynasty's isolationist policies in his previous life.

Applied to this world, it would be like closing off the entire realm.

But why use the laws of heaven and earth to lock the entire world away, cutting off all contact with the outside world?

He couldn't understand, and he didn't know whether this was done by people outside the world or by people within it.

Moreover, most importantly, he sensed from the laws of heaven and earth that this world did not allow the emergence of Saints.

This restriction not only restricted humans but also demons.

But now, the demons had ninth-tier Saints; this made no sense.

Could demons disregard the laws of heaven and earth and defy the heavens to become Saints?

If they could do that, then the demons possessed even more terrifying power!

Li Yue was filled with doubt until he saw a very hidden flaw in the laws of heaven and earth.

"Hmm? This is..."

His divine sense fluctuated, revealing a hidden opening, like a rift torn open in the sky, incredibly deep and dark.

A sealed world had actually developed an opening!

Li Yue was incredulous. His divine sense tried to contact the opening, wanting to explore the outside world, but was blocked by terrifying laws of heaven and earth.

"The laws of heaven and earth are repairing the cave entrance?"

He saw a faint white light flickering at the edge of the cave entrance, then it was being repaired at an extremely slow pace.

The original cave entrance must have been quite large; it had taken a very long time for the laws of heaven and earth to repair it to its current state.

Li Yue found it hard to imagine what kind of being could break through the boundary forged by the laws of heaven and earth.

Based on his current fourth-tier Holy Mountain strength, he estimated it was completely impossible.

Even if he advanced one or two more tiers, it would still be difficult to achieve this.

This was the law of heaven and earth, equivalent to contending with the Heavenly Dao itself. Perhaps only at the seventh tier or higher would he have a chance to try.

Continuing to examine the repair marks, Li Yue roughly calculated the original size of the cave entrance.

At least ten zhang wide!

Now it was less than two zhang wide. The repair by the laws of heaven and earth was extremely slow; at this rate, it would take about two years to repair one zhang.

It had already repaired more than eight zhang, meaning it had taken eight or nine years to repair.

The cave entrance appeared eight or nine years ago. What happened eight or nine years ago?

Wasn't that the year the demons began their rampage?

Li Yue immediately remembered.

Nine years ago, a blood-red meteorite fell from the sky, declared by the Imperial Observatory as the year of calamity.

That same year, both the demon and monster factions produced a Saint-level expert, reversing the roles of attacker and defender, with the Great Chu human race becoming the victim.

Thus, disaster struck.

"The blood-red meteorite... could it be that meteorite that created this cave?"

Li Yue suddenly understood; everything made sense.

No wonder the demons could produce a ninth-tier Saint-level expert; it was all because of this blood-red meteorite.

But was this just a coincidence, or was there some force behind it?

Li Yue felt there was more to it than met the eye. The blood-red meteorite landed somewhere unknown, probably outside the Great Chu borders, in the demons' stronghold.

If he could examine the blood-red meteorite, perhaps he could find out something.

But since he couldn't see it now, he could only use his divine sense to sense the area near the cave entrance in the sky, trying to find clues about it.

...

Kong Wang had been away from Qianshan Town for ten days.

During these ten days, he had no direction, traveling aimlessly, drinking dew when thirsty, eating wild vegetables and fruits when hungry, and sometimes even catching and eating prey like rabbits when he encountered them.

In his mind, this was the law of nature, and he had no taboos.

At this moment, Kong Wang was sitting cross-legged in a mountain forest.

Since receiving the blessing of the divine talisman, his cultivation had reached the peak of the Profound Realm, only one step away from breaking through.

Now he was in the process of breaking through.

Like Wu Ze, he condensed his primordial spirit, and then his primordial spirit was destroyed by a strange phenomenon.

This process lasted for more than an hour, experiencing more than twenty primordial spirit collapses.

Then, on the twenty-seventh attempt to condense his primordial spirit, he finally succeeded.

He broke through to the fourth-tier Enlightenment Realm cultivator in one fell swoop!

Kong Wang opened his eyes, his joy barely concealed.

This breakthrough made his journey much more secure.

After a short rest and experiencing the new realm, he didn't linger and continued on his way.

His destination was Qingzhou City.

The city remained incredibly prosperous.

Peddlers, laborers, drinkers, and scholars—it was a scene of bustling prosperity.
