A distinguished guest had arrived at the prefectural government residence today.

Qiu He, the highest-ranking official in Qingzhou, could only stand, like a junior.

The same was true for the monk Sanwu.

"This junior greets Second Master Ancestor. I was unaware of your personal arrival and failed to greet you properly. Please forgive me," Sanwu said, head bowed, his tone filled with trepidation.

He knew his sect would send experts to Qingzhou, but he hadn't expected his master to come in person.

What secret manual had his junior brother Sangui acquired?


Chapter 141 Gathering of Major Forces

The young novice monk remained calm, glancing at Sanwu.

“Enough with the useless talk, quickly tell me the whereabouts of the manual,” he said indifferently.

Sanwu hesitated, stammering, “Master…Grandmaster, I haven’t been able to ascertain the whereabouts of the manual. I only know that Junior Brother Sangui died in Peach Blossom County, Liaoyun Prefecture. As for the manual itself, its whereabouts remain unknown.”

He was filled with remorse. If he had known the manual was so important, that his Grandmaster would have risked leaving the capital in his reincarnation to come personally,

he should have gone to Liaoyun Prefecture much earlier to retrieve the manual, or at least found out its location first.

Now, having done nothing and knowing nothing, he was extremely flustered, fearing his Grandmaster’s wrath.

“Hmm?”

The young novice monk’s expression darkened slightly, his gaze towards Sanwu filled with intense displeasure.

Seeing this, Sanzhi quickly stepped forward to speak for his junior brother.

"Grandmaster, what Junior Brother Sanwu did isn't necessarily a bad thing. The secret manual is of utmost importance; if we alert the enemy and cause unnecessary trouble, it would be a greater loss than gain."

The young novice didn't speak, but nodded slightly.

Seeing this, Sanwu breathed a sigh of relief and gave his senior brother a grateful look.

"Lord Qiu, Qingzhou is your territory. Immediately order Liaoyun County to gather all the information related to the secret manual!" Sanzhi said to Qiu He.

Qiu He's expression stiffened, and he smiled wryly, "Master Sanzhi, you overestimate me. Even if Liaoyun County weren't under my control, and even if it were my territory, I wouldn't dare interfere."

"What do you mean?" Sanzhi frowned.

As the prefecture, not daring to interfere in the counties under your jurisdiction? What kind of talk is that?

Qiu He glanced cautiously at the young novice monk before speaking, "Currently, I'm in a standoff with the General in Qingzhou. Liaoyun County was originally the General's territory, under his control."

"Then let the General come here. With our Grandmaster here, does he dare not cooperate?" Sanzhi sneered.

He thought it was something else, but it turns out it's a struggle between the prefecture and the General.

These things only dared to happen within Qingzhou. Now that our Grandmaster is here, with an eighth-tier Buddha present, who would dare to engage in such petty actions? Would they be courting death?

"Even if he comes, it's useless. I'm saying that what was originally the General's territory has now changed hands," Qiu He said.

"Changed hands?" Sanzhi was taken aback, not understanding what he meant.

"Could it be that it's been conquered by demons?"

They knew about the Baijiang Prefecture incident.

If it really was conquered by demons, then this would be troublesome; those were two sixth-tier demons.

Unless his master personally intervenes, he and Sanwu alone are no match for him.

"No," Qiu He shook his head.

Then he recounted everything about Qianshan Town, including the sacred mountain behind it.

The monk Sangui died at the hands of the people of Qianshan Town. If he carried any secret manuals, they were very likely within Qianshan Town!

Upon hearing this, Sanzhi's face darkened.

His first thought was disbelief!

This Qiu He was actually trying to fool him with deities? Did he really think he was a foolish commoner?

The little novice opened his eyes and looked at Qiu He.

"You said there's a sacred mountain in Liaoyun County, and a deity there?" he said slowly.

"Yes, senior! If I have uttered a single lie, may I be struck by lightning!" Qiu He bowed his head and swore.

The young novice then looked at Sanwu, who quickly said, "Grandmaster, Qiu He is telling the truth. However, this disciple believes that it is not a deity, but rather a rogue cultivator collecting offerings."

"What kind of rogue cultivator can collect offerings for so long without suffering a backlash?" Sanzhi frowned and said in a deep voice.

He then turned to the young novice and bowed his head, saying, "Grandmaster, this disciple is willing to go to Liaoyun County to retrieve the secret manual!"

The young novice shook his head: "This matter is suspicious. You are only at the peak of the Yin God Realm; going there will only be suicide."

Sanzhi wanted to refute something, but stopped himself.

According to what Qiu He had just said, that so-called mountain god could take the life of a Yin God Realm cultivator from a thousand meters away. With that kind of strength, going there would indeed be suicide.

"Then what should we do? Grandmaster, will you go personally?" Sanzhi asked cautiously.

"No rush, someone's scouting the way for us," the young novice said calmly.

When they left the capital, he sensed several forces secretly following them.

As an eighth-level Buddha, even in his reincarnated form, how could he not sense these things?

He simply chose not to bother.

Now that they'd encountered this unexpected situation, he'd use these people who had followed them to scout the way and see what lay behind that sacred mountain.

If it weren't for the many restrictions imposed on him as a reincarnated being, he wouldn't have gone through all this trouble.

Meanwhile.

Many unfamiliar faces arrived in Qingzhou City.

The Dragon-Slaying Sect's group entered the city and found a place to hide.

"Holy Maiden, did those two bald monks come to Qingzhou because of the Baijiang Prefecture matter?" a man in black asked.

The saintess frowned and shook her head. "Not necessarily. If it's because of what happened in Baijiang Prefecture, why not go directly to Baijiang Prefecture?"

"Because the demons want to attack Qingzhou?" the man in black guessed.

"Not necessarily. The bald monk from Jinlong Temple isn't that kind-hearted. He's most likely doing it for his own private matters. Let's observe secretly first."

Meanwhile, in an alley near the General's Mansion.

The old Taoist priest uncapped his wine jug and took a large, hearty gulp.

"Refreshing!" Savoring the wine's aroma, he finally remembered his purpose.

"What could Qingzhou possibly have? It can't be here to contend with the two sixth-tier demons from Baijiang Prefecture, can it?" the old Taoist muttered to himself.

He thought Jinlong Temple wasn't exactly acting out of noble principles, but he couldn't figure out the reason.

That young novice monk was too unfamiliar; he'd never seen him in the capital before.

Moreover, the aura emanating from him made him feel threatened—terrifying!

"What could it be? Could it be a Buddhist disciple trained by the Golden Dragon Temple?" The old Taoist frowned.

Unable to figure it out, he could only continue to observe.

He turned and headed towards the General's Mansion. He had some connections with Xiang Tiandao, so he could stay there for a while.

Meanwhile, outside Qingzhou City.

Three people wearing bird feathers met in a grove of trees.

"Wu Cheng, what brings you here?" Hua Wuzei asked, somewhat surprised to see him.

"I came to track someone down," Wu Cheng explained succinctly, stating his reason for coming.

Hua Wuzei and Yang Youshui exchanged bewildered glances.

Golden Dragon Temple, Dragon-Slaying Temple, Xuanqing Temple, plus their own Heavenly Patrol Bureau.

This was practically a hodgepodge of various powerful forces.

What major event has occurred in Qingzhou that has drawn so many forces here?

Hua Wuzei immediately thought of the sacred mountain where his brother-in-law resided.

A deity exists there; if the world knew of it, it would indeed attract various powerful forces.

But the problem is, not many people know of the mountain god's existence, and even if they did, most wouldn't believe it.

If that's not the reason, then what is it?

Wu Cheng then revealed the reason, leaving Hua Wuzei and his companion speechless.

The reason was that the Golden Dragon Temple had come to Qingzhou, followed by the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Temple, then the old Taoist priest of the Xuanqing Temple, and finally Wu Cheng.

So, it's a case of the mantis stalking the cicada, unaware of the oriole behind, and then there's the eagle, and the dragon, right?


Chapter 142 Empty Expectations Enter the City

"Wait a minute, Wu Cheng, you followed them here, so isn't it possible that someone else was following you too?"

Hua Wuzei seemed to have thought of something, and his expression suddenly changed slightly.

Upon hearing this, Wu Cheng's expression became unnatural.

"No...no way, I was very careful the whole way and didn't notice anyone following me," he said uncertainly.

"You're cautious? Aren't Xuanqing Temple, Jinlong Temple, and Falong Sect cautious too?" Hua Wuzei's tone was heavy.

Wu Cheng completely lost confidence and looked around, his expression extremely wary.

Suddenly I felt like there was something wrong with everything around me.

"How about we talk somewhere else?" Wu Cheng suggested hesitantly.

"Change it, better safe than sorry."

Hua Wuzei said in a serious tone.

After several transfers, the three entered Qingzhou City and found a tavern to stay in.

They breathed a sigh of relief after confirming that no one was following them.

"People these days are too cunning; they resort to stalking all the time," Wu Cheng said with lingering fear.

Hua Wuzei glanced at him, thinking to himself, "Aren't you talking about yourself?"

"Alright, we don't care what Jinlong Temple and Xuanqing Temple are up to. Our mission is to capture the demoness of the Dragon-Slaying Sect!"

Hua Wuzei spoke in a serious tone.

"The demoness is currently in the state capital. We'll contact the Yin God Realm experts in Qingzhou City and capture her immediately!" Yang Youshui said.

"No, the demoness's methods are unpredictable. We must set up a sky-surveying formation to guarantee that we can capture her one hundred percent."

Wu Cheng said with a solemn expression.

The Sky Patrol Bureau had dealt with the Dragon Slayer Cult countless times before.

This demoness is elusive and unpredictable, employing a variety of methods to hide and escape, making her quite troublesome.

The fact that she was revered as a saint and that the Dragon Slayer Sect dared to allow her to move freely within the territory of the Great Chu indicates that she was very powerful.

"We can act in two directions. First, contact the Yin God Realm here, and then simultaneously set up the Sky Patrol Array."

Hua Wuzei pondered for a moment.

Ultimately, Wu Cheng agreed to Hua Wuzei's proposal and began his attempt to contact a Yin Shen Realm expert.

Outside the city gate of Qingzhou, a handsome and clean-looking monk arrived.

The soldiers guarding the city glanced at the monk a couple of times, then let him in.

"This monk is much better looking than the two who entered the city yesterday," a soldier guarding the city muttered.

Yesterday, there were two monks, one old and one young. The older one looked kind and gentle, but his aura was somewhat sinister.

The young one, however, looked quite eerie, making people afraid to approach him.

Kong Wang remained calm, and the murmurs of the soldiers guarding the city did not escape his ears.

He was a little curious about why two monks had come to Qingzhou City, but he didn't think much of it.

As he entered Qingzhou City, he began to stroll around, admiring the bustling scene that Liaoyun County lacked.

Kong Wang, who had never seen such a sight before, was slightly surprised and slowed his pace to watch quietly.

At the same time, his appearance also attracted the attention of many people.

"A monk at the fourth level of the Divine Enlightenment Realm has entered the city. What's going on? Have people from the Golden Dragon Temple come again?"

In a courtyard in the city, this is the stronghold of the Dragon-Slaying Cult in Qingzhou City.

The saintess frowned slightly as she listened to her subordinate's report.

Most coincidentally, the monk was heading in their direction, as if he knew where they were hiding.

“Your Highness, we may have been exposed. We must move to a different location immediately.” A man in black said gravely.

The monks who came towards them were probably the vanguard sent by Jinlong Temple.

"No, if they were really discovered, they certainly wouldn't alert them, much less send a fourth-level Enlightenment Realm cultivator to test them. Wouldn't that be suicide?" the Holy Maiden speculated.

"It's better to be safe than sorry, Your Highness. Let's retreat first," said the man in black.

"Protector Chen, when did you become so cowardly? Even if we are discovered, as fellow Yin Gods, do you think our Holy Church fears the imperial court's lackeys?"

The saintess's tone was tinged with emotion, clearly showing some dissatisfaction.

This was her first time leaving the Dragon-Slaying Sect and entering the territory of the Great Chu to carry out a mission.

There are many rumors within the Dragon Slayer Cult that she, the Holy Maiden, is just a pretty face, chosen only because of her extreme beauty and allure.

Therefore, she urgently needs to achieve something to prove her strength and shatter all these doubts.

Chen Hu was speechless, unsure how to respond.

The main reason is that the Holy Maiden holds the highest status in the Dragon Slayer Cult, aside from the leader and vice-leader.

He was merely a protector, and there was a Heavenly King above him, so he had absolutely no say in the matter of the Holy Maiden.

"Alright, do as I say. Stay here and see what this bald monk is up to," the saintess said coldly.

Her tone was very cold, and no one knew what the saintess's expression was beneath the black veil.

Chen Hufa and the others had no choice but to obey the Holy Maiden's orders.

About an incense stick's time later, there was a gentle knock on the courtyard door.

"Is anyone there?" A clear voice rang out, and the members of the Dragon Slayer Sect looked at each other in surprise.

"This bald monk really came for us!"

Protector Chen's expression was grave; he was ready to fight with all his might.

Go open the door.

The saintess spoke calmly. Protector Chen wanted to remind her of something, but in the end he obeyed her and went to open the door.

The door opened, and a young monk appeared before the crowd.

“Amitabha, this humble monk Kongwang greets all benefactors.”

Kong Wang chanted a Buddhist prayer and bowed to the crowd.

However, when his gaze fell on the woman wearing a cloak and veil, his eyes gleamed with golden light and lingered for a moment.

"The monks of Jinlong Temple!?"

Protector Chen's expression was very unpleasant, and he sneered.

Kong Wang shook his head and said, "This benefactor has misunderstood. This humble monk comes from the sacred mountain."

"Sacred Mountain? Is there a Buddhist sect called Sacred Mountain Temple?"

Protector Chen frowned and began searching his memory for information about the sacred mountain.

In the end, he couldn't remember anything.

"It's just a sacred mountain, not a Buddhist temple," Kongwang explained.

These words made the members of the Dragon Slayer Sect frown.

If you're not from a Buddhist sect, then how did you become a monk?

What kind of thing is this sacred mountain?

"Monk Kongwang, what brings you here?"

At this moment, the saintess's voice rang out, her tone cold and aloof.

Kong Wang looked up at the woman whose face was covered by a black veil and said with a faint smile, "This humble monk sensed that someone here was confused, so I came."

"Confused?" The saintess was slightly taken aback.

Before she could say anything, Protector Chen sneered, "Bullshit! Who here is confused? You monk, get out of here! If you bother us again, believe me, I'll beat you up!"

"In that case, I shall take my leave. If any of you benefactors are confused, you may come to me for guidance." With these words, Kong Wang turned and departed.

"How inexplicable, a crazy monk."

As Chen Hufa watched Kong Wang's departing figure, he couldn't help but curse.


Chapter 143 The Aftereffects Arrive

They showed up at my door out of the blue, claiming they were looking for someone who was feeling lost.

General's Mansion.

He even lived long enough to send his son, Xiang Tiandao, to Qingzhou as a general.

Xiang Tiandao wasn't angry at all. The two had been friends since their youth, and their friendship remained strong after all these years.

"I have bad news for you. Two of your disciples died in Qingzhou," Xiang Tiandao suddenly said.

Qian Qu's hand holding the teacup paused for a moment, but he quickly recovered.

Why would his disciple travel thousands of miles to such a remote place?

Baizhou City.

The news that the Xuanqing Temple disciple had returned with the divine water had spread, causing exclamations of surprise among the city's residents and envy among the cultivators.

However, the divine water was ultimately taken by the prefectural governor and a group of Yin God Realm experts, leaving only a small amount for Xuanqing Temple.

This made everyone feel quite relieved.

Seeing you recover made everyone feel bad.

Seeing you suffer made everyone feel better.

This is a normal human psychological reaction.

Those Yin God Realm experts who received the divine water a few days ago have already consumed it.

The effects of the divine water were unexpectedly astonishing. It brought clarity to their true nature, directly dispelling all the confusion and inner demons accumulated over years of cultivation, and their cultivation levels actually increased significantly.

At the same time, their spiritual energy was purified, becoming even purer than before.

This astonishing effect caused everyone to exclaim in their hearts, "Truly worthy of being a divine object!"

It was just a pity that although there was a lot of divine water, when divided equally among everyone, each person only received a small amount.

Chu Wuliu, Hu Zhengqiang, and the old monk from Jinlong Temple received the most, three times the amount of the others.

In the prefectural residence.

Chu Wuliu had been consuming the divine water for two consecutive days, and the effects it brought were quite remarkable.

His original cultivation level was already at the peak of the Yin God Realm; now, after consuming the divine water, he was infinitely close to the peak of the Great Perfection Realm.

He could break through to the Yang God Realm at any moment.

This astonishing effect left him utterly shocked.

At the same time, a strange emotion rose within him.

"This divine water is so powerful, then the deity who created it must be even more powerful," Chu Wuliu thought to himself.

He had heard from the clear spring at Xuanqing Temple that the divine water came from a sacred mountain, where a deity called the Mountain God resided.

Chu Wuliu suddenly felt immense reverence for the Mountain God who created the divine water.

"If I have the chance, I must personally visit and witness the magnificence of the sacred mountain."

Thinking this, he took out another bottle of divine water and drank it all in one gulp.

In the General's Mansion.

After drinking the divine water, Hu Zhengqiang hadn't closed his eyes for two days.

He frequently praised the power of the divine water, truly deserving of its divine status.

Today, he not only praised the divine water but also the sacred mountain.

"Once the divine water is used up, this general will definitely go to Qingzhou personally to ask the Mountain God for more divine water to aid my cultivation!" Hu Zhengqiang muttered to himself.

He then drank the remaining divine water in one gulp.

The same scene was unfolding in Jinlong Temple.

The monks who used to chant sutras and recite Buddhist prayers were no longer bowing to the Buddha.

Instead, they stayed in the back hall, purging themselves on the divine water.

“Brother, this divine water is too powerful! I truly don’t know how that mountain god created such a thing,” one monk exclaimed.

The old monk lowered his head slightly and said, “The mountain god is a deity of heaven and earth, sacred and extraordinary, comparable to the Buddha. Naturally, it cannot be compared to cultivators.”

The junior monk nodded in complete agreement.

At this moment, in their hearts, the Mountain God held the same status as Buddha.

Meanwhile, the other Yin Gods who drank the divine water all developed a deep reverence for the Mountain God.

They praised the divine water highly and held the Mountain God, who created it, in the highest esteem.

Qian Kun, who had been observing from the shadows, witnessed all of this.

The changes in the crowd astonished him.

In just a few days, these people, who had previously disbelieved in deities, had become 'believers' who revered the Mountain God.

This was terrifying!


Is Chapter 144 confirmed to be a legitimate deity?

Qian Kun returned to Xuanqing Temple, his heart pounding.

His expression was constantly shifting, his thoughts unreadable.

Qingquan, seeing her uncle return, noticed his unusual expression and asked, "Uncle, what's wrong? Could it be that the prefectural officials have discovered something wrong with the divine water?"

Thinking of this, her heart leaped with worry.

If they were discovered, Xuanqing Temple would face the wrath of over a dozen Yin God Realm experts!

"They haven't discovered anything," Qian Kun waved his hand dismissively.

Hearing this, Qingquan breathed a sigh of relief, then asked疑惑地, "If they haven't discovered anything, then why do you look so troubled, Uncle?"

"Because of the divine water," Qian Kun said, then turned to Qingquan with a serious expression and said earnestly, "Qingquan, tell your uncle, are you sure that mountain god in Qingzhou is a legitimate deity?"

Qingquan was taken aback: "Wh...what is a legitimate deity? What exactly do you want to ask, Uncle?"

She was somewhat confused.

So Qian Kun recounted everything he had just witnessed.

Upon hearing that the monks of Jinlong Temple had stopped worshipping Buddha and started revering the mountain god, Qingquan's eyes nearly popped out of their sockets.

These bald monks, who usually claimed to be the most steadfast believers and spent their days brainwashing and spreading Buddhism, had actually stopped believing in their own Buddha?

Qingquan initially thought her uncle was joking, but when she looked up and met Qian Kun's incredibly serious expression, she knew it wasn't a joke.

“It should be a legitimate deity, right? That mountain god protects the people, vanquishes demons, and his followers are all virtuous. He seems like a righteous god no matter how you look at it,” Qingquan said hesitantly.

Qian Kun, however, said, “If it’s a legitimate deity, would they have such an evil divine water? Drinking this stuff is comparable to being brainwashed by a dozen old monks chanting scriptures for ten days, or even worse.”

Speaking of this, he felt somewhat relieved.

Luckily, he hadn’t drunk the divine water carelessly, otherwise, his fate would have been the same as Chu Wuliu and the others.

“Then what should we do, Uncle-Master? They all took the divine water, doesn’t that mean they’ve all been ‘brainwashed’?”

Qingquan looked incredulous. Such a drastic change in just a few days was too outrageous.

At this moment, she completely understood why the mountain god was willing to help her restore her foundation.

So that was their plan all along! They subdued over a dozen Yin God Realm experts without lifting a finger!

"There's no need to worry too much. They didn't obtain much divine water, and the effects they've suffered so far are only minor. They're probably still in the stage of worshipping the mountain god, so it won't cause any serious consequences."

"Of course," Qian Kun said slowly.

"If there were enough divine water, and they were made to keep kowtowing, it would be hard to explain what the consequences would be."

“Besides, this effect might fade over time. Even a Yin Spirit Realm expert isn't so easily brainwashed,” Qian Kun reassured himself.

“But…” Qingquan suddenly looked uneasy, her tone hesitant.

“But what?” Qian Kun looked at her, puzzled.

Qingquan pursed her lips and said, “I promised Bai Liang in Qianshan Town that after delivering the divine water, I have to go back to get more and deliver it again, at least three times.”

"What!?" Qian Kun's expression suddenly changed.

Help deliver the divine water three times?

This was serious! Three times? Wouldn't that really brainwash Chu Wuliu and the others?

"You're foolish! How could you agree to this so easily?" Qian Kun sighed.

Qingquan lowered his head, not daring to face his martial uncle.

Qian Kun sighed for a while, then his expression quickly returned to normal. He said, "Forget it, it has nothing to do with our Xuanqing Temple anyway. Better to die for a friend than for myself. Delivering it a few more times won't hurt."

He had completely let go of the past. Whatever the mountain god wanted to do, whatever his conspiracy, it was all irrelevant to him.

As long as he could remain unscathed, that was enough.

It was others who were being brainwashed, not his family and friends. What did it matter?

He considered reporting this to the Xuanqing Temple in the capital, but then thought it best to avoid trouble.

She was already isolated and ostracized, so why bother telling her sect?

She should just live her own life.

"Uncle-Master, you agreed?" Qingquan asked tentatively.

She really hadn't expected her Uncle-Master to accept so quickly.

Qian Kun nodded, saying calmly, "When Chu Wuliu and the others run out of the divine water and come knocking, return to Qingzhou to retrieve it. Remember, when they come, act extremely unwilling; you have to get them some benefit from you."

"Yes, Uncle-Master!" Qingquan nodded emphatically.

Qingzhou City.

After leaving the Dragon-Slaying Sect's stronghold, Kong Wang found an inn to stay in alone.

It was already night. Qingzhou City had no curfew, and the city was brightly lit; even in the middle of the night, people were still strolling through the night market.

The most bustling places were the taverns and brothels, especially the brothels, which were always full of customers.

Kong Wang couldn't stand the decadent music, so he stayed in inns located in secluded and quiet areas.

He wasn't asleep at the moment, but rather sitting cross-legged on the bed, cultivating.

After leaving Thousand Mountain Town, he could clearly feel that his cultivation speed had slowed down considerably.

After all, the outside world didn't have the incredibly rich spiritual energy of the Divine Mountain.

However, although his cultivation speed had slowed down, his insights along the way were profound, and he had gained a deeper understanding of the mysteries of Buddhism.

Realms above the sixth rank weren't simply about absorbing the spiritual energy of heaven and earth to improve cultivation; they relied more on comprehension.

Therefore, comprehension determined whether you could break through to a higher realm.

"Clang, clang, clang!"

Suddenly, the sound of knocking on wooden planks came from the roof.

Kong Wang opened his eyes, looked up, and then spoke, "Since you have come, why do you not show yourself?"

Silence followed; no one answered.

After a long while, a dark figure suddenly appeared in the room—it was the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect, wearing a veil and cloak, her figure graceful.


Chapter 145 Half-human, Half-demon

"Speak," the Holy Maiden asked coldly, her tone indifferent.

Kong Wang calmly replied, "Because this humble monk can see it."

"How can you see it?" the Holy Maiden pressed.

"With this humble monk's eyes."

Kong Wang's eyes flashed with golden light, his pupils turning golden in an instant.

At the same time, in his eyes, the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect before him was surrounded by a violent demonic aura, yet mixed with the pure spiritual energy of a human cultivator.

This scene was quite bizarre.

"Hmm? You monk, what can you see?" the Holy Maiden asked suddenly with interest.

"This humble monk can tell at a glance that you are not human!"

Kong Wang said calmly.

Upon hearing this, the Holy Maiden's aura fluctuated violently, her eyes beneath her veil fixed intently on Kong Wang.

"I'm not human? Do you think I'm a demon?"

Kong Wang shook his head and said, "Not a demon either, more like half-human, half-demon."

Upon receiving the answer, the Holy Maiden remained silent for a long time, then scrutinized Kong Wang from head to toe, her tone low and somber, "That's right, you're correct. This Holy Maiden is indeed half-human, half-demon. But I'm curious, how can you, a mere Enlightened God Realm cultivator, not only see through my origins but also remain unfazed before me? What's your secret?"

She was a peak Yin God Realm cultivator, the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect, possessing top-tier strength, and also cultivated demonic arts.

Without the Yang God Realm, she was invincible.

"My secret is given to me by the Mountain God," Kong Wang said calmly.

The Holy Maiden frowned slightly. She had just heard Kong Wang mention the Divine Mountain, and now there was a Mountain God.

This monk's sect name is too unusual!

"That's understandable, though. Their Dragon-Slaying Sect leader even calls himself the Holy Lord."

"So, you, a mere Enlightenment Realm cultivator, have the confidence to face a Yin God Realm cultivator?"

The Holy Maiden slowly stepped forward, her aura gathering. She prepared to test the waters of this monk before her, to see if he was truly capable.

"Of course," Kong Wang replied.

Seeing the woman about to attack, he quickly assessed the situation. At the Enlightenment Realm, he couldn't possibly fight her head-on.

However, using the power of the Divine Concealment Talisman to ensure his invincibility was quite easy.

The next moment, Kong Wang activated the Divine Concealment Talisman, and he vanished from his spot.

At the same time, all his aura dissipated, completely disappearing, as if he had never been there at all.

The Holy Maiden, who was about to attack Kong Wang, paled upon seeing this.

Her divine sense frantically expanded, searching every corner, but she couldn't find Kong Wang's figure, not even a trace of his aura.

The saintess, refusing to believe it, drew a soft sword and cleaved through the room, leaving no space untouched.

But ultimately, it had no effect; she still couldn't find Kong Wang.

"What kind of magic is this?" she wondered, somewhat shocked.

A fourth-tier Enlightenment Realm cultivator, able to evade the divine sense detection of a Yin God Realm cultivator, and so perfectly at that—this was utterly unexpected.

She had used all her trump cards.

The Nine-Tailed Fox's innate ability, the Realm of Illusion, allowed her to observe the real world by entering an illusion, thus bypassing various concealment spells and directly accessing reality.

But even using the Realm of Illusion, she still couldn't find Kong Wang's figure.

After trying for a long time, the Holy Maiden finally gave up.

This monk's concealment technique was simply unparalleled, impossible to break.

"Alright, come out. I've already witnessed your strength, monk," she said, gesturing to her surroundings.

As soon as she finished speaking, Kong Wang's figure reappeared in the spot where he had just vanished, even his movements were exactly the same as when he disappeared.

It was as if he had been standing there the entire time.

Seeing this, the Holy Maiden frowned, beginning to take the monk before her more seriously.

"Is there anything else you wish to ask, benefactor?" Kong Wang said slowly.

"Yes!" the Holy Maiden said, "Since you can see my confusion, then tell me what's wrong with me, and why?"

Kong Wang nodded, then said, "Because you are half-human, half-demon, that's why I'm confused."

"Just because of that?" the Holy Maiden couldn't help but scoff.

“Yes,” Kong Wang continued, “This humble monk wishes to ask you, benefactor, which side do you favor: humans or demons?”

“Is that even a question? I am the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect, naturally my heart is with the humans.” The Holy Maiden’s tone left no room for argument.

“The Dragon-Slaying Sect cannot represent the human race,” Kong Wang said calmly.

“Then do you think the imperial court, the Golden Dragon Temple, and the Xuanqing Temple can represent the human race?” The Holy Maiden sneered.

Kong Wang shook his head: “Neither can they.”

“Neither?” The Holy Maiden hadn’t expected Kong Wang to answer like that, then laughed: “Are you implying that your sacred mountain can represent the human race?”

This time, Kong Wang nodded, saying, “Representing the human race is a bit of an exaggeration, but it could indeed be considered one.”

“What a joke! What sacred mountain? This Holy Maiden has never heard of it.”

She seemed to have heard a joke, her tone becoming somewhat mocking.

However, Kong Wang ignored her laughter and continued, "Your confusion stems from your half-human, half-demon identity. Therefore, you don't know which side to align yourself with."

"Although you are now the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect, the sect itself is an anti-cult, and its headquarters are located outside the Great Chu, in demon territory. Whether it's truly a human force is questionable."

Upon hearing this, the Holy Maiden's expression turned serious, and she said calmly, "Since you've put it that way, then I'll tell you. I have nothing to be confused about. Whoever is stronger, I will side with them. So no matter who wins or loses in the end, I will be the winner."

"But you've missed one point. No matter which side you choose, your other half's bloodline will reject you. That's the real reason you feel confused."

Kong Wang pointed out the key issue.

The Holy Maiden fell completely silent upon hearing this.

As a half-human, half-demon, she could indeed bet on both sides, and ultimately, she would be the winner no matter who won.

But the drawback was that no matter which side she chose, she would face rejection.

She had vaguely sensed this before, but hadn't thought about it deeply.

Now that Kong Wang had pointed it out completely, her heart was far from at peace.

After standing there in silence for a while, she turned and left without saying a word.

Kong Wang watched the Holy Maiden leave before closing the window and going to bed to rest.

Mount Divine.

Chapter 146 Qilin, Black Dragon, Fire Phoenix

Qianshan Town had gained another Enlightenment Realm expert!

Good news naturally spread, and people's spirits lifted considerably upon hearing it!

You must know that a fourth-tier Enlightenment Realm expert was something only a prefecture-level city could possess.

Without exception, all of these people were sent to the Hundred Refinement Hall for training.

“I don’t know.”
