While the rumors about Thousand Mountain Town are legendary, their veracity is questionable. The mountain god in the rumors may not actually be a deity.”

“Regardless of whether it’s true or not, we have to go and see for ourselves. If it’s good, we’ll stay; if not, we’ll leave,” Qilin said calmly.

Black Dragon and the other nodded, saying nothing more, and continued on their way.

About an incense stick later, the three arrived at Thousand Mountain Town.

Upon arriving, the three were astonished by the density of spiritual energy here.

“The spiritual energy here is comparable to a blessed land!”

Black Dragon exclaimed incredulously.

“Hiss! Cultivating here will at least increase our cultivation speed by two or three times. The effect is amazing!”

Qilin tried absorbing the spiritual energy and was immediately awestruck.

It seems the rumors about the sacred mountain are true; there really are deities here. Otherwise, how could the spiritual energy be so dense?

Then, gazing at the newly risen town before them, the Qilin raised an eyebrow, a hint of surprise flashing in its eyes.

"Huh, just like the rumors say, a town appeared out of nowhere!?" the Black Dragon exclaimed in astonishment.

They had passed through this area a year ago; back then, it was all barren mountains and forests, without a single village, let alone a town.

Now, a year later, a town had sprung up, bustling with people, practically a small county town.

"Looks pretty reliable," the Qilin said.

The three then entered Thousand Mountain Town and began to observe the newly established town.

Merchants, restaurants, brothels—everything was there.

The main streets were crowded with people, and patrols of three to five people were patrolling, proving that the area was orderly.

What surprised the three most was the genuine smiles on the faces of the residents of Qianshan Town.

Their eyes lacked the despair of someone facing an uncertain future; instead, they radiated hope and anticipation.

This showed they had great faith in Qianshan Town and felt secure there.

This was something unseen in other counties, prefectures, or even state capitals.

In a world plagued by demons, no one knew when their homes would be attacked, so almost everyone was filled with uncertainty about the future.

Days were lived in a daze, devoid of any passion.

The prevailing thought was, "Since Great Chu is doomed anyway, and demons might be at our doorstep tomorrow, we might as well give up and just live one day at a time."

This widespread mentality created a lifeless atmosphere, and through mutual influence, the environment became stagnant.

Qianshan Town, however, was completely different; the situation was the exact opposite.

"I've only seen this kind of atmosphere in Great Chu nine years ago," Black Dragon suddenly said.

Qilin and Fire Phoenix nodded. Nine years ago, before the crimson meteorite landed, the human race had completely overwhelmed the demons.

"This time, we might have really come to the right place. That guy Ximen Longyu didn't lie to us."

Fire Phoenix's expression showed some interest.

"Let's find the person in charge first and see if they're willing to take us in," Qilin said.

He was also somewhat tempted. Putting everything else aside, the incredibly rich spiritual energy of this place alone was enough reason for him to stay.

However, before they could find the person in charge, Wu Ze approached them.

"Fellow Daoists, welcome to Thousand Mountains Town."

Wu Ze flew from the direction of Mountain Tower and landed in front of the three of them in the next moment.

He Liyuan and Bai Liang followed closely behind.

Having experienced the incident of the Dragon-Slaying Sect infiltrating Thousand Mountains Town, Bai Liang had set up hidden sentries throughout the town.

Any unfamiliar faces and suspicious, furtive individuals were required to be reported immediately.

The three rogue cultivators were spotted by sentries as soon as they entered Qianshan Town.

Bai Liang frowned slightly as he looked at the three, sensing that they were all at the peak of the Enlightenment Realm.

However, with Wu Ze present and the protective array of Qianshan Town, there was little to worry about.

Qilin narrowed its eyes, scrutinizing Wu Ze and the other two.

Two at the Enlightenment Realm, one at the Profound Realm.

However, he sensed a dangerous aura emanating from two of them; it was an inexplicable feeling, a feeling he sensed solely through his untrained intuition as a rogue cultivator.

"Are you gentlemen the leaders of Thousand Mountains Town?"

Qilin cupped his hands, speaking politely.

"Indeed," Bai Liang replied succinctly.

Upon learning that they were the leaders of Thousand Mountains Town, Qilin directly stated his purpose.


Chapter 147 Full Moon Yin God

"I am Qilin, and these two are my brothers, Black Dragon and Fire Phoenix. The three of us are all independent cultivators, without sect, faction, or faith."

"We heard of the Divine Mountain a few days ago and, filled with longing, have come to seek refuge!"

Qilin spoke solemnly, cupping his hands in greeting.

Black Dragon and Fire Phoenix also returned the gesture with solemn expressions, performing the proper courtesies.

The three of them exchanged glances, somewhat surprised.

They had initially thought these people had come to cause trouble, but unexpectedly, they had come to seek refuge.

It wasn't uncommon for cultivators to seek refuge, but they were all of relatively average cultivation, the highest being a recently recruited cultivator at the Profound Realm.

These three, however, were all at the peak of the Divine Enlightenment Realm!

Moreover, they claimed to be independent cultivators; independent cultivators are generally much stronger, especially at the same cultivation level.

Bai Liang processed the information, then turned to the three and asked,

“Fellow Daoists, since you are all rogue cultivators, why do you wish to join us?”

Most rogue cultivators dislike being restrained, which is why they don't join any faction.

These three, however, unusually wish to join Thousand Mountain Town, which made Bai Liang suspicious.

“You seem to have misunderstood us rogue cultivators. Not all rogue cultivators choose this path because they don't want to be bound by rules; some are forced into it.”

Qilin answered calmly.

“Forced into it?” Bai Liang was somewhat surprised, then seemed to realize something and looked at the three with some surprise.

“Are you criminals wanted by the imperial court?”

Upon hearing this, the three remained silent, tacitly agreeing.

Being wanted by the imperial court meant that no faction or sect would accept them.

That left only two paths: join the Dragon-Slaying Sect as a rebel, or become a rogue cultivator.

Clearly, all three had chosen to become independent cultivators.

"Well, I wonder if Thousand Mountain Town is willing to accept the three of us brothers?" Qilin continued to ask.

"Wait a moment, let us discuss it," Bai Liang said after thinking for a moment.

Three peak-stage Enlightenment Realm cultivators—this is already considered top-tier combat power.

We must treat this with caution.

"Alright!" Qilin readily agreed.

"Brother He, you stay with these three fellow cultivators for now," Bai Liang said, turning to He Liyuan.

He Liyuan nodded.

After Bai Liang and Wu Ze had walked some distance, Bai Liang asked, "Brother Wu Ze, what do you think? Should we take in these three independent cultivators?"

Wu Ze pondered for a moment, then said, "We can take them in. Right now, Thousand Mountain Town is very lacking in strong individuals."

"But Brother Wu Ze, have you considered that they are all at the peak of the Enlightenment Realm, and there are three of them? If they harbor ulterior motives, they could be a disaster," Bai Liang said, frowning.

If the Mountain God hadn't gone into hiding, he wouldn't have had this worry.

Under the Mountain God's watchful eye, who would dare harbor any disloyalty?

But now it's different.

"If it's just a peak-stage Enlightenment Realm cultivator, I can handle it," Wu Ze said.

He was confident now.

Although his cultivation was lower than the three Qilin, with the Divine Travel Talisman's enhancement, he wasn't even afraid of the Yin God Realm.

"What are you thinking? If you don't want to take him in, then forget it," Wu Ze said, looking at him.

Although he coveted these three powerful individuals who had conveniently come to his door, taking them in would be risky.

Bai Liang smiled and said, "I also want to take them in."

"Why?" Wu Ze pressed.

The two whispered among themselves and continued chatting for a while before stopping.

Meanwhile, He Liyuan chatted with Qilin and the other two, mostly answering their questions.

He Liyuan himself was a member of the martial arts world and frequently interacted with rogue cultivators.

During the conversation, he was also trying to gauge the situation of Qilin and the other two.

He could roughly determine that the three were indeed rogue cultivators, not some organization posing as such.

"I didn't expect Brother He to have been a镖师 (bodyguard/escort) before. We're practically colleagues now," Qilin said with a smile.

"Oh? Brother Qilin, you used to be a镖师?" He Liyuan asked in surprise.

Qilin shook his head, and Heilong chimed in, "We weren't镖师, we were 貴道人 (protectors/guardians of the Dao)."

"貴道人?" He Liyuan suddenly understood.

A protector is someone who safely delivers people to their destination.

A caravan escort protects goods, while a protector protects people.

They're essentially in the same profession.

"Then why did you quit and become a wandering cultivator?" He Liyuan asked, somewhat puzzled.

The profession of protector is quite sought-after in this world.

Not only are the rewards high, but it also allows you to build a wide network of connections.

Many people want to be protectors but don't even qualify, because it requires strength.

"Because we killed our employer," the black dragon grinned.

He Liyuan was stunned, sensing a chill in the black dragon's smile.

"Killed?"

"Yes, killed!" the qilin said coldly.

He Liyuan glanced at the three of them, noticing their uneasy expressions, and didn't press further.

There's probably more to this than meets the eye; the three being wanted by the court might be because of this incident.

After waiting a while longer, Bai Liang and Wu Ze finally returned.

Their reply was that they agreed to take in the three Qilins.

Upon hearing this, the three Qilins' tense expressions relaxed.

"Thank you for taking us in!" Qilin said, clasping his hands in thanks.

Next, the three settled into Thousand Mountain Manor.

Bai Liang first led them to the temple to worship the Mountain God.

The three were incredibly curious about this Mountain God.

They had come to seek refuge precisely because they had heard of the Mountain God's name.

However, in the temple, they did not witness the Mountain God manifest, but they did feel the divine power emanating from it—a pressure from the depths of their souls that cultivators lacked.

This completely convinced the three, confirming that the rumors about the sacred mountain were absolutely true.

After drinking the divine water, the three felt immense awe and reverence for the Mountain God.

The powerful effects of the divine water almost caused them to break through to the Yin God Realm on the spot.

Fortunately, they managed to suppress it at the crucial moment.

They couldn't break through yet; they had to wait for the right opportunity.

Bai Liang was puzzled. Why weren't they breaking through when they could?

When he asked this question, the Qilin smiled and replied, "Breaking through to the Yin God Realm involves many intricacies; it can't be done casually."

"Oh? What are the intricacies?" Bai Liang asked curiously.

He was actually only a novice when it came to cultivation.

His current achievements were entirely due to the divine talisman.

"There are two levels to breaking through to the Yin God Realm. One is the ordinary one, which can be broken through at any time. The other is the Full Moon Yin God, which requires breaking through on the night of a full moon, in an extremely Yin year, and at the correct month, day, and hour. Only the Yin God achieved in this way is the most perfect."

Qilin explained to Bai Liang.

The reason the three brothers hadn't broken through yet was that they were waiting for the right opportunity to achieve the Full Moon Yin God status.


Chapter 148 The Zhou Family

Upon first hearing the concept of the Full Moon Yin God, Bai Liang was somewhat surprised.

"What's the difference between the two?" he couldn't help but ask.

The Qilin replied, "The difference is huge! First, there's a significant gap in strength. Second, there's the solidity of the Yin God. The Full Moon Yin God is close to perfect, allowing for a brief separation of the primordial spirit during the day."

After listening to the Qilin's explanation, Bai Liang suddenly understood and said, "So you've been delaying your breakthrough to the Yin God realm to achieve the Full Moon Yin God? But it requires the perfect conditions of an extremely Yin year, month, day, and hour. How long will that take?"

He frowned slightly; these conditions were far too demanding.

If deliberately waiting for the extremely Yin time to achieve the Full Moon Yin God would waste time, it wouldn't be worth it.

"It happens every twelve years. We've waited two years. The extremely Yin time will arrive in half a year," the Qilin said, his tone subtly excited.

For a full two years, they suppressed the urge to break through to the Yin God realm and become powerful, all for the sake of achieving the Full Moon Yin God!

"Six months left. If we suppress our cultivation for another six months, we'll achieve our goal."

"Six months?"

Bai Liang was surprised. Thinking about it this way, Wu Ze seemed to have a chance to reach the peak of the Enlightenment Realm in six months, and could even attempt to become a Full Moon Yin God.

As for him, it was doubtful.

Regarding the arrangements for the three Qilin brothers, Bai Liang and the others discussed it and decided to have them stationed in Qianshan Town.

Three peak Enlightenment Realm cultivators were enough to keep things under control.

This way, even if they encountered a Yin God Realm cultivator, they would have enough strength to deal with and delay them.

However, since the three had just arrived and their trust was low, they weren't given too much power.

They were simply to serve as a protective force.

The three Qilin brothers accepted this arrangement.

It was normal for Bai Liang and the others to be wary of them since they had just joined Qianshan Town.

Besides, they were already quite satisfied with their current treatment.

The houses Bai Liang arranged for them were located inside Qianshan Town, near the Divine Mountain.

The spiritual energy here is much denser than the surrounding area. Although they are currently suppressing their cultivation levels and not cultivating, they can still use this abundant spiritual energy to hone their foundations and prepare for breaking through to the Yin God Realm.

“Qilin, it seems we made the right choice coming here!” Fire Phoenix said.

Qilin nodded: “Qianshan Town is different from other forces. The people here worship the Mountain God and don’t care about outside affairs; they just live their own lives.”

“Isn’t that exactly what we want?” Black Dragon grinned.

They just wanted to cultivate quietly and improve their strength in order to protect themselves in this chaotic world.

But the situation outside is chaotic, full of intrigue and backstabbing, making it impossible to even cultivate in peace.

There are very few places as pure as this Divine Mountain.

“We’ve found it here. It’s just that the two lords are still wary of us, but that’s normal. It will naturally dissipate over time,” Qilin said calmly.

The three chatted casually, mentioning the Divine Water and praising it highly.

"They say this divine water was created casually by the Mountain God. Truly worthy of being a deity!" Fire Phoenix exclaimed in amazement.

The other two agreed wholeheartedly, their faces filled with reverence.

Having cultivated as rogues for many years, they had accumulated countless resentments within them, making it impossible for them to calm their minds.

But since consuming the divine water, all their long-standing resentment vanished, allowing them to see their true nature.

"It's a pity we had no chance to witness divine power."

The Qilin's expression was one of deep regret.

After drinking the divine water, the three of them held the Mountain God in high esteem and requested to pay their respects.

They later visited the temple, but failed to witness the Mountain God's manifestation.

Bai Liang explained to them that only those with the right destiny could witness the Mountain God's divine intervention.

The three Qilin and his companions were disappointed and resentful upon learning they had no chance, but ultimately had to accept it.

Meanwhile, Chen Jianchi completed the task assigned by Bai Liang, successfully recruiting an alchemist for Qianshan Town.

This alchemist was an old man named Zhou Sanhai, known as Old Zhou.

With the strength of a third-tier Profound Realm cultivator, he was quite proficient in alchemy, capable of refining seven or eight kinds of pills, including the Crimson Spirit Pill.

However, the merchants who bought the spirit rice were too shrewd; they only agreed to help find an alchemist, but the cost of hiring one would have to be covered by Qianshan Town.

Fortunately, Qianshan Town was now wealthy and powerful, and directly announced that anyone who came to Qianshan Town would have an unlimited supply of spirit rice, and there would also be a supply of divine water.

To ensure that others were unaware of the effects of the divine water, Chen Jianchi specifically explained it again.

With such generous conditions, Zhou Sanhai readily agreed upon hearing this.

However, he also made a request: the entire Zhou family had to be relocated to Qianshan Town.

Wherever he was, the Zhou family would be.

Because the Zhou family was currently facing a generational gap, if he left, the Zhou family would have no chance of standing in Qingzhou City and would likely be quickly devoured by other powerful families.

Chen Jianchi naturally agreed to this condition.

Qianshan Town had plenty of other things, but it lacked people.

Especially a cultivation family like the Zhou family, though weak and small, is still a renowned cultivation family!

And they also know how to refine pills! That's the most crucial point!

Therefore, when the Zhou family, numbering over a hundred, arrived in Thousand Mountain Town, Bai Liang personally came out to greet them and led Elder Zhou into the Mountain Pavilion.

The two talked in detail for half an hour in the pavilion before Elder Zhou left with a satisfied smile.

“Jianchi, deliver the Crimson Spirit Grass to the Pill Pavilion tomorrow. Do everything you can to meet Elder Zhou's needs!” Bai Liang said.

“Yes!” Chen Jianchi nodded and turned to make arrangements.

.............

Qingzhou City.

These past few days, Kong Wang had traveled throughout the entire city, witnessing many joys and sorrows.

Of course, what concerned him most was the half-human, half-demon Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect whom he had met a few days ago.

Before this, he had never seen, nor heard of, that a human and a demon could have offspring together.

This was the first time he had ever seen a situation like the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Cult.

Demons at the seventh rank and above can transform into human form.

Therefore, theoretically, they could intercourse with humans, but whether they could bear offspring was unknown.

After all, no one had ever tried it, and even if they had, they wouldn't dare to make it public.

Humans and demons were sworn enemies; if this were to happen, they would be condemned and ostracized by everyone.

Kong Wang's pupil technique wouldn't be wrong; the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Cult was indeed half-human and half-demon.

In the past few days, the demoness hadn't come looking for Kong Wang, and Kong Wang hadn't deliberately sought her out.

Having confirmed that the demoness didn't possess the tyrannical aura of the demon race and wouldn't indiscriminately slaughter human civilians, he no longer cared about her.

Half-human, half-demon, ultimately inherited humanity and were still different from the demon race.

These days, Kong Wang made a new discovery.

He discovered a faint, almost imperceptible, Buddhist aura within Qingzhou City.


Chapter 149 The Feast at Hongmen

This Buddhist nature was incredibly powerful, far exceeding the range of his pupil technique.

At a glance, all he could see was a fiery red sea, dazzlingly bright, like a Buddha himself.

This incredibly powerful phenomenon astonished Kong Wang.

He had only encountered this level of aura with the Mountain God.

"Is there a powerful being in Qingzhou City?" Kong Wang wondered.

He wanted to explore further, but the opponent's strength was overwhelming. Even with the Divine Concealment Talisman, he wasn't very confident.

"Forget it. Although this Buddhist nature is somewhat ethereal, like a rootless duckweed, it's not something I can fathom."

In the end, Kong Wang gave up, ceasing his attempts to probe the Buddhist nature and instead deliberately avoiding it.

Because he had repeatedly used his pupil technique to probe, the opponent seemed to have noticed him and began searching for his whereabouts.

Fortunately, with the help of the Divine Concealment Talisman, Kong Wang successfully evaded the search.

“Strange, could it be my imagination?”

On the rooftop of a single-story house, the young novice monk frowned, quite puzzled.

For the past few days, he had felt a gaze upon him, a subtle, almost imperceptible feeling, like being watched by an ant.

But when he followed the direction of the gaze, he found nothing.

Even with his eighth-level Buddha's divine sense, he couldn't find a trace, so it could only prove that it was truly his imagination.

“It must be a side effect of this reincarnation.”

Finally, he attributed it all to the reincarnation itself.

After all, it was merely a fifth-level Yin Spirit Realm body; how could it perfectly withstand the true spirit of an eighth-level Buddha?

Even a small portion of the true spirit was beyond the capacity of a fifth-level Yin Spirit Realm cultivator to withstand.

Shaking his head, the young novice monk turned and disappeared.

When he reappeared, he was already in a courtyard.

"Grandmaster, where did you go just now? I have something to ask you."

Seeing his grandmaster appear, Sanwu quickly stepped forward and asked.

His grandmaster had suddenly disappeared, and he thought something had happened.

"Nothing," the young novice monk shook his head and said, "Just say what you need."

Sanwu bowed his head respectfully and said, "The people from the Celestial Patrol have found Qiu He, saying they need his help to capture the demoness of the Dragon-Slaying Cult."

"The Celestial Patrol?" the young novice monk said calmly, seemingly lost in thought.

"Yes, the Celestial Patrol. They've discovered the location of the demoness of the Dragon-Slaying Cult; she's currently in Qingzhou City. They've also contacted the General's Office," Sanwu continued.

"Very good, the scouts have arrived."

the young novice monk said calmly.

"Scouts?" Sanwu was slightly taken aback, then remembered what his grandmaster had said a few days ago—that someone would scout for them.

At the time, he had been puzzled, but now he understood completely.

It turned out his grandmaster had foreseen all of this.

"Grandmaster's Buddhist teachings are profound, reaching a state of perfection!" Sanwu said with utmost respect.

Sanzhi also suddenly understood, deeply admiring his grandmaster.

Truly worthy of being an eighth-level Buddha, his foresight is unparalleled.

"With Grandmaster here, we will definitely retrieve the secret manual this time!" Sanzhi said.

The young novice monk looked at the two and said, "Invite the people from the Celestial Patrol Bureau, including those from the General's Mansion. There's also a Taoist priest there."

"A Taoist priest? Xuanqing Temple?"

Sanzhi was somewhat surprised; he hadn't expected people from Xuanqing Temple to come.

All the major powers had indeed gathered together.

"What about the Dragon-Slaying Sect?" Sanwu couldn't help but ask.

"Just do as those people from the Sky Patrol Bureau said. First, surround and capture her, then have them scout ahead for us," the young novice monk said calmly.

Sanwu and Sanzhi accepted the order and respectfully withdrew.

The two then found Qiu He and discussed their plan.

There were over a dozen Yin God Realm cultivators in Qingzhou City.

Five were under the General's command, and four were under the Prefectural Government's command.

With so many Yin God Realm cultivators united, capturing the Dragon-Slaying Sect's Holy Maiden would be more than enough.

However, according to the Sky Patrol Bureau, this demoness had a unique escape method, requiring a Sky Patrol Array to capture her.

Then they needed to plan carefully.

Qiu He, on behalf of the Prefectural Government, extended an invitation to the Sky Patrol Bureau, and also invited Xiang Tiandao.

In the General's Mansion.

Xiang Tiandao frowned slightly upon receiving Qiu He's invitation.

"What is Qiu He trying to do?" he asked, puzzled.

Qian Qu finished reading the letter Qiu He had sent, then put it down, deep in thought. "I came here to track down the bald monk from Jinlong Temple and the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect, with people from the Sky Patrol Bureau following behind."

"Now that Qiu He has sent this invitation, it's clear the bald monk from Jinlong Temple already knows they're being followed. But what do they mean by this? Are they inviting them to join in capturing the Dragon-Slaying Sect's demoness?"

"Could it be that they've concealed their whereabouts and come to Qingzhou so furtively just to lure the Dragon-Slaying Sect's Holy Maiden? But this is the Sky Patrol Bureau's job; what does it have to do with Jinlong Temple?"

Qian Qu's brows furrowed deeply; he couldn't understand what the bald monk from Jinlong Temple was up to.

Qiu He was a member of Jinlong Temple, that much was certain, so his invitation was essentially Jinlong Temple's intention.

Xiang Tiandao shook his head; he was even more confused.

He had only just learned that two Yin God Realm experts from Jinlong Temple had arrived in Qingzhou City.

At first, he thought Qiu He had brought reinforcements to prepare for an attack on the General's Mansion.

“So, are you going to the Hongmen Banquet?”

Xiang Tiandao turned to look at Qian Qu.

The letter had also mentioned Qian Qu, though not by name, but it did mention the Xuanqing Temple Daoist who had just arrived in Qingzhou City.

“Go! This old Daoist wants to see what they're up to.” Qian Qu said with a smile.

With the Heavenly Water Agreement binding them, and everyone being at the Yin God Realm, he wasn't afraid even if Jinlong Temple had some conspiracy.

“Alright, let's go!” Xiang Tiandao said in a deep voice.

Then he summoned two trusted confidants, both Yin God Realm experts.

Including the two of them, a total of four Yin God Realm experts, they headed to the state government residence together.

Meanwhile, on the other side…

In an inn, Wu Cheng, holding the letter Qiu He had delivered, said with a grim face, "What does Qiu He mean by this? Is he trying to trick us?"

He had issued orders to Qiu He using the token of the Celestial Patrol Bureau, instructing him to assist in the capture of the demoness from the Heaven-Slaying Cult.

Now, Qiu He was turning around and asking them to come over to discuss the plan to capture the demoness.

The Celestial Patrol Bureau reported directly to the Emperor and was the most unique institution in the Great Chu Dynasty; no official had the authority to give orders to it.

On the contrary, the Celestial Patrol Bureau also had the power to command officials in various regions outside the capital.

Now, Qiu He had completely ignored the Celestial Patrol Bureau's token.

"It must be the Golden Dragon Temple's doing," Hua Wuzei said, frowning.

“According to what you said, the Golden Dragon Temple sent two Yin God Realm experts to Qingzhou, and Qiu He is from the Golden Dragon Temple, so he should be working for them now,” Yang Youshui guessed.

“Should we go?”

Wu Cheng’s expression was somewhat unpleasant.

The Celestial Patrol Bureau is no longer what it used to be. If it were during the late emperor’s time, would they have been ignored by these people?

“Of course we should go. No matter what, the Golden Dragon Temple wouldn’t dare to harm us,” Hua Wuzei said.


Chapter 150 The Cicada is a Golden Cicada

Having made their decision, Hua Wuzei and his two companions set off for the state government residence.

As soon as they arrived at the gate, they ran into Xiang Tiandao and his group.

“What are you doing here?” Wu Cheng frowned, looking at Xiang Tiandao and the others.

“I was just about to ask you the same thing. Did Qiu He also extend an invitation to your Observatory?”

Qian Qu’s face showed doubt. He had a vague feeling that something was amiss, but he couldn’t quite put his finger on it.

Wu Cheng nodded and said in a deep voice, “I found Qiu He first and asked them to help capture the demoness of the Dragon-Slaying Cult. But Qiu He turned around and asked us to come.”

“Asked you to come?” Xiang Tiandao frowned.

Qiu He was indeed up to no good. Inviting both the Observatory and them at the same time—what was he trying to do?

“Let’s go in together and see what tricks that bald monk from the Golden Dragon Temple is up to!”

Qian Qu took out his wine gourd, took a big gulp, and then strode inside.

The others followed, entering the state government residence.

The group consisted of seven people, all Yin God Realm experts.

This force was not to be underestimated; within the Great Chu territory, bound by the Heavenly Water Pact, they could practically roam freely.

“Qiu He, hurry up and come out to see us!”

Upon arriving at the main hall and finding no one around, Xiang Tiandao shouted loudly.

“Haha, Brother Xiang, how have you been? And fellow Daoists, welcome to my humble abode.”

Laughter rang out, and everyone looked to see Qiu He appear, followed by two monks.

Qiu He approached the group and greeted them with a smile, his manners impeccable.

Wu Cheng, his face cold, said, “Let’s skip the formalities. Qiu He, you know why we’re here. Let’s get straight to the point!”

“Alright, the people from the Celestial Patrol Bureau are certainly straightforward. Then I won’t serve you tea; please, everyone, have a seat.” Qiu He waved his hand.

After everyone was seated, Qian Qu was the first to launch an attack, looking at Sanzhi and Sanwu with a mocking expression. He laughed, "Fellow Daoist Sanzhi, if I remember correctly, your Golden Dragon Temple sent two people from the capital. Why are only you here? Where is the other fellow Daoist?"

Qian Qu knew that Sanwu was a disciple of the Golden Dragon Temple stationed in Qingzhou.

"This is a matter for our Golden Dragon Temple. Fellow Daoist Qian Qu, please don't pry," Sanzhi replied calmly.

They knew each other; the Sanzhi generation of the Golden Dragon Temple and the Qianzhi generation of the Xuanqing Temple were of the same generation, so they had interacted quite a bit.

Seeing that Sanwu remained silent, Qian Qu seized the opportunity to make a few more sarcastic remarks before stopping.

"Qiu He, I used the Heavenly Patrol Token to order your Qingzhou City to assist us in capturing the demoness of the Dragon-Slaying Sect. Why did you disobey my order?!" Wu Cheng stared intently at Qiu He, speaking coldly.

Qiu He quickly smiled and said, "Brother Wu Cheng, don't be in such a hurry. I never said I wouldn't assist you. It's just that capturing the demoness of the Dragon-Slaying Cult is a major matter and requires careful consideration."

"That's why I've invited you all here. Now we need to discuss how to capture the demoness of the Dragon-Slaying Cult in one fell swoop!"

Wu Cheng, however, looked unconvinced: "You'd better really think that way!"

"How could I dare deceive Brother Wu Cheng?" Qiu He said with a smile.

Then he turned to Xiang Tiandao, his expression becoming more composed, and said calmly, "Brother Xiang, you've heard me too. I invited you here precisely to discuss the matter of capturing the demoness. What are your thoughts, Brother Xiang?"

"If it's about capturing the demoness, this general will certainly assist with all his might!" Xiang Tiandao replied in a deep voice.

"Good! Then it's decided. The ten of us will join forces to capture the demoness; we'll definitely catch her easily!" Qiu He immediately made the decision.

The others harbored their own thoughts. Xiang Tiandao's group questioned Qiu He's motives, simply not believing he would be so concerned about capturing the demoness.

The Golden Dragon Temple must be plotting something behind this, but they didn't know what it was.

The three from Wucheng were equally skeptical, but given the current situation, they could only wait and see.

Hua Wuzei was disgusted. He hated this kind of intrigue and backstabbing the most.

If only they hadn't sought Qingzhou City's assistance; the three of them would have gone themselves. Letting the demoness escape was one thing; it wasn't the first time she'd gotten away.

Now, they were caught up in the incident, and extrication seemed impossible.

Wu Cheng's expression was somewhat grim. He had initially thought it was a case of the mantis stalking the cicada, unaware of the oriole behind, but now it seemed that the cicada wasn't an ordinary one, but a golden cicada.

Now, this golden cicada might very well turn the tables and devour them.

After reaching a decision, they began discussing how to capture the demoness.

The demoness of the Dragon-Slaying Sect had only appeared within the Great Chu territory this year, renowned for her unpredictable methods.

She was hailed by the Dragon-Slaying Sect as a genius rarely seen in a millennium.

The demoness possessed a mastery of escape techniques, easily escaping even from a Yang God Realm expert.

Therefore, to capture the demoness, they needed to set up a Sky-Patrolling Array.

This task was assigned to Wu Cheng and his two companions; only they possessed the skills.

As for Xiang Tiandao and his group, they, along with Qiu He and his group, were responsible for the actual capture of the demoness.

If the Sky-Patrolling Array could trap the demoness, the seven of them could easily capture her.

Having finalized their plan, the group began to act.

First, they located the demoness's hiding place, then Wu Cheng and the other two began setting up the Sky-Patrolling Array around her.

The rest waited for the array to be completed, ready to act at any moment!

Unbeknownst to them, a young monk in the shadows was observing everything.

"Are they planning to attack that half-human, half-demon female benefactor?" Kong Wang muttered to himself, withdrawing his gaze.

Of these people, only Hua Wuzei and Yang Youshui were acquaintances. Hua Wuzei was Bai Liang's brother-in-law, practically one of their own.

Therefore, Kong Wang chose to observe from a distance, because his pupil technique could see that a Buddhist aura was approaching, hidden behind the group.

Meanwhile, in the hidden stronghold of the Dragon-Slaying Sect.

The members here were unaware of the impending danger, but some gradually sensed something was amiss.

"Guardian Chen, I just went out and found the surrounding streets deserted. Something seems wrong!" a member of the cult reported gravely.

Guardian Chen's heart sank upon hearing this.

He rushed inside and found the Holy Maiden, who was cultivating.

"Your Highness, something terrible has happened! We may have been exposed. We must evacuate immediately!" Guardian Chen said in a deep voice.

"I already know."

The Holy Maiden spoke calmly, her words catching Guardian Chen off guard.

"Your Highness already knows? Does that mean you have a plan?" Guardian Chen's face lit up with joy.

"No." The Holy Maiden shook her head, stood up, and said, "Ten Yin God Realm cultivators have arrived, and they're still setting up the Sky Patrol Array. It's too late to retreat now!"

"What!? The Sky Patrol Array!?"

Protector Chen's expression changed drastically. As the old adversary of the Sky Patrol Bureau, he naturally knew what the Sky Patrol Array was.

Once this thing was completed, no one within the same realm could escape.


Chapter 151 Exposure

"What do we do now, Your Highness? Why don't you take advantage of the fact that the Sky-Patrolling Array hasn't fully formed yet and leave on your own? I'll bring men to buy you some time!"

Protector Chen said through gritted teeth.

For now, this was the only option.

They would fight their way out, delaying the formation of the Sky-Patrolling Array, giving the Holy Maiden a chance to escape—this was their only hope!

"There's no way to escape." The Holy Maiden shook her head again.

"Your Highness, don't give up! The Sky-Patrolling Array is powerful, but it takes too long to form. With us buying you time, you have a chance to break out of Qingzhou!" Chen Huyu said urgently.

"If that's all, then there is indeed a chance. But there are powerful figures watching from behind."

The Holy Maiden spoke calmly.

She had sensed being surrounded quite some time ago, before the Sky-Patrolling Array was even set up.

But just as she considered leading her people to safety, an incomparably powerful aura, like a blazing sun, suddenly pressed down on her.

It was as if it were a warning: if she dared to escape, her fate would be dire!

It was at that moment that she learned a powerful expert above the Yin God realm had arrived in Qingzhou City.

This shocked her immensely. Despite the restrictions of the Heavenly Water Pact, a powerful expert above the Yin God realm from Great Chu had actually left the capital.

And she already guessed who it was.

It must be the young novice monk from the Golden Dragon Temple she had been following outside the capital.

At the time, she had suspected something was amiss with that young novice monk; she hadn't expected him to be a cultivator above the Yin God realm.

Moreover, judging from that incredibly powerful aura, it's likely not just at the Yang God Realm, but even stronger!

"A powerful expert?" Protector Chen understood instantly upon hearing this.

His face turned furious, and he roared, "The tyrannical Chu actually broke the Heavenly Water Agreement, allowing a Yang God Realm expert to leave the capital?! Don't they know this will anger the demons? When the demons are enraged, it's the common people who will suffer, and then the tyrannical Chu will feign ceding several states as reparations, and that's it."

This kind of thing had happened before; Protector Chen was already very familiar with it.

But now, no amount of anger would help; they were already trapped.

Suddenly, the workings of heaven and earth changed; the spiritual energy of heaven and earth instantly stopped.

The Heavenly Patrol Array was complete!

Protector Chen's expression changed. He stepped out the door and looked up at the sky, but saw no blue sky or white clouds, only a hazy gray.

"Brothers, we're surrounded! There's no way to retreat! Pick up your weapons and fight these imperial lackeys!" Protector Chen roared.

The remaining members of the Dragon-Slaying Sect were immediately roused, their faces flushed, and they all grabbed their weapons, ready for a desperate fight.

"Your Highness, you are exceptionally talented and the future hope of the Holy Sect. You must find a chance to escape! I believe in Your Highness's abilities!"

Protector Chen turned around, his face resolute.

With that, he turned and flew into the air, a long spear appearing in his hand, and laughed coldly.

"Imperial lackeys, come out and face me!"

"Traitors and traitors, you dare to be so stubborn even when death is imminent! Take this!"

A cold snort rang out, and a blade of light ripped through the sky, slashing directly at Protector Chen.

Protector Chen's expression darkened, and white light gathered at the tip of his spear as he thrust it forward to meet the blade's attack.

With a loud bang, a burst of intense white light exploded, spreading outwards like ripples.

Protector Chen was thrown back several meters, blood trickling from the corner of his mouth.

Before he could catch his breath, another blade of light struck, but this time, a sword aura swept past, directly blocking the attack.

“Witch! You’ve finally appeared!”

Xiang Tiandao saw the cloaked, veiled woman and spoke coldly.

Following this, Qian Qu, Qiu He, San Zhi, and the other Yin God Realm cultivators appeared, instantly surrounding the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect.

The Holy Maiden looked around, longsword in hand, standing silently in the air.

“Your Highness, you shouldn’t have saved me. You should have found a chance to escape.”

Protector Chen wiped the blood from his mouth, his tone somewhat affected.

“Escape? The Sky-Patrolling Array has been set up. Where can you escape to?”

Qiu He laughed heartily, looking at the two from the Dragon-Slaying Sect, his gaze finally settling on the veiled woman.

"This is the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect? The prodigy the Sky Patrol Bureau couldn't catch even after a year? Now it seems she's nothing special." He sneered.

"Shameless! If you hadn't broken the Heavenly Water Pact and sent powerful experts, you piece of trash thought you could capture our Holy Maiden? Dream on!" Protector Chen gritted his teeth.

"Nonsense! You're courting death!" Sanzhi's face turned cold, and he raised his hand, unleashing a golden palm strike towards Protector Chen.

But just before it hit, it was blocked by azure sword energy.

"Attack!" Sanzhi said in a deep voice upon seeing the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect make her move.

Thus, the group simultaneously attacked the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect, unleashing a barrage of deadly attacks.

The Holy Maiden was overwhelmed, but her strength was indeed formidable, and she possessed a variety of methods, holding her own against three opponents.

"She's got some skill." Xiang Tiandao was somewhat surprised by the demoness's strength.

Then he and Qian Qu joined the battle, and the five of them managed to suppress the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect.

The remaining Sanwu and Qiu He began slaughtering Protector Chen and the rest of the Dragon-Slaying Sect members.

Protector Chen fought valiantly, but ultimately couldn't withstand the onslaught of two Yin God Realm experts and died in battle.

"Your Highness! Escape!" he shouted to the Holy Maiden, who was struggling against the attacks of five Yin God Realm experts, before he died.

Hearing the shout, the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect turned around and saw Protector Chen dead and the rest of the sect members being killed.

Her body suddenly froze, revealing an opening that Sanzhi seized upon. A palm strike landed on her cheek, knocking off her cloak and veil.

The veil fell, revealing an extremely stunning face, flawless, with a rosy complexion.

Especially her long, slender eyes, which seemed capable of captivating the soul and mesmerizing at first glance.

Upon seeing the Holy Maiden's true appearance, Xiang Tiandao and the others' expressions changed, their eyes filled with shock.

It wasn't her stunningly beautiful face, but rather her pair of fluffy fox ears.

"Demonization features...she's a demon!?"

Qian Qu's face darkened, but then he realized something was amiss.

For a demon to transform into human form, it must be a seventh-tier demon king.

This Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect clearly hadn't reached that level; she was only a fifth-tier Yin God Realm cultivator.

But if she wasn't a demon, how could she possess demonization features?

Suddenly, Qian Qu realized something, and looked up at the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect with a surprised expression.

"You're a hybrid born from the union of a human and a demon!?"

Yes, only a half-human, half-demon could produce such a result.

The Holy Maiden didn't answer. Now that her demonic features had been seen, she no longer concealed her face.

"I didn't expect the rebels to have begun colluding with demons? A half-human, half-demon can be a Holy Maiden?" Xiang Tiandao said coldly.

"Enough talk, gentlemen. Let's quickly capture this demoness. The Heavenly Patrol Array won't last much longer," Sanzhi said.


Chapter 152 The Royal Family of Demons

Sanzhi's heart sank slightly. Protector Chen's words indicated that the Dragon-Slaying Sect already knew their master had left the capital.

This matter was of paramount importance; if the news spread, it would cause a catastrophe.

Even the entire Golden Dragon Temple couldn't bear such a responsibility.

Therefore, he needed to kill the Dragon-Slaying Sect demoness before him as soon as possible.

Xiang Tiandao and the others had also heard Protector Chen's words.

They were all somewhat suspicious. A powerful figure had broken the Heavenly Water Pact and intervened? Could it be that one of them had already broken through to the Yang God Realm?

But that didn't make sense; none of them were concealing their auras, all being at the Yin God Realm—this couldn't be faked.

Qian Qu suddenly thought of another monk who hadn't yet shown himself, the young novice whose aura he found strange.

"Could it be him?" he suddenly realized.

But he couldn't afford to think about that now; the demoness was still putting up a fierce resistance. They had to capture her first!

The five of them joined forces and surrounded the demoness.

To withstand five opponents for so long, this demoness's strength was indeed formidable.

They weren't ordinary Yin God Realm cultivators; each possessed their own signature skills, yet even combined, they hadn't been able to subdue her.

This showed just how monstrous her talent was.

Wu Cheng's shout came from afar. The three of them were maintaining the operation of the Heavenly Patrol Array and couldn't join the battle.

Dealing with this demoness was already so troublesome; capturing her alive would be even more difficult.

Fortunately, Qiu He and Sanwu arrived immediately to assist after dealing with Protector Chen and the remaining members of the Dragon-Slaying Sect.

The battle now became seven Yin God Realm cultivators against the Dragon-Slaying Sect's Holy Maiden.

With such a vast disparity in strength, the Holy Maiden was utterly powerless to retaliate.

Under the onslaught of numerous Yin God Realm cultivators, she was forced to retreat steadily, sustaining various wounds.

If Xiang Tiandao and the others hadn't intended to capture her alive, they would likely have killed her on the spot.

Qiu He looked at the extremely beautiful Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect and smiled coldly.

“Seeking death!”

The Holy Maiden's eyes flashed coldly. Several wounds had appeared on her face, but they couldn't conceal her stunning beauty.

She turned gracefully, her longsword flicking lightly, thrusting directly towards Qiu He.

Even if she died, she would deliver a swift and deadly blow!

Qiu He snorted coldly, clasping his hands together, a barrier of Buddhist light appearing before him.

He had thought he could easily block the longsword, but to his surprise, the barrier was pierced like a sponge, heading straight for his heart.

"Damn it!" Qiu He's expression changed drastically. Realizing his carelessness, he didn't hesitate to summon his defensive magic weapon.

A piece of skull blocked the longsword.

With a clang, the longsword struck the skull, causing a tiny crack to appear in the bone before it shattered. The longsword was blocked.

Seeing that she hadn't succeeded, the Holy Maiden felt regret and didn't continue the pursuit. Instead, she quickly retreated, avoiding the attacks of the others.

But now she was completely surrounded, with nowhere to retreat, and could only take the attacks of the Yin God Realm cultivators head-on.

A burst of azure light appeared, and a gigantic azure fox phantom emerged, roaring towards the sky, its fur standing on end.

The Holy Maiden's face was pale. After summoning the azure fox phantom, she felt weak all over, barely able to stand.

"Be careful, this demoness has revealed her true demon form." Xiang Tiandao took a few steps back, warily eyeing the azure fox phantom.

"This is an azure fox, royalty among demons. No wonder it's so powerful!"

Qian Qu's expression was grave.

He hadn't expected this demoness to be connected to demon royalty.

As for why the azure fox was royalty among demons, it was because the current Demon Saint was a nine-tailed azure fox!

It is said that the azure fox possesses an innate divine power; each time it breaks through a realm, it grows a tail and gains a pseudo-life.

When critically injured and on the verge of death, one can recover from their injuries and return to their peak by consuming pseudo-life.

Upon reaching the ninth rank and achieving sainthood, pseudo-life completely transforms into true life, allowing one to be resurrected even after death.

"Good! I didn't expect it to be a member of the demon royal family. We've caught a big one!" Qiu He laughed, his attacks becoming even more ruthless.

The seven of them joined forces and began attacking the illusory blue fox.

The Holy Maiden remained within the illusory blue fox, recovering from exhaustion.

Summoning her true demon form was her last trump card.

While she was indeed strong, even the strongest cannot withstand overwhelming numbers.

The illusory blue fox couldn't hold out for long before being defeated by the combined attacks of the Yin God Realm experts.

"Pfft!" The Holy Maiden coughed up blood, her face turning even paler.

"Take down the demoness!" Sanzhi coldly snorted, his large hand reaching directly for the Holy Maiden's neck.

But the saintess vanished the next moment, reappearing as a small, thin blue fox, escaping ten meters away.

"A futile struggle," Qian Qu glanced at her, unconcerned.

Even with the Sky-Patrolling Array in place, and even if the array were dispelled, in her current state, this demoness had no hope of escaping their grasp.

The blue fox darted into a small alley and disappeared.

The group, however, remained calm, for they had firmly locked onto the blue fox's location; there was no way it could escape.

However, they were quickly proven wrong.

In an instant, the aura and connection to the Azure Fox vanished completely, leaving not a trace.

"What happened!?"

Xiang Tiandao was shocked. The Azure Fox he had almost obtained had simply disappeared right before his eyes?

Everyone's expression changed, and they quickly went to the spot where the Azure Fox had disappeared to search, but found nothing.

"She escaped? Or did she die somewhere?" Qiu He asked, his face grim.

"She couldn't be dead. If she were dead, her aura wouldn't disappear."

Qian Qu frowned, his fingers moving rapidly as he began to calculate.

But the result was a dense fog; he couldn't decipher anything.

He even saw a colossal mountain rising to the sky within the fog, its imposing presence awe-inspiring.

This startled him, and he immediately stopped calculating, a sense of lingering fear washing over him.

What terrifying being was this?

He had never encountered anything like this before.

Sensing something was amiss, Qian Qu fell silent.

The others continued to curse, saying they had been careless and had let the demoness escape.

Sanzhi's expression shifted constantly, glancing in a certain direction every now and then.

He wondered if his master had intervened and captured the demoness.

It must have been him. Otherwise, who in Qingzhou City would have the power to rescue the demoness right under their noses?

Especially right under his master's nose.

But he wondered what his master intended to do with the demoness. Was it because he coveted her half-human, half-demon bloodline?


Chapter 153 To avoid implicating this poor monk

With this kind of mobilization to capture the saintess, it's clearly a done deal.

Because he didn't believe Jinlong Temple could do anything to him, and because his surname was Xiang.

Logically speaking, the Divine Concealment Talisman is extremely powerful. Last time, even the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Cult couldn't detect him, but this time he was discovered.

She has half the blood of the Azure Fox, who are the royal family of demons and possess innate supernatural powers.

The Green Fox's innate supernatural power is knowing one's destiny.

This supernatural power can be used to find opportunities, or to seek a glimmer of hope, and so on.

Extremely powerful.

In the final moments, when she was at her wit's end, she refused to give up and used her innate supernatural power once more.

Surprisingly, this time she found a glimmer of hope.

After listening to the saintess's explanation, Kong Wang suddenly understood.

It turns out it was his fault.

At first, he watched the show from outside the Sky Patrol Array, but later, wondering if the Divine Concealment Talisman could hide from the Sky Patrol Array, he tried to go inside.

The result is that the Divine Concealment Talisman can deceive the Sky Patrol Array.

“Fate is truly wondrous,” Kong Wang remarked.

In a single moment, a decision was made, leading to the current outcome.

Is this some kind of preordained cause and effect, or fate?

Kong Wang's understanding of Buddhism deepened, reaching a higher level.

"It would be best if you, benefactor, did not stay in Qingzhou City and left as soon as possible." Kong Wang looked at the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect, paused, and then said, "to avoid implicating this humble monk."

The saintess initially thought that Kong Wang was concerned about her safety, but she didn't expect him to say something like that later.

She wasn't one to be coy, so since the other party was starting to ask her to leave, she wouldn't stay any longer.

"By the way, my name is not Benefactor, but Nangong Hu."

After confirming that the person had left, Kong Wang closed the doors and windows tightly.

Then he returned to his bed, sat cross-legged, and began to contemplate Buddhist teachings.


Chapter 154: Sending troops to Liaoyun County?

Inside the state capital's residence.

Liaoyun County, Qianshan Town, Divine Mountain.

But that's a real deity! Even if you're a powerful being, going there would just be suicide.

To be secretly happy about danger—wasn't that insane?

But since they didn't say anything, he didn't press further.

He trusted these two colleagues completely; after all, they had worked together for many years.

But the result he obtained was quite uncertain.

Whether it was a blessing or a curse was unclear; it was all a mystery.

A powerful figure had actually broken the agreement with Tianshui and left the capital?! Was he courting death?

But he was merely a lowly Enlightenment Realm expert, unable to even speak, and could only lower his head and remain silent, answering whatever they asked.

The questions asked by the three experts were mostly related to Peach Blossom County and the Divine Mountain of Thousand Mountains.

Ximen Longyu quickly replied, “I wouldn’t dare. It’s just that the situation at the sacred mountain is very special. If I were to tell you, you might not believe me.”

“Just tell me!” Sanzhi said calmly.

Surprised by Qianshan Town's development, he was more concerned about the situation in Taohua County.

After all, Sangui had died in Taohua County.


Chapter 155 Improving Elixirs

"Peach Blossom County is now a vassal state of Thousand Mountain Town. Half of the county's residents have already migrated to Thousand Mountain Town. As for Master Sangui's death, there's a 90% chance it's related to the Divine Mountain."

Jinlong Temple knew this, which was why they were being used as cannon fodder to investigate.

If they really encountered a deity and offended divine power, could they survive?
