Thinking of the divination he had just performed.

Unable to discern fortune or misfortune, it could actually be considered an auspicious divination.

Because as long as the divination isn't negative, it can always be considered auspicious.

"I hope so," Qian Qu sighed inwardly.

While they were heading towards Qianshan Town.

On the other side, near Baijiang Prefecture.

A cloud of blackish-red blood mist appeared, and Dao Shuai emerged from it.

He had followed the vanguard team to this place, capturing many human blood sacrifices along the way, extracting some information from them.

"A sacred mountain? A mountain god? So that's it. This bastard killed the subordinates I sent to Qingzhou?"

Dao Shuai's gaze was cold as he looked towards a majestic, imposing mountain in the distance.

That was the direction of Qianshan Manor.

He licked his lips and sneered, "Kill my men, then I, the commander, will annihilate your Divine Mountain!"

Black mist swept in, and without pausing, he headed straight for the Divine Mountain.

...............

Meanwhile, the residents of Thousand Mountain Town were completely unaware that two forces were approaching.

Everyone was busy with their own affairs.

The Zhou family had just settled in Thousand Mountain Town, and everything was unfamiliar.

However, Bai Liang valued the Zhou family highly and specifically instructed people to take extra care of them.

This made Zhou Sanhai very grateful, and he offered to lower his reward.

But Bai Liang shook his head and refused.

One thing at a time; the reward promised to Zhou Sanhai was definitely not to be reduced.

In this world, alchemists were a scarce talent, and preferential treatment was normal.

Chen Jianchi had been making frequent trips to the Zhou family lately, initially delivering Crimson Spirit Grass and various alchemy materials.

Even after delivering the materials, he continued to visit the Zhou family regularly.

Bai Liang inquired about Zhou Sanhai, who stroked his beard and laughed heartily, "That lad has taken a liking to my granddaughter, that's why he's been coming so often."

His granddaughter was also a cultivator, but her cultivation level was relatively low, having just entered the Refining Technique Realm, and she had only reached that level through the use of pills.

However, she was exceptionally beautiful, gentle and refined, and well-versed in pharmacology and alchemy, already able to refine some simple pills on her own.

Bai Liang was somewhat surprised to hear this; Zhou Sanhai's hearty laughter indicated that he approved of the matter.

So he smiled and said, "Then I'll have to trouble Elder Zhou to take care of Brother Jianchi from now on."

"We're all family, no problem, no problem!" Zhou Sanhai laughed heartily.

After exchanging some pleasantries, he took out several medicine bottles from his pocket and placed them on the table.

"These are Crimson Spirit Pills. I've refined three batches over the past few days, and each one was successful, totaling twenty-seven pills."

Zhou Sanhai's expression turned serious when he spoke of the pills.

"Three batches all successful!?"

Bai Liang was pleasantly surprised. He knew that for alchemists, only one in ten batches succeeded; a success rate of one out of ten was already quite good.

But Zhou Sanhai had refined three batches, and all of them were successful—this was quite unexpected.

"So Elder Zhou is a genius in alchemy. We juniors have been negligent." Bai Liang's expression became respectful.

"No, young friend Bai, please don't flatter me. This isn't my achievement."

Zhou Sanhai waved his hands repeatedly, then said, "In the past, my pill-refining success rate was only one in twelve; my skill is quite average."

"One in twelve?" Bai Liang was puzzled.

If it was one in twelve batches that succeeded, then why were three batches successful in succession after coming to Thousand Mountain Town?

"Could it be...?"

"Young friend Bai, you should know why now, right?"

Zhou Sanhai looked at Bai Liang, his tone slightly excited.

"Because this is a sacred mountain!" Bai Liang said, finally understanding.

"That's right, it's because this is a sacred mountain, but there are other reasons as well."

Zhou Sanhai continued excitedly, "The spiritual energy here is incredibly rich, greatly increasing the success rate of alchemy. Most importantly, I added divine water when refining the pills!"

"Divine water!?" Bai Liang was astonished, not expecting Old Zhou to have added that.

"That's right, it's because of the divine water that every batch of pills I refined was successful!" Zhou Sanhai laughed.

"Furthermore, after adding the divine water, the efficacy of these Crimson Spirit Pills is 30% better than ordinary Crimson Spirit Pills, while also retaining some of the effects of the divine water."

Upon hearing this, Bai Liang picked up a Crimson Spirit Pill, carefully smelling it. The fragrance was rich, with a faint, sweet scent of divine water.

He put it in his mouth; it melted instantly, the Crimson Spirit Pill transforming into a medicinal liquid that flowed down his throat.

A warm current of spiritual energy circulated throughout his body, nourishing his limbs and bones.

At the same time, he also felt the effects of drinking divine water, gaining clarity of mind and purifying his spiritual energy.

"Good stuff!" Bai Liang's eyes lit up, and he couldn't help but exclaim in admiration.

Although the Crimson Spirit Pill had little effect on him, it would be of great benefit to a novice cultivator!

Bai Liang was delighted and also greatly admired Elder Zhou.

Unexpectedly, despite his advanced age, the other party possessed many ingenious ideas, even considering adding divine water as an auxiliary ingredient to alchemy.

"These Crimson Spirit Pills will be supplied to the Hundred Refinement Hall from now on. With the aid of these pills, I believe we can quickly cultivate another batch of cultivators," Bai Liang said with a smile.

Zhou Sanhai nodded and then said, "Then this old man will go back to refine pills and try to refine several batches."


Chapter 156 An Imminent Meeting

After seeing Elder Zhou off, Bai Liang took the Crimson Spirit Pill to the Hundred Refinement Hall.

The Hundred Refinement Hall now had over twenty students.

Those who awakened their talents by drinking divine water continued to emerge, providing the Hundred Refinement Hall with a steady stream of new talent.

"Master Bai," the students greeted him, stopping their cultivation.

Bai Liang nodded and then took out the Crimson Spirit Pill.

"This is a pill Elder Zhou just refined. You can take it while you cultivate."

Hearing about the pill, the students' faces lit up with joy.

They each stepped forward and received a pill.

Bai Liang had one of them take it to see how it worked.

That person immediately swallowed the Crimson Spirit Pill, then sat cross-legged on the ground and began to regulate his breathing to absorb the pill's effects.

After a short while, his face turned crimson, steam rose from his skin, and sweat poured down his face.

This continued for about half an incense stick's time before stopping.

He opened his eyes, his gaze bright and piercing.

“Excellent pill! My cultivation has improved by two levels. With a little more time, I can break through to the Refining Technique Realm and become a cultivator!”

Hearing this, everyone was extremely excited, amazed by the effects of the Crimson Spirit Pill, and wished they could try it immediately.

Bai Liang, however, smiled faintly: “Don’t be too happy yet. The reason you have such a great effect after taking it is because it’s your first time taking the pill. Subsequent times won’t be as effective.”

“That’s still very powerful, Master! This pill is equivalent to more than ten days of my arduous cultivation!” the man exclaimed excitedly.

“Then cultivate diligently!” Bai Liang smiled and offered a few words of encouragement, then checked the cultivation progress of the students present.

Two people had already broken through to the Refining Technique Realm.

One was Lin Qian, the most talented young man, and the other was Chai Ziyan, a woman who awakened her talent after drinking divine water.

The rest were still in the entry-level stage, honing their skills, and would have some time before breaking through to the Refining Technique Realm.

However, with the aid of the Crimson Spirit Pill, these people should be able to be trained into Refining Law Realm cultivators very quickly.

At that time, Thousand Mountain Town will have its own force.

Although this force will still be very weak.

The remaining Crimson Spirit Pills will naturally be distributed to Du Tiandao, Chen Jianchi, Qian Ke, and others.

They are the backbone and core members of Thousand Mountain Town.

Afterwards, Bai Liang arrived at the residence of the three Qilin brothers.

Since joining Thousand Mountain Town, the three brothers have quietly stayed in their residence to cultivate, occasionally venturing out to patrol Thousand Mountain Town. Their lives are much more peaceful than when they were cultivating in the wild.

"Qilin, Master Bai is here." Black Dragon called out to Qilin and the other two upon seeing Bai Liang.

After a while, Qilin and Fire Phoenix came out.

Bai Liang smiled and said, "Brothers, how are you finding your stay in Qianshan Town? Are you comfortable?"

"Very comfortable. To be honest, after getting used to the life of a reclusive cultivator, this stable life is a bit unusual," Qilin said with a smile.

He then joked that on the first day Black Dragon settled in Qianshan Town, he was startled awake in the middle of the night by a cat, and was so frightened that he thought a demon had appeared.

Later, he found out that it was just a cat coming out in heat at night, engaging in sexual intercourse with a female cat.

Black Dragon, looking embarrassed, explained, "It's normal to be startled awake by noises at night. That cat's meowing was so awful, like a demon."

Bai Liang and the others exchanged knowing smiles.

Over the past few days, they had become much more familiar with each other, and both sides had lowered their guard.

Especially after the three Qilin brothers consumed the divine water, their relationship had grown even closer.

Bai Liang took out the Crimson Spirit Pill and asked the three Qilin brothers if they needed it.

The Qilin brothers took it, examined it, and then shook their heads, saying, "This pill won't be of much use to us. Master Bai, you should give it to someone else."

They were all at the peak of the Enlightenment Realm, and the Crimson Spirit Pill wouldn't have much effect on them.

Bai Liang nodded and took back the Crimson Spirit Pill.

After chatting for a while, Bai Liang left and headed to a cave halfway up the mountain.

He now cultivated there; the incredibly rich spiritual energy greatly increased his cultivation speed.

Li Xue, who regularly cultivates here, successfully broke through to the Profound Realm a few days ago, and her realm has now stabilized.

Given Li Xue's relentless cultivation, she'll likely catch up to Bai Liang soon.

Bai Liang watched with envy.

But there was nothing he could do; he still had many affairs to handle in Thousand Mountain Town, while Li Xue didn't need to, only needing to cultivate.

"Uncle Bai, you're here."

Sensing someone enter, Li Xue opened her eyes and smiled upon seeing Bai Liang.

Bai Liang nodded with a smile and said, "Don't call me uncle anymore. Little Xue, your cultivation is almost catching up to mine. I reckon in a few months, I'll have to call you senior."

"Uncle Bai, are you teasing Little Xue? Breaking through to the Divine Realm isn't that easy; it takes at least three or four months, right?" Li Xue thought seriously for a moment before replying.

"Three or four months..." Bai Liang smiled wryly. At this cultivation speed, even Brother Wu Ze would probably be caught up.

He shook his head, deciding not to dwell on it any longer. He found a spot, sat cross-legged, and began cultivating.

....................................

Meanwhile.

Jinlong Temple and the others were rapidly approaching Qianshan Town, their outline already visible in the distance.

“We’re almost there! That’s Qianshan Town!”

Qiu He gazed at the distant town, his expression grave.

Sanzhi could see the silhouette of a massive mountain standing there in the distance.

Is this the legendary sacred mountain?

From a distance, it didn’t look particularly special; it was just an ordinary mountain.

The young novice’s pupils glowed faintly as he stared intently at the sacred mountain ahead, his brows furrowed.

He had sensed an unusual aura, an aura usually only found on altars used for worshipping deities.

But now he had encountered it on a mountain in Qingzhou. What did this mean?

It meant that this sacred mountain might very well contain an altar for worshipping deities, or perhaps, as the rumors said, this sacred mountain itself was a deity!

The thought that the secret manual was likely hidden in Thousand Mountain Town filled him with apprehension.

Fortunately, he had hastily gathered a group of able-bodied men who could scout ahead for them.

The young novice glanced at Sanzhi, who immediately understood.

"Gentlemen, Thousand Mountain Town is just ahead. Let's hurry!" Sanzhi said calmly.

Qian Qu and the others looked grim.

Having come this far, one last step wouldn't hurt, so they gritted their teeth and continued flying towards Thousand Mountains Town.

Hua Wuzei and Yang Youshui exchanged a secret glance.

They were already communicating through their eyes; once they entered Thousand Mountains Town, they would immediately find Bai Liang, their brother-in-law, and inform him of the situation.

With that mountain god present, they believed these bald monks wouldn't be able to cause much trouble.

However, they still needed to be wary. That young novice monk was unfathomable, and very likely a powerful figure.

Otherwise, he wouldn't have dared to come personally, knowing that a deity resided here.


Chapter 157 Defeated Escape

Meanwhile, the sixth-tier Sword Commander was rapidly approaching Thousand Mountains Town.

He could sense from afar the incredibly abundant human fortune gathering there.

Although the gathering fortune was very weak, it was slowly increasing, like a trickle converging into a river.

This was the first time he had encountered such a situation.

Since the chaos caused by demons and monsters, human fortune had only ever dissipated, never gathered.

For it to suddenly gather in this remote corner of Qingzhou was like the sun rising in the west.

This made the Sword Commander feel uneasy.

Coincidentally, the vanguard team's tracks were also heading towards Thousand Mountains Town, so he came directly.

Upon arriving at the outskirts of Thousand Mountains Town, the Sword Commander saw its entirety.

It was a newly built human town under construction, or rather, the size of a county town.

Furthermore, human refugees were continuously arriving from all directions seeking refuge, making Thousand Mountains Town seem like a land of light.

"Is this it?" Dao Shuai's tone was icy.

The vanguard team's trail ended here; if nothing unexpected happened, they must have been slaughtered by the human cultivators.

His eyes were cold. Since they dared to kill his demons, then this human town could go down with them, becoming his prey!

However, just as he was about to approach Thousand Mountain Town, he suddenly sensed a large aura of Yin God Realm experts.

Upon closer inspection, there were nearly ten Yin God Realm experts!

Dao Shuai's expression changed slightly. Why were there suddenly so many human cultivators here? Something was wrong!?

Could it be that they knew he was coming to Qingzhou and had laid an ambush beforehand?

But he was a sixth-tier Demon Commander; no matter how many Yin God Realm cultivators came, it would be useless.

The difference in strength was too great; numbers alone couldn't make up for it.

"Good, very good! I didn't expect this trip to yield such unexpected gains. So many Yin God Realm cultivators—once I absorb them, my cultivation will surely increase greatly!"

The Sword Commander sneered.

He thought to himself that the Demon Lord Nian, clever all his life, had made a foolish mistake this time, actually fearing such a small place as Qingzhou.

Now, all this Yin God Realm blood food would be his to enjoy.

With his increased strength, the Demon Lord Nian would be no match for him when he returned to Baijiangzhou.

Thinking this, the Sword Commander couldn't wait any longer and headed directly towards the group of human Yin God Realm cultivators.

Meanwhile, the Three Wisdoms and their group were approaching Qianshan Town.

At this moment, they were still marveling at the development of Qianshan Town, how a town could have sprung up out of thin air in a wilderness.

This feat wouldn't have been anything special in the past. But now, it was a world of demons and monsters!

Suddenly, the little novice monk sensed something, his expression changed slightly, and he said in a deep voice, "Be on guard! Demons are approaching!"

Upon hearing this, everyone was shocked!

Before they could even consider whether the words were true or false, everyone present tensed up, their divine senses alert to their surroundings, adopting a defensive posture.

A moment later, a figure carrying black mist flew in from afar.

It exuded an incredibly sharp and violent aura, giving off a terrifying sense of oppression.

Feeling the suppression brought by this aura, Qian Qu and the others were terrified!

"A sixth-tier demon!?"

San Zhi exclaimed in disbelief.

"When did a sixth-tier demon appear in Qingzhou!?" Wu Cheng's face was extremely grim, his eyes fixed intently on the demon before him, twin blades already appearing in his hands.

"A sixth-tier Blade Demon, they must have come from Baijiang Prefecture! They've set their sights on Qingzhou!"

Qian Qu recognized the demon before him as one of the two sixth-tier demons that had wreaked havoc in Baijiang Prefecture recently—the Blade Demon!

"Good eye, but useless, because you'll soon become my prey!"

The Blade Demon surveyed the numerous Yin God Realm cultivators before him, his crimson eyes gleaming with greed, and he licked his lips.

"Bullshit! You demons break your promise! The Heavenly Water Agreement stipulates that sixth-tier cultivators cannot enter the territory. You've violated the Heavenly Water Agreement!"

Wu Cheng said, his face grim.

"Violated? When did I violate it? It said sixth-tier cultivators couldn't enter, but I broke through to the sixth tier after entering. How is that a violation?" The Blade Demon sneered.

"Exploiting a loophole? In that case, we can also break through to the sixth-tier Yang God Realm after leaving the capital!"

Wu Cheng spoke in a deep voice.

"You can try," Dao Shuai said disdainfully. "The Heavenly Water Pact will cease to exist then. Do you dare bear the consequences?"

Wu Cheng was speechless upon hearing this, feeling incredibly aggrieved.

Yes, the demons might disregard the Heavenly Water Pact, but the human race could not.

Once the restrictions of the Heavenly Water Pact were lifted, and demons of the fifth rank and above began to enter, it would be the end of the human race.

Therefore, since the demons had violated the Heavenly Water Pact, Great Chu could only tolerate it, turning a blind eye.

As long as the demons didn't completely break ties and allow sixth-rank demons to enter, it would be fine.

The officials in the capital only wanted time. They didn't care about sacrifices as long as they could delay further.

This feeling of being attacked and then being told not to retaliate was incredibly frustrating.

San Zhi turned to look at the little novice monk.

At this moment, only the Grandmaster had the strength to contend with a sixth-rank demon.

But the little novice monk shook his head at him.

He wouldn't act unless absolutely necessary.

Otherwise, if the demons learned that a powerful human had left the capital, they would inevitably face trouble.

Therefore, he could only endure it a little longer.

At most, he could protect Sanzhi and the others as they left; he wouldn't kill the sixth-tier demon.

Leaving the capital in his reincarnated form was already a high price to pay, and the most important objective of this trip hadn't been achieved yet.

The secret manual was essential; it was the key to becoming a saint, and it couldn't be lost.

"Sanzhi, does your Golden Dragon Temple intend to stand idly by?"

Qian Qu turned around, seeing that the Golden Dragon Temple group wanted to retreat, and angrily demanded.

Sanzhi didn't answer, instead avoiding Qian Qu's angry glare.

"Hmph! Trying to escape? Do you think you can escape from me?" Dao Shuai sneered.

But the next moment, the young novice monk produced a string of prayer beads, enveloping Sanzhi, Sanwu, and Qiu He in golden light. In the blink of an eye, the four vanished.

"Hmm?" Dao Shuai realized something was wrong and unleashed a dozen sword strikes, but the people had already disappeared; the swords only struck thin air.

Dao Shuai's face was incredibly grim. Four human prey had actually escaped—a severe slap in the face.

"Something's wrong. Is there a powerful expert?"

He immediately realized that the young novice monk who had just attacked was no ordinary person; otherwise, he couldn't have escaped so easily.

After the four escaped, Dao Shuai became vigilant, keeping a close watch on Qian Qu and the others, his divine sense locking onto them tightly.

Qian Qu and the others looked extremely grim, realizing they were likely doomed today.

It was all those bald monks from Jinlong Temple's fault; they'd ruined them.

It was one thing for powerful figures to leave the capital, but to be so terrified by a sixth-tier demon and flee in fear was utterly shameful.

"Everyone, there's no turning back today. We have no choice but to fight to the death against this demon!"

Qian Qu spoke in a deep voice.


Chapter 158 Alarmed, Observing the Battle from a Distant Away

Hearing Qian Qu speak, Wu Cheng and the others' expressions turned grave.

They knew in their hearts that this time, the odds were against them.

“Brother Wu, try to get as close to Qianshan Town as possible; it’s our only way out!” Hua Wuzei suddenly shouted.

He and Yang Youshui had already begun to approach Qianshan Town.

Faced with a sixth-tier demon, they had absolutely no hope of resistance.

The only hope they had was to enter Qianshan Town and rely on the sacred mountain for protection.

“Qianshan Town?”

Wu Cheng was puzzled, but seeing that Hua Wuzei and Yang Youshui had already begun to move, he didn’t think too much about it and started following them.

Qian Qu was numb. Shouldn’t they be uniting to fight the Blade Demon now?

Why were they running away before the battle even started? They were cowardly before the fight even began.

The problem was, even if they ran, could they escape to the sixth-tier Blade Demon?

They didn’t have the abilities of the little novice monk; they were just ordinary Yin God Realm cultivators.

"Master Qian, hurry up and leave, or it'll be too late."

The two Yin God Realm experts from the General's Mansion had already sprung into action, following behind the three from the Sky Patrol Bureau.

"Great Lord Wuliang Tianzun, wait for this old Daoist!"

Qian Qu's expression changed. He didn't care whether fleeing to the Divine Mountain was right or wrong; he immediately followed.

But the Blade Demon had already locked onto them. This time, there was no way they could escape.

"Struggle without fear. Run a few more steps, and you'll devour those ordinary blood delicacies too."

The Blade Commander sneered, following behind Hua Wuzei and the others, toying with them like a cat playing with mice.

A sharp blade struck. Qian Qu, lagging behind, was shocked. He didn't even have time to turn around. His Daoist robe billowed, creating a barrier to block the blade.

With a bang, the billowing robe shattered into pieces of rags.

Qian Qu flew forward, passing the two fleeing General's Mansion experts, and crashed in front of Wu Cheng.

"Ugh!"

A mouthful of blood spurted out, Qian Qu's face was incredibly weak, his lips blackened.

A deep, gruesome knife wound clung to his back.

He quickly took out his gourd and drank a mouthful of medicinal wine, his complexion immediately regaining some color.

Wu Cheng and the others, witnessing this, paled.

A sixth-tier Demon General, indeed formidable. Qian Qu's strength, even among fifth-tier Yin God Realm cultivators, was considered top-tier.

But even so, he couldn't withstand a single attack from a sixth-tier Blade Demon.

Wu Cheng hesitated for a moment, then paused and pulled Qian Qu up.

"Thank you," Qian Qu said weakly.

"We're both under his claws; all we can do is work together," Wu Cheng said solemnly.

Qian Qu nodded, and then the two continued fleeing towards Thousand Mountain Town.

In just this short delay, the two from the General's Mansion had already overtaken them.

"Why are you running so fast? Stay here!"

The Sword Commander glanced at Hua Wuzei and Yang Youshui, who were fleeing at the front, and sneered. Then, he casually unleashed two sword beams.

Hua Wuzei sensed danger and turned around. Seeing the sword beams aimed at them, his eyes narrowed.

Without hesitation, he threw out his Sky-Patrolling Net, and Yang Youshui did the same, both nets meeting the sword beams.

Tear!

The sword beams sliced ​​through the Sky-Patrolling Nets in two as easily as cutting paper, but the beams were also significantly weakened in the process.

Hua Wuzei and Yang Youshui grunted, struggling to defend themselves against the weakened sword beams.

But a sixth-tier cultivator is still a sixth-tier cultivator. Even with their combined strength, the two couldn't withstand the blade's light and were sent flying.

They were only slightly better off than Qian Qu earlier, not seriously injured.

Blood trickled from their mouths, but there was no time for them. After getting up, they hurriedly headed towards Thousand Mountain Town.

If they didn't reach Thousand Mountain Town, they would truly be dead.

"Very persistent," the Blade Demon said calmly.

Then, not forgetting to finish off the two men from the General's Mansion in the middle.

These two weren't so lucky. One of them was only at the early stage of the Yin God Realm and couldn't withstand the Blade Demon's attack at all, being instantly cut in half.

The Blade Demon directly collected their corpses, absorbing them as food to increase his cultivation.

Wu Cheng and the others felt a chill in their hearts, wondering if they would be next to die, so they fled for their lives.

In the distance, the figures of the four from Golden Dragon Temple appeared.

They stood there, watching from afar as the Blade Demon devoured the corpse of the Yin God Realm expert, their expressions unchanged.

"This sixth-tier Blade Demon, why is it in Qingzhou? This is troublesome!" Sanzhi said in a deep voice.

The secret manual was most likely in Qianshan Town, and now a sixth-tier demon was blocking their way, greatly increasing the difficulty of retrieving it.

"Senior brother, you're mistaken. The appearance of this sixth-tier Blade Demon might actually be a good thing for us," Sanwu suddenly said.

"Why? Why would it be a good thing?" Sanzhi frowned, puzzled.

Sanwu smiled and said, "Shenshan is not simple, that's beyond doubt. To be honest, even if we send Qian Qu and the others to investigate, they might not find anything. But if a sixth-tier demon goes, the effect will be different."

Sanzhi's heart skipped a beat upon hearing this.

Yes, a sixth-tier demon would definitely target the people of Qianshan Town—nearly 100,000 people to feed on. Although not many, it was still a treat.

As long as the Blade Demon sets his sights on Thousand Mountains Town, he will clash with the so-called Mountain God.

Then they can sit back and watch the tigers fight, and even see what the Mountain God's true origins are.

"But what if the Blade Demon takes the manual?" Qiu He asked hesitantly.

Sanzhi and the other two didn't know what to do either, so they looked at their grandmaster.

The little novice monk said calmly, "If the manual appears, I will naturally take action."

Once he got the manual, he wouldn't care about being exposed.

Sanzhi and the others were relieved to hear this.

They turned their heads and coldly looked at Qian Qu and the others who were being chased.

Meanwhile, in Thousand Mountains Town.

The three Qilin brothers were the first to notice the commotion outside Thousand Mountains Town.

The three of them had just come out on patrol when they were startled by the violent and fierce aura in the distance.

"What's going on? Such a powerful aura! A peak fifth-tier demon!?"

The black dragon flew into the air, frowning as it stared into the distance.

The Qilin and Fire Phoenix followed closely behind. The Qilin glanced at them and said in a deep voice, "Fire Phoenix, quickly inform Master Bai, big trouble is coming!"

The Fire Phoenix nodded and turned to leave quickly.

"Black Dragon, come with me!" the Qilin said, flying directly out of Thousand Mountain Town.

The two arrived outside and saw several figures fleeing in disarray.

Behind these figures was a massive form, emanating black mist.

That violent and fierce aura emanated from this figure.

The black dragon's eyelids twitched, and he gasped, exclaiming, "A sixth-tier demon!"

This aura couldn't possibly belong to a fifth-tier demon!

"It's that sixth-tier demon general from Baijiang Prefecture; it's come to Qingzhou."

The Qilin's face darkened. With a flick of his wrist, a bone blade appeared in his hand. He held it horizontally across his chest and roared at the figures fleeing towards Qianshan Town:

"Dare to take another step forward, and don't blame my bone blade for its mercilessness!"


Chapter 159 Activating the Guardian Array!

The Qilin's shouts echoed through the sky, and Hua Wuzei and the others who were fleeing heard them immediately.

Before Hua Wuzei could speak, Wu Cheng interjected, "Fellow Daoist, we are from the Imperial Astronomical Bureau, being hunted by demons. Could you lend us a hand?"

"No! Change direction immediately and don't go near Qianshan Town!"

Qilin roared, his face full of murderous intent, and a chilling aura emanated from him.

However, even at the peak of his Enlightenment realm, he could make Wu Cheng feel threatened.

Wu Cheng wanted to say something more, but the Blade Demon behind him attacked again at that moment.

The other Yin Shen realm general at the very back was cleaved in two before he could even let out a scream.

Both the Qilin and the Black Dragon were so frightened by what they saw that their eyelids twitched.

A sixth-tier monster is simply impossible to resist.

Black Dragon was furious. He grabbed his axe, wishing he could chop Wu Cheng and the others into pieces.

These sixth-tier sword demons were clearly lured here by these people; they're here to harm their Thousand Mountain Town.

"Fellow Daoist, I am Hua Wuzei, Bai Liang's brother-in-law. Please let us in!"

At this moment, Hua Wuzei spoke up, saying that if they didn't go into Qianshan Town soon, they would be the ones to die.

Qilin frowned, not believing Hua Wuzei's words.

“I have already informed Master Bai. If you are indeed Master Bai’s brother-in-law, he will naturally let you in when he arrives,” he said.

"Brother Hua, you've really put us in a terrible situation now!" Wu Cheng looked at the Blade Demon, who was devouring blood, with a face full of despair.

Qian Qu coughed a few times, his face already extremely weak.

"The old Taoist priest said long ago that if we join forces to resist the sixth-order demon, with the strength of the six of us, we might have a chance to survive if we can delay it."

As it turned out, Hua Wuzei and his companion led the escape, and the army was completely routed.

Hua Wuzei was extremely anxious at the moment, and had no mood to refute Wu Cheng and the other man's words.

After devouring the corpse of the Yin God Realm expert, the Blade Demon licked his blood-stained lips, his cold gaze fixed on the two Qilin figures blocking the way in front of Thousand Mountain Town.

"Not bad, two more snacks have arrived. I'll use you two to whet your appetites!"

The Demon Blade grinned sinisterly and reached out his large hand to grab Qilin and the other two.

The Qilin and the Black Dragon were shocked. They were no match for a sixth-tier demon, and they couldn't even escape.

But at that very moment, the ground began to tremble, and the primordial energy overflowed from underground, quickly gathering into a semi-circular protective shield that enveloped the entire Thousand Mountain Town.

The Wind-Stabilizing Pearl has been activated, and the protective array has been initiated!

Upon seeing this situation, the Blade Demon's eyes flashed with surprise and doubt.

"Hmph, I didn't expect this small town to be protected by an array. But before me, it's all just bubbles, easily shattered!"

The Blade Demon scoffed, his massive hand reaching out without hesitation to grab at the protective array, intending to crush it.

But the moment his large hand touched the great formation, his expression suddenly changed drastically.

"Not good!" The Blade Demon's large hand reflexively withdrew quickly.

But it was too late. The protective array emitted a dazzling, dark yellow light, giving a strong counterattack to the sword demon's invasion.

A reaction force struck the sword demon directly, with the same force as his attack, instantly sending him flying dozens of meters away, his body staggering.

The sudden scene shocked everyone present.

"What happened? The Blade Demon was blocked?"

Qian Qu looked incredulous, wondering if he was hallucinating due to his serious injuries.

A sixth-order demon, a being that could almost run rampant in the Great Chu territory, was actually blocked by an array.

Wu Cheng was also shocked and didn't understand what had happened.

Hua Wuzei and Yang Youshui showed joy and breathed a sigh of relief.

This time they made the right bet.

The Qilin and the Black Dragon looked at each other in bewilderment.

Their first reaction was that the mountain god had appeared, but the commotion didn't seem like it. Upon closer inspection, they realized that the formation had blocked the attack of the sixth-tier sword demon.

Just as the two were confused about the situation, Huo Feng arrived with Bai Liang and the others.

"Qilin, Master Bai and Master Wu are here!" Fire Phoenix shouted.

The group descended from the sky and came to the side of Qilin and the other person.

Bai Liang frowned and looked outside the formation. Qilin then explained the situation.

There was a group of Yin God Realm cultivators being chased by a sixth-tier demon, who then lured this sixth-tier sword demon to their Thousand Mountain Town.

If it weren't for the formation, he and the black dragon would have died here.

Finally, Qilin said, "Master Bai, there's a guy over there named Hua Wuzei who claims to be your brother-in-law and asked me to let him in, but I didn't."

"Hua Wuzei?" Bai Liang was taken aback, and then he saw his brother-in-law's figure outside the formation.

When Hua Wuzei saw Bai Liang appear, his face lit up with joy, and he quickly waved and shouted.

"Brother-in-law?" Upon seeing this, Bai Liang hurriedly called for Hua Wuzei to come in quickly.

Taking advantage of the moment when the Blade Demon was repelled by the formation, Hua Wuzei and his companion quickly flew to the edge of the formation.

Bai Liang manipulated the formation, allowing the two of them to enter.

"Wait, we haven't gone in yet!"

Wu Cheng shouted in a panic and he and Qian Qu rushed to the formation.

The Blade Demon behind had recovered and raised his hand to unleash more than a dozen blade energies, sparing no effort to cut Wu Cheng and the other two into dust.

“Bai Liang, Brother Wu is my brother-in-law’s colleague, and the other one is a Taoist priest from Xuanqing Temple. They can’t be considered bad people,” Hua Wuzei quickly said.

Bai Liang nodded, for in the face of demons, humanity's righteousness comes first.

Since they are all human and not particularly wicked, it's only natural to help if there's an opportunity.

He manipulated the formation, opening a gap.

Upon seeing this, Wu Cheng and his companion quickly crawled inside, and the gap closed swiftly after they entered.

The very next second after the two entered the formation, more than a dozen blades of light arrived in succession, striking the formation and making a crackling metallic sound.

The formation shone with a dark yellow light, blocking all the sword flashes.

Upon seeing this, Wu Cheng and the others breathed a sigh of relief. Although they were incredibly shocked by the powerful formation here, they didn't have the energy to think about it too much. They all collapsed to the ground, panting heavily.

Outside the formation, the Blade Demon saw that everyone was hiding inside the formation, and his eyes almost spit fire.

He was furious and unleashed a terrifying force, sending countless blades slashing at the formation with tremendous force, but they could not shake the formation in the slightest!

The Blade Demon was utterly shocked. How could such a powerful formation possibly appear here?

He suspected that there were powerful beings above the Yin God level in Qianshan Town, otherwise how could he explain the current situation?

"Good! Very good! You humans dare to break the covenant with the Heavenly Water, then prepare to bear the wrath of us demons!" the Blade Demon roared.

far away.

Isn't this just using him as a tool?

Inside the formation.

From their arrival in Qingzhou City, to their capture of the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect, to their escape by the Holy Maiden, they were then coerced by hidden masters from the Golden Dragon Temple and forced to come to Liaoyun County.

All of this is because of the monks at Jinlong Temple.

Logically speaking, someone with such strength shouldn't be afraid of a sixth-tier sword demon.

"Brother-in-law, do you know why these bald monks have their eyes on our Qianshan Town?" Bai Liang suddenly asked.

Bai Liang was even more confused after hearing this.

"You came all the way to Qianshan Town from the start? Why?"


Chapter 163 The Demonic Thoughts Take Flight, Li Yue Awakens

News of Qianshan Town should, in theory, only circulate within Qingzhou.

Even if they knew, it was impossible for a powerhouse above the Yin God level to break the Heavenly Water Pact and leave the capital for Qingzhou.

There must be another reason.

Wu Ze, standing nearby, speculated, "Could it be because of the death of Monk Sangui? Jinlong Temple has decided to seek revenge?"

After Monk Sangui's death, they had been on guard against Jinlong Temple causing trouble.

Having been tricked by the bald monks of Jinlong Temple, he was filled with resentment!

Having experienced the previous incident where the Three Refuges Monk colluded with demons, he naturally believed that the monks of Jinlong Temple were capable of such depravity.

But now they've gone mad. The Three Refuges Monk's collusion with demons was always done in secret.

Now they're openly colluding with the Sword Demon, wanting to jointly attack Qianshan Town?

If word gets out of this, even if Jinlong Temple is the number one Buddhist sect in the world, it will definitely suffer a huge impact!

Fight your way out? What a joke.

One is a little novice whose strength is unknown, and the other is a sixth-tier Blade Demon. Which one can they handle?

Meanwhile, in Baijiang Prefecture.

In the blood-soaked ruins, a black command arrow fell into the ground.

The alluring Nianmo opened her eyes and caught the command arrow directly.

After crushing it, her expression suddenly changed.

"Qingzhou, cultivators of the fifth rank or higher? The human race has violated the Heavenly Water Pact!?"

This message had two meanings: one, it asked her to help relay a message back to the Holy Court; two, it requested her assistance.

They wanted her to go to Qingzhou to resolve this thorny problem together.

"To go? Or not to go?" Nianmo was conflicted.

Mainly, she had previously sensed that something had changed in Qingzhou, and now it seemed her premonition was correct.

Not going would naturally be the best option.

But the appearance of a fifth-rank or higher expert among the human race had already violated the Heavenly Water Pact, and the news would inevitably have to be relayed back to the Holy Court.

The Holy Court, upon learning this, would surely take action.

If the Holy Court found out that the Blade Demon had sought her help and she had refused, she would certainly be punished.

"Damn Blade Demon, why did you go to Qingzhou? And drag me into this!"

The Mind Demon was furious.

But ultimately, she had no choice but to set off for Qingzhou.

Before departing, she activated her command arrow, transmitting the situation the Blade Demon had described back to the Holy Court.

The Holy Court would receive news of the human race's breach of the Heavenly Water Pact within about a day.

Outside Thousand Mountains Town.

The Blade Demon was now prepared, unleashing all her power in a relentless attack on the great formation.

Countless sharp blade energies slashed towards the formation, merging together as they approached, condensing into a gigantic ink blade.

The ink blade was incredibly sharp, producing a tearing sound as it sliced ​​through the air, as if cleaving through space itself.

With a deafening roar, accompanied by ripples spreading in all directions, the ink-black blade slammed down upon the great formation.

The people within the Mustard Seed Buddha Kingdom stared intently at the formation.

They noticed that the formation had momentarily cracked open the instant the ink-black blade struck.

The crack was small, appeared for a very short time, and was instantly repaired.

"It works! Blade Demon, continue the attack!" Sanzhi exclaimed with delight.

The Blade Demon's expression shifted, and he continued to exert all his strength, unleashing his full blade energy to slash at the formation.

Bai Liang and the others within the formation watched the Blade Demon's attack, their brows furrowed.

Qian Qu glanced at the golden light behind the Blade Demon, suddenly realizing something, and his expression drastically changed.

"Hiss! Mustard Seed Buddha Kingdom! It's the Mustard Seed Buddha Kingdom! One of the most famous Dharma gates of the Golden Dragon Temple! Oh no, this is a huge problem!" Qian Qu's voice was filled with disbelief.

They hadn't expected the Golden Dragon Temple to actually bring out this Dharma gate; they were truly determined to take over Qianshan Town.

"The Mustard Seed Buddha Kingdom!?" Wu Cheng and the others' expressions changed drastically upon hearing this.

Only Bai Liang and Wu Ze were somewhat confused, not understanding what the Mustard Seed Buddha Kingdom was.

Hua Wuze quickly explained to Bai Liang, and after listening, Bai Liang and the others suddenly understood, finally realizing what the Mustard Seed Buddha Kingdom was.

Wu Ze's face darkened. He said, "They want the Blade Demon to attack the formation, creating a gap, and then use the Mustard Seed Buddhist Kingdom to enter Thousand Mountain Town through that gap!?"

"That's right. Those bald monks are incredibly insidious; they actually cooperated with demons. If I survive this, I will definitely return to the capital and report this to His Majesty!" Wu Cheng said coldly.

The Blade Demon's attacks continued, but not every attack could create a brief gap in the formation.

Therefore, he could only keep trying, searching for an opportunity for the Mustard Seed Buddhist Kingdom of Golden Dragon Temple.

On the sacred mountain, Li Yue's divine sense was immersed in the天地 (heaven and earth).

When comprehending the laws, there was no concept of time. He felt as if only a minute had passed, yet also as if hundreds of years had gone by.

The more he comprehended the development of this world, the more immersed Li Yue became.

Because he had made a significant discovery, a discovery no less significant than the fall of the blood-red meteorite.

However, something seemed to be happening outside; the banging sounds made him extremely irritated.

"It seems like someone is striking the protective array formed by the Wind-Stabilizing Pearl?"

Li Yue sensed something.

The initial taps weren't very forceful; to the Wind-Stabilizing Pearl, it was just a tickle.

But the more it tapped, the more energetic it became, eventually using its full strength, and the tickle turned into a severe gua sha (scratching).

Although this level of attack couldn't break through the Wind-Stabilizing Pearl's defenses, it was already disrupting Li Yue's comprehension.

So Li Yue became somewhat angry.


Chapter 164 Immovable as a Mountain, Yet Unleashing Thunder

Li Yue's divine sense awoke from his comprehension of the laws of heaven and earth.

The instant he awoke, he swiftly scanned everything within the divine mountain's range, immediately noticing the scene in Thousand Mountain Town.

The Wind-Stabilizing Pearl hovered beneath the ground, radiating a dazzling, profound yellow light.

This light penetrated the earth, transforming into a massive array that enveloped the entire Thousand Mountain Town, perfectly protecting it.

Outside the array, a demonic creature exuding a violent aura was frantically attacking it.

Each attack caused the array to tremble, emitting a buzzing sound.

Not far from the demonic creature, there was a cluster of objects radiating golden Buddhist light. Li Yue's divine sense swept over it and discovered several tiny figures hiding inside.

Were these the things that were disturbing his comprehension of the laws of heaven and earth?

Li Yue was extremely displeased.

And just as his divine sense revived...

The Blade Demon seemed to sense something—a tremor emanating from his very soul, as if a supreme being were looking down upon him from above, filling him with dread.

"What's going on? What just happened? Was it an illusion?" The Blade Demon stopped attacking, his expression hesitant.

Within the Mustard Seed Buddha Kingdom, Sanzhi and the others were puzzled by the Blade Demon's sudden halt.

"Blade Demon, what are you standing there for? We're about to break the formation! Why aren't you attacking again!"

With their patriarch present, Sanwu's tone hardened as he urged the Blade Demon on.

Because they were hidden within the Mustard Seed Buddha Kingdom, they hadn't sensed a divine sense sweeping over them.

Only the little novice frowned, vaguely sensing something amiss.

"Didn't you sense anything wrong?"

The Blade Demon's face darkened, his voice cold.

"Something wrong? What's wrong? Are you thinking of backing down?" Sanzhi asked, frowning.

Hidden within the Mustard Seed Buddha Kingdom, they felt nothing.

Therefore, they suspected the Blade Demon was lying.

“Nothing?” Seeing that the monks' expressions didn't seem fake, the Blade Demon wondered if he was imagining things.

Indeed, that feeling just now was terrifying; he had only ever experienced it with the Demon Saint and the Demon Venerable.

Humans couldn't possibly possess such a powerful being, otherwise, this situation wouldn't exist today.

“Looks like I'm overthinking it.” The Blade Demon shook his head, then continued attacking the great formation.

Under his continuous attacks, the great formation had easily developed cracks.

However, the cracks appeared only for a fleeting moment. Therefore, it wasn't enough for the Mustard Seed Buddha Kingdom to seize the opportunity to enter; he needed to hold out a little longer.

Inside the formation.

Bai Liang and the others all looked at the Blade Demon, who was relentlessly attacking the formation, with grave expressions.

In this situation, they didn't know what to do.

“If this continues, the formation will eventually reveal a flaw, giving them an opportunity to use the Mustard Seed Buddhist Kingdom to infiltrate Thousand Mountain Town.”

Qian Qu realized that although the formation was strong, the Mustard Seed Buddhist Kingdom was one of the Golden Dragon Temple's top-tier techniques—it was no joke.

Wu Ze and Bai Liang exchanged a glance; they had already made their decision.

If the formation truly couldn't hold them off, then they would have no choice but to let Li Xue intervene.

“Aren't you afraid?” Bai Liang patted Li Xue's head tenderly.

Li Xue simply smiled slightly and shook her head, saying, “No, fighting for the Divine Mountain is my responsibility!”

It's simply a matter of sacrificing another fifty years of her lifespan; if that's not enough, add another twenty.

She's currently at the Profound Realm, with a lifespan of two Jiazi (60-year cycles), or one hundred and twenty years.

The previous sacrifice consumed fifteen years of her lifespan, so roughly calculated, Li Xue still has over ninety years left.

Sacrificing seventy years of her lifespan leaves her with only twenty years.

Wu Ze remained silent, clenching his fists.

If he were stronger, he wouldn't have needed to send this young girl, Li Xue, to step forward.

Lifespan is irreversible.

Li Yue's divine sense hovered high in the sky, coldly watching the Blade Demon relentlessly attack the formation.

Just now, he scanned the area below with his divine sense and finally understood the situation.

A sixth-tier demonic creature, and that Buddhist supernatural power, the Mustard Seed Space.

They do have some brains, trying to use this to break the formation and enter Thousand Mountain Town?

But that's all they'll do. Do they really think he, the Mountain God, doesn't exist?

Without hesitation, Li Yue unleashed his Dharma Body, causing the sacred mountain to tremble instantly. Seven-colored rays of light descended from the sky, illuminating its summit.

A towering figure rose from the mountain, like a celestial being descending to cleanse the world of its filth.

This extraordinary phenomenon immediately stunned everyone in Thousand Mountain Town.

Qian Qu and the others paled, feeling an irresistible pressure that made them want to prostrate themselves.

They were utterly terrified. What was happening? Could it be that a Saint-level demon had attacked?

Bai Liang and the others, seeing the phenomenon on the sacred mountain, were first stunned, then overjoyed.

"The sacred mountain! It's the sacred mountain! The mountain god has manifested!" Du Tiandao shouted excitedly.

Wu Ze trembled with excitement, his emotions beyond words.

"The Mountain God has manifested His power! Thousand Mountain Town is safe!"

Bai Liang beamed with joy, his tense nerves instantly relaxing.

The three Qilin brothers had never witnessed the Mountain God's manifestation before, but having drunk the divine water, they already held him in immense reverence.

Now, seeing the Mountain God appear, their faces were filled with excitement.

Hua Wuzei and Yang Youshui exchanged a glance, their eyes filled with shock.

They had gambled correctly this time; this incomparably powerful aura was enough to prove the Mountain God's strength.

Facing the sixth-tier Blade Demon and the experts from Golden Dragon Temple, it was a piece of cake.

Most of the residents of Thousand Mountain Town were unaware that their town had just faced a crisis.

They only saw the strange phenomenon appearing on the divine mountain—the towering golden giant.

The new residents, still not understanding what was happening, were stunned into silence.

The old residents already knew this was the Mountain God's manifestation, and they all excitedly knelt down towards the sacred mountain, their eyes sincere and devout.

"The Mountain God has manifested once again! Everyone, come and worship!"

An old man shouted, pulling a young boy to his knees in worship.

"This humble subject begs the Mountain God to protect my grandson and ensure his safety throughout his life..."

The boy, his head pressed down by the old man, kowtowed several times, then looked up with wide, curious eyes at the golden giant on the sacred mountain.

Similar scenes began to appear throughout Thousand Mountain Town.

Outside Thousand Mountain Town.

The Blade Demon, who had just attacked the formation, looked at the sacred mountain in disbelief. The golden giant emitted rays of golden light that reached the heavens, truly like a god.

The Blade Demon felt a chill run down his spine, standing frozen in place.

It wasn't that he didn't want to move, but rather that an incomparably sacred aura locked onto him, rendering him immobile.

"This...what is this thing? Has a saint appeared among humans?!"

The Blade Demon's eyes were filled with deep shock as he swallowed hard.

Even now, he couldn't believe that such a terrifying being existed here.

That soul-piercing oppression—was that the might of the saint?


Chapter 165 Awe-inspiring throughout the world

The Blade Demon couldn't believe it, believing that a sage had appeared among the human race.

No wonder he dared to disregard the Heavenly Water Pact; he finally had the confidence to confront the demons.

In the Mustard Seed Buddha Kingdom, Sanzhi and the others were already speechless with shock.

They were even more shocked than the Blade Demon, because they had no idea what this golden giant before them was.

Human? That might not be it.

"Divine Mountain, Mountain God, there really is a deity here!"

Sanzhi's eyelids twitched, fear creeping into his heart.

Qiu He opened his mouth, countless words of pleading flashing through his mind, along with how he would beg for mercy.

"Grandmaster, what is going on? Wasn't it said that there are no more gods in the world?" Sanwu asked tremulously.

The little novice monk's heart was in turmoil.

As an eighth-level Buddha, he was more shocked than anyone else present.

While others merely doubted whether the golden giant before them was a deity, he was absolutely certain it was!

A true deity!

A deity actually exists in this world?!

The young novice was beginning to doubt the veracity of the rumors about the severing of the heavens and earth recorded in the Golden Dragon Temple.

If the severing of the heavens and earth were truly possible, how could a god have escaped?

"I don't know either, but I can confirm it's a true deity!"

The young novice slowly shook his head; he didn't understand either.

Sanzhi and the others were utterly shocked upon hearing this.

A true deity?!

"Hiss! A true god has descended to earth; this is about to usher in a new era!" Sanzhi exclaimed in amazement.

Sanwu, however, looked grim. "It's over," he said. "We came here to cause trouble for the Divine Mountain, and we even joined forces with the Blade Demon. Now that the Mountain God has manifested, he certainly won't let us go. Grandmaster, let's escape quickly and let the Blade Demon hold them off."

"Yes, Senior! We should escape quickly!" Qiu He said hastily.

"Too late, we can't escape." The young novice shook his head, his expression grim.

"The celestial connection here has been locked. Unless the true body of the original body arrives personally, it cannot be broken."

"Ah? What do we do then?" Sanwu and the others panicked.

"Wait to die."

The young novice said calmly.

He was currently only a reincarnated body; even if he died, the loss wouldn't be significant.

It was just that he hadn't obtained the secret manual, which he found incredibly regrettable.

That was the key to becoming a saint!

Upon hearing the words "wait to die," Sanzhi and the others instantly turned ashen.

A golden giant, imbued with supreme divine might, leaped down from the divine mountain, landing directly outside Thousand Mountain Town.

The ground trembled upon landing, emitting a dull thud.

Li Yue, manipulating his Dharma Body, stared indifferently at Dao Mo and the others.

“Senior… I meant no offense. I was truly blind to the divine mountain. I hope you will forgive my rudeness. I am but a lowly general under the Holy Court's Demon Lord. I hope you will show leniency for the Demon Lord's sake.”

Dao Mo's voice trembled slightly as he forced a smile.

He could only hope that they feared the Holy Court and the Demon Lord, and would spare his life.

“Demon Lord? What trash.”

Li Yue spoke in a deep voice, his voice resounding through the heavens, accompanied by rolling thunder.

He was already furious about being disturbed during his meditation.

Therefore, regardless of their background or status, he would not let them go.

The golden giant took a single step, kicking up dust like a sandstorm that hurtled towards the Blade Demon.

The Blade Demon's expression changed drastically. He hadn't expected the giant to disregard even the Demon Lord's dignity, calling him trash.

He was both shocked and furious, but before he could beg for mercy, the sandstorm created by the golden giant's step completely engulfed him.

The Blade Demon roared, unleashing all his power to try and defend himself, but how could a firefly's light compete with the moon's brilliance?

Instantly, he was blasted dozens of meters away by the sandstorm, his demonic body becoming unstable and beginning to leak demonic energy.

The onlookers, witnessing this scene, all twitched their eyelids, their expressions filled with shock.

The dust kicked up by a single step had sent the Blade Demon flying dozens of meters.

Just how terrifying was this power?

Then, the golden giant attacked again, this time directly reaching out its massive hand to grab the Blade Demon.

The Blade Demon coughed up a mouthful of demonic blood, unable to even rise from the ground, forced to watch helplessly as it was lifted into the air by a giant hand.

"Spare...spare my life..."

The Blade Demon tried to beg for mercy again, but Li Yue didn't even glance at it, crushing it instantly. The Blade Demon was dead!

With this demon dead, it was the monks hiding in the Mustard Seed Buddha Kingdom's turn next.

Sanzhi and the others were pale; they had witnessed the Blade Demon's fate.

He was completely powerless to resist; it was utterly hopeless.

"Your Excellency, we are human, not demons. We beg Your Excellency to show mercy," Qiu He hurriedly pleaded, trying to find a way to survive.

But how could Li Yue possibly believe him?

With his Mind Mirror, nothing could escape his notice, especially since this place was within the range of his divine sense. How could he not know what had happened?

"Get out of here, don't embarrass yourself." The young novice frowned, shoving away Qiu He, who was trying to kneel and beg for mercy.

He looked up at the golden giant, took a deep breath, and stepped out of the Mustard Seed Buddhist Kingdom.

"I've cultivated for hundreds of years, and this is the first time I've seen a true deity with my own eyes. Today has truly opened my eyes." The young novice clasped his hands together, staring intently at Li Yue's golden body.

"Hmm? An avatar?"

As soon as the young novice stepped out of the Mustard Seed Buddhist Kingdom, Li Yue noticed something was amiss.

His physical body was only at the peak of the fifth-order Yin God Realm, but his true spirit was incredibly powerful, at least at the seventh order or higher.

"Your Excellency's eyes are sharp; it's just a small trick." The young novice said slowly.

Li Yue didn't bother to say more. So what if it's at the seventh order or higher? He'd kill it without hesitation!

Seeing the golden giant move, the young novice's expression turned solemn.

He knew he couldn't contend with the mountain god, but he wanted to use this opportunity to test the limits of this deity.

"A grain of sand, a world."

The young novice monk formed a hand seal, and in an instant, countless grains of sand fell from the sky, swirling around the golden giant.

Each grain of sand contained boundless spatial power, like a star, holding a world within it.

With a low shout from the young novice monk, the sand began to fly towards Li Yue.

Li Yue observed the young novice monk's abilities, thinking that he was indeed somewhat skilled, but only somewhat.

He simply raised his hand, absorbing countless grains of sand into his palm.

The sand was heavy; each grain was equivalent to a small world, its weight almost bending the golden giant's wrist.

As Li Yue tightened his grip, countless grains of sand instantly turned to dust.

The young novice monk's expression changed drastically upon seeing this.

"A World Within a Grain of Sand" is the supreme divine power of the Golden Dragon Temple. Although its full power cannot be unleashed until one reaches the Saint Realm, its ease in defeating it is simply too exaggerated.

Before he could even utter a sound of astonishment, the golden giant's attacks followed in quick succession.

The physical body of a fifth-tier Yin God Realm cultivator was utterly unable to resist and was instantly smashed to pieces.

The Mustard Seed Buddha Kingdom also shattered instantly, and the three people hiding inside fell out.


Chapter 166 Even the True Spirit Must Be Preserved!

The young novice's physical body was smashed, and a wisp of pale golden light flew out from within, containing Buddhist and Taoist truths, attempting to break free.

Li Yue sensed this light and was slightly surprised.

This pale golden light could actually break through his divine sense's seal and try to escape.

"This is a True Spirit?"

Li Yue immediately realized what it was—a True Spirit, something that only cultivators of the seventh rank or higher could possess.

Compared to the Primordial Spirit, the True Spirit is purer and conforms to the ultimate principles of Heaven.

As long as the True Spirit is immortal, it will never die. It can even extend its life through reincarnation and other means.

The young novice's True Spirit was not complete; only a small part of it remained.

Li Yue naturally wouldn't let this True Spirit escape. Since it was already here, he might as well keep it!

The golden giant reached out its right hand and, in the blink of an eye, grasped the golden True Spirit in its hand. The True Spirit struggled desperately, emitting a dazzling light, trying to break free.

But all his struggles were futile; the golden hand remained unmoved, like a mountain.

Seeing he couldn't break free, the True Spirit grew anxious and began to emit light, sending out a wave of divine thought.

The gist of it was that he was one of the two founders of the Golden Dragon Temple, the foremost Buddhist sect in the world.

An eighth-tier Venerable Realm expert; his senior brother was a half-saint in Buddhism, only one step away from the Saint Realm.

He was also a top-tier human warrior, belonging to the same side as the old master of Xuanqing Temple and the emperor of the Great Chu Dynasty.

He was telling Li Yue to leave him a way out, so they could meet again in the future.

Even if you are a god, gods are not invincible.

If you destroy this part of his True Spirit, it will be a complete break with the capital!

Li Yue felt the True Spirit's consciousness fluctuations and almost laughed in anger.

You brought people to Thousand Mountain Town to cause trouble, and then you even cooperated with demons to attack the protective formation.

Now, instead of letting him go, they've even brought up several semi-saints from the capital—are they trying to threaten him?

Unfortunately, Li Yue is resistant to threats, especially soft ones.

Ignoring the intense fluctuations in the true spirit's consciousness, the golden giant's eyes were indifferent. With a forceful swipe of its hand, it crushed the golden true spirit on the spot.

"No! You're heading down a dead end!"

The golden true spirit let out a defiant roar before its annihilation.

Meanwhile, in the capital.

The Golden Dragon Temple, covering hundreds of acres, boasts various magnificent Buddhist halls, resplendent in gold, like a celestial palace, utterly sacred.

In one of the innermost halls, an old monk, cross-legged and with his eyes closed, suddenly opened them.

A flicker of pain flashed in his cloudy eyes.

"No! My true spirit!"

The old monk gritted his teeth; the pain of having his true spirit annihilated was unbearable for ordinary people.

Cold sweat beaded on his forehead, his heart filled with hatred for the mountain god who had attacked him.

"Damn it! This wild god who appeared out of nowhere dared to destroy my true spirit! He has already committed suicide!" the old monk thought bitterly.

The true spirit is extremely important to cultivators.

Especially for a powerful eighth-tier Venerable like him, to achieve the Saint realm, he must maintain a perfect true spirit.

Now that a part of his true spirit is missing, he has no chance of reaching the Saint realm.

Furthermore, he hadn't obtained the key secret manual for becoming a Saint. This trip to Qingzhou had been a huge loss.

The old monk's heart bled, filled with resentment and anger, a venomous glint flashing in his eyes.

"So this is a divine mountain! It annihilates my true spirit! Just you wait!"

With that, he vanished from the spot, going to inform the other half-saints.

Outside Thousand Mountain Town.

After obliterating the golden true spirit, Li Yue's gaze turned to the remaining three.

"Your Excellency, I am innocent! I am not a monk from Golden Dragon Temple, but from the prefectural governor of Qingzhou! Please, Your Excellency, forgive my ignorance and spare my life!"

Qiu He was terrified. With the powerful figures of Golden Dragon Temple wiped out, they had no power to resist.

San Zhi and the other two, drenched in cold sweat, also knelt on the ground, begging for their lives.

What is backbone in the face of life and death?

The golden giant coldly swept his gaze over the three, completely ignoring their pleas for mercy. He mercilessly raised his massive hand and, amidst the three's terrified and desperate gazes, slowly brought it down.

"No...!"

Before they could even scream, the three were reduced to a pile of mush by the golden giant's massive hand.

The Yin Spirits fled, but Li Yue was prepared and struck again, crushing the three Yin Spirits in mid-air.

Thus, the enemies who had invaded Thousand Mountain Town were completely annihilated!

Those within the formation, witnessing this scene firsthand, were all utterly stunned.

Their faces were filled with horror, their eyes wide with disbelief.

Bai Liang and the others, however, were incredibly excited, their gazes towards the golden giant filled with reverence.

"Truly worthy of the Mountain God, easily annihilating the invading enemies."

Wu Ze exclaimed in amazement, yet also felt a pang of shame.

He hadn't expected that in the end, it was still the Mountain God's divine protection.

Ultimately, their strength was too weak; they couldn't even protect themselves.

The three Qilin brothers were filled with shock and awe.

This was the first time they had witnessed the Mountain God in action. His overwhelming divine power, sweeping across the world, effortlessly annihilated a sixth-tier Blade Demon and a powerful expert from the Golden Dragon Temple.

At this moment, they all felt that choosing to pledge allegiance to the Divine Mountain was the best decision they had ever made!

With such a powerful deity's protection, wasn't this a hundred times, a thousand times, stronger than the so-called imperial fortune?

"Qilin, thank goodness we listened to your suggestion in the end, otherwise we wouldn't have come to the Divine Mountain," the Black Dragon said excitedly.

Initially, they had two choices: one was to pledge allegiance to the Dragon-Slaying Sect, as suggested by the Fire Phoenix; the other was the Divine Mountain, as mentioned by the Qilin.

However, at first, they didn't know much about the Divine Mountain, only that they had heard rumors about it while in Qingzhou.

So they decided to go to the Divine Mountain first to see what was going on. If it was suitable, they would stay. If not, they would go abroad to pledge allegiance to the Dragon-Slaying Sect.

Qilin was incredibly excited. He admitted that there was an element of gambling involved in his initial decision.

But the result proved that he had gambled correctly.

This sacred mountain not only boasts abundant spiritual energy and divine waters, but also various extraordinary spiritual substances. More importantly, it is protected by a deity, making it superior to any other place.

Hua Wuzei and the others were speechless with shock.

Wu Cheng swallowed hard.

This was the first time he had ever seen a living deity. The terrifying divine power he exuded was more oppressive than even when facing His Majesty.

No wonder Hua Wuzei had rushed here in the first place; he knew from the beginning that a deity was protecting this place.

Thinking back to his foolish attempt to capture Qilin and his companions, Wu Cheng felt a chill run down his spine.

Thankfully, the other party didn't pursue the matter, or he would have been in grave danger.

Qian Qu's mind was in turmoil. No wonder the divination he had received was so strange.

It turned out this place was inhabited by a deity.

His understanding was overturned. The old master had said that in ancient times, an ancient sage severed the connection between heaven and earth, and since then, there had been no more deities in the world.

But what was happening now?


Chapter 167 Gathering of Fortune, Passing on the Flame

Qian Qu couldn't fathom the deity's strength, but it was at least at the semi-saint level.

Such immense power would undoubtedly have a significant impact on the current situation.

After dealing with the invading enemies, Li Yue dispelled the Dharma Body, allowing the golden giant to slowly dissipate.

Seeing the golden giant vanish, Bai Liang and the others immediately knelt respectfully towards the sacred mountain.

"Disciples thank the Mountain God for His divine protection!"

The three Qilin also knelt.

Hua Wuzei and Yang Youshui hesitated for a moment, then promptly knelt, thanking the Mountain God for saving their lives.

Wu Cheng and Qian Qu felt somewhat awkward, but ultimately knelt sincerely, expressing their gratitude to the Mountain God.

The residents of Qianshan Town were even more excited, bowing in thanks.

The Mountain God's divine intervention was rare; to them, it was an auspicious omen.

Li Yue's divine sense returned to the sacred mountain and witnessed this scene in Qianshan Town.

In an instant, his merit points surged. Having not checked his panel for a long time, he was somewhat astonished upon glancing at it.

This one immersion in comprehending the laws of heaven and earth had increased his merit points by over three million?

[Realm: Fourth-Order Sacred Mountain]

[Merit Points: 3.41 million/10 million]

[Divine Abilities: Mountain God's Blessing, Dharma Body of Heaven and Earth, Resumption of Incense Offerings, Gratitude for the Source of Water]

[Spell: Manifestation]

[Magic Artifacts: Mountain-Suppressing Seal, Heart Mirror, Divine Weapon Forging Furnace, Wind-Stabilizing Pearl]

Seeing so many merit points, Li Yue's first thought was how long he had been immersed in comprehension.

Scanning his divine sense across Thousand Mountain Town, he knew how much time had passed.

Only a few months.

The rapid increase in merit points was due to the development of Thousand Mountain Town.

When he was immersed in comprehension, the population of Thousand Mountain Town was only around sixty to seventy thousand.

The population has now surpassed 100,000, reaching over 100,000.

This large population allows him to accumulate a significant amount of merit points daily.

And the growth rate is still increasing. It's estimated that in another six months, he will reach ten million merit points and advance to the fifth-tier Holy Mountain.

In Thousand Mountains Town.

The manifestation of the Mountain God has filled everyone with excitement.

The somber mood caused by the appearance of the sixth-tier Blade Demon has long since vanished, replaced by excitement and exhilaration.

Bai Liang and his companions immediately went to the temple, offering incense and gratitude with utmost respect.

They also reported on the various developments, large and small, in Thousand Mountains Town over the past few months.

Bai Liang knew the Mountain God wouldn't concern himself with these matters, but he couldn't leave them uninformed.

Qilin and his two companions stood nervously outside the temple.

To Thousand Mountains Town, they were newcomers.

Before, when the Mountain God was in hiding, they could freely enter the temple without any qualms.

However, the manifestation of the Mountain God gave them a sense of oppression when they looked at the temple, filling them with immense tension.

Finally, Wu Ze pulled them inside before they tentatively stepped into the temple.

Then, following Bai Liang and the others, they offered incense and kowtowed.

After paying their respects to the Mountain God, it was time to deal with other matters.

The Wu Cheng group was settled in the Mountain Pavilion, temporarily hosted by He Liyuan.

With the Mountain God's manifestation before them, these individuals, though only at the Yin God Realm, dared not act rashly.

Moreover, Hua Wuzei was on Bai Liang's side.

Inside the Mountain Pavilion.

The atmosphere was somewhat silent. Qian Qu and the other Yin God Realm cultivators exchanged glances, not daring to speak.

The main reason was the suffocating pressure emanating from the Mountain God.

Without a doubt, the Mountain God could see and hear their every move in Thousand Mountain Town.

This caused even the usually talkative Yin God Realm cultivators to become quiet.

"Um... Brother Hua, when did you learn about Thousand Mountain Town?"

Wu Cheng broke the silence first, looking at Hua Wuzei and asking.

"Chasing the rebels of the Dragon-Slaying Sect, I unexpectedly came here. It just so happens that the person in charge here is my brother-in-law, so naturally I learned about it," Hua Wuzei said casually.

He even seemed a little proud when he mentioned that the person in charge was his brother-in-law.

"I see. Brother Hua is truly blessed to have such an extraordinary brother-in-law."

Wu Cheng said, a hint of envy in his tone.

This roundabout way of connecting with a deity could be considered as such.

That's a deity, perhaps even the only one in the world. To be able to establish a connection with him is truly enviable.

Qian Qu also looked at Hua Wuzei, feeling a similar envy.

Regardless of the mountain god's origins, He is ultimately a deity.

At this moment, the door was pushed open, and Bai Liang and the others walked in.

"Brother-in-law, we've kept you waiting," Bai Liang said with a smile.

"Not at all, we're the ones who troubled you. If it weren't for the protection of the sacred mountain, we would have perished in Qingzhou this time," Hua Wuzei said, standing up.

After exchanging a few pleasantries, Bai Liang and his companions took their seats.

"You are all at the Yin God Realm. What are your thoughts on our Thousand Mountain Town?"

Bai Liang looked at Wu Cheng and the others and asked.

"We wouldn't dare! How could we possibly comment on a deity's subordinate?" Qian Qu quickly shook his head.

Wu Cheng also waved his hand, afraid of saying something wrong and being overheard by the mountain god—that would be disastrous.

"It's alright, please speak your minds,"

Bai Liang continued.

Seeing Bai Liang's insistence, Qian Qu and the others exchanged glances, and finally Wu Cheng spoke:

"When I first saw Qianshan Town, I was somewhat surprised by its prosperity, after all, just a year ago this was a wasteland. Entering the town, I was even more astonished. The spirit and energy of the residents are something I have never seen in all these years; even the people of the capital don't have such a spirit."

Qian Qu nodded in agreement, saying, "Not only is their spirit and energy abundant, but what astonishes me even more is that Qianshan Town has actually condensed a trace of fortune. This fortune is not divine fortune, but genuine human fortune, condensed from the hundreds of thousands of people of Qianshan Town. This... this is the flame of humanity!"

Qian Qu's voice trembled slightly at the end.

Human fortune, since the blood-red meteorite fell and the demons wreaked havoc, human fortune had long since died.

It only decreased, never increased, like a stagnant pool.

Every bit consumed reduces it further; when the fortune is exhausted, that will be the end of humanity.

This is one of the reasons why those in power in the capital are so disheartened.

With the fate of the nation gone, is there any hope for humanity?

Fate is the best indicator of a race's future. When fate is strong, the future will be prosperous. When fate is weak, the future will decline and perish.

The fate of the Great Chu Dynasty is a sign of decline.

Several semi-saints had tried various methods to stimulate the recovery of human fortune, but all had ultimately failed.

But now, Qian Qu had witnessed the gathering of human fortune in the small town of Qianshan.

What did this mean?

He dared not delve deeper into the question.

Upon hearing Qian Qu's words, Wu Cheng and the others who understood fortune were all shocked!

Even Bai Liang and the others, who only had a vague understanding, vaguely sensed the profound significance of this matter.


Chapter 168 Offering one's face from afar

The fate of Qianshan Town remained a mystery to everyone present.

Bai Liang and the others believed it was the work of the Mountain God, but Qian Qu and his companions disagreed.

While deities were powerful, the concept of fate was shrouded in mystery.

The Mountain God might play a role, but it couldn't have been created by him.

This topic wasn't discussed further, and the conversation quickly shifted.

The next point of discussion was that, after this battle, the Divine Mountain was now fully exposed.

It was believed that within a few days, all the major powers in the world would know of the Divine Mountain's existence.

Bai Liang looked at Wu Cheng and asked, "If the Emperor heard about the Divine Mountain, what would his reaction be?"

Wu Cheng remained silent for a long time before replying, "It's not that I'm unwilling to speak, but His Majesty is the Son of Heaven, and we, his subjects, cannot fathom His Majesty's thoughts."

Bai Liang frowned and looked at Hua Wuzei.

Hua Wuzei nodded, confirming that Wu Cheng was telling the truth.

Although the Imperial Astronomical Bureau officials were the emperor's confidants, they spent most of their time on missions, rarely seeing the emperor even once a year.

Asking them to guess the emperor's reaction was clearly impossible.

Wu Cheng thought for a moment and said, "His Majesty is capricious. Apart from the Grand Preceptor, no one can fathom his thoughts. When news from here reaches the capital, His Majesty might fly into a rage and become an enemy of the Divine Mountain. He might also be overjoyed and want to form an alliance with the Divine Mountain. Or he might remain indifferent, pretending the Divine Mountain never existed, and continue with his music and dance."

Everyone felt a little strange upon hearing this.
