Du Tiandao directly expressed his dissatisfaction: "Is this emperor so incompetent? How can he rule the Great Chu Dynasty?"

Upon hearing this, everyone except the people from Qianshan Town was ashamed and dared not respond.

"Would he have signed such a shameful treaty as the Treaty of Tianshui if he wasn't incompetent?" Black Dragon said disdainfully.

Wu Cheng frowned, unable to tolerate anyone slandering the emperor.

But this was a sacred mountain, and even if he was dissatisfied, he could only keep it to himself.

Then Bai Liang turned his gaze to Qian Qu.

Before he could ask, Qian Qu said directly, "If Xuanqing Temple knows that there are deities on the sacred mountain, they will definitely verify their authenticity first. Finally, the Old Master and the other half-saints will decide whether to be friends or enemies."

"Based on my understanding of the Old Master, he might be very interested in deities. This involves many ancient secrets, which are beyond my knowledge."

Bai Liang frowned: "So, the half-saints of Xuanqing Temple might come to the sacred mountain in person?"

"It's possible, but not certain. After all, there's the Heavenly Water Pact. Half-saints are strong, but they also fear the Demon Saint and the Demon Lord," Qian Qu sighed.

Although a half-saint carries the title of "saint," they are worlds apart from true saints.

According to legend, ancient saints, with mortal bodies, rivaled deities.

Every saint has reached the pinnacle of a great path, surpassing even some gods in power.

Some ancient saints could even defy the heavens and slay gods, staining the sky with blood.

"I think Bai Liang, you two need to be wary of the demons' reaction. The Blade Demon has already sent a message back; it's likely they'll receive news from the Divine Mountain soon. At that time, they might face extremely troublesome problems," Hua Wuzei said, his expression worried.

The demons possess absolute power, with two saints at the realm of saints.

If they learned of the unstable variable of the Divine Mountain, they would definitely take measures.

"I know that," Bai Liang said.

They had no way to prevent it.

Even a sixth-tier Blade Demon couldn't handle it, let alone the even more powerful experts sent by the demons.

However, with the Mountain God present, there shouldn't be any major problems.

It was foreseeable that the situation would become more complicated in the future, and it was hard to say whether the Heavenly Water Agreement could continue.

Bai Liang chose to see Wu Cheng and Qian Qu out.

The two breathed a sigh of relief upon being able to leave Qianshan Town.

The pressure from the Divine Mountain was simply too intense; staying there made even breathing difficult.

Hua Wuzei and Yang Youshui didn't plan to stay in Qianshan Town any longer. After resting briefly, they bid farewell to Bai Liang and left.

Meanwhile,

on the mountain road from Baijiang Prefecture to Qingzhou, Nianmo was still traveling at a leisurely pace.

She wasn't in a hurry; on the contrary, she deliberately slowed her pace.

The Blade Demon's request for help meant he had encountered an opponent he couldn't defeat.

As a fellow sixth-tier demon, if the Blade Demon couldn't handle it, then she naturally couldn't either.

Why rush to your death against an enemy you can't defeat?

"That Blade Demon better not betray me,"

Nianmo thought coldly.

After entering Qingzhou, she remained vigilant, following the direction recorded by the command arrow.

Soon she entered Liaoyun County and began to approach the Divine Mountain.

Along the way, she sensed something was amiss, and her vigilance grew stronger.

Suddenly, she felt a terrifying pressure descend upon her, causing her to break out in a cold sweat.

Without hesitation, the Nianmo turned and fled.

But it was too late. A golden hand descended, slapping her hard across her delicate face.

'Smack!'

With a crisp sound, the Nianmo's body flew hundreds of meters backward. Her right cheek, slapped by the golden hand, swelled up like a pig's head, bright red.

No time for anger; filled with terror, she burned her life force without reservation, desperately fleeing.

"Hmm? So decisive, she ran really fast. But it seems she ran in the wrong direction."

Li Yue's divine sense silently watched the Nianmo escape its range.

He felt a pang of regret; he had acted too quickly.

He should have let her get a little further in before attacking.

Given the monster's wariness, Li Yue assessed the cost-effectiveness of the pursuit and, finding it low, decided against it.

It was merely a sixth-tier monster; it couldn't cause much trouble.

During the past few dozen days of comprehending the laws of heaven and earth, Li Yue had gained a great deal.

What surprised him most was that the laws sealing off the entire world were artificially created from the inside out.

In other words, it wasn't outsiders who sealed off the world, but rather those inside who closed the door, even welding the windows shut, leaving no cracks.

The appearance of the blood-red meteorite was like someone outside smashing open a window, creating a hole.

Li Yue couldn't understand why the door was closed. Was it to prevent outsiders from entering?

Or to prevent those inside from leaving?

And who could have orchestrated such a massive undertaking?

Severing the connection between heaven and earth—what level of cultivation could achieve such a heaven-defying feat?

Li Yue, considering himself merely a fourth-tier Sacred Mountain cultivator, knew he couldn't achieve this yet.

Perhaps breaking through one or two more realms would allow him to.

More importantly, after the severing of the Heavenly Connection, it was impossible for any being of the Saint Realm to appear in this world.

Whether human, demon, or monster, none could attain the Saint Realm.

However, both the demon and monster factions had produced a Saint Realm being, which was quite thought-provoking.


Chapter 169 The capital city is in turmoil!

Li Yue guessed it was most likely related to the blood-red meteorite.

Either outsiders brought in an item capable of granting sainthood, or it was two Saint-level beings among the demons, beings not originally from this world.

Li Yue believed the latter was most likely.

Two ninth-tier Saints among the demons were extraterrestrial beings!

Why would outsiders want to come in? And bring in two Saint-level beings?

Why would those inside sever the connection between heaven and earth, isolating them from the outside world?

Li Yue couldn't understand. Too many secrets were involved. Perhaps the capital city would hold some answers.

Those few half-saints might know something.

Sweeping away his thoughts, he turned his attention back to Thousand Mountain Town.

Thousand Mountain Town was now far more developed than before, its population sufficient to support its expansion into a county town.

The town's industries were thriving, and its residents lived in peace and prosperity.

What surprised Li Yue most was the Hundred Refinement Hall.

The number of students in the Hundred Refinements Hall has now increased to over fifty, with five of them reaching the Refinement Realm.

This is undoubtedly thanks to Zhou Sanhai, whose pills have proven extremely effective in enhancing healing.

Upon learning that Zhou Sanhai had added divine water to his pills, Li Yue inwardly exclaimed, "What a genius!"

These pills are terrible! He always remembered the side effects of divine water.

Thinking of divine water, Li Yue thought of Qingquan, the pawn he had casually placed earlier.

"I wonder how well she's done with her mission. Has she spread the divine water?"

Li Yue wondered.

If Qingquan had indeed spread the divine water, he could gain a group of followers for free.

Of course, Li Yue currently looked down on ordinary followers.

Therefore, he had Qingquan target cultivators, specifically those with relatively high cultivation levels.

However, he wasn't particularly concerned about this.

Mainly, he disdained using divine water to recruit believers; after all, it was too...unseemly, somewhat like certain criminal organizations from his past life.

It was just a casual act, and he didn't care about the outcome.

However, Li Yue didn't expect that Baijiang Prefecture would be turned upside down because of his casual action.

Since drinking the divine water brought back by Qingquan, Chu Wuliu had been thinking about it day and night, deeply savoring its effects.

He held the mountain god who created the divine water in boundless reverence.

"Have you asked? When will Qingquan go to Qingzhou again?" Chu Wuliu asked his subordinate.

The subordinate bowed his head and replied, "Sir, Miss Qingquan said she'll go after she's recovered from her injuries."

"Send my black lotus to her, tell her to recover quickly and go to the sacred mountain in Qingzhou as soon as possible to pray to the mountain god for more divine water!" Chu Wuliu immediately ordered.

The subordinate was startled and hurriedly said, "Sir, that black lotus is a rare and precious medicinal herb. How can you give it to a junior like Qingquan?"

If he didn't know the prefect's temperament, he would have thought Chu Wuliu had taken a liking to Qingquan.

"That's not quite right. Young friend Qingquan now works for the Divine Mountain and is under the Mountain God's command. Giving her the black lotus is perfectly reasonable," Chu Wuliu said calmly, not finding anything wrong with it.

The subordinate was stunned. It was the Mountain God again! Ever since his lord drank the divine water, he'd been constantly talking about the Mountain God.

At first, he just called him the Mountain God, but now he was addressing him as "Lord Mountain God."

Could anyone tell him what this "Lord Mountain God" was all about?

The subordinate gritted his teeth and forced a smile, saying, "My lord, who exactly is this Mountain God that you revere so much? Ever since you drank the divine water, you've shown him such utmost respect. My lord, I believe there's something wrong with the divine water. Please go and have Master Muling examine your health."

Upon hearing this, Chu Wuliu's expression instantly darkened.

"Are you trying to teach me how to do things?" he coldly rebuked, then slapped him across the face, angrily shouting, "How dare you question the Mountain God? You want me to go find Master Muling? Do you think there's something wrong with my health?"

The subordinate, dazed from the slap, still wanted to grit his teeth and continue to persuade the prefectural magistrate, but meeting Chu Wuliu's terrifying gaze, he instantly shut his mouth.

"My lord, spare me! I was confused; I shouldn't have said such nonsense." He confessed sincerely.

"Hmph! Watch your words in the future, especially towards the Mountain God!" Chu Wuliu sneered.

The changes in Chu Wuliu were simultaneously occurring in the other Yin God Realm cultivators in Baijiang Prefecture.

To be precise, in all the Yin God Realm cultivators who had consumed the divine water.

Qian Kun was initially somewhat gloating, but now the more he looked, the more frightening and eerie it seemed.

He was somewhat afraid, and went to Qingquan to confirm again and again whether the Mountain God was indeed some kind of evil god.

Qingquan responded with absolute certainty: it wasn't an evil god, but a righteous one!

These words instantly aroused Qian Kun's suspicion.

His expression changed, and he quickly said, "Junior niece, you didn't drink the divine water too, did you?"

"No, Uncle, you're overthinking it. I'm telling the truth," Qingquan said, shaking her head.

She had been under house arrest for nearly half a year, witnessing the development of Qianshan Town step by step.

The things Bai Liang and the others did were all traceable, each one beneficial to the common people.

When promoting the mountain god, there was no brainwashing rhetoric; it was simply that if people believed in the mountain god, he would protect them.

In fact, the mountain god did deliver on this promise. Those under the mountain's protection lived peacefully, untouched by demons.

"That's good. That stuff is too evil; you absolutely mustn't touch it," Qian Kun said, still shaken.

Finally, he warned Qingquan that when he went to Qingzhou later, he absolutely must not have any contact with the people from the Divine Mountain.

Take the divine water and leave, lest he be brainwashed.

........

The capital city.

News of the Divine Mountain had already reached the capital.

The major powers in the capital were shaken, and everyone immediately questioned the veracity of the news.

A deity!

That's a deity! Something that hadn't appeared for countless years had appeared at this moment!

The imperial court immediately summoned all civil and military officials to the Golden Palace.

Officials of all ranks in the capital rushed to the palace.

In the court, all the officials gathered together.

Among them, two stood at the head. One wore a black robe, his hair and beard were completely white, his eyes closed and head bowed, exuding the air of a great scholar.

This was the head of the civil officials, Shangguan Liumo.

The other man, clad in soft armor, stood tall and imposing, his brows like swords, his eyes sharp and piercing, like a drawn blade.

This was Cha Yunpo, the head of the military officers.

The two represented two opposing factions in the court, locked in a standoff.

"Fellow colleagues, you've arrived early," a gentle voice rang out, followed by a handsome middle-aged man who slowly entered the hall.

"The Grand Preceptor has arrived."

The officials turned to look at the middle-aged man.

The Grand Preceptor was His Majesty's most trusted advisor; in many cases, the Grand Preceptor made the decisions in court.


Chapter 170 Taixu

The Imperial Advisor entered the hall and walked directly to a position half a step ahead of Shangguan Liumo and Cha Yunpo, intending to subdue them.

Shangguan Liumo's expression remained largely unchanged, merely chuckling lightly.

"I didn't expect the Imperial Advisor, at his age, to still possess such sharpness. Truly, he has devoted himself to the utmost," the scholar said, seemingly praising, but actually subtly sarcastic.

Cha Yunpo, however, was less tolerant, coldly retorting, "He's merely relying on His Majesty's favor."

Hearing their words, the Imperial Advisor smiled slightly, not responding, but instead saying, "It's better than some civil officials who dare not speak out against demons, or some military officials who dare not act against demons!"

"Yunji! What do you mean by that!" Cha Yunpo immediately flew into a rage.

Shangguan Liumo's face also instantly darkened.

The Imperial Advisor's words struck a nerve, filling both of them with shame and indignation.

Yunji smiled faintly and said, "It's nothing. Why are you all so anxious?"

"You..." Cha Yunpo's face flushed red, but this was the Golden Palace; no matter how angry he was, he couldn't possibly make a move.

In the end, he could only angrily fling his arm away, glaring at Yunji.

Shangguan Liumo's tone was gloomy as he said, "The Imperial Preceptor is extremely busy, handling all affairs of the capital. However, the capital has been rather turbulent recently. I hope the Imperial Preceptor won't make any mistakes and disappoint His Majesty's expectations."

These words were a blatant threat.

If I can't touch you, Yunji, can't I at least create trouble for you?

"That's none of your concern, sir. You're getting old; go home and retire. Give the younger generation more opportunities. Don't always try to cling to power. Don't you agree, Prime Minister?" Yunji said coldly.

"Hmph." Shangguan Liumo didn't respond, turning away and refusing to speak to Yunji again.

The emperor had repeatedly urged him to retire, but he was reluctant to relinquish his position and always refused with excuses.

A moment later, a sharp shout rang out, and everyone straightened up.

"The Emperor has arrived!"

All eyes turned to see a young man striding confidently up the platform and seated on the dragon throne.

The man's gaze was piercing as he swept over the civil and military officials, radiating an aura of imperial majesty.

"Greetings, Your Majesty! Long live the Emperor!"

The officials bowed in unison.

"No need for formalities," the Emperor said gently, gesturing for the officials to rise.

"My esteemed ministers, you are all aware of the events in Qingzhou, are you? What are your thoughts?" the Emperor asked directly.

As soon as the Emperor spoke, murmurs arose in the hall.

Then Shangguan Liumo stepped forward and said, "Your Majesty, Qingzhou is part of the territory of our Great Chu. The sacred mountain naturally belongs to the Great Chu Dynasty as well. This old minister can send an envoy to the sacred mountain to negotiate and have it guard Qingzhou for our Great Chu, resisting the demons."

Many civil officials immediately agreed.

But soon, someone objected.

"Your Majesty, this minister believes the Prime Minister's suggestion is inappropriate," Cha Yunpo stepped forward and continued, "The news of the sacred mountain has spread throughout the land. Who doesn't know that a deity exists there? Lord Shangguan's idea of ​​having a deity guard Qingzhou and protect Great Chu from demons is wishful thinking. Is that even possible?"

He didn't elaborate further.

The deity's power is formidable; Great Chu cannot subdue it.

If they truly followed Shangguan Liumo's suggestion and sent an envoy to the sacred mountain, they would anger the mountain god, and the consequences would be unimaginable.

Cha Yunpo's words were also approved by the military officials.

The civil officials disagreed and began to object, and the court quickly turned into a battleground of arguments between the two sides.

Emperor Chu sat on his dragon throne, watching without intervening, and no one could guess what he was thinking.

"Grand Preceptor, what do you think?" Emperor Chu suddenly looked at Yunji.

The court immediately fell silent; everyone stopped arguing and turned their gazes to the Grand Preceptor.

"Your Majesty, I believe the appearance of the sacred mountain is too bizarre. Gods shouldn't exist in this world, yet they have appeared. It's difficult to say whether this is a good thing or a bad thing given the current situation. Therefore, I believe we should remain unchanged in the face of all changes," Yunji slowly spoke.

"Oh? Does your minister mean we shouldn't do anything?" Emperor Chu became interested; he shared the same thought.

“Yes! We don’t need to worry about anything. Didn’t that mountain god want territory? We’ve already given him Liaoyun County, so giving him Qingzhou won’t hurt.”

“Qingzhou is a border region, bordering the demon’s stronghold. The demons should be the ones worrying, not us. We don’t need to do anything; we can just wait and see how things develop.”

Yunji explained his reasoning.

The Chu Emperor, upon hearing this, found it quite reasonable.

Since the sacred mountain hadn’t shown any hostility, then there was no need to concern himself with it.

As for the disciples who died at Jinlong Temple, that was Jinlong Temple’s business; what did it have to do with me?

Although the Chu Emperor was very curious about the deities, wondering why a deity would appear after the separation of heaven and earth,

he didn’t have time to pay attention to these things. The appearance of the deities didn’t conflict with his Dao, so naturally, he didn’t need to concern himself with it.

“Then it’s settled.” The Chu Emperor immediately made the decision.

Shangguan Liumo wanted to say something, but seeing this, he could only swallow his words.

Yunji smiled; he knew the Chu Emperor very well.

Currently, nothing could concern the Chu Emperor except achieving sainthood.

Suddenly, the Chu Emperor sensed something and frowned slightly.

Then, he dismissed all the civil and military officials. Once he was alone in the Golden Palace, his true spirit left his body and entered the Great Void.

This was a special space accessible only to those who had touched the Saintly Realm and comprehended their own Great Dao—those at least a Half-Saint.

Within the Great Void, no matter how far apart they were, they could meet there.

The Great Void was desolate, deathly silent, an endless expanse of nothingness.

This nothingness wasn't pitch black; it was empty, deep, and devoid of light.

Only the true spirit could reside in the Great Void; the physical body could not enter.

“Tai Xuan, Jia Bi, what do you wish to summon me for?”

The Chu Emperor's true spirit entered the Great Void and saw two figures who had been waiting there for a long time.

One figure had a kind face and wore an earth-yellow monk's robe, looking like an ordinary old monk.

The other figure was a four- or five-year-old Taoist boy, with bright eyes, white teeth, and fair skin; one might mistake him for an innocent child.

"It's naturally about the sacred mountain," the boy said in a childish voice.

The old monk, somewhat exasperated, frowned and said, "Tai Xuan, how did you manage to refine yourself into this ghastly state? Can you ever recover? This old monk simply can't bear it anymore."

Even the Chu Emperor couldn't stand it anymore. The thought that this seemingly innocent child was actually an ancient monster who had lived for hundreds of years made him feel nauseous.

"After the Nine-Turn Human Pill is refined, it can only be refined again after forty-nine days. Only thirty days have passed now," Tai Xuan said, his tone still childlike.

"If you can't do it, then change your voice!" The Chu Emperor simply couldn't take it anymore.


Chapter 171 The Situation Changes Dramatically

Finally, Tai Xuan used magic to process the voice, restoring it to its original state.

Emperor Chu and Jia Pi felt somewhat at ease and began discussing the matters they had met to discuss.

“The records of the Severing Heaven and Earth Connection cannot be wrong. But why are there still gods who have escaped the net?” Jia Pi asked in a deep voice.

The appearance of a deity was not a good sign for him.

Especially after his junior brother found him and recounted his journey to Qingzhou, learning that the key to sainthood lay in the Divine Mountain, he became restless.

“Two saints have appeared among the demons, so isn’t it normal for gods to escape the net?” Tai Xuan said slowly.

“Tai Xuan, we both know perfectly well where the two saints among the demons came from. Only the appearance of this mountain god remains unexplained!” Jia Pi said coldly.

“Could it be an imposter? Or perhaps a cultivator truly attained enlightenment through the aid of incense offerings?” Emperor Chu speculated.

"Impossible! The aura of a deity cannot be faked, and as for cultivators achieving enlightenment through incense offerings, that's even more impossible."

Tai Xuan shook his head and said, "Your Majesty, you may not understand incense offerings. My master perished because of the backlash from incense offerings. The path of incense offerings is not for ordinary people. The karmic consequences one bears cannot be borne without an extremely high destiny and status."

"Since ancient times, only those with great fortune and merit have been able to bear the karmic consequences of incense offerings. As for the rest, they are probably beings born with high status."

From ancient times to the present, everyone knows that incense offerings are a good thing, and everyone wants to try them.

But without exception, the results are always extremely tragic.

Some people think that accumulating merit can help them bear the karmic consequences of incense offerings. Then they go on to do good deeds and save the common people, but ultimately they still cannot withstand the karmic backlash.

Some believed themselves to be exceptionally lucky, experiencing endless opportunities after beginning their cultivation journey. Sometimes, even finding rare treasures while urinating, they thought they could bear the consequences of their actions, only to be consumed by the backlash and perish.

Others were prodigies who began practicing swordsmanship at the age of three, achieving perfect harmony between themselves and their swords. At five, they were invincible among their peers; at seven, they conquered all other prodigies, becoming unrivaled. At twenty-seven, they reached the level of a half-saint, believing themselves to be unparalleled in talent, and sought the path of immortality through offerings.

Their fates, too, were tragic, ending in utter carcasses.

“If it’s not an imposter, and it’s not a cultivator achieving enlightenment through offerings, then why would a deity appear?” Emperor Chu asked, puzzled.

“It’s very likely a primordial deity!” Jiabi suddenly said.

“A primordial deity?”

This statement startled Emperor Chu and Tai Xuan, who then quickly realized what was happening.

Deities are said to be either primordial or acquired.

Acquired deities are those who, through offerings, grow from ordinary beings into gods.

"Innate deities, literally meaning those born deities, are those who are divine from the moment they are born.

They don't need incense offerings to establish their divine status."

"It's possible. If it's an innate deity, then everything makes sense," Tai Xuan said.

Emperor Chu and Jia Pi also believed that the mountain god might be an innate deity.

"Then how should we deal with this mountain god?" Jia Pi asked.

"Deal with? I have already summoned all the civil and military officials to discuss this. We will remain unchanged in the face of all changes," Emperor Chu said calmly.

Tai Xuan also spoke up: "This humble Taoist has been busy refining elixirs and has no time to concern himself with matters in Qingzhou."

Seeing the two men's indifferent expressions, Jiabi's face darkened, and he said,

"Your Majesty, Fellow Daoist Taixuan, the emergence of a deity is a major event. At the very least, it affects the current situation; at worst, it affects our path to enlightenment. Isn't it inappropriate to ignore it?"

"Affects the situation? The Heavenly Water Pact wouldn't last long anyway; if it's broken, so be it. Demons who want to act will have to get past the divine mountain first."

"As for affecting our path to enlightenment? It's affecting you, Jiabi, isn't it? No matter what that mountain god is like, it can't affect my ability to refine myself into a peerless elixir, can it?" Taixuan said dismissively.

To achieve sainthood, one must choose a path, also known as enlightenment.

Taixuan's method of enlightenment was to refine himself into a peerless elixir, hoping to achieve enlightenment and break through to the saint realm!

"Even a deity can't affect my path to enlightenment," the Chu Emperor said calmly.

Jiabi's expression turned somewhat grim upon hearing this.

So he was the only one whose path to sainthood was blocked by that mountain god?

That secret manual was too crucial; he had to obtain it!

Meanwhile.

The Holy Court.

This place was a thousand miles away from the Great Chu Dynasty, formerly part of Great Chu territory, but no longer.

The land was barren, with dust flying everywhere, devoid of any life.

Demonic monsters and monstrous creatures could be seen roaming freely from time to time.

Unlike humans, demons didn't cultivate territory. After occupying a large continent, they would wantonly slaughter until all living beings were wiped out.

So all that remained was a lifeless wasteland.

The demons' stronghold, the Holy Court, did have a sliver of life.

Because it kept many humans and other creatures to serve the demons.

The demons' Holy Court was magnificently constructed, hanging high in the sky, reaching into the clouds.

This was the work of two sages; with a mere wave of their hands, they could elevate a mountain from the ground.

With another wave, they could make the clouds bow their heads and bend their backs.

The Holy Court sat atop the mountain, shrouded in mist, its magnificent architecture exuding an aura of grandeur.

In the main hall, two chairs were placed equally.

These were the seats of the two sages.

Normally, the sages did not show themselves, so all affairs of the Holy Court were handled by demonic half-saints.

But today was different. A black command arrow arrived at the Holy Court, immediately attracting the attention of the half-saint.

After reading the message conveyed by the command arrow, the demonic half-saint was enraged.

“Rebellion! The human race dares to violate the Heavenly Water Pact! They disregard the Holy Court!”

Qing Yuci roared angrily.

At this moment, he wished he could mobilize a large army and utterly annihilate the Great Chu in one fell swoop!

But he could not make the decision himself; this matter still required the approval of the two sages.

Just as he was about to report this matter to the two Sages, a voice slowly appeared in his mind.

“I understand.”

It was the voice of the Demon Sage.

In the vast void, two blurry figures appeared.

It could be vaguely discerned that these two figures were not human, but other beings.

“What’s wrong with the Human Race? Daring to break the Heavenly Water Pact? Do they really think they can withstand the Holy Court’s army?”

One of the figures spoke coldly, its tone icy.

“Heh, withstand? With what? By using the entire fortune of the Human Race to arrive? I’m afraid they don’t have the guts.” The other voice said mockingly.

“This is unusual. Let Yu Ci go and take a look.”

"He's a demigod. If he enters the Great Chu, the Heavenly Water Pact will be completely destroyed."

"Destroyed is destroyed. The time is almost right. It's time to reap the rewards. They can't wait any longer; they've been urging us on."

"What a pity. I originally wanted to nurture him a little longer, but who knew the human race would be so eager to die."

"..."


Chapter 172 Changing a Town into a County

The people of Qianshan Town were unaware of the commotion in the capital and at the Holy Court.

Everyone was still immersed in the joy of the Mountain God's renewed manifestation.

The temple had been packed with people for the past few days, its incense burning brighter than usual.

Qianshan Town's population was already steadily increasing, and now, after the Mountain God's miracle, even more people were coming to seek refuge.

An entire county even wanted to pledge allegiance to the sacred mountain.

The county magistrate came directly to Qianshan Town with his official seal to discuss the matter with Bai Liang.

When the magistrate approached Bai Liang, Bai Liang was quite taken aback, somewhat surprised that a court official would openly seek refuge with the sacred mountain.

Only at the end did he realize that the magistrate had chosen the wrong side.

Liaoyun County was originally under the command of the General's Office, but Qiu He of the prefecture coveted Liaoyun County and wanted to extend his influence.

Ostensibly, Qiu Zhen was sent to Tianyue County, but secretly, Qiu Zhen contacted the magistrates of several other counties, promising them benefits and persuading them to side with the prefectural government.

Magistrate Wang, tempted, agreed. After accepting the bribes, he waited anxiously for Qiu He to surpass the General's Mansion and seize Liaoyun County from Xiang Tiandao.

However, Qiu He and several monks offended the sacred mountain and were directly killed by the mountain god.

Magistrate Wang was terrified, living in constant anxiety, fearing that the General would find out about his secret dealings and he would be punished.

After much deliberation, he realized that confessing would ruin his career, so he gritted his teeth and decided to take a drastic step: surrender to the sacred mountain.

So he arrived in Qianshan Town with his official seal.

Bai Liang, after hearing Magistrate Wang's reason for surrendering to the sacred mountain, suddenly understood.

So he was driven to a dead end; no wonder he dared to openly surrender to the sacred mountain.

However, this man was a traitor, having betrayed the General's Mansion once, which made Bai Liang somewhat wary.

Magistrate Wang clearly knew this as well. He gritted his teeth and said, "Master Bai, my loyalty is beyond question. If you don't believe me, I can settle my wife, children, and elderly parents in Qianshan Town, along with my savings accumulated over the years."

Bai Liang didn't respond.

How could that prove anything?

Some people don't even care about their families.

Magistrate Wang's face showed despair.

Finally, he gritted his teeth and whispered something to Bai Liang.

Bai Liang's eyes narrowed slightly upon hearing this, and he glanced thoughtfully at Magistrate Wang.

"You really are a restless fellow," he commented on Magistrate Wang.

Magistrate Wang quickly replied modestly, "I wouldn't dare, I just really want to advance."

"Wanting to advance is a good thing, but the prerequisite is that you have enough loyalty!"

Bai Liang stood up and agreed to Magistrate Wang's allegiance to Shenshan.

Seeing this, Magistrate Wang's face lit up with joy, and he finally breathed a sigh of relief.

After joining the Divine Mountain, Magistrate Wang quickly received his first benefit: the legendary, incredibly precious divine water.

Wang Lan was also a cultivator, at the early stage of the third-tier Profound Realm.

After drinking the divine water, his eyes lit up immediately. Feeling the changes brought about by the divine water within his body, he was filled with amazement.

"This...this is divine water? Truly worthy of being a divine object created by the gods! The Mountain God is too powerful!" Wang Lan exclaimed sincerely.

The county where Wang Lan lived was called Stone Tree County.

The population was over 100,000, but recently 10,000 to 20,000 people had fled to Qianshan Town, so now it's only around 100,000.

Shishu County is over 100 li (approximately 50 kilometers) from Qianshan Town, even farther than Tianyue County.

Bai Liang plans to arrange for Zhang Shiye, Qian Ke, and Chen Jianchi to accompany Wang Lan to Shishu County.

Just like with Taohua County, they'll first build a mountain god temple and have the county's inhabitants worship the mountain god.

Finally, they'll consider whether to relocate everyone to Shenshan.

Zhang Shiye and Qian Ke have experience with these matters, and they were somewhat surprised after receiving Bai Liang's notification.

They didn't expect Shishu County to also submit to Shenshan. If that's the case, Shenshan's unification of Liaoyun County isn't far off.

Infiltrating Tianyue County is also underway. Rumors of Shenshan are widespread, and many people have begun to worship the mountain god.

It's believed that soon, Tianyue County, the second largest county in Liaoyun County, will fall under Shenshan's control.

However, before that, Qianshan Town had a major matter to attend to.

That was to upgrade the town to a county!

Qianshan Town now had a population of over 100,000, making it a viable county seat.

However, Bai Liang and the others had been busy with cultivation, and since the matter wasn't considered particularly important, it had been temporarily shelved.

Now, it was time to address it.

Bai Liang summoned the core members of Qianshan Town to the Mountain Tower.

This included Qian Ke and his group, who hadn't yet gone to Shishu County, as well as Wang Lan, the newly appointed county magistrate.

Upon arriving at the Mountain Tower, everyone was curious about what Master Bai had called them over for.

Soon, He Liyuan arrived as well, bringing Xiao Xue with him.

Seeing everyone had arrived, Bai Liang smiled and said, "Since everyone's here, I won't take up any more of your time. Let's get straight to the point."

"Qianshan Town now has a population of over 100,000. Calling it a town is no longer appropriate. So, I've gathered everyone here today to announce that from today onwards, Qianshan Town will be upgraded to a county. From now on, it will no longer be called Qianshan Town, but Qianshan County!"

Upon hearing this, everyone smiled, feeling a surge of excitement.

Although it was just a name change, there was no real change.

But it was a symbol, a symbol of continuous improvement and growth!

Following this were the various changes to official positions within Qianshan County.

Bai Liang and Wu Ze actually didn't want to follow the system of the Great Chu Dynasty.

However, they couldn't think of a better one in the short term, so they could only make some modifications to the Great Chu Dynasty's system and then adopt it.

Generally, a county only has four main official positions: County Magistrate, County Assistant, Chief Clerk, and Registrar.

After Bai Liang and his team made changes, only the county magistrate and the county assistant magistrate remained as the main official positions, with several smaller positions below them.

Wu Ze was appointed as the county magistrate of Qianshan County, and Bai Liang as the county assistant magistrate. However, Wu Ze rarely handled official business; Bai Liang was the one in charge.

“From now on, we’ll have to address the two lords as ‘Sir’!”

Chen Jianchi couldn’t help but tease.

Everyone smiled upon hearing this.

“Call them whatever you like, but don’t be formal. We’re all working for the Mountain God; there’s no distinction of rank or status,”

Bai Liang said earnestly.

Wu Ze nodded in agreement with Bai Liang.

Being called "Master" by his brothers made him extremely uncomfortable.

"In front of outsiders, you should still address him as 'Master'," He Liyuan said.

Rules need to be established, otherwise, management will be difficult.

As for private matters, they can call him whatever they want.

Everyone nodded, agreeing with He Liyuan.

After the discussion, Bai Liang dismissed everyone.

From that moment on, Qianshan Town became Qianshan County!


Chapter 173 Qingquan Revisited

The news of Qianshan Town being upgraded to a county immediately sparked cheers from the townspeople.

Everyone was incredibly excited and thrilled.

Qianshan Town becoming Qianshan County signified its continuous growth and development.

This rising prosperity was unparalleled by the decline of other counties.

The change to Qianshan County also stimulated many businesses.

Merchants, always eager for profit, immediately sensed the business opportunity in Qianshan County's rapid development.

This led many businesses to invest even more money in Qianshan County.

Various restaurants, shops, and rice stores opened branches.

More and more grain was continuously transported to Qianshan County from all directions.

Suddenly, Qianshan County had become the most prosperous area within a hundred miles.

Even Tianyue County, with a population of 700,000, was not as prosperous as Qianshan County, because all the businesses had flocked to Qianshan County to do business.

Besides ordinary merchants, many cultivator-merchants also frequented Qianshan County.

After the last spirit rice trade, the spirit rice of Qianshan County had become known in some areas, attracting many eager buyers.

Although it was far inferior to genuine spirit rice, its quantity was enormous!

Furthermore, Zhou Sanhai's pills were being produced at an industrial-scale pace.

He had recently become addicted to pill refining. The 100% success rate after adding divine water astonished him.

Pill refining was like fishing. If you frequently failed and came up empty-handed, it would definitely feel very boring.

You might even think that you shouldn't refine pills or fish anymore.

But what if you could catch a giant fish every time, without ever going home empty-handed?

Just like with pill refining, every pill was a masterpiece, which greatly satisfied Zhou Sanhai.

So this old man was addicted.

Every time Bai Liang came to visit him, he wanted to advise him to rest and not push himself too hard.

Zhou Sanhai, however, pretended not to hear and continued to immerse himself in alchemy.

Thus, under Zhou Sanhai's relentless alchemy, Crimson Spirit Pills were continuously produced.

Only after exhausting all the Crimson Spirit Grass did the old man reluctantly stop.

Bai Liang counted the number of Crimson Spirit Pills he had produced—over a thousand in total—and was utterly astonished.

"Haha, a 100% success rate! A thousand Crimson Spirit Pills—that's not unusual," Zhou Sanhai said proudly.

This was indeed something to be proud of; a 100% success rate would terrify any alchemist in the world.

"Old Zhou, you've truly worked hard! You are a great hero of Qianshan County!" Bai Liang exclaimed.

"Hahaha, no, no. Uh... what did you say, Qianshan County?"

Halfway through his laughter, Zhou Sanhai realized that Bai Liang had mentioned Qianshan County, not Qianshan Town.

He had been engrossed in alchemy lately, so he hadn't been present on the day the town was upgraded to a county.

Bai Liang told Zhou Sanhai about the upgrade, which immediately brought a broad smile to Old Zhou's face.

The better Qianshan County was, the better his choice would be.

After all, he had moved the entire Zhou family to Qianshan County!

Of the thousands of Crimson Spirit Pills, Bai Liang took half and sent them to the Hundred Refinement Hall for the students to consume.

These were the vital forces of Qianshan County.

He distributed a portion of the rest to the cultivators in Qianshan County.

There were about three hundred pills left.

These could be sold to exchange for other pills or cultivation resources.

Chen Jianchi had already gone to Shishu County, so these matters were entrusted to the three Qilin brothers.

They were instructed to take the spirit rice and Crimson Spirit Pills to the visiting cultivator merchants to negotiate.

As for the matter Wu Cheng had mentioned earlier—that the three Qilin brothers had murdered a prince and were now wanted by the emperor—Bai Liang had already inquired about it.

The Qilin brothers didn't hide anything and explained the reason.

The second prince wanted to recruit the three of them as his subordinates, but the Qilin brothers disagreed, expressing their unwillingness to be drawn into the power struggle among the princes.

The second prince then tried to bribe them, and when that failed, he resorted to threats. The three Qilin brothers refused again.

Finally, the second prince became enraged. He, a prince destined for the throne, was already being generous by accepting these three江湖bandits as his subordinates, and yet they dared to refuse?

He then ordered his men to kill Qilin and his companions. Forced into a corner, Qilin and his companions had no choice but to find an opportunity to coerce the Second Prince into fleeing.

However, the Second Prince, using some unknown method, let out a strange laugh, and then his entire body suddenly deflated like an inflated sack, turning into a dry, withered skin.

The three Qilin members, seeing this, were startled and, sensing something was wrong, quickly fled.

Upon seeing the Second Prince's death, the court immediately designated the three as murderers. The Emperor was furious and issued an arrest warrant!

Bai Liang, upon hearing this, was also surprised, wondering what had happened to the Second Prince.

This was the first official mission Qilin and his companions had received since joining the Divine Mountain.

Qilin took this very seriously, assuring Bai Liang, "Master Bai, please rest assured, I will definitely not let Qianshan County suffer!"

Bai Liang, however, didn't care much about the exchange; as long as it was an equal exchange, that was enough.

Wu Ze had returned to the mountainside to cultivate, taking Li Xue with him.

Since they couldn't help much at the foot of the mountain, they spent most of their time cultivating.

Wu Ze was preparing to break through to the Yin God Realm, while Li Xue aimed to break through to the Enlightenment Realm.

He Liyuan guarded the Divine Water, nourished day and night by the divine energy emanating from it, and his cultivation speed unexpectedly increased.

This excited him immensely; he felt he had a chance at the Yin God Realm, and the Yang God Realm was not out of the question.

After waking up this time, Li Yue, after dealing with the sixth-tier Blade Demon and the monks of the Golden Dragon Temple, did not immediately return to continue comprehending the laws of heaven and earth.

In the past few days, he had been observing the development of Qianshan County.

He was somewhat surprised by the three Qilin brothers who had just joined him; their strength was quite formidable.

They were only one step away from the Yin God Realm, and it seemed they were suppressing their cultivation level, otherwise they would have already reached the Yin God Realm.

Li Yue used his mental mirror to examine the three people's hearts; there was nothing wrong with them.

Of course, even if there were problems, they would be resolved after drinking the divine water.

His merit points had increased rapidly these past few days; just a few days ago it was only 3.41 million, but now it had reached over 3.6 million.

Because the people in the city had been so enthusiastic these past few days, the temples were packed, and even at night, the incense was still burning brightly.

At this moment, within Li Yue's divine sense range, he suddenly saw a familiar figure.

"Qingquan? She's back?"

Li Yue was surprised, then thought of the task he had given Qingquan last time.

He wondered if she had completed it. The Divine Water Cleansing... how many cultivators had it benefited? What realm were these cultivators at?

Dozens of miles away from Qianshan County, Qingquan raised her head, gazing in the direction of the Divine Mountain.

"We'll be there soon. I wonder how Qianshan Town is now?"

She thought to herself.


Chapter 174 Bai Jiangzhou is about to change his surname to Li.

As they drew closer to the sacred mountain, Qingquan's emotions grew increasingly complex.

The divine water had truly overwhelmed her.

So many Yin God Realm experts from Baijiang Prefecture had only drunk from it a few times, and now each of them resembled the sacred mountain.

She had only come to Qingzhou at the insistence of Chu Wuliu and the others.

If they hadn't been tied up, they probably would have accompanied her on this pilgrimage to Qingzhou, to see firsthand the mountain god she had never met, yet who already held her in high esteem.

Whenever she saw the burning gazes in Chu Wuliu and the others' eyes when they mentioned the mountain god, Qingquan felt a chill of fear.

Qian Kun, meanwhile, kept his eyes downcast, pretending not to notice.

He turned and warned Qingquan, "Whatever you do, never give the divine water to your own people! This stuff is far too evil!"

Qingquan wholeheartedly agreed.

This trip to Qingzhou was at Bai Liang's request: once the divine water was delivered, she would return to retrieve more and continue delivering it.

She soon approached Qianshan County.

Before she even got close, she was astonished by the scene before her.

When she returned to Baijiang Prefecture, Qianshan Town was just a small town, developing rapidly but limited to its town size.

But what was this? Had she taken the wrong road?

But the sacred mountain towered into the clouds; such an obvious landmark couldn't be wrong.

This county town before her was Qianshan Town!

No, it should now be called Qianshan County.

On the newly built county gate hung three large characters that had just been carved:

Qianshan County!

Qingquan walked into Qianshan County, completely unfamiliar with the place, and then she saw the bustling scene within the county town.

Vendors hawked their wares, people came and went in a constant stream, and horse-drawn carts loaded with goods passed by frequently on the main road.

It was a scene of unparalleled prosperity.

"This is Qianshan County?" Qingquan exclaimed in surprise.

Just then, a patrol team approached, led by Erqing.

Erqing caught sight of the woman dressed as a Taoist priest and recognized Qingquan. She asked with some surprise, "You're Qingquan from Xuanqing Temple? Didn't you return to Baijiang Prefecture? Why are you in our Qianshan County again?"

Qingquan snapped out of her reverie and looked at Erqing.

Erqing had spent some time in Taohua County, so the two were somewhat acquainted.

"I'm here to see Master Bai. Where is he now?" Qingquan asked.

"Master Bai? You should call him Lord Bai now," Erqing corrected her first, then replied, "Lord Bai is handling affairs at Shanyue Tower. Brother He is also there; it seems there's something going on."

"Could you take me there?" Qingquan asked quickly.

Erqing thought for a moment and replied, "I can take you there, but you can't go upstairs until I announce your arrival."

Qingquan nodded, and Erqing then led her to the front of the Mountain Pavilion.

Erqing went inside to announce her arrival, while Qingquan waited outside.

Inside the Mountain Pavilion.

"Brother He, you mean many spiritual herbs have grown beside the Divine Spring? And there are so many of them?"

Bai Liang's tone was somewhat surprised upon hearing He Liyuan's report.

He Liyuan had been guarding the Divine Spring and had even built a wooden hut beside it for his rest.

"That's right. These spiritual herbs are nourished by the divine water, and they grow with spiritual power. The spiritual energy around the Divine Spring is abundant; this environment is most suitable for the growth of spiritual creatures!" He Liyuan said.

"Do these spiritual herbs have any effect?" Bai Liang asked, getting to the heart of the matter.

"Yes, but not much. They're basically just wild herbs, only nourished by the divine spring."

He Liyuan shook his head, his tone somewhat regretful.

After the spiritual herbs grew, he had tried them himself.

The results were mediocre.

Ultimately, they were just wild herbs containing spiritual energy.

"Is that so..." Bai Liang pondered, then said, "In a few days, I'll have Elder Zhou go and see if we can try planting some spiritual herbs near the divine spring."

"If that's possible, that would be wonderful!"

He Liyuan said expectantly.

The difference between spiritual herbs and spiritual medicines is vast.

Even the Crimson Spirit Grass used to refine the Crimson Spirit Pill is only a slightly better, lowest-grade spiritual medicine.

Therefore, the price of Crimson Spirit Grass is quite outrageous, beyond the reach of ordinary people.

If Qianshan County could cultivate its own spiritual herbs, it might no longer have to worry about cultivation resources.

Just then, Erqing knocked on the door.

Bai Liang let him in. Seeing it was Erqing, he smiled and said, "Erqing, what brings you to Shanyue Tower? Did you discover something during your patrol?"

"Nothing," Erqing shook his head, then said, "Qingquan is here. She wants to see you."

"Qingquan?" Hearing this name, Bai Liang was surprised.

He hadn't expected her to return to the Divine Mountain so quickly.

He remembered that the Mountain God had assigned Qingquan a task, one he still didn't quite understand.

"Let her in directly," Bai Liang said quickly.

Erqing nodded, turned, and went out.

When she returned, she was followed by a female Taoist priest.

Compared to before, Qingquan's complexion was much better now.

She was already quite beautiful, belonging to the beauty category.

Her previous severe injuries had left her listless and pale, which explained her less-than-stunning appearance.

After returning to Baijiang Prefecture and recovering, her complexion returned to a rosy hue, making her look much better.

"Long time no see, how have you been?" Bai Liang greeted her politely, then invited Qingquan to sit down.

"How have you been, Master Bai...?"

Qingquan replied politely in return, but hesitated slightly when she got to the form of address.

Bai Liang didn't seem to mind. After Qingquan sat down, he personally brewed her a cup of tea.

Looking at the tea placed before her, Qingquan picked it up to drink, but then suddenly seemed to remember something. Her expression changed slightly, and she put the tea down from her lips.

Bai Liang noticed this detail, frowned, and asked, "What's wrong? Is Miss Qingquan having some concerns?"

He felt a slight displeasure. Did she suspect him of poisoning the tea?

"No, Lord Bai, you've misunderstood." Qingquan realized his actions were somewhat inappropriate and quickly said, "Is the water used to brew this tea the divine water of Qianshan County?"

Bai Liang shook his head: "Of course not. Although the divine water is abundant, we are not so extravagant as to indulge in such things."

Hearing that it wasn't, Qingquan breathed a sigh of relief.

So he confidently picked up the tea and drank it all in one gulp.

Bai Liang, seeing this, was even more confused.

Why was he relieved after hearing it wasn't divine water?

Divine water was an incomparably precious treasure; others couldn't even dream of it.

"Miss Qingquan, what do you mean? Did something go wrong with the divine water I gave you last time?" Bai Liang frowned.

"Lord Bai, don't you know the effects of the divine water?"

This time, Qingquan was taken aback, somewhat puzzled.

"Effects? Isn't it simply to understand one's true nature and purify spiritual energy? And isn't it also about giving talented individuals the opportunity to acquire cultivation talent?"


Chapter 175 Green Feather Thorn

“Not only that, there’s more…”

Qingquan began, then suddenly hesitated, unsure how to express herself.

Would she say this divine water was poison, capable of brainwashing?

This was a sacred mountain; saying something like that would likely land her in serious trouble.

“What else?” Bai Liang pressed.

He Liyuan, standing nearby, was also watching Qingquan intently. He was always by the sacred spring; how could he not know if the divine water had other effects?

Erqing also looked puzzled. He had drunk the divine water many times and hadn’t experienced any other effects.

And judging from Qingquan’s hesitant expression, the effects were probably not good.

Qingquan, seeing that Bai Liang and the others genuinely didn’t know, was also incredibly perplexed.

Weren’t they the Mountain God’s subordinates? How could they know so little about the divine water?

After a moment’s thought, she suddenly understood.

Bai Liang and the others held the Mountain God in utmost reverence; for them, the divine water could only be beneficial, with no 'harm'.

Because they couldn't perceive any "disadvantages."

After carefully considering her words, Qingquan recounted the events that unfolded after she brought the divine water to Baijiang Prefecture.

Bai Liang and the others listened quietly, their expressions constantly shifting, quite a sight.

Even after Qingquan finished speaking, their expressions remained complex.

"That's how it is. I don't know what happened either. But I was somewhat frightened, so I dared not drink the divine water easily. I'm sorry," Qingquan said apologetically.

Bai Liang and He Liyuan exchanged a glance before saying, "We never expected the divine water to have such miraculous effects. It truly is a divine object created by the Mountain God."

After hearing Qingquan's explanation, Bai Liang and his companions didn't feel anything amiss; on the contrary, they were somewhat excited.

What kind of side effect was this? It was practically a divine effect!

After drinking the divine water, regardless of their previous allegiance, they would be subtly transformed into believers of the Mountain God.

For those of them who already worshipped the Mountain God, this side effect was as if it didn't exist.

This side effect only worked on those who didn't worship the Mountain God.

This solved a problem that had been troubling Bai Liang for a long time.

That was the loyalty of those below him.

As more and more people drank the divine water, the number of those awakening their cultivation talents also increased.

In this period, the number of students in the Hundred Refinements Hall had reached three digits.

Among them, double digits had already broken through to become cultivators of the Refinement Realm.

This was obviously very good news, but most of these people had only recently joined Qianshan County, making it difficult to discern whether there were any disloyal individuals among them.

Now that Qingquan had brought this news, he completely put aside his concerns.

Since he had already drunk the divine water, there was no problem.

Even if someone wanted to join Qianshan County in the future, he could just give them the divine water, and any disloyalty they might harbor would vanish.

He also wondered if the pills Elder Zhou used to refine with the divine water also inherited its effects.

And what about planting spiritual herbs near the divine spring? Would that have the same effect?

These thoughts led him to a bold idea.

Meanwhile, He Liyuan, standing nearby, inquired about the situation in Baijiang Prefecture.

"Miss Qingquan, are you saying that there are over a dozen Yin God Realm cultivators in Baijiang Prefecture who, after drinking the divine water, have developed a deep reverence for the Mountain God?" He couldn't help but ask, somewhat astonished.

If that were true, wouldn't Qingquan have gained over a dozen Yin God Realm followers for the Divine Mountain just by returning to Baijiang Prefecture?

That's too outrageous!

Qingquan nodded: "Yes, if it weren't for protecting Baijiang Prefecture, they might have come to the Divine Mountain on pilgrimage."

He Liyuan and Bai Liang exchanged a glance, their eyes gleaming.

Afterwards, Bai Liang asked Qingquan a few more questions before finally instructing He Liyuan to take Qingquan to the Divine Spring to retrieve the divine water.

Bai Liang specifically instructed Qingquan to bring back a lot more divine water this time, at least two or three times more than last time.

Such a divine item should not be left unshared; it would be a real waste.

Li Yue withdrew his divine sense from the Mountain Tower.

He had overheard their entire conversation.

He was somewhat surprised by what Qingquan had said about Baijiang Prefecture.

His casual action last time had yielded unexpectedly great results.

A dozen or so Yin God Realm believers—if used properly, they would be a considerable force.

He carefully sensed the surroundings and suddenly detected over a dozen points of light in the distance, the direction of Baijiang Prefecture.

These tiny points of light represented believers who had offered incense to him.

Looking further down, he could see a dense cluster of points of light, with many more scattered nearby.

This was Qianshan County, his base of operations, and currently his main source of merit points.

Within these points of light, Li Yue also saw faint golden light slowly gathering.

This unexpected discovery surprised him.

"Is this what fortune is?"

Qian Qu had mentioned earlier that Qianshan County was gathering fortune, but Li Yue hadn't paid much attention.

Now, seeing the fortune materialize was quite unexpected.

Carefully sensing this fortune, he discovered it differed from the current fortune of the Great Chu Dynasty.

The fortune of Qianshan County possessed a vibrant, burgeoning vitality.

The fortune of the imperial court, however, gave off a sense of stagnation and decay.

One represented new life, the other represented decay.

"Does this represent the future trajectory?" Li Yue wondered.

..............................

The Holy Court.

In the magnificent palace, Qing Yuci, a half-saint of the demon race, received instructions from the sage.

He was instructed to question the Great Chu Dynasty about why they had violated the Heavenly Water Pact.

This was a test, and also a warning.

Upon receiving the sage's order, Qing Yuci immediately closed his eyes, his true spirit entering the void.

In the vast emptiness, everything is nothingness, yet coordinates still exist.

He arrived at a certain coordinate, and then his true spirit stirred the void, affecting reality.

The capital city.

Emperor Chu, Tai Xuan, and Jia Bi opened their eyes almost simultaneously, their expressions changing.

Someone had stirred the void; a being at the demigod level had come knocking!

Instantly, the three of them entered the void with their true spirits and saw the culprit.

“Azure Feather Thorn, it’s you!?”

Jia Bi saw that it was an eight-tailed azure fox stirring the void and recognized its identity.

The Demon Saint’s first general, a peak eighth-tier, demigod-level demon, Azure Feather Thorn!

In the Great Void, all living beings reveal their true forms.

“Old fox, it’s been so long, and you still haven’t grown your ninth tail? It seems your demon race’s method of achieving sainthood is an exaggeration.”

Tai Xuan spoke, his gaze fixed intently on Qing Yu Ci.

Emperor Chu’s expression turned cold, his tone displeased. “Qing Yu Ci, you came alone, needlessly disturbing the Great Void and startling us. Aren’t you afraid of being killed here?”

Currently, the three of them were facing Qing Yu Ci, a single demon, giving them a significant advantage.

“Heh, you’d better dare to make a move,” Qing Yu Ci sneered.


Chapter 176 A Nominal Existence

These words caused the three Chu Emperors to grimace.

Indeed, they lacked the confidence to attack Qing Yuci, not because of his strength, but because of the two Saint Realm experts behind him.

The Saint Realm experts of the demons were like a mountain pressing down on their hearts, making it hard to breathe.

The Chu Emperor said coldly, "I can't kill you here, but making you suffer is easily within my grasp."

Qing Yuci's expression changed. Seeing the three surrounding him, he quickly said, "Please don't be hasty. I've come on the orders of the two Saint Venerables. You'd better consider carefully before making a move."

"Saint Venerables?"

Upon hearing this, the three Chu Emperors frowned, a bad premonition rising in their hearts.

"You've come because of the Qingzhou matter, haven't you?" Jia Pi asked calmly.

After the events in Qingzhou, they had already prepared for the demons to come knocking.

"Hmph! It seems you all know the reason. Since you know why I've come, then give me an explanation: why did you break the Heavenly Water Pact?!" Qing Yuci coldly questioned, his fox-like eyes flashing.

"When has the Great Chu Dynasty ever broken the Heavenly Water Pact? You must be mistaken," the Chu Emperor said calmly.

"Still trying to argue even now? My Holy Court lost a sixth-tier Demon General in Qingzhou. Are you trying to say that the sixth-tier Blade Demon's dying message was false?"

Qing Yuci's tone turned angry, feeling that the Chu Emperor was deceiving him.

Having enjoyed two years of peace, have they truly forgotten the fear they once felt under the threat of their Holy Court's demon army?

"That's incorrect," Emperor Chu said calmly and unhurriedly. "Although Qingzhou nominally belongs to the Great Chu Dynasty, it's long been a dead entity. The one who killed the sixth-tier demon that day wasn't from the Great Chu Dynasty."

"What do you mean by that? Are you trying to shift the blame? You don't even acknowledge the territories under your own control? Is the human emperor really this weak?"

Qing Yuci scoffed coldly, completely disbelieving Emperor Chu's words, believing he was trying to shirk responsibility and buy time.

Emperor Chu's expression remained unchanged. He said calmly, "I never lie. If you don't believe me, you can investigate. In short, the Great Chu Dynasty has never violated the Tianshui Agreement!"

"Heh, putting aside the matter of Qingzhou for now, why did the command mention that a powerful figure from the Golden Dragon Temple left the capital? How do you explain that?!" Qing Yuci said coldly.

"Amitabha, Your Excellency, please don't speak nonsense."

Jiabi stepped forward directly and said calmly, "The experts of Golden Dragon Temple have never left the capital. You need to provide evidence before you speak."

"Evidence? The Blade Demon has already been killed. What evidence is there?"

"So there's no evidence? No evidence is just empty talk, baseless rumors. How can such news be considered valid?" Jiabi said.

Qing Yuci was furious. He thought the Chu Emperor was shameless enough, but this old monk was even more shameless.

His face immediately darkened, and he said coldly, "You gentlemen truly refuse to admit to violating the Heavenly Water Agreement, is that it?"

"We have never violated it, so how can you say we don't admit it?" the Chu Emperor said slowly.

At this moment, Tai Xuan spoke up, saying, "Qing Yu Ci, your Holy Court doesn't need to use such an excuse to cause trouble. Come and try! Although the Great Chu Dynasty lacks a Saint, the accumulated human fortune since its founding is still formidable!"

Fortune is the foundation of the Great Chu Dynasty's current strength.

The two Saints of the demon realm had previously attempted to conquer the Great Chu Dynasty in one fell swoop, but the accumulated human fortune over the years was not to be underestimated.

Even if you are a Saint and have comprehended a path, you cannot withstand the boundless fortune.

Qing Yu Ci's eyes flickered slightly. He thought to himself that the human race still possessed such confidence; it seemed they still retained a considerable amount of fortune.

"Good, very good! Since you do not acknowledge Qingzhou as the territory of the Great Chu Dynasty, then my Holy Court sending powerful figures into Qingzhou will not be considered a violation of the Heavenly Water Agreement," Qing Yu Ci suddenly laughed.

Thinking to himself, "Since you want to shirk responsibility, I'll just go with the flow and bring Qingzhou completely under the Holy Court's control.

Now let's see how you respond."

Upon hearing this, the three Chu Emperors exchanged subtle glances. Tai Xuan was the first to step forward, his face flushed with anger. "Qing Yu Ci, what do you mean by this?! Although Qingzhou has broken free from the control of the Great Chu Dynasty, it still nominally belongs to the Great Chu Dynasty. Sending powerful figures into the country—isn't that violating the Heavenly Water Agreement?"

"That's right!" The Chu Emperor's face darkened, and he said coldly, "Qingzhou is the territory of the Great Chu Dynasty. Now that the Heavenly Water Agreement restricts it, the Holy Court shouldn't allow powerful figures to enter."

"Heh, if Qingzhou is the territory of the Great Chu Dynasty, then you have violated the Heavenly Water Agreement! So you can make your own decision!" Qing Yu Ci sneered.

The three Chu Emperors all looked grim. They exchanged glances, seemingly discussing something in secret.

Finally making his decision, Emperor Chu, his face ashen, coldly said, "Although Qingzhou is the territory of the Great Chu Dynasty, it is now merely a name without substance. I will order all court officials and civilians in Qingzhou to evacuate to Chizhou."

"Could the court officials? No, the civilians!" Qing Yuci immediately retorted.

If the civilians were to leave, their gains from conquering Qingzhou would be halved.

That's tens of millions of lives at stake! Who knows how many demons could be bred from that?

Emperor Chu frowned, seemingly unable to make a decision.

Finally, Tai Xuan stepped forward and sighed, "Alright! Then let the civilians stay."

Qing Yuci smiled upon hearing this.

This time, their goal had been achieved.

With the Great Chu Dynasty ceding Qingzhou, the Holy Court would temporarily let the matter drop.

"Congratulations to the Great Chu Dynasty, you've lost another province, hahaha..."

After a burst of laughter, Qing Yuci's true spirit swiftly vanished from the void.

He worried that the Chu Emperor and his companions would become enraged and attack him.

However, after he had completely left, the three of them did not show any anger; instead, their expressions were somewhat subtle.

“They’re far enough away now, Your Majesty can laugh if you want,” Tai Xuan said with a faint smile.

Hearing this, the Chu Emperor finally smiled and scoffed, “That Qingyu Thorn is as brainless as ever, easily fooled.”

“Indeed, if it were that Demon Clan Half-Saint, he wouldn’t have been so easily fooled,” Tai Xuan remarked.

Two of the demons were demigods, existing from the very beginning.

Before the demons wreaked havoc, these two demigods confronted the three of them, with the Great Chu Dynasty holding the upper hand.

As for the two Saint-level demons, they appeared out of thin air; no one knows where they came from.

"But we've completely lost Qingzhou," Emperor Chu sighed.

Although he had previously decided to cede Qingzhou to the deity of the Divine Mountain,

that was only verbal; now, Qingzhou has been completely severed.

From this day forward, Qingzhou no longer belongs to the Great Chu Dynasty.


Chapter 177 Great Change

Without the protection of the Great Chu Dynasty, the court's powerful figures have fled, posing a catastrophic threat to Qingzhou.

However, there's a variable: the suddenly appearing divine mountain.

They had previously assessed that the mountain god of Qingzhou was no less than an eighth-tier Venerable, possibly even a Half-Saint!

Furthermore, divine status far surpasses that of cultivators, so this mountain god's strength must be even higher.

A strength above Half-Saint should be enough to protect Qingzhou.

Provided the two Saint-level beings of the Holy Court don't intervene.

"Your Majesty, do you think the mountain god can withstand the demon army?" Jiabi suddenly asked.

His eyes gleamed; his mind had been preoccupied with the secret manual from the very beginning.

That was the key to his ascension to sainthood!

Emperor Chu shook his head: "I don't know. Perhaps it will work, perhaps it won't. But in any case, it has at least slowed down the demons' advance, buying us some more time."

Tai Xuan nodded in agreement: "Time is of the essence. This humble Daoist must return to prepare materials to refine myself. I hope this time will be successful."

With that, he vanished, leaving the void.

Emperor Chu and Jia Bi also dissipated, their true spirits returning to their physical bodies.

Time left for the Great Chu Dynasty was truly running out!

The next day, an imperial edict was issued from the capital, shocking the entire world.

The court actually ordered all officials of Qingzhou to evacuate and retreat to Chizhou.

What did this mean? Was Qingzhou being completely abandoned?

This edict was immediately met with resistance from most officials, who submitted memorials pleading with His Majesty to rescind the order.

Then Emperor Chu summoned all officials for a court meeting, after which the officials all fell silent.

But while they were silent, others were not.

The people felt an overwhelming sense of sorrow and anger. Two years later, were they about to cede territory again?

The court officials had withdrawn, but what about the people of Qingzhou?

They remained in Qingzhou.

Without the protection of fortune and powerful court officials, these people were like fish on a chopping block, at the mercy of others.

Many recalled the states ceded to demons a few years ago.

Which one hadn't become a living hell?

Especially the incredibly wealthy Jiangnan State, with its population of over a hundred million, merchants everywhere, and its capital city practically a mini-capital.

After being ceded to demons, that mini-capital city had long since become a living hell.

And now, Qingzhou was about to follow in Jiangnan State's footsteps.

Qingzhou City.

Since Qiu He's death, Xiang Tiandao had gradually taken control of Qingzhou City, and the people who had previously served under the state government were being purged one by one.

Just when he thought he had completely controlled Qingzhou City and would henceforth belong to the Xiang family, the Chu Emperor suddenly issued this edict.

Xiang Tiandao was stunned when he received the imperial decree.

Hua Wuzei and his companions happened to be in Qingzhou City, and their expressions were incredibly complex upon hearing the news.

"His Majesty has abandoned Qingzhou," Wu Cheng said in a deep voice.

"Yes, he has abandoned it. This is the first time since the battle of Tianshui Prefecture three years ago that territory has been ceded to the demons," Xiang Tiandao said in a low voice.

His gaze was somewhat dejected.

Even if he was the heir apparent, what could he do against the threat of the demons and the emperor's orders?

"General, let's retreat," Hua Wuzei sighed.

Having ceded Qingzhou, he could already foresee what would happen to it next.

He had also heard the news that Qianshan County had been downgraded from a town to a county.

With Qingzhou ceded and no longer protected by the Tianshui Pact, could Qianshan County withstand the full-scale threat of demons?

Thinking of this, Hua Wuzei began to worry about his brother-in-law.

He knew he had to immediately send a message to Bai Liang, urging them to prepare as soon as possible.

“Withdraw,” Xiang Tiandao uttered weakly, his already wrinkled face appearing even older.

Thus, orders were issued from the General's Office, reaching all the major prefectural and county towns.

All court officials, including disciples of Xuanqing Temple and Jinlong Temple, were ordered to evacuate Qingzhou immediately and not linger.

As for the common people, they were powerless to take them with them.

This was helpless, but unavoidable.

The emperor's edict specifically forbade the migration of civilians; the people had to remain.

The best course of action would be a quiet evacuation, avoiding any disturbance.

But how could this possibly avoid chaos?

The imperial edict was proclaimed throughout the land: Qingzhou was no longer under the control of the Great Chu Dynasty.

Although it was just a brief statement, ordinary people were simply unable to cultivate, not stupid.

Naturally, some clever individuals understood the edict's implied meaning, and the entire Qingzhou erupted in chaos!

The court was abandoning Qingzhou, ceding it to demons, sending everyone to their doom.

This news filled everyone with despair and boundless rage.

"You dog emperor, you incompetent wretch! Giving Qingzhou to demons, you dog emperor, you will surely be doomed!"

"Run! Run to Chizhou quickly, there's still a glimmer of hope!"

"Heavens above, save us poor people!"

The people of Qingzhou were ignited with fury.

But this was ultimately a world of cultivators; what use were numbers?

Although Xiang Tiandao was reluctant, he could only order the troops stationed in Qingzhou to suppress the unrest.

The prefectural city was garrisoned by prefectural soldiers, and the county towns by county soldiers.

In short, ordinary people were no match for cultivators.

After executing the ringleaders, the people completely lost hope.

Xiang Tiandao, along with a group of court officials, began to retreat towards Chizhou.

Qingzhou was bordered only by Baijiangzhou and Chizhou.

The roads to Chizhou were blocked, and the adjacent area in Baijiangzhou had fallen to demons.

With both roads blocked, the people of Qingzhou had nowhere to escape.

Finally, someone mentioned the sacred mountain in Liaoyun Prefecture, saying that a deity there had appeared and could protect all living beings.

People were skeptical, but with nowhere else to go, some began heading towards the sacred mountain in hope.

The sacred mountain, Qianshan County.

Because the sacred mountain was located on the border of Qingzhou, the emperor's edict reached them somewhat late.

When the residents of Qianshan County learned that the imperial court had completely abandoned Qingzhou and ceded it to demons, panic inevitably ensued.

After all, they were ordinary people who had lived in the Great Chu Dynasty since childhood.

Although the court was incompetent, it had still tried its best to protect them.

Now, suddenly losing the court's protection, the people began to panic.

Fortunately, Bai Liang reacted quickly and immediately went out to calm everyone's emotions.

They declared that while the imperial court had abandoned them, the sacred mountain would never abandon them.

With the protection of the Mountain God, even if Qingzhou lost the protection of the Heavenly Water Pact, Qianshan County would be safe!

Hearing of the Mountain God's protection, the people felt reassured and gradually calmed down.

However, some lingering worries remained.

Inside the Mountain Pavilion.

At this moment, all the core members of Qianshan County were gathered here.

Bai Liang and Wu Ze looked solemnly at the assembled crowd.


Chapter 178 Planning Ahead

"Everyone, you should all be aware of the imperial edict, right?"

Bai Liang spoke in a deep voice, his expression grave.

The crowd nodded, the atmosphere heavy.

"The Great Chu Dynasty has abandoned Qingzhou. My brother-in-law's letter states that Qingzhou has been ceded to the demons. I fear that powerful demons will soon enter Qingzhou and begin their slaughter."

Bai Liang said.

These words caused everyone's expressions to change.

He Liyuan said angrily, "How could the court do this? Abandon the lives of tens of millions of people in Qingzhou just like that?"

The others were also filled with indignation. The thought of Qingzhou being isolated and exposed to the threat of the demon army filled them with dread.

Qilin said coldly, "Those officials in the capital never care about the plight of the people. In their eyes, the lives of the commoners don't matter!"

He had long seen through the true nature of the Great Chu Dynasty.

Especially those officials in the capital, full of empty rhetoric about benevolence and morality, fell silent the moment they were asked to leave the capital and fight demons in the provinces.

Having spent their entire lives cowering in the capital, clinging to life only because of the Great Chu Emperor's influence, they had long since lost their will to fight.

"In the end, it is the common people who will bear the brunt of it all," Wu Ze sighed.

The crowd fell silent.

Tens of millions of people in Qingzhou, now separated from the court, had no cultivators to protect them.

If demons were to invade on a large scale, it would quickly become a living hell.

Thinking of this, everyone present felt a tightness in their chests, an immense sense of suffocation.

"Our Qianshan County is protected by the Mountain God, so we will be safe. But how many people can Qianshan County protect? A few hundred thousand, maybe a million. But Qingzhou has tens of millions!"

Bai Liang spoke in a deep voice, truly unable to bear watching so many people suffer at the hands of demons.

"Do your best and leave the rest to fate," Wu Ze said.

One's abilities are limited; doing one's best is enough.

Moreover, the Emperor's decree has a significant impact on Qianshan County.

The sacred mountain is here; Qianshan County naturally cannot move it.

But now this place is no longer within the territory of the Great Chu Emperor; the demon army will soon arrive and occupy Qingzhou.

Then, Qianshan County will be the first to bear the brunt!

Whether they can survive the demon army's offensive is the test they now face.

"I will go and pray to the Mountain God, begging him to guide Qianshan County."

Finally, Bai Liang made the decision.

Wu Ze then said, "It has been over a year since we last worshipped the Mountain God. This time, we can prepare a more grand sacrificial ceremony to thank the Mountain God for his protection."
