They have only held one sacrificial ceremony since settling on the sacred mountain.

At that time, Qianshan County was just a small village, not even a proper village. Therefore, it was very rudimentary, and the offerings for sacrifices were simply three sacrificial animals and a half-demonized tiger.

Back then, Wu Ze had said that when conditions allowed, he would definitely hold a grand sacrificial ceremony for the Mountain God.

Previously, Qianshan County was still developing and there hadn't been a suitable opportunity to hold a sacrificial ceremony.

Now, however, was a good opportunity, and it could even be used to solidify the hearts of the residents of Qianshan County.

"The offerings for the sacrificial ceremony cannot be taken lightly!" Bai Liang said.

Wu Ze nodded seriously; the offerings had to be carefully considered.

Qilin and the others were unfamiliar with the sacrificial ceremony, but had heard some details about it.

He asked, "Lord Bai, I'd like to ask, what offerings did you make when you worshipped the Mountain God before?"

This question made Bai Liang a little embarrassed. He coughed and said, "Just a low-ranking demon tiger."

"Low-ranking?" Qilin thought he had misheard. Offering a low-ranking demon tiger to a deity? That was a bit too... inappropriate!

He Liyuan explained.

Qianshan County had only been developing for less than two years.

At the last sacrificial ceremony, Bai Liang and his group only numbered about twenty.

Upon hearing this, Qilin understood.

He pondered for a moment, then said, “The sacrificial ceremony is of paramount importance. I believe we must do our utmost to obtain the best offerings for the Mountain God. Currently, many fifth-tier demons still exist within Qingzhou. If we can hunt them down, we can not only use them as offerings for the Mountain God on the spot, but also prevent them from harming Qingzhou.”

Wu Ze’s eyes lit up upon hearing this.

“Qilin is right, hunting fifth-tier demons as offerings. However, fifth-tier demons are incredibly powerful, and not many of us are qualified to hunt them.”

As he spoke, Wu Ze frowned slightly.

The three of them, including Qilin, could handle a fifth-tier Yin God Realm cultivator together.

He, with his Divine Speed ​​Talisman, could also deal with a fifth-tier demon.

But that was all.

They couldn’t very well have Li Xue use her Divine Fate Talisman to risk her life for another, could they?

That was clearly unrealistic.

Qilin also frowned; this was indeed a difficult problem.

“Perhaps we can invite some Yin God Realm experts to help us.”

At this moment, Bai Liang suddenly said.

He Liyuan also seemed to realize something, his lips twitching as he looked up at Bai Liang.

He thought of Baijiang Prefecture, which Qingquan had mentioned.

There were a bunch of Yin God Realm experts there who had drunk the divine water.

“You can invite Yin God Realm experts to help us?” Wu Ze turned around in surprise, assuming Bai Liang wanted to ask Hua Wuze for help.

Hua Wuze was Bai Liang’s brother-in-law and also had a favorable impression of Qianshan County.

If Bai Liang asked, Hua Wuze certainly wouldn’t refuse.

However, when he asked Bai Liang if he wanted to ask Hua Wuze, Bai Liang smiled and shook his head, saying, “No need to ask my brother-in-law, there are more suitable people, and quite a few at that!”

“More suitable people? Who are they?”

Wu Ze was somewhat puzzled.

Besides Hua Wuze, did Bai Liang know any other Yin God Realm experts?

Qilin and the others looked at Bai Liang with curiosity, wondering if there were other hidden masters in Qianshan County.

Only He Liyuan knew who Bai Liang was referring to.

Bai Liang didn't keep them in suspense and recounted everything Qingquan had said that day.

After listening, Wu Ze and the others were stunned and speechless for a long time.

"Truly worthy of the Mountain God, to be able to create such a divine object!"

Finally, Wu Ze couldn't help but exclaim, breaking the silence.

The others also came to their senses, deeply shocked.

They hadn't expected the divine water to have such effects; this would eliminate the potential for ungrateful people to appear in Qianshan County.

However, the three Qilin brothers had somewhat strange expressions.

They vividly remembered being warmly fed divine water on their very first day at the sacred mountain. At the time, they were incredibly touched, thinking they were already enjoying such a divine substance so soon after joining the pilgrimage.

However, judging from Wu Ze and the others' reactions, it seemed they had only recently learned of the divine water's side effects, and their expressions didn't seem feigned.

Considering they were now already citizens of Qianshan County, there was no point in dwelling on these thoughts any longer, so Qilin and the others dismissed the subtle idea from their minds.


Chapter 179 Leaving the Mountain to Hunt Demons!

Soon, Qingquan was also invited to the Mountain Pavilion.

After retrieving the divine water, she didn't immediately return to Baijiang Prefecture, so she was still in Qianshan County.

Being invited to the Mountain Pavilion, Qingquan was somewhat puzzled, but she came anyway.

She had already heard the Emperor's decree and knew that Qianshan County was facing a great crisis.

Upon entering the Mountain Pavilion, Bai Liang, seeing her arrive, directly explained the reason for her invitation.

"Miss Qingquan, now that Qingzhou has seceded from the Great Chu Dynasty, and the court's powerful figures have retreated, the lurking demons are bound to stir. Therefore, we want to deploy our forces to deal with the internal demon threat before the foreign demons invade Qingzhou."

"However, we in Qianshan County don't have enough manpower, so we will have to trouble you to go to Baijiang Prefecture and ask the powerful figures there for assistance," Bai Liang said.

Upon hearing this, Qingquan immediately understood Bai Liang's meaning.

He wanted her to return to Baijiang Prefecture and summon all the Yin God Realm cultivators who had drunk the divine water and been brainwashed to Qingzhou to serve as henchmen for the Divine Mountain?

Having figured this out, she couldn't help but ask, "You sent me back to Baijiang Prefecture just to wait for this moment?"

"No, it just happened to happen," Bai Liang shook his head.

Qingquan didn't want to say much. She knew the crisis Qingzhou was facing; tens of millions of people were living under a sword of Damocles, and if nothing unexpected happened, they would soon become food for demons.

She let out a heavy breath, her eyes hardening, and said, "I will pass on the message and have them all come to Qingzhou to help you."

As the saying goes, it's better to sacrifice a friend than myself.

As for what will happen to Chu Wuliu and the others after they arrive in Qingzhou, that's none of her concern.

After agreeing, Qingquan didn't linger.

Qingzhou was now in complete chaos, and to reach Baijiangzhou, they had to set off as soon as possible, otherwise, leaving would be difficult.

Qingquan left with the divine water, embarking on his journey back to Baijiangzhou.

Meanwhile, Bai Liang and the others began making arrangements. Once their help from Baijiangzhou arrived, they could immediately set off to hunt demons!

At this critical juncture, Bai Liang and He Liyuan also wanted to contribute by hunting fifth-tier demons.

However, their strength was simply insufficient.

Bai Liang was currently at the peak of the Profound Realm, just one step away from the Divine Enlightenment Realm.

He Liyuan had already reached the Divine Enlightenment Realm, but without the blessing of a divine talisman, his own strength was insufficient to contend with fifth-tier demons.

Only Wu Ze and the three Qilin brothers were allowed to go hunting demons.

Qilin had spent some time in Qingzhou and had some understanding of the demons lurking within its borders.

"The number of fifth-tier demons in Qingzhou basically corresponds to the number of Yin God Realm cultivators in the imperial court. Therefore, their numbers will not exceed double digits."

"It speaks," the Qilin said.

Black Dragon chimed in, "We've wandered the wilderness before and encountered several fifth-tier demons, so we know their hiding places. As for the others, we know their general locations too; we can just search carefully when the time comes."

Wu Ze nodded in agreement.

Knowing the demons' locations would make hunting them much easier.

But there's still a problem: the news of Qingzhou's secession from the Great Chu Dynasty. If the demons within Qingzhou were to find out, it would be disastrous!

"The news has already spread throughout Qingzhou; they probably already know," Fire Phoenix said in a deep voice.

Now that Qingzhou is in chaos and the imperial court's powerful figures have withdrawn, it's the perfect opportunity for the demons to reap their spoils.

But they can't leave yet; their reinforcements haven't arrived. The four of them alone can't contend with so many demons.

"Hopefully, these demons will have some reservations and won't dare to attack for now," Qilin sighed.

Li Yue naturally already knew about the major events in Qingzhou.

To be honest, he hadn't expected the Great Chu Dynasty to be so decisive, abandoning Qingzhou outright.

A province, tens of millions of people—that's forty to fifty million, not twenty or thirty million!

So many ordinary people, sent to the demons' jaws—it can only be said that those in power in the capital are truly ruthless!

Li Yue felt a chill in his heart.

Although he was a mountain, he was once human, and he also had compassion.

If he couldn't see it, so be it, but Qingzhou was right under his nose. How could he possibly watch tens of millions of people be slaughtered by demons?

Bai Liang and the others' actions didn't disappoint him.

They planned to take action first, eliminating the demons within Qingzhou's borders.

To fight external threats, one must first secure internal stability. They needed to resolve internal problems before they could unite to fight the demon army from beyond the borders.

Li Yue wholeheartedly agreed with this approach.

He was already preparing to convey the orders to Bai Liang and the others at the sacrificial ceremony.

At the same time, Bai Liang and the others were still too weak. Previously, Li Yue hadn't wanted to force them to grow too quickly, so he had let them cultivate on their own.

But now, the situation was beyond their control; they had no choice but to force them.

They needed to select a few more people for the Mountain God's Blessing; they needed to quickly cultivate strong individuals capable of standing alone!

Li Yue looked at the panel.

[Realm: Fourth-Order Sacred Mountain]

[Merit: 3.91 million/10 million]

[Divine Abilities: Mountain God's Blessing, Dharma Body of Heaven and Earth, Rebirth of Incense, Gratitude for the Source of Water]

[Spell: Manifestation]

[Magic Artifacts: Mountain-Suppressing Seal, Heart Mirror, Divine Weapon Furnace, Wind-Stabilizing Pearl]

The total merit is 3.91 million, still more than halfway to advance to the fifth-order Sacred Mountain.

It's impossible to accumulate enough in a short time.

However, the imperial court's abandonment of Qingzhou has left tens of millions of people without its protection.

This is a great opportunity for him.

If he could convert half of these tens of millions of people into his followers, that would be a terrifying amount of merit points!

Meanwhile, in Qingzhou City.

The General's Mansion and the Prefectural Government Residence are already deserted.

The imperial officials and troops have all withdrawn; this place has become a city without jurisdiction.

Chaos erupted in the city, with many thugs taking advantage of the chaos to commit murder and arson, creating utter disorder.

Some low-level cultivators remained in Qingzhou City.

They began to rally, killing the thugs and temporarily stabilizing the situation.

But soon, more thugs, this time cultivators, began to wreak havoc, and the situation quickly spiraled out of control. Countless people fled the city, but most remained safely inside their homes.

At the window of an inn, Kong Wang stood calmly, observing the chaos in the city.

"Amitabha," he murmured a Buddhist chant.

The next moment, he vanished from his spot, reappearing on a street.

Two groups of people were fighting. Most of them were cultivators at the Refining Law Realm, including two at the Second Stage of the Enlightenment Realm.

"Qingzhou is in chaos! You, as cultivators, not only do you fail to maintain order, but you also take advantage of the situation to wreak havoc, killing, burning, looting, and raping! Are you even human?!"

The man with his severed right arm, bleeding profusely, glared angrily at the sneering scarred man before him, completely unaware that a monk had suddenly appeared beside him.


Chapter 180 Maintaining Stability

"Heh, maintain the status quo? Disaster is imminent. The imperial court has abandoned Qingzhou. What can we ants do to maintain the status quo? With your hot blood?"

The scarred man sneered, continuing, "Since we're going to be trampled by demons anyway, we might as well let the brothers have some fun. Dying under our blades is better than dying in the demons' mouths, helping them grow stronger!"

"Boss is right!"

The group behind him echoed, laughing wildly.

In their eyes, Qingzhou's chaos was inevitable.

Since destruction was inevitable, why not indulge it to the fullest?

"Beast!"

The one-armed man was furious, but his opponent was stronger, so he could only watch the scarred man laugh resentfully.

"Although you're stupid, you still have some passion and reason. I admire that, so I'll spare your life. Get lost!" the scarred man said calmly.

"I'm not leaving! You want to cause trouble in the city? I won't allow it! Unless you step over my dead body!"

The one-armed man said coldly.

"Step over your dead body? What are you? You really think that's heroic, that people will treat you like a hero? Utterly foolish!"

The scarred man sneered, then his expression hardened!

"You won't listen to reason, so I'll grant your wish! Since you want to die, I'll satisfy you!"

With that, he swung his broadsword, took a step forward, and appeared in front of the one-armed man in the blink of an eye. A cold glint flashed in his eyes, and the veins on his hands bulged as he slashed down fiercely.

The one-armed man's expression changed, but he didn't retreat.

Although he only had one arm, he still managed to raise his hand to block, holding a blue sword across his chest, trying to use it to block the broadsword.

But the difference in strength was too great, and coupled with his existing injury, he couldn't withstand it at all.

A loud bang rang out as weapons clashed.

The green sword was slashed, leaving a nick in its hilt. The one-armed man was sent flying, crashing onto the brick road, kicking up a cloud of dust.

He spat out a mouthful of blood, his face turning deathly pale.

The cultivators standing with him dared not help, fearing the scarred man's strength and the possibility of being implicated.

"See that? You like to stand up for the people, and now you're lying on the ground. Your companions, the people you saved, don't even have the courage to help you up. Pathetic, pathetic! I'll send you on your way!" the scarred man sneered.

The one-armed man turned to look at his companions, but they all avoided his gaze, heads bowed, unable to meet his eyes.

He then looked at the ordinary people he had saved, but they kept their distance, unwilling to even look at him.

He gave a bitter smile, his mind lost in thought.

The scarred man's eyes were cold as he stepped forward with his large knife, ready to kill the one-armed man.

But suddenly, he seemed to bump into something. Looking closely, he saw a monk standing before him.

The monk was incredibly handsome, with fair skin.

The scarred man's expression changed. He quickly took a few steps back, staring coldly at the monk before him, and demanded, "Where did this monk come from? What brings you here?"

"Amitabha, this humble monk is Kong Wang. I came from the sacred mountain and have been watching here for a while," Kong Wang said calmly.

"A sacred mountain?" The scarred man frowned slightly, then thought of a rumor that had just spread.

It was said that there was a sacred mountain in Liaoyun County, with a village of deities that could protect all living beings.

He naturally didn't believe such rumors.

If there truly were deities protecting all living beings, how could the imperial court have completely withdrawn and abandoned Qingzhou?

"You've been here for quite a while? Spying in secret?" the scarred man said coldly.

He was a cultivator at the Tongfa realm, and he hadn't noticed anyone approaching at all.

To be able to approach unnoticed, this monk's cultivation level must be higher than his; he might even be a cultivator at the Entry-level Profound realm.

The scarred man's mind raced. He thought that if someone were at the Entry-level Profound realm, they would have already fled with the imperial officials; why would they stay in Qingzhou?

Only cultivators like them, with low cultivation levels and no status, would be abandoned.

Realizing the monk might be bluffing, the scarred man's face hardened, a cold glint in his eyes.

He suddenly lunged forward, intending to strike while the monk was distracted.

Seeing Kong Wang remain unmoved, seemingly caught off guard, the scarred man's face lit up with joy!

"Gotcha!"

But the next moment, his expression drastically changed, turning to terror.

His broadsword slashed fiercely at Kong Wang's bald head, but instead of the expected head splitting in two, the broadsword bounced off with a 'clang,' leaving Kong Wang's head intact.

"Benefactor, you were too hasty," Kong Wang said softly.

"Master, spare my life, I meant no offense..."

Before the scarred man could finish his plea, Kong Wang flicked his finger, a golden light striking his chest, causing him to bleed profusely and fly back several meters.

Seeing this, the henchmen behind him turned pale with fright. Those who reacted quickly turned and ran, while others were so terrified their legs went weak, afraid to flee for fear of being hunted down by Kong Wang.

"Thank you for saving my life, Master," the one-armed man said weakly as he approached Kong Wang.

"Do you regret it?" Kong Wang asked calmly.

The one-armed man was taken aback, not understanding the meaning of Kong Wang's words.

Kong Wang continued, "Do you regret saving those people?"

Now the one-armed man understood. He first gave a bitter smile, then his eyes hardened, and he said earnestly, "No regrets. I saved them not for gratitude, but for the sake of clarity of mind."

"Then, if you encounter something like this again, will you still help?" Kong Wang continued to ask.

"Yes!" the one-armed man replied without hesitation.

"You're very good, your Dao heart is incredibly firm," Kong Wang praised.

"What good is a firm Dao heart if I'm merely a cultivator at the Tongfa Realm? Even with lofty ambitions, I lack the power to reach them."

The one-armed man's expression didn't show any despair as he spoke; on the contrary, it became even more resolute.

His companions, seeing the one-armed man rescued by the appearing monk expert, wanted to go forward and ask what had happened.

But ultimately, they were too ashamed to approach and turned away.

Yue Qiu's severed arm was still bleeding, but he didn't care. Enduring the pain, he used his sword to scrape away the rotten flesh, and blood gushed out instantly.

He frowned, then tore off a piece of his clothing and used it to completely wrap the bleeding wound, barely managing to stop the bleeding.

After finishing these tasks, Yue Qiu turned around and asked with some confusion, "Master, the imperial court has abandoned Qingzhou and evacuated all its experts. Why haven't you left yet?"

"This humble monk is not a member of the imperial court, so how can there be any talk of evacuation?" Kong Wang replied.

"Isn't it Jinlong Temple?" Yue Qiu recalled that Master Kong Wang had mentioned earlier that he came from Shenshan.


Chapter 181 The Holy Court's Actions

A sacred mountain!?

Yue Qiu had heard of it. He was actually planning to visit the sacred mountain to see if it was truly as the rumors suggested, blessed by deities and enjoying prosperity.

If it were, then the people of Qingzhou would have a glimmer of hope.

However, just as he was about to set off, he encountered the scarred man committing evil deeds. Seeing injustice, he naturally had to intervene!

A thought struck Yue Qiu, and he asked, "Master Kongwang, are the rumors about the sacred mountain true?"

Having witnessed Kongwang's strength, he was already somewhat convinced.

Kong Wang nodded slightly: "Of course it's true. The sacred mountain is protected by the mountain god; the people there are safe and have no need to fear demons."

Upon hearing this, Yue Qiu was immediately excited and said, "Then wouldn't it mean that if everyone went to the sacred mountain, the lives of the people of Qingzhou could be saved?"

"It's easier said than done. The sacred mountain is vast, but people can only live at its foot. Currently, its population is only a little over 100,000. The people of Qingzhou are countless, numbering in the tens of millions. Where would they get food, drink, and shelter if they went to the sacred mountain?"

Kong Wang shook his head slightly. This was only one problem.

Tens of millions of people—the court officials who were supposed to manage them have long since fled. In other words, no one can control them now.

To put it nicely, they are the people; to put it bluntly, they are rioters.

If someone with impure intentions stirs up trouble, it will trigger a riot.

Therefore, what Yue Qiu said is simply unrealistic.

Qianshan County can accommodate some people, but at most one or two million; any more than that is simply unbearable.

Yue Qiu's heart sank: "What should we do? Can we ask that mountain god to intervene and protect Qingzhou?"

Kong Wang shook his head again.

"We must first stabilize the situation and prevent Qingzhou from descending into chaos. Otherwise, before the demons even arrive, Qingzhou will be in turmoil, with nearly half the population dead or wounded," Kong Wang said.

"Master Kong Wang, I'll go with you!" Yue Qiu immediately said.

Kong Wang nodded, and the two then set to work.

To stabilize the situation, they must first stabilize the prefectural city.

Only after stabilizing the prefectural city could they proceed to appease the other counties.

Qingzhou City was already in chaos, with arson, murder, looting, rape, and plunder occurring everywhere.

Several pavilions were engulfed in flames; if not extinguished, they would soon spread throughout the entire city.

Kong Wang and Yue Qiu had to deal with the arsonists and looters first.

The two men appeared in every corner of the city, showing no mercy to any thugs they encountered, killing them instantly!

It took them over an hour, chilling countless people to the bone, and those who had planned to cause trouble abandoned their schemes.

Rumors began to circulate in the city that a powerful figure remained in Qingzhou City, and any thugs who tried to take advantage of the chaos would be killed on the spot!

This greatly deterred the city's residents, and the situation temporarily stabilized.

Then came the task of extinguishing the fire. It wouldn't be easy for just two people to put out the fire; by the time they finished, nearly 30% of the city would have been burned down.

Therefore, Yue Qiu stepped forward, organizing the city's residents and calling on everyone to help put out the fire, otherwise they would all be burned to death in the city.

Initially, people were afraid to go out, but Yue Qiu repeatedly assured them that the thugs had been killed and Qingzhou City was now safe, so they could venture out without worry.

After a moment of silence, someone tentatively ventured out, followed by more and more people.

"Everyone, let's put out the fire! Protect our homes!" Yue Qiu shouted.

In the air, Kong Wang waved his hand, drawing water from the well below with spiritual energy. The water then formed a curtain and slammed into a burning pavilion.

With a hiss, the fire was instantly extinguished.

This scene, witnessed by everyone, instantly gave them hope.

The rumors were true; there really were still powerful figures stationed in Qingzhou City, and they were fighting the fire and protecting the city!

"Put out the fire! Don't just let the master do it alone; we have to help too!"

An old man shouted, instantly stirring up a wave of enthusiasm.

Men, women, young and old, all joined the firefighting effort.

Seeing this, Yue Qiu was thrilled and began leading the people in extinguishing the fire.

With everyone working together, and with the combined efforts of countless people, the fire in Qingzhou City was finally extinguished in less than half a day.

After extinguishing the fire, everyone looked up at Kong Wang descending from the sky.

"Immortal Master, it really is the Immortal Master!" People exclaimed excitedly.

"Master Kong Wang, we succeeded! The situation in Qingzhou City is stable!" Yue Qiu said excitedly.

Kong Wang nodded and said, "This is only the first step. Countless counties outside are already in chaos. To stabilize the situation in Qingzhou, we need more manpower."

Yue Qiu frowned upon hearing this.

Now there are only the two of them, and he is the useless one. With only the strength of the Tongfa Realm, how can he stabilize the situation?

"Then what should we do? It seems that Master Kong Wang is the only strong person left in Qingzhou." Yue Qiu said heavily.

"You're wrong. It's not just me." Kong Wang smiled, then looked towards the direction of the Divine Mountain and said, "Help should arrive soon."

He believed that with Qingzhou in such a state, the Mountain God would certainly not stand idly by. Bai Liang and the others would also take action.

...

The Holy Court.

Upon returning, Qing Yuci immediately reported the entire conversation in the Void to the two Saints.

However, he didn't pay much attention to the suddenly appearing divine mountain, only mentioning it briefly.

But the two Saints were incredibly perceptive, immediately grasping the key phrase "divine mountain."

Upon further inquiry, they learned that a change had occurred in Qingzhou.

"A change, not to be underestimated. At this critical juncture, it's best to prevent anything from happening; we must ensure absolute safety!" This was what the Demon Saint said.

The Demon Saint, however, remained unconvinced: "What variable? It's nothing more than the Great Chu Dynasty's futile struggle before its demise. Are they trying to use Qingzhou to test us?"

"They're very confident; it seems they have a lot of luck reserves."

"A lot? Then let's continue to deplete it. This loss of Qingzhou has reduced their luck. Let's destroy a few more states and see how much luck the Great Chu Dynasty has left?"

"In that case, for the sake of caution, let's continue to deplete the Great Chu Dynasty's luck. As for their anxious urging, let's pretend we didn't see it."

"Hmm, then it's decided. Let Qingyu Thorn take Qingzhou first."

"......"

Finally, Qingyu Thorn received instructions to dispatch demons to flatten Qingzhou!

This was a huge advantage; with tens of millions of lives at stake in Qingzhou, whoever was among the first to enter would feast to their hearts' content.

Therefore, Qingyu Ci immediately appointed his subordinates as the vanguard.

However, the demons on the other side were instantly outraged.

Why should the demon race be the vanguard, entering Qingzhou first and feasting, while they, the demon race, had to lag behind and drink the leftovers?

Although the demon race's semi-saints were still in seclusion, they still managed to find two eighth-tier demon lords to demand an explanation from Qingyu Ci.

As a result, the two sides argued endlessly about who should be the first to enter Qingzhou, causing a delay in the invasion.


Chapter 182 The Helper Has Arrived

Within the Holy Court, the demon race and the monster race were locked in a fierce struggle.

Each side wanted its own people to serve as the vanguard entering Qingzhou.

The lure of tens of millions of human lives as food was so immense that even some seventh-tier demons were tempted.

Qing Yuci sensed something was amiss.

Further delay could lead to unforeseen complications.

For example, a large number of demons were still lurking within Qingzhou. If they managed to take advantage of the situation, it would be a laughingstock.

Therefore, he immediately sought out several eighth-tier demon lords of the monster race to discuss the matter.

Originally, Qing Yuci wanted to give up 30% of the slots to the monsters, but the demon lords immediately objected.

Wasn't this bullying the honest?

The monsters and monsters were of equal strength; the slots should be divided at least 50/50, not 70/30?

Did they really think that just because the monsters' half-saints were in seclusion, they were easy to bully?

The demon lords were furious, showing no respect to Qing Yuci, and left after giving him a cold shoulder.

Qing Yuci's expression darkened upon seeing this.

However, infighting was forbidden within the Holy Court; this was a rule established by the saints, and even a half-saint could not violate it.

Finally, he lowered his demands to 60/40, but the demons still refused to agree.

Seeing that he couldn't gain the upper hand, and having heard that the demon lords were preparing to go to the Demon Abyss to awaken a demon half-saint who was in seclusion.

Given this, Qing Yuci had no choice but to give up and agree to the demons' demands for a 50/50 split.

The first batch of demon vanguard entering Qingzhou would be split equally between the demon and monster races.

With the spoils divided, the next step was to assign manpower.

Qing Yuci and the demon lords naturally assigned their trusted subordinates.

Once the troops were fully equipped, two sixth-tier demons would lead the advance into Qingzhou!

However, considering the previous slaying of the sixth-tier Blade Demon in Qingzhou, it was likely that a powerful human was lurking within the divine mountain.

After careful consideration, Qingyuci decided to have a seventh-tier Demon King accompany her to deal with the divine mountain.

Baijiang Prefecture.

Qingquan traveled at full speed and finally arrived in Baijiang Prefecture City after a day and a half.

Upon entering the city, a Yin God Realm expert sensed her arrival.

“Miss Qingquan, did you bring the divine water?”

A Yin God Realm expert appeared before Qingquan, his gaze fixed intently on the sheepskin pouch at her waist.

This was a storage magic artifact, given to Qingquan by Chu Wuliu, who wanted her to carry back more divine water.

“I brought it back,” Qingquan replied simply.

“Excellent! The Prefectural Governor has been waiting for you for a long time,” the Yin God Realm expert said with a smile.

“I also have urgent business with the Prefect.”

“Urgent business?” The man was taken aback, but didn’t ask further, and then led Qingquan to the Prefect’s residence.

Chu Wuliu was currently resting with his eyes closed, but his mind was preoccupied with the divine water.

The divine water he received last time was far too little; its effects had amazed him.

He estimated that with a little more divine water, he could dispel the obstacles accumulated over the years and break through to the sixth-tier Yang God Realm in one fell swoop!

He already knew of the Emperor’s edict; Qingzhou had been abandoned, which gave him a sense of crisis.

Baijiangzhou had already lost half its territory; if Qingzhou fell as well, Baijiangzhou would not be far from destruction.

“Lord Chu, Miss Qingquan has returned.” A voice came from outside.

Chu Wuliu opened his eyes and said, “Quickly, invite her in!”

A moment later, Qingquan was led into the main hall.

“Haha, Miss Qingquan, you must be tired. Did you get to see the Mountain God on your trip to Qingzhou?”

Chu Wuliu stood up enthusiastically, his expression genuinely concerned for the Mountain God.

“The Mountain God is supreme; how could I possibly see him? However, he did manifest himself some time ago, slaying a sixth-tier sword demon!” Qingquan said slowly.

“A sixth-tier sword demon?” Chu Wuliu was surprised, wondering if this was one of the two sixth-tier demons in Baijiangzhou.

He pressed for details and finally confirmed that the Mountain God had indeed slain the sixth-tier sword demon that plagued Baijiangzhou!

“Truly worthy of being the Mountain God I admire! Manifesting himself to suppress evil and protect all living beings—this is a true deity!” Chu Wuliu said reverently.

The Yin God Realm expert who had led Qingquan there was also full of praise, his face showing admiration.

Qingquan, seeing this, was inwardly puzzled.

She then took the sheepskin pouch from her waist and handed it to Chu Wuliu.

“My lord, the divine water is inside,” Qingquan said.

“Excellent, you’ve worked hard this time.” Chu Wuliu’s eyes lit up, and he immediately took the sheepskin pouch. He probed it with his divine sense, checked the amount of divine water, and looked satisfied.

“Such a divine item, the Mountain God is so generous in giving it to us, people he’s never even met. Only a god could be so magnanimous!”

Chu Wuliu exclaimed in admiration.

In his heart, he thought that as a follower of the Mountain God, he couldn’t be selfish; such a good thing as divine water had to be shared.

He happened to have a friend in Chizhou who had been trapped in the Yin God Realm for many years, repeatedly failing to break through to the Yang God Realm.

"Sending him the divine water should help him successfully break through to the Yang God Realm, right?"

"My lord, besides the divine water, I have something urgent to discuss," Qingquan said, his expression serious.

"Hmm? What urgent matter? Speak freely," Chu Wuliu said.

Qingquan carefully considered his words before explaining the current situation in Qingzhou and Bai Liang's request for help from Chu Wuliu in Qianshan County.

Finally, Qingquan mentioned that Qianshan County was a force protected by the Divine Mountain and was currently growing stronger.

Furthermore, Bai Liang and his companions were divinely favored by the Mountain God himself.

Originally ordinary people without cultivation talent, after a year, the strongest, Wu Ze, was already in the late stage of the Enlightenment Realm.

Even the weakest divinely favored was at the peak of the Profound Realm.

Qingquan said all this because he was worried that Chu Wuliu wouldn't want to help.

But he underestimated the reverence Chu Wuliu held for the Divine Mountain.

"What did you say? To hunt demons as sacrifices for the Mountain God!?"

Chu Wuliu completely ignored Qingquan's words about saving the people of Qingzhou from their suffering, focusing instead on the sacrificial ceremony.

"Uh...yes, to hunt fifth-tier demons within Qingzhou as sacrifices." Qingquan was somewhat surprised by Chu Wuliu's reaction.

"Such a momentous matter concerning the Mountain God, how could I be absent?" Chu Wuliu's expression was solemn as he waved his hand, saying, "Miss Qingquan, there's no need for further words. I will certainly go to Qingzhou to do my part for the Mountain God!"

"Such an important matter, I must go too!" The Yin God Realm expert beside him also spoke with a serious expression.

"Are we manpower enough? If not, I'll call Hu Zhengqiang and that old bald monk from Jinlong Temple too!" Chu Wuliu asked Qingquan.

Qingquan was somewhat stunned, then hesitated and said, "It probably isn't enough. There are dozens of fifth-tier demons in Qingzhou."

"If not enough, then call for reinforcements!"

Chu Wuliu said with great magnanimity.


Chapter 183 Qingzhou Demons, Calamity Imminent

Mount Shen, Qianshan County.

In the Mountain Pavilion, Wu Ze and the three Qilin brothers were ready to leave the mountain to hunt demons.

Wu Ze polished the ink-black blade, newly forged in the Divine Weapon Forge, until it gleamed.

This was a fifth-tier magic weapon, forged just two days ago.

After Bai Liang learned that Wu Ze was going to hunt demons, he mobilized the resources of Qianshan County, gathering considerable cultivation resources and collecting numerous magic weapons from various places.

First-tier was forged into second-tier, second-tier into third-tier, and third-tier into fifth-tier.

After exhausting their reserves of Crimson Spirit Pills and expending tens of thousands of kilograms of spirit rice, they finally gathered enough low-tier magic weapons to forge a fourth-tier magic weapon.

In the end, they forged this fifth-tier ink-black blade.

The three Qilin brothers kept glancing sideways at the ink-black blade in Wu Ze's arms, their eyes filled with astonishment!

Previously, they hadn't quite understood why Bai Liang was collecting so many low-level magical artifacts, but now, seeing the fifth-tier ink blade, they understood somewhat.

It turned out he wanted to forge high-level magical artifacts. But where in Qianshan County could they find a weapon refiner? Why had they never seen one before?

Moreover, fifth-tier magical artifacts weren't so easily forged.

Even the three brothers were still using fourth-tier magical artifacts.

Fifth-tier magical artifacts were extremely rare, and many cultivators' artifacts were inadequate for their cultivation level.

Low-tier magical artifacts, however, were countless.

High-tier magical artifacts, on the other hand, were incredibly difficult to obtain.

Wu Ze didn't hide anything from the three Qilin brothers, smiling as he said, "On the Divine Mountain, there are divine artifacts placed by the Mountain God, capable of forging all the magical artifacts in the world..."

He explained the Divine Weapon Forge to the three Qilin members, leaving them utterly stunned.

Ten magical artifacts could be combined to create a higher-tier artifact, with a 100% success rate?

What kind of heaven-defying artifact was this? However, considering it was the work of the Mountain God, it seemed normal.

Immediately, the Qilin members felt a surge of excitement. With the Divine Weapon Forge, didn't that mean they also had a chance to forge high-tier magical artifacts?

However, we don't have that many low-level magical artifacts on hand right now, so we'll have to wait for another opportunity to discuss it.

The most important thing right now is to first eliminate the demons within Qingzhou and hold a sacrificial ceremony to use them as offerings.

"Has Bai Jiangzhou's reinforcements not arrived yet? I wonder if Qingquan has delivered the message." Bai Liang walked into Shanyue Tower and said somewhat anxiously.

"It should be soon. If we travel at full speed, we can reach Baijiang Prefecture within two days."

Qilin thought for a moment and said.

As they were talking, Du Tiandao rushed into the Mountain Tower, his face beaming with excitement. "My lord, our helpers have arrived!" he exclaimed.

This news brought joy to Bai Liang and the others.

"Where are they? Quickly, invite them in!" Wu Ze immediately ordered.

Du Tiandao replied, "They're outside the city!"

Upon hearing this, the group left the Mountain Tower together and flew towards the outskirts of the county town.

Outside Qianshan County, a dozen or so Yin God Realm cultivators stood in mid-air.

They gazed up at the towering mountain before them, soaring into the clouds, majestic and imposing, exuding a chilling aura.

"This...this is the Divine Mountain? Such divine power, so majestic and towering, truly worthy of being the abode of gods."

"They say a mountain is not high because of its height, but because of the immortals dwelling there. But those who say this, upon seeing the Divine Mountain, would probably change their minds."

"With the Mountain God before us, all other mountains in the world pale in comparison!"

Chu Wuliu and the others were all astonished, their expressions filled with utmost reverence.

"Amitabha! This is the sacred mountain of my heart! After years of cultivation, I have finally arrived at the sacred mountain. May Buddha have mercy!"

The old monk was deeply moved, his eyes filled with fervent respect.

As for the Golden Dragon Temple?

Sorry, he didn't know it.

Hu Zhengqiang gazed at the sacred mountain, his heart pounding with excitement.

To see the Mountain God in this lifetime is truly a blessing!

When Bai Liang and the others arrived outside Qianshan County, they encountered Chu Wuliu and his companions worshipping the sacred mountain.

Their faces were filled with utmost reverence, even bordering on fanaticism.

For a moment, it was impossible to discern who were the true followers of the Mountain God.

"Fellow Daoists, I thank you all for coming from afar to help me." Bai Liang stepped forward and bowed politely to everyone.

Chu Wuliu immediately waved his hand and said, "You flatter me. We have all come to the sacred mountain to pay homage to the Mountain God. Moreover, the demons we hunted this time are to be offered as sacrifices to the Mountain God. As believers of the Mountain God, we naturally have to do our part!"

"With the sacred mountain before us, the Mountain God is the Buddha of this world. Even if the path ahead is destroyed, he will calmly move forward. This is what we believers should do," the old monk said solemnly.

The other Yin God Realm cultivators echoed this sentiment, indicating that they were all acting on behalf of the Mountain God.

Bai Liang and the others exchanged bewildered glances.

To be honest, they all had a few doubts in their hearts: were these people putting on an act?

They had never met before, and this was their first time visiting the sacred mountain, yet their reverence for the Mountain God surpassed even their own.

The effects of this divine water... are amazing!

After confirming several times that Chu Wuliu and the others were sincere and there were no problems, Bai Liang warmly invited the ten Yin God Realm cultivators into Qianshan County.

First, he showed them around the development of Qianshan County, and finally led them to the temple to pay homage to the Mountain God.

The ten Yin God Realm cultivators, upon entering the temple, all wore incredibly solemn and respectful expressions as they worshipped the Mountain God.

Among them, the two old monks from Jinlong Temple were the most respectful, their eyes filled with fervor, making Chu Wuliu and the others feel somewhat ashamed.

They thought to themselves: Damn it, these two monks stole the spotlight. I wonder if the Mountain God is even watching.

In fact, Li Yue had been watching everything from beginning to end.

To be honest, he felt somewhat uncomfortable with this unexpected group of Yin God Realm followers.

Ultimately, it was because his methods were less than honorable—relying on the side effects of the divine water to subdue these people.

Li Yue thought to himself that his moral compass was too upright; he even felt a slight sense of guilt.

If he were free from such burdens, he probably would have given the divine water to everyone.

Unfortunately, Li Yue wouldn't do that. The divine water could be used to subdue cultivators, but for ordinary people, there was no need for it.

After all, there were legitimate ways to recruit followers, so why use the divine water?

Besides, there wasn't that much divine water.

Looking at Chu Wuliu and his group of ten, Chu Wuliu, Hu Zhengqiang, and one of the old monks were the strongest, all at the peak of the Yin God Realm, just one step away from entering the Yang God Realm.

With them, along with the three brothers Wu Ze and Qilin, dealing with the demons within Qingzhou would be a piece of cake.

Looking into the temple, Li Yue thought that these people had traveled a long way to worship him, and he couldn't just leave without showing any appreciation.

"I'll just manifest a little divine power to show my approval," Li Yue thought to himself.

So, after Chu Wuliu and the others finished paying their respects to the Mountain God, a dazzling golden light suddenly appeared on the sacred mountain and descended into the temple.


Chapter 184 The Hunting Time Has Arrived

The sudden change startled everyone present.

Before they could react, divine light erupted from the temple, and a divine aura enveloped them, inspiring awe in everyone.

"This...this is the Mountain God manifesting!" Qilin exclaimed, his voice filled with surprise.

Chu Wuliu and the others, experiencing the divine power of a deity for the first time, were both deeply shaken and incredibly excited.

"The Mountain God has manifested! This means he acknowledges us!"

Chu Wuliu's voice was urgent, his face flushed with excitement.

The two monks' eyes shone with fervor.

The others were equally excited.

"Mountain God, please rest assured, we will do our utmost to eradicate the demons within Qingzhou and rid the people of this scourge!" Chu Wuliu promised excitedly.

The golden light seemed to respond, flickering a few times, and strands of starlight descended from the sky, falling upon everyone. Instantly, they felt refreshed and enlightened, as if a fog had been cleared, revealing a sudden clarity.

Everyone present felt they had benefited greatly, gaining much insight. They felt that if they digested this knowledge carefully after returning home, they would reap significant rewards.

The three Qilin brothers' faces were flushed red, almost unable to suppress their cultivation and breaking through to the Yin God Realm.

"Qilin, I can't hold on much longer," the Black Dragon said, his eyes bloodshot.

This starlight contained divine essence, its aid to cultivators was immense, practically divine.

The Fire Phoenix was also flushed, and just as the two were about to give in and break through to the Yin God Realm,

the starlight fell upon them again, instantly calming their restless cultivation and suppressing it in the blink of an eye.

The Black Dragon and the Fire Phoenix both exhaled, having almost broken through to the Yin God Realm prematurely and missed the Full Moon Yin God Realm.

Fortunately, the Mountain God noticed them at the last moment and easily suppressed their cultivation levels.

Bai Liang benefited greatly; his cultivation level loosened, and he vaguely touched the threshold of the Enlightenment Realm. He estimated that after a few days of seclusion, he would successfully break through to the fourth level of the Enlightenment Realm.

Wu Ze, He Liyuan, Du Tiandao, and others also benefited immensely, each of them experiencing a significant leap in their cultivation levels.

Chu Wuliu and the others also gained a great deal. Among them, the old monk, whose cultivation was the highest, vaguely felt the stirrings of the Yin God.

This was a sign of breaking through to the Yang God Realm.

This filled him with immense joy!

The Mountain God truly lived up to his reputation as a living Buddha; with just a slight intervention, he had allowed him to touch the edge of the Yang God Realm.

Everyone once again sincerely thanked the god for his grace.

Then, the golden light slowly dissipated, and everyone seemed to breathe a sigh of relief.

The divine might had been far too oppressive for them.

Although the Mountain God didn't intentionally display his power, a deity is still a deity; the invisible divine might he exuded was enough to terrify these cultivators.

After leaving the temple, Bai Liang led everyone to the Mountain Tower.

Chu Wuliu and the others were still immersed in the Mountain God's divine might when Wu Ze brought up the main matter, gradually bringing them back to their senses.

"Within Qingzhou, there are currently fourteen known fifth-tier demons. Among them, five are at the peak of the fifth tier," Qilin said in a deep voice.

These are only the demons openly lurking; who knows how many more are hidden in the shadows.

"Those demons are nothing but chickens and dogs, all at the fifth rank!" Hu Zhengqiang said arrogantly, looking down on the demons within Qingzhou.

He had the confidence to say this; his peak fifth-rank strength, coupled with his Great Mountain and River Hand Seal, was enough to allow him to roam freely in Qingzhou.

"Amitabha! How can demons sleep soundly in the land of Mount Ling?" The old monk revealed a fierce and menacing expression.

"Just say it directly. Those demons are all over there. Let's go straight there and kill them all!" Chu Wuliu said coldly.

Wu Ze and the others exchanged glances, surprised that their helpers from Baijiangzhou were even more eager than they were.

"Time is of the essence. In that case, let's set off now, leave the mountain, and hunt down the demons!" Wu Ze immediately said in a deep voice.

Then, the dozen or so people flew directly from the Mountain Tower, heading towards a certain direction in Qingzhou.

Wu Ze and the three Qilin brothers, along with ten Yin God Realm experts from Baijiang Prefecture, began their hunt!

Bai Liang and the others remained in Qianshan County to prepare for the sacrificial ceremony.

In a valley somewhere in Qingzhou.

Two demonic creatures, resembling wolves, lay prone, their eyes filled with ferocity.

The wolf demon said, "Brother Wolf, now is the perfect time. Why not leave the valley and enjoy the blood sacrifice delivered to our doorstep?"

The two demons already knew of the chaos in Qingzhou.

The Wolf demon said ominously, "No rush. We can leave the valley after the other demons have made their move. With tens of millions of lives to be sacrificed in Qingzhou, are we worried about not having enough?"

Upon hearing this, the wolf demon's massive head bobbed up and down.

"Brother Wolf is absolutely right. Qingzhou already belongs to the Holy Court; there's plenty of blood sacrifice. There's no need to worry about not having enough," it said slowly.

No sooner had he finished speaking than three or four auras, all at the fifth-tier Yin God Realm, emanated from above the valley.

“You wicked creatures! Colluding with each other, this old monk saw you hiding here at a glance!” The old monk, holding his alms bowl, glared angrily down at the wolf demon and the wolf-like creature below.

“What?! How did a human Yin God Realm expert appear here?”

The wolf demon looked up, staring incredulously at the four figures above the valley, unable to help but gasp.

Four Yin God Realm experts had suddenly appeared! Weren't the imperial court's experts all supposed to have retreated?

Could this be a false alarm, a ploy to confuse the situation before swiftly annihilating the demons?

“Brother Wolf, run!” the wolf-like creature's voice rang out. The wolf demon turned around, but the creature had already vanished.

“Amitabha, you think you can escape from this old monk?” The old monk sneered, raising his hand to unleash the signature Buddhist spell, the Great Luo Seal!

A gigantic Buddha's hand descended from the sky, crashing directly into a thicket of bushes.

Instantly, blood splattered for dozens of meters, and the two wolf-like demons tumbled out in a sorry state.

The other Yin God Realm experts didn't hesitate either. Those with magical artifacts unleashed them, while those without unleashed their full power, giving them no chance.

In an instant, countless spells and magical artifacts rained down. The two wolf-like demons didn't even have time to scream before they were instantly killed.

Before dying, both demons couldn't understand why the humans had gone mad, sending so many experts to attack them.

Hadn't the imperial court ceded Qingzhou?

Shouldn't Qingzhou have been slaughtered by demons, with countless humans being used as food?

How come it's the other way around, and they're the ones being hunted down?

"Leave the corpse intact! Don't make it too gruesome; the offering must be of good quality," the old monk said.

Meanwhile, similar scenes were unfolding in various corners of Qingzhou.

Wu Ze and his group split into three teams, searching one demon's hiding place after another.

The demons were basically killed the moment they showed themselves; in less than half a day, half of them had been wiped out!


Chapter 185 Even the demons were dumbfounded

Such a commotion couldn't be kept secret.

Soon, the remaining demons also learned that a group of human cultivators at the Yin God Realm had banded together to slaughter demons within Qingzhou's borders.

Moreover, it was rumored that they were leaving the corpses intact, claiming they would take them back as sacrifices.

This immediately bewildered the surviving demons, and simultaneously enraged them!

Rebellion! Rebellion!

When did they demons fall to such a state, being hunted by humans and used as sacrifices?

If demons didn't show their power, would they be considered mere wild beasts?

So the remaining demons began to unite, and even found some demons hiding in the shadows—a dozen or so fifth-tier demons in total—and banded together to launch a counter-encirclement against Wu Ze and his group!

Wu Ze and his group, of course, weren't fools. A direct confrontation might have an advantage, but it would be difficult to gain the upper hand.

Therefore, they temporarily abandoned their demon-hunting plan and retreated to Qingzhou City.

Upon entering Qingzhou City, Wu Ze happened to meet Kong Wang. Having not seen each other for a long time, the two exchanged pleasantries.

After the greetings, Kong Wang looked at Chu Wuliu and the others, his expression somewhat puzzled.

Seeing this, Wu Ze whispered in his ear the reason why Chu Wuliu and the others had come to Qingzhou to help.

Kong Wang was astonished upon hearing this.

He hadn't expected the divine water to have such miraculous effects; it had instantly provided him with more than ten trustworthy Yin God Realm helpers.

Kong Wang then learned about Wu Ze's group's achievements.

In half a day, they had slain five demons and three monsters.

They had wiped out half of the demons and monsters in Qingzhou, and all of them had intact corpses.

"These demon corpses are to be used for the sacrificial ceremony for the Mountain God?" Kong Wang asked in surprise.

Wu Ze nodded and smiled, "We can't hunt any stronger demons, so we can only compensate with numbers. About twenty fifth-tier demons should be enough."

"This is a bit tricky!" Qilin walked over and looked at Wu Ze, saying, "The remaining demons are grouped together; the plan to catch lone ones won't work. We need to think of another way."

Chu Wuliu and his group also gathered around, ready to discuss countermeasures with Wu Ze.

"Is there any way to disperse them?" Wu Ze pondered for a moment before speaking.

Hu Zhengqiang shook his head: "Demons aren't stupid. Being alone means death; they won't separate."

Similarly, if they were alone, they would be in danger.

"That's troublesome." Wu Ze frowned.

He could use the Swift Travel Talisman to harass the demons, but what good would that do?

Besides consuming his own spiritual energy, it wouldn't cause much trouble for the demons.

"Let's just fight them head-on. It's just a battle; we can't possibly lose to these demons," Chu Wuliu said in a deep voice.

Their group was slightly stronger than the demons.

This was thanks to catching the demons off guard and wiping out half of them with a preemptive strike.

"No, that would be too much of a loss. You all came all the way from Baijiang Prefecture to help us; we can't let anything happen to you," Wu Ze immediately shook his head.

A head-on clash would at least result in the death of half their men.

Although he wasn't familiar with Chu Wuliu and the others, they were all working for the Divine Mountain; they were one of their own.

"Perhaps this humble monk could leave the city and carry out an assassination,"

At this moment, Kong Wang suddenly spoke up.

Everyone turned to look, and Kong Wang's aura was undisguised—late-stage Enlightenment Realm.

Chu Wuliu frowned, puzzled, and asked, "And you are...?"

Wu Ze quickly introduced Kong Wang to everyone. Upon learning that this young monk was a chosen one blessed by the Mountain God, everyone's gaze immediately changed.

They had all witnessed Wu Ze's performance in the demon-slaying.

His extraordinary speed had truly astonished them.

He was only at the peak of the Enlightenment Realm, yet even the most powerful old monk present couldn't match his speed.

He truly deserved to be a chosen one blessed by the Mountain God; his strength was extraordinary!

"Kong Wang, are you confident?" The old monk treated Kong Wang more kindly, after all, they were all members of the Buddhist sect.

Furthermore, Kong Wang had received the Mountain God's blessing; in the old monk's eyes, Kong Wang was no different from a Buddhist disciple.

Kong Wang nodded slightly.

The Divine Concealment Talisman gave him this confidence; as long as they weren't above the Yang God level, he could come and go freely and without restraint.

Wu Ze knew about Kong Wang's talisman, so he felt comfortable letting Kong Wang leave the city.

"This humble monk will go out to assassinate. If the demons become confused, you should seize the opportunity to attack and wipe them out in one fell swoop!" Kong Wang left these words and concealed himself as he left the city.

The others remained inside Qingzhou City, watching the demons outside, waiting for their chance.

Outside the city.

A multitude of demons gathered, their demonic aura overwhelming.

On the ground lay many people, brutally murdered and reduced to withered bones, who had been captured by them to regain their strength.

"Disgrace! Today is a disgrace to the Holy Court! We are the ones who hunt humans, but today the tables have turned, and the humans have taken advantage of our unpreparedness to hunt down seven or eight demons!"

The leader, the Big-Nosed Bull, was furious, his nostrils spitting out hot breath that wilted the wild grass instantly upon contact.

"We'll break through the city today! Qingzhou City's fortune has been lost. The court has withdrawn its officials, and they must have taken the official seals that control the fortune with them as well."

A humanoid demon covered in maggots and oozing foul pus spoke coldly.

"Heh, Corpse Demon, do you even have a brain? Even without fortune, can we defeat those humans in the city?" the Big-Nosed Bull scoffed.

"Just because we can't defeat them doesn't mean we shouldn't. What's the point of staying here?" the Corpse Demon retorted coldly.

"Wait for reinforcements. The Holy Court has already sent people. We just need to hold off these human powerhouses, and once our help arrives, we can take them all in one fell swoop!" the Big-Nosed Bull said calmly.

The Corpse Demon snorted coldly after hearing this, but said nothing more, tacitly agreeing with the Big-Nosed Ox's words.

Unbeknownst to them, a monk had already entered their midst and overheard the conversation between the demon and the monster.

Kong Wang, his figure concealed as if he were in an empty space, silently approached a chicken demon at the edge after listening to the conversation.

This was a chicken demon with incredibly vibrant feathers, and two gray combs drooped down its head.

Kong Wang seized the opportunity, taking out the fifth-tier sharpening knife that Wu Ze had lent him, and slashed down at the chicken demon's neck.

With a soft thud, blood spurted several meters, and a chicken head the size of a basketball fell to the ground.

The head was severed from the body, yet the chicken demon could still let out a piercing scream!

Kong Wang, having landed a successful blow, immediately retreated several hundred meters.

The sudden turn of events caused the demons' expressions to change drastically.

Upon seeing the chicken demon's horrific state, their hearts sank even further.

"Be careful! A human expert has infiltrated and is hiding nearby!" Big-Nosed Ox gritted his teeth, constantly scanning his surroundings.


Chapter 186 When the enemy advances, we retreat; when the enemy camps, we harass.

"No, Brother Kun!" A fifth-tier demon general cried out in anguish at the dead chicken demon, then his eyes turned red as he glared around.

"Who dared to assassinate my Brother Kun? Show yourself!" it roared.

The other demons were also extremely vigilant, feeling uncertain.

What kind of human cultivator was this? To be able to silently infiltrate their ranks and assassinate them—this was far beyond their imagination!

The Corpse Demon's pale, dead fish eyes darted around as it surveyed its surroundings. It said coldly, "It should be a powerful human expert skilled in concealment! Everyone, don't scatter, gather together!"

Upon hearing this, the demons sprang into action, their previously scattered formation instantly becoming denser.

Between all of them, no matter how well the opponent concealed themselves, they would have nowhere to strike.

In the distance, the Void Demons frowned slightly at their response.

This had indeed caused them considerable trouble.

But that didn't matter; he wasn't fighting alone.

In Qingzhou City.

Wu Ze and the others were overjoyed to see Kong Wang succeed in his attack, slaying the fifth-tier chicken demon.

"He succeeded!" Wu Ze said with a smile.

"The Chosen One truly lives up to his name! Such a method of concealment is completely flawless. The Mountain God's blessing is indeed powerful!"

Chu Wuliu and the others were incredibly amazed!

To this day, they still didn't know how Kong Wang had made his move; they only saw the chicken demon's head fly high into the air in an instant, instantly dead.

The demon race didn't have the concept of Yin or Yang gods, so a severed head meant death.

However, the demons quickly found a way to counter it, all gathering together, revealing no flaws.

Seeing this, Wu Ze said in a deep voice, "With this formation, Kong Wang has no way to attack."

"Then it's up to us!" Chu Wuliu said.

Thus, Wu Ze and his group of Yin God Realm experts stormed out of the city, their momentum overwhelming, and charged directly at the demons.

The demons, who had been on guard against Kong Wang's assassination attempts, were nearly terrified to the sight of a group of powerful humans emerging from the city.

"Good! They dare to come out of the city! Everyone, let's fight these humans!" The big-nosed bull roared furiously, its hooves shattering the ground.

The remaining demons also turned fierce, roaring as they charged towards the humans.

The two sides clashed instantly, the ground trembling, explosions echoing through the sky, and countless blood blossoms blooming in the air.

In the first exchange, both sides were bleeding, but the demons were at a disadvantage, having been caught off guard.

The sight of blood ignited the demons' fighting power, their eyes gleaming with bloodlust, as if they had activated a bloodthirsty rage mode.

"Retreat! Don't engage them head-on."

Wu Ze surveyed his group's situation. Several men were injured, but thankfully not seriously.

Continuing the fight would still give them the upper hand, but there was no need.

The others nodded and began their retreat, protecting each other.

"Ambush us and then try to escape? There's no such thing as a free lunch!"

The Big-Nosed Bull's fur turned a deep red with rage. With a deafening moo, its horns flashed red, and a gigantic phantom of a savage bull appeared, dozens of feet tall.

The phantom roared to the sky and then charged towards Wu Ze and the others.

Just as Chu Wuliu was about to make a move, the old monk stopped him.

"Fellow Daoist Chu, please step back and let this old monk handle this," the old monk said calmly.

Upon hearing this, Chu Wuliu stopped his spellcasting.

The old monk lowered his head and began chanting Buddhist scriptures.

One by one, complex and incomprehensible Sanskrit characters emerged from the void and began to swirl around the group.

The Sanskrit characters continued to appear, flying rapidly, and finally formed a dazzling golden bell, trapping Wu Ze and the others within.

The phantom of the bull roared deafeningly and charged directly at the Sanskrit bell.

A clear, resounding "Dong!" rang out.

The phantom of the bull stiffened, then fell straight down, slowly dissipating.

The bull with the large nostrils coughed up a mouthful of blood and swayed precariously.

After blocking the charging bull, Wu Ze and his group continued their retreat until they successfully entered Qingzhou City.

The demons could only stand outside the city, glaring angrily, but dared not advance further.

"Damn human blood sacrifices! When the Holy Court army arrives, you will pay a bloody price!" the Big-Nosed Bull roared furiously.

But before he could finish speaking, another scream rang out among them.

Turning around sharply, they saw demonic energy continuously spewing from the black mist, followed by a demon corpse falling lifelessly.

The human assassin lurking in the shadows had returned!

The Big-Nosed Bull and the Corpse Demon's faces were terrifyingly grim, their hearts already burning with pent-up rage.

"It's that despicable assassin again!" the Corpse Demon said sinisterly.

The remaining demons felt a chill run down their spines, constantly glancing around, fearing they would be the next to be assassinated.

“Don’t be afraid, gather together! As long as we stick together, those rats in the gutter won’t have a chance to strike!” the Big-Nosed Ox said again.

So, the demons quickly gathered together.

While the Big-Nosed Ox was filled with anxiety, Wu Ze and the others in the city, seeing the demons huddled together again, shouted, “Everyone, Kong Wang has succeeded again! The demons are scared and have banded together. Let’s go out again, harass them, and create an opportunity for Kong Wang!”

Then a group of human Yin God Realm experts once again stormed out of the city.

Seeing the aggressive human experts approaching, the Big-Nosed Ox’s face twisted with anger, and his defenses crumbled.

Were they being played like monkeys?

With the group of human Yin God Realm experts about to reach them, the demons had no choice but to steel themselves and form a battle formation, engaging the humans in combat.

But just like before, after a single exchange, Wu Ze and his group quickly retreated, leaving the demons with numerous wounds.

Similarly, Wu Ze's side also suffered injuries, with the three Qilin members being the most seriously wounded, as they were only at the Enlightenment Realm.

Wu Ze instructed the three to heal their wounds first, leaving the rest to them.

The demons had lost a fifth-tier demon general, and many others sustained injuries of varying degrees.

Seeing Wu Ze and his group retreat into Qingzhou City, the Big-Nosed Ox, before it could even vent its anger, immediately said, "Quickly regroup, be careful..."

Unfortunately, before it could finish speaking, a bloodcurdling scream was heard.

Looking in the direction of the sound, a massive bear demon collapsed to the ground, a deep gash in its chest!

The Corpse Demon was utterly enraged, roaring, "We can't just sit here and wait to die! If this continues, we'll be worn down by the humans sooner or later!"

His face hardened, and he coldly declared, "If they come out again, we'll fight them to the death! Even if the humans retreat into the city, don't be afraid, just charge in!"

This was the only way to break the deadlock; otherwise, the dozen or so demons present would be assassinated one by one by the hidden human powerhouses.

"Kill! Fight them!" the other demons roared furiously, their eyes bloodshot.


Chapter 187 Offense and Defense in Transformation

Kong Wang retreated a hundred meters away, the demonic blood still dripping from his ink-black blade.

In this commotion, he had already killed two demons and one monster.

Wu Ze and his group had also killed a fifth-tier demon, directly reducing the demons' fighting strength by four.

Now only nine demons remained.

Hearing the Corpse Demon's words, Kong Wang knew there was no longer a chance for assassination.

What followed was a fierce battle.

However, this was enough. They already held the advantage, and now the demons had lost four fighting strength, with many more wounded.

It would be much easier to deal with them now.

Seeing there was no chance, Kong Wang concealed himself and returned to Qingzhou City.

Upon returning to the city, he told Wu Ze and the others about the situation.

"That Corpse Demon is quite intelligent, what a pity. Otherwise, we could have continued to wear them down and easily taken down this group of demons," Hu Zhengqiang said regretfully.

"That's not bad. The rest are nothing but chickens and dogs. We can take them down with a little effort," Chu Wuliu said confidently.

"Then let's make preparations. We'll leave the city soon and fight those demons!"

Wu Ze said solemnly.

Everyone nodded and began to meditate. Those with injuries took pills to heal, trying to recover as quickly as possible.

The three Qilin brothers, enduring their injuries, approached Wu Ze, expressing their desire to participate in the final battle.

But Wu Ze refused outright, saying in a deep voice, "Qilin brothers, I see your desire to contribute to the Divine Mountain. Now that you're injured, focus on healing. Leave the rest to us."

Seeing Wu Ze's firm refusal, the Qilin brothers could only helplessly retreat to heal their wounds.

"Kong Wang, during the final battle, you should remain hidden," Wu Ze said to Kong Wang.

Kong Wang nodded, indicating he understood.

"This humble monk can leave the city first and ambush them from behind, catching them completely off guard," Kong Wang said.

Wu Ze nodded, and let Kong Wang leave the city first.

A quarter of an hour later, after everyone had finished their breathing exercises, Wu Ze said in a deep voice, "Fellow Daoists, please follow me out of the city for the final battle!"

With that, Wu Ze led the charge out of the city gate.

Chu Wuliu and the others followed closely behind, each of them filled with surging excitement.

It was exhilarating! So exhilarating!

They hadn't experienced this feeling of freely slaying demons for a very, very long time.

In the past, every conflict with demons was one of forbearance, retreat, and defense.

When had they ever taken the initiative to attack? Let alone hunt demons.

Today's action filled Chu Wuliu and the others with unparalleled excitement, exhilaration, and satisfaction!

After being suppressed by demons for so many years, their pent-up emotions had finally been released today.

All of this is thanks to the Mountain God. Without his backing, they wouldn't have had the confidence to hunt demons.

From today onward, the roles of offense and defense between humans and demons may be reversed!

Wu Ze led a vast army of Yin God Realm experts out of the city gates.

This time, he wielded his ink-black blade, his eyes resolute, his Divine Speed ​​Talisman held back, waiting for an opportunity.

The demons, seeing the humans attack again, were enraged.

This time, they wouldn't be treated like dogs.

"They're here again! Kill them! Fight these humans to the death!"

The Corpse Demon roared, the maggots on its face falling off incessantly.

"Moo! Kill!" The Big-Nosed Bull charged forward, tearing off its two horns, which instantly transformed into two curved blades.

It struck first, attacking before Wu Ze and the others could even approach.

The horned blades in its hands were incredibly sharp, cutting even the air itself.

Wu Ze was the first to be hit; his Divine Enlightenment Realm strength was naturally no match for the Big-Nosed Bull's attack.

"I'll take it!" Chu Wuliu wanted to shield Wu Ze from the Big-Nosed Bull.

However, Wu Ze waved his hand, saying in a deep voice, "No need, leave this bull to me!"

Activating his Divine Speed ​​Talisman, Wu Ze took a step, seemingly teleporting, easily dodging the Big-Nosed Bull's attack.

"This... what kind of speed is this!?"

The bull demon's face showed horror, completely unable to comprehend it.

While the bull demon was stunned, Wu Ze appeared behind it, raised his ink blade, and slashed down fiercely.

A sizzling sound rang out. The bull demon, a peak fifth-tier cultivator, reacted with incredible speed, dodging the falling ink-black blade just as it struck, but a patch of its hair was still sliced ​​off.

These hairs, as hard as steel needles, fell to the ground and embedded themselves deeply in the earth.

"How is this possible? You're only a peak fourth-tier cultivator, how can you possess such strength? And this speed? This is unreasonable!"

The bull demon's eyes widened like saucers, utterly incredulous.

Then it remembered the assassin who had been lurking in the shadows. This person before it was just as eerie as that assassin!

The others also engaged the demon in battle.

Since Chu Wuliu and his group were already stronger, and outnumbered, the battle was one-sided from the very beginning.

The demon was suppressed, almost powerless to fight back.

"Kill! Fight them!"

The Corpse Demon roared, chunks of rotting flesh falling from its body until only a skeleton remained. But this wasn't weakening; it had grown stronger.

"This place is filled with evil spirits. My Buddhist teachings are specifically designed to counter these!"

The old monk directly confronted the Corpse Demon, chanting scriptures incessantly, but the scriptures seemed to have no effect on it.

The Corpse Demon sneered, "Bald monk, is this what you call Buddhist teachings?"

The old monk looked somewhat embarrassed, inwardly furious.

"Hmph! Since you, this beast, don't understand Mahayana Buddhism, then I, this old monk, know a little bit of martial arts!"

With that, the old monk rolled up his sleeves, his hands glowing with golden light, and swung his fists at the Corpse Demon's face.

Behind him, Kong Wang, watching the one-sided battle, also began to move.

He moved directly behind a demon desperately resisting an attack, and struck it unexpectedly, delivering a palm strike to its back.

The demon let out a scream, then died instantly from the follow-up blow.

Chu Wuliu and Hu Zhengqiang were the two strongest besides the old monk.

The two of them, working together, killed a demon in just a few rounds, extremely efficiently.

In less than a quarter of an hour, only the Corpse Demon and the Bull Demon remained alive.

The Corpse Demon was relentlessly beaten by the old monk, its bones shattering on the ground.

Finally, the old monk seemed to have satisfied his anger, smashing his alms bowl onto the Corpse Demon's head, killing it completely.

The Bull Demon, however, continued its fierce battle with Wu Ze.

Chu Wuliu and the others did not step forward to help, because they realized that Wu Ze was using the Bull Demon as practice.

Most importantly, they realized that Wu Ze was not in danger. His superhuman speed was simply too much for the clumsy bull demon to handle.

The bull demon had already witnessed the deaths of all its teammates, and its heart sank to the bottom.

It wanted to retreat, but the humans had already surrounded it.

It felt incredibly aggrieved. How could it have imagined that it would one day be suppressed and toyed with by the humans?


Chapter 188 A Triumphant Return

Wu Ze used the Swift Travel Talisman to relentlessly harass the bull demon.

Although he was only at the Enlightenment Realm, his constant harassment could eventually kill it.

Without interference from others, the bull demon finally couldn't withstand this toying.

The human's speed was simply too fast. It tried its best but couldn't even touch the hem of his clothes. It couldn't fathom where a human cultivator at the Enlightenment Realm could possess such speed.

After another quarter of an hour, Wu Ze seized the bull demon's momentary lapse in attention and decapitated it with a single stroke, blood splattering into the air.

He was utterly exhausted and nearly fell from the sky.

Fighting against a higher-level opponent, even with the Swift Travel Talisman, had exceeded his limits.

In truth, the bull demon wasn't weak. If it weren't for Chu Wuliu and the others watching over it, instilling despair and making the bull demon realize it had no chance of survival, thus instilling fear,

Wu Ze certainly wouldn't have been able to defeat it if the bull demon had fought desperately.

"A Divine Enlightenment Realm cultivator slaying a peak fifth-tier demon—truly a miracle!" Chu Wuliu and the others exclaimed in astonishment.

"It's all thanks to the Mountain God's blessing; otherwise, how could I possess such strength?" Wu Ze said modestly.

After resting for a while, the group began collecting the demon corpses.

This time, it could be said that all the fifth-tier demons within Qingzhou were wiped out in one fell swoop.

For the time being, the crisis for the people of Qingzhou was averted.

After counting the demon corpses, there were a total of seventeen bodies. Two corpses had been smashed apart due to the force of the attack.

It was Hu Zhengqiang who did it; the Great Mountain and River Seal was too domineering, unleashing its strongest power from the outset, making it impossible to hold back.

Chu Wuliu was somewhat displeased with this, saying, "Old Hu, can't you be a little gentler each time? You've destroyed all the offerings for the Mountain God. If there aren't enough, your mistake will be huge!"

Hu Zhengqiang knew he was in the wrong, but he didn't take Chu Wuliu's reprimand to heart at all. He turned his head, snorted, and didn't respond.

Of the seventeen demon corpses that remained intact, several were of questionable quality.

For example, the Rotting Corpse Demon's rotten flesh had already fallen off, but after its death, it had strangely grown back, crawling with maggots, densely packed, utterly disgusting.

These nauseating demon corpses were definitely unsuitable as offerings.

After careful selection, four more corpses were finally chosen, all of them demonic creatures.

For no other reason than that demonic creatures looked bizarre and were quite terrifying, making them unsuitable as offerings.

Demons, on the other hand, were perfectly suitable.

In the end, twelve demon corpses were chosen as sacrifices, all of superior quality.

This was a very successful trip, relieving the crisis for the people of Qingzhou and finding a considerable number of sacrifices for the sacrificial ceremony—a double benefit.

Wu Ze found Kong Wang and asked if he wanted to return to the Divine Mountain to participate in the sacrificial ceremony.

Kong Wang thought for a moment and finally nodded, indicating that he would return to the Divine Mountain to participate in the sacrificial ceremony.

This was an important matter, and he naturally had to return.

However, before returning, he had some matters to attend to, so he wouldn't return with Wu Ze and the others.

Wu Ze nodded and, without waiting for Kong Wang, returned directly with Chu Wuliu and the others.

Kong Wang stayed in Qingzhou City and went to find Yue Qiu.

"Master Kong Wang, are all those seniors from the Divine Mountain?" Yue Qiu's tone was quite excited at this moment.

He had never seen so many powerful humans gathered together, even united to hunt demons.

This scene was truly exhilarating.
