Especially when the demon corpses were dragged into the city, Qingzhou City trembled.

The people were initially horrified, then filled with excitement.

Even fifth-tier demons had been hunted down. He'd heard those immortal masters came from the Divine Mountain. Was the Divine Mountain really that powerful?

Kong Wang nodded calmly: "Yes, they are helpers from Baijiang Prefecture, but they all worship the Mountain God."

Yue Qiu was even more shocked upon hearing this.

The seniors from Baijiang Prefecture had become believers of the Divine Mountain. This proved that the Divine Mountain's strength was not to be underestimated!

"Master Kong Wang, doesn't that mean Qingzhou is saved!?" Yue Qiu said excitedly.

"Everything is still uncertain; we'll have to see what the demons do."

Kong Wang shook his head. A monk doesn't lie. Who knows what kind of powerful being the demons will send?

Yue Qiu's excited heart calmed down.

The Divine Mountain is so powerful, but it relies entirely on that mysterious mountain god.

But who knows if that mountain god will intervene to protect the entire Qingzhou?

Or perhaps the Holy Court will send even stronger demons that the Divine Mountain might not be able to handle.

Outside Qingzhou City.

In a dark, narrow valley crevice.

A figure hid within—it was Nianmo, summoned by Dao Mo to provide support, only to be slapped as soon as she arrived near the Divine Mountain.

She looked slightly disheveled; the bright red mark on her cheek was still there.

A trace of divine power lingered on it, constantly burning her demonic energy.

Nianmo was greatly terrified. What kind of being resided on that Divine Mountain?

She'd been slapped from so far away, and the burn was still fresh.

She was a sixth-tier Demon General!

Such strength would place her in the upper-middle core tier even within the Holy Court.

Ever since realizing the terror of the Divine Mountain, Nianmo had gone into hiding, rarely showing herself.

Now, she didn't even dare return to Baijiang Prefecture, fearing another slap when passing through Liaoyun County.

Moreover, today she'd witnessed the people of the Divine Mountain hunting down demons, completely wiping out all the demons within Qingzhou.

It terrified her, but thankfully, their attackers were all fifth-tier Yin God Realm cultivators, unable to detect her presence.

"That monk, and that one with the knife, they're all acting so strangely. How can someone at the Enlightenment Realm possess such terrifying abilities?"

Nianmo murmured.

She couldn't understand it, but she was certain it was all related to the terrifying being on the Divine Mountain.

"Forget it, as the humans say, 'Better a friend die than I.' As long as I'm alright, I'll find a chance to contact them once the Holy Court's army enters Qingzhou, then we'll withdraw. I'll never come back to this hellish place again!" Nianmo sighed inwardly.

A shadow had already formed in her heart, especially seeing the direction of the Divine Mountain gradually gathering human fortune, which made her feel vaguely uneasy.

...

The Divine Mountain.

Wu Ze and his group returned to Qianshan County and left the twelve well-preserved demon corpses on an open ground.

The surrounding people were all stunned by this scene.

Bai Liang immediately flew out of the Mountain Tower, smiled upon seeing this, and shouted, "Congratulations to Magistrate Wu on your successful demon hunt!"

Hearing Bai Liang's words, the people below realized that their Lord Wu had gone out to hunt demons.

Then, their excitement surged, and they began to cheer.

"Brother Wu Ze, congratulations! Everyone, you've worked hard!" Bai Liang approached Wu Ze and the others, and politely invited them into the Mountain Pavilion.

A great victory deserves a proper welcome.


Chapter 189 The Astounding Effects of the Divine Water!

In the Mountain Pavilion, a lavish feast was indulging.

Bai Liang ordered a banquet to be prepared to entertain Chu Wuliu and his companions.

For this occasion, he even brought out a spirit wine, brewed for the first time using spirit rice and divine water—the masterpiece of Zhou Sanhai.

Upon hearing that the wine was brewed with divine water, everyone's eyes lit up, and they began to taste it.

The wine was smooth and mellow on the palate, sweet with a hint of spirit rice aroma. As it flowed down the throat, a spicy sensation followed, transforming into a warm current that soothed the entire body.

"Excellent wine!" everyone exclaimed after finishing it.

After welcoming them and washing away the dust of their journey, it was time to discuss the sacrificial ceremony.

Bai Liang also invited Chu Wuliu and the others to join the discussion. Originally, they had planned to return to Baijiang Prefecture after helping out.

However, upon learning that Qianshan County was holding a sacrificial ceremony, they changed their minds.

As the most loyal followers of the Mountain God, how could they miss the sacrificial ceremony?

"The sacrificial ceremony is scheduled for three days from now, to be held at the main temple. All preparations for the ceremony are complete, and the offerings are in place," Bai Liang said.

"Besides the offerings, what else has been prepared?"

The old monk asked.

Bai Liang explained, listing all the preparations to everyone.

Including the most important sacrificial text.

After listening, everyone agreed that there were no problems and nodded.

They discussed some more details and finalized everything.

With everything finalized, they simply waited for the sacrificial ceremony in three days.

Bai Liang had already arranged accommodations for Chu Wuliu and the others, and had someone take them back to rest.

However, Chu Wuliu and the others were full of energy and didn't want to go back to rest, choosing instead to wander around Qianshan County.

Qilin and the other two were injured, and Wu Ze wanted Zhou Sanhai to refine some healing pills.

Zhou Sanhai smiled wryly, "I can indeed refine healing pills, but they are all low-grade pills, only effective for cultivators below the Profound Realm. For those in the Enlightened Spirit Realm, they're barely worth anything."

Seeing no other way, Wu Ze had no choice but to give up.

However, among the Yin Spirit Realm cultivators from Baijiang Prefecture, one stepped forward.

"Alchemy? I know how!"

Bian Nong said confidently.

"You're an alchemist?" Wu Ze looked surprised, not expecting that there was actually an alchemist among the people from Baijiang Prefecture.

Bian Nong nodded and said, "I come from a family of physicians. I've studied medicine since childhood and am proficient in pharmacology. After embarking on cultivation, I studied various aspects of alchemy. I wouldn't dare say I'm a grandmaster, but I'm not far off. A mere healing pill is a piece of cake!"

Chu Wuliu stepped forward to corroborate this, smiling, "Brother Bian Nong is the number one alchemist in Baijiang Prefecture, self-taught, with extremely high talent in alchemy!"

"Then I'll trouble you, Fellow Daoist Bian Nong!" Wu Ze said respectfully.

"No trouble at all. We're all working for the Mountain God."

Bian Nong waved his hand and said.

Afterwards, Wu Ze had Zhou Sanhai take Bian Nong to the alchemy room to refine pills.

Those who have attained enlightenment are teachers. Although Zhou Sanhai was already white-haired, he treated Bian Nong with great respect and asked him many questions about alchemy.

Bian Nong was a kind and gentle person, and he answered all of Zhou Sanhai's questions one by one.

However, when he heard that Zhou Sanhai had added divine water to the alchemy process and achieved a success rate of nearly 100%, he couldn't help but widen his eyes in disbelief.

"How is that possible? A 100% success rate! Do you know what that means?" Bian Nong was somewhat skeptical.

But then, remembering it was divine water, he wavered.

Finally, he personally tried refining the pill, adding the divine water midway through, and it actually succeeded!

However, he still had some doubts, so he tried refining several more batches of pills, including the most difficult fifth-tier healing pill, and all of them were successful!

"Hiss! It really is close to a 100% success rate!?" This time, Bian Nong was completely shocked.

Zhou Sanhai was just a small-time alchemist with limited experience; he might know that a 100% success rate would surprise many people.

But Bian Nong knew more than that. A 100% success rate was terrifying; even he couldn't achieve a 100% success rate when refining beginner-level pills.

As long as divine water was added, the success rate could reach 100%.

Then... wouldn't that mean!?

He suddenly thought of the legendary Nine-Turn Human Pill from Xuanqing Temple, said to grant sainthood upon successful refinement!

"Hiss..." Bian Nong dared not think further.

However, after a moment's thought, he shook his head.

Divine Water was indeed powerful, but the legendary Nine-Turn Human Pill was a Saint Pill!

Divine Water could guarantee the success of other pills, but not necessarily a Saint Pill.

Suppressing these thoughts, Bian Nong focused on refining a powerful healing pill.

Zhou Sanhai observed him closely, and in less than half an hour, he had refined several batches of healing pills.

Then he gave the pills to Qilin and the other two, instructing them to consume them to heal their injuries.

Li Yue's divine sense covered the entire Qianshan County.

It wasn't unexpected that Wu Ze and the others had returned with a full load.

After all, they were only fifth-tier demons, easily dealt with.

His current attention was mainly focused on the border of Qingzhou.

The demons were bound to make a move, but the lack of any activity was puzzling.

While the demons remained silent, an ever-growing influx of people from all over Qingzhou had arrived. Qianshan County was now overflowing with people, exceeding 150,000 – a truly alarming sight.

Furthermore, many who couldn't enter Qianshan County had set up makeshift thatched huts outside.

This sudden influx had caused a population explosion in Qianshan County.

The officials in charge were frantic. Du Tiandao patrolled dozens of times a day, and in the past two days alone, they had apprehended nearly 100 troublemakers.

The people coming to Qianshan County were a mixed bag.

Li Yue couldn't possibly use his Mind Mirror to examine each person individually; he found it too troublesome.

He didn't want to deal with ordinary people, leaving it to his subordinates.

He had initially thought that with such a population surge in Qianshan County, his merit points would also increase dramatically, but unfortunately, that hadn't happened.

Li Yue glanced at the panel; his merit points were currently 4.5 million.

[Realm: Fourth-Tier Sacred Mountain]

[Merit Points: 4.5 million/10 million]

[Divine Abilities: Mountain God's Blessing, Dharma Body of Heaven and Earth, Rebirth of Incense, Gratitude for the Source of Water]

[Spell: Manifestation]

[Magic Artifacts: Mountain-Suppressing Seal, Heart Mirror, Divine Weapon Furnace, Wind-Stabilizing Pearl]

He wasn't even halfway to advancing to the fifth tier.

However, the sacrificial ceremony was in three days, at which time his merit points would surge, and he might even unlock new spells.

His only current spell is the ability to manifest an avatar, which he doesn't use much because it's rather useless.

But thinking about it, it's understandable. Spells are a level weaker than supernatural powers, at most serving a supporting role.

As for why his merit points haven't increased much despite the massive population growth in Qianshan County,

Li Yue probably already had the answer.

It's because these newcomers don't believe in him.

Most of them are just following the crowd, wanting to see if there's divine protection at the sacred mountain as rumored.


Chapter 190 The Struggle Between the Old and the New

Li Yue surveyed the development of Qianshan County, which was now transforming from a county town into a true city.

The young man Bai Liang had promoted was quite capable; after training, he was now remarkably adept at handling Qianshan County's affairs.

Like the original villagers of Qianshan Manor, these were all veterans, and often difficult for those in charge to manage.

Although they hadn't broken any rules, they were notorious for slacking off and shirking their duties.

Unless they committed any major offenses, even Bai Liang and his men couldn't say anything.

After all, they were the elders of Qianshan County, having endured the hardships along with the newcomers from the very beginning.

However, this always had a negative impact, causing resentment among those who came later.

In the past few days, these elders had clashed with the newcomers.

The reason, naturally, was the allocation of tasks. The elders, relying on their connections and privileges, were assigned fewer and easier tasks, while the newcomers were given heavy and arduous work, and ultimately received less pay than the elders.

This wasn't the first time; pent-up emotions had finally erupted.

A fight broke out, resulting in injuries on both sides. It was only after Erqing arrived with a patrol team that they were separated.

This incident had a very negative impact, even alarming Bai Liang, who was in seclusion for cultivation.

However, upon learning that the original elders were involved, he became troubled and ultimately sent Xiao Nian to handle the situation.

Xiao Nian was also quite helpless about the matter.

If he punished the elders too harshly, Bai Liang and the others would certainly not allow it.

If he didn't punish them, with so many eyes watching, the impact would be too bad.

The newcomers, actually, weren't exactly new.

They had been with Qianshan Manor for almost a year, but compared to the original members, they were considered newcomers.

The conflicts between the two sides weren't isolated incidents.

In fact, since the Zhao Yihe incident, these elders had become much more subdued.

But time erodes everything, including the fear of the rules.

Seeing that it was Xiao Nian who came to handle the matter, the older members felt emboldened. They knew Xiao Nian was promoted by Bai Liang and would naturally side with them.

The newcomers, however, were resentful, and seeing the older members' attitude only fueled their anger.

Xiao Nian first inquired about the cause of the conflict, and the answer, as he expected, was still the older members' fault.

The reason was that after being assigned very few and light tasks, the older members had gone to the newcomers to boast, using this to demonstrate their superiority.

Who wouldn't be angry in this situation?

Xiao Nian was quite indignant, thinking, "The two elders have shown you a lot of favor because you are veterans, and this is how you keep causing trouble?"

He genuinely wanted to take back these older members' 'privileges'.

But in the end, Xiao Nian regained his composure. This matter was too complicated to handle that way.

Finally, he made a decision to completely separate the older members, arranging new work areas for them.

By separating the two sides, the conflict would naturally cease.

As for how to handle this matter, the elderly were naturally given a lenient punishment, while the elderly were given ample face on the surface, but all the due punishments were still imposed.

The newcomers were dealt with in the opposite way: severe punishment but light penalties.

They received stern reprimands, but the actual punishment was very minor and negligible.

This approach was acceptable to both sides, and the matter was thus resolved.

From then on, the older generation worked in specially arranged areas, no longer given the opportunity to show off to the newcomers—a form of mandatory "retirement."

Bai Liang was quite satisfied with Xiao Nian's handling of the situation.

After all, it was really difficult for him to intervene. After Xiao Nian's solution was announced, some older members came to him to complain, saying that Xiao Nian was bullying them, these "veteran contributors," and was "discarding them after they've served their purpose."

Bai Liang simply shrugged, indicating that he was no longer involved in affairs; these matters were being handled by Xiao Nian, and he couldn't interfere.

After seeing off the complaining older members, Bai Liang praised Xiao Nian.

"Xiaonian, you've grown very quickly. I think I can entrust the management of Qianshan County to you," Bai Liang said earnestly.

Of course, he was referring to ordinary affairs in Qianshan County; if cultivators were involved, he would still have to handle it.

"This student is willing to share Master Bai's burdens!"

Xiao Nian greeted Bai Liang with the respect due to a disciple, as Bai Liang had taught him many things.

Bai Liang nodded, officially handing over the management of the ordinary people of Qianshan County to Xiao Nian.

From now on, the first floor of the Mountain Tower would be the place for handling mundane affairs of Qianshan County.

The second floor and above would be the place where Bai Liang and his group discussed and handled important matters.

The Hundred Refinement Hall was no longer under Xiao Nian's management; it was now overseen and guided by the three members of the Qilin Sect.

There were already over a hundred students there; upon reaching the Refinement Realm, they would graduate and join the patrol team.

Dozens had already graduated.

Lin Qian, the most talented, was already attempting to break through to the Common Enlightenment Realm and had become a squad leader in the patrol team.

These were cultivators truly cultivated by Qianshan County itself.

Those who had previously joined did not count.

Li Yue witnessed all of this and was quite satisfied.

This is just the beginning. As more and more talented individuals emerge, the number of cultivators on the sacred mountain will surge like bamboo shoots after a spring rain.

In a cave halfway up the mountain, the young girl from before is now attempting to break through to the Divine Enlightenment Realm; her progress is unmatched.

Li Yue, restless and yearning for change, once again unleashed his Manifestation.

An elderly Taoist priest, with an air of otherworldly grace, appeared in a corner of Qianshan County.

"It's been so long since I've been human; it feels a bit strange."

Fiddling with his limbs, Li Yue felt somewhat out of place.

Having spent so long on the mountain, he felt himself becoming increasingly withdrawn, unwilling to move.

Stepping out of the alley, the elderly Li Yue walked along the street, feeling the warmth of human life, and suddenly felt a sense of alienation.

"Candied hawthorns for sale! Three coins a skewer, three skewers for ten coins!"

"Roasted yams, piping hot, fresh out of the oven!"

Street vendors hawked their wares, and the street teemed with people, many children playing and laughing.

Li Yue watched this scene, feeling a sense of disorientation.

Two years ago, this was a barren wasteland, devoid of even a soul.

He recalled the first six months after his transmigration, when he had become a mountain, motionless, spending his time watching the animals chase each other out of boredom, excited for days at the sight of even a single person passing by.

Finally, a group of refugees arrived at the foot of the sacred mountain, shattering the tranquility.

These refugees were none other than Wu Ze and his group.

“Boss, give me a skewer of candied hawthorns,” Li Yue said as he approached the vendor.

“Oh, old gentleman, you have quite an appetite! To be able to eat sweets at such an advanced age, you must be in very good health!” The vendor seemed slightly surprised to see Li Yue’s white hair and beard.


Chapter 191 Have I ever seen myself?

"Sir, that'll be three coins." The vendor took down the candied hawthorn and handed it to Li Yue.

After paying, Li Yue took the candied hawthorn and continued strolling.

The candied hawthorn vendor watched Li Yue's departing figure and muttered to himself, "Strange that someone so old is still eating candied hawthorn!"

And this old man's energy and spirit are so strong; he doesn't look like he's in his seventies.

Furthermore, he possesses an indescribable, inexplicable aura, quite like a sage.

After buying the candied hawthorn, Li Yue only took one bite. The sweet and sour taste was still the familiar one from his memories.

He had originally thought that the candied hawthorn in this world would be different, but it seems he was overthinking it.

He only tasted it once before tossing the candied hawthorn to a corner by the roadside, where several stray cats were.

The stray cats saw the candied hawthorn on the ground and immediately rushed over, then started fighting. In the end, a calico tabby cat won and enjoyed the candied hawthorn alone.

Li Yue then visited restaurants, brothels, and various newly opened shops, observing everything in Qianshan County from a human perspective.

"It's quite different from observing with divine sense; it's more realistic," Li Yue thought to himself.

His divine sense swept over everything like watching a movie, a cursory glance.

It didn't feel as real as experiencing it firsthand.

"Old sir, are you looking for someone?"

A voice rang out. Li Yue looked up; he had unknowingly wandered into the Hundred Refinement Hall.

The one who spoke was a student of the Hundred Refinement Hall.

"No, I'm just taking a walk and looking around."

Li Yue shook his head. This student seemed to be new to the Hundred Refinement Hall; his cultivation level appeared low, still some distance from the Refinement Realm.

"I see. Would you like to come into the Hundred Refinement Hall and have a look, old sir?" the student asked with a smile.

"May I go in and take a look?" Li Yue asked, intrigued. "The Hundred Refinements Hall is of utmost importance in Qianshan County, said to be the most important place besides the temple. Aren't you worried about problems if you just let strangers in?"

The student, somewhat taken aback by Li Yue's words, chuckled, "It seems you've lived in Qianshan County for a long time, knowing so much about it."

"I've heard a little about it," Li Yue replied calmly.

"You may not know this, but while the Hundred Refinements Hall is indeed important, it's not as exaggerated as the rumors suggest. As long as you're from Qianshan County, you can come in if you want."

The student explained, then added, "Moreover, the Hundred Refinements Hall is overseen by three great figures: Qilin, Black Dragon, and Fire Phoenix. It won't be easy for ordinary people to cause trouble."

Li Yue nodded.

Indeed, with the three of them overseeing it, even a Yin God Realm expert would have to remain submissive.

"Then please take me in and have a look," Li Yue said with a smile.

"Alright! Let me help you, sir."

The student smiled and agreed, stepping forward to assist Li Yue.

Li Yue waved his hand, "No need, I'm in excellent health, no need for assistance."

Then he strode up the steps with remarkable agility.

This left the student quite astonished; he hadn't expected such an elderly man to be in such good physical condition, seemingly even stronger than himself.

"Please come this way, sir." The student then led the way for Li Yue.

A moment later, led by the student, Li Yue arrived at the Martial Arts Hall of the Hundred Refinements Hall.

This was the core of the Hundred Refinements Hall, where many students practiced and sparred daily.

At this moment, two students were sparring in the hall; both were cultivators at the Refinement Realm.

"Those two are my senior brothers, the first students to enter the Hundred Refinements Hall. They've all reached the Refinement Realm now and will graduate soon to join the patrol team."

Wang Fanhao spoke with a hint of envy.

Li Yue looked over and saw two people sparring on the central platform.

Both were using swordsmanship, wielding wooden swords. Upon closer inspection, the swordsmanship was revealed to be He Liyuan's Great River Swordsmanship.

Wang Fanhao explained that because Wu Ze was using swordsmanship, and the students greatly admired Wu Ze, the strongest fighter in Qianshan County, they had all started learning it.

Because Qianshan County currently has very few cultivation techniques, He Liyuan's Great River Saber Technique has become one of the main techniques practiced by the students.

"Not bad, very energetic!" Li Yue commented after taking a few glances.

"Right? Old sir, let me tell you, Qianshan County is going to be something else in the future! So many young cultivators! Just give Qianshan County another ten years, and I guarantee there will be many powerful figures emerging!" Wang Fanhao said proudly.

Of course, his prediction of many powerful figures included himself.

After walking around the martial arts hall a few more times, Wang Fanhao expressed some regret that the three masters presiding over the Hundred Refinements Hall weren't there today; otherwise, he could have witnessed their prowess.

"By the way, old sir, have you met the Mountain God?"

Suddenly, Wang Fanhao looked at Li Yue with expectant eyes.

Li Yue was taken aback. Had he met himself? How was he supposed to answer that?

After thinking for a moment, he nodded and said, "Of course I have."

Upon hearing Li Yue's answer, Wang Fanhao's eyes lit up, his face filled with excitement, and his voice grew louder: "Old sir, you really have seen the Mountain God!?"

His voice was loud enough for all the surrounding students to hear.

Everyone's gaze instantly focused on him, their eyes shining, and they all walked towards Li Yue and Li Fanhao.

Even the two people sparring in the middle of the martial arts hall stopped their match and came over.

Wang Fanhao realized that he had been too excited, causing his voice to be too loud and attracting the attention of everyone in the Hundred Refinements Hall, and he immediately felt a little embarrassed.

"I'm sorry, old sir," Wang Fanhao said apologetically.

Li Yue waved his hand, not minding at all.

"Junior Brother Wang, you just said that this old gentleman in front of us has seen the Mountain God?"

A female student stepped forward, asking hesitantly, while also curiously scrutinizing Li Yue.

The others also chimed in, asking if what Wang Fanhao said was true.

Faced with so many questions, Wang Fanhao was at a loss and could only look to Li Yue for help.

Li Yue smiled and readily admitted, "You heard me right, I have seen the Mountain God."

Upon hearing this, everyone present gasped in surprise, and their gazes towards Li Yue changed completely.

"You really saw the Mountain God?! Old sir, could you tell us about the Mountain God's deeds?"

"Old sir, wasn't the Mountain God extraordinary, surrounded by divine light, like a celestial being?"

The students eagerly surrounded Li Yue, their voices brimming with excitement as they bombarded him with questions about the Mountain God.

Their eyes held reverence, admiration, worship, and even fanaticism.

Wang Fanhao, too, looked at Li Yue with expectant eyes, eager to learn about the Mountain God's deeds.


Chapter 192 The Mysterious Old Man

Faced with the enthusiastic inquiries of these young men, Li Yue was at a loss for words.

Should he praise himself?

Or should he feign humility? But seeing the fervor in their eyes, if he were to offer 'humility,' he feared he would be attacked by the group, questioned about why he was 'belittling' the Mountain God.

"Ahem, the Mountain God... well, this old man cannot describe him. The appearance of a deity is indescribable. If you wish to know, you will only be able to see him when the Mountain God manifests again," Li Yue said with a smile.

The students were immediately disappointed.

They had hoped to learn what the Mountain God looked like from the old man's words.

"Old sir, could you then tell us about the Mountain God's deeds?" another student asked.

All eyes turned to Li Yue again, eagerly awaiting his answer.

Li Yue thought for a moment, feeling there wasn't much to say, but he still recounted some well-known deeds.

The students listened, somewhat bored.

“Sir, we’ve heard all of this before. Is there anything we haven’t heard of? For example, is the Mountain God male or female?” one student boldly asked.

A hush fell over the room. This question was indeed somewhat presumptuous. Although they all wanted to know, they dared not raise their voices in agreement.

Li Yue thought for a moment and smiled, “This old man feels that the Mountain God is probably male.”

“Really? How did you know, sir?” The student who had asked was somewhat surprised, not expecting Li Yue to actually answer.

“This old man guessed,” Li Yue said with a faint smile.

“Huh?” The youths were speechless, thinking that the old man Wang Fanhao had brought knew nothing.

They had originally thought they would learn some inside information, but now they seemed somewhat disappointed.

“Instead of sparring, what are you all doing here?”

A deep voice rang out, and everyone’s expression changed abruptly.

Turning around, they saw a middle-aged man with a slightly fierce appearance walking towards them, his hands behind his back.

"Hall Master!" the students hurriedly called out.

The newcomer was Qilin, now the head steward of the Hundred Refinement Hall, responsible for managing and instructing all the students.

Qilin nodded, then said sternly, "Why are you gathered here instead of practicing and sparring?"

He looked at the crowd and then saw an elderly man with an air of otherworldly wisdom in the middle, which immediately startled him.

The enigmatic aura and subtle mystery surrounding the old man made him realize that this old man was no ordinary person.

At this moment, Wang Fanhao stepped forward and truthfully explained how he met the old man, how he brought him into the Hundred Refinement Hall, and how, after everyone heard that the old man knew about the Mountain God's deeds, they all gathered around to question him.

"Hall Master, I was wrong. I shouldn't have brought a stranger into the Hundred Refinement Hall," Wang Fanhao said, bowing his head.

Qilin frowned, waved his hand, and said, "Go to the confinement room and cultivate for three days before coming out!"

The Hundred Refinement Hall has explicit rules against bringing outsiders in.

However, this rule isn't very strict, and many people have brought outsiders in before.

But rules are rules, and as the head of the Hundred Refinement Hall, he naturally has to enforce them; otherwise, who will follow them in the future?

"Yes." Wang Fanhao nodded, then glanced back at Li Yue, before gritting his teeth and saying, "Master, this old gentleman is a good person. Please don't make things difficult for him."

"Do you think I, the Master, am the kind of person who would make things difficult for others?" Qilin glared at Wang Fanhao.

Although he looked a bit fierce, as the ancients said, appearances can be deceiving; he was actually quite friendly.

That's what he thought.

Wang Fanhao gave an awkward smile, then slipped away to the solitary confinement room.

Since the Master had said so, he certainly wouldn't make things difficult for the old gentleman he brought in, so he felt relieved.

Seeing this, the others also dispersed, afraid of being punished by the Master if they were even a step too slow.

After everyone had left, Qilin looked at the old man before him, somewhat unsure of his character, and asked, "Old sir, are you a resident of Qianshan County?"

Li Yue smiled and nodded slightly.

"Sort of," he replied casually.

"So, old sir, what do you think of the Hundred Refinement Hall?" Qilin suddenly asked.

"The Hundred Refinement Hall? Hmm, not bad. It's good for nurturing younger generations and strengthening Qianshan County, preventing a generational gap," Li Yue commented.

Qianshan County's current strength, like Bai Liang's, is all thanks to his blessing.

Without him, they would be helpless.

For some reason, Qilin felt as if he were facing a senior master.

Unsure of the old man's intentions, Qilin asked a few questions and then politely ushered him out.

"Old sir, where are you going? Should I send someone to escort you?"

Qilin asked at the entrance of the Hundred Refinement Hall.

"No need, I'm going to the temple," Li Yue waved his hand.

After their brief interaction, he could tell that Qilin was very wary. While outwardly respectful, he was actually on high alert.

He probably sensed something.

Li Yue didn't care. Although he concealed his cultivation level in his manifested form, his innate aura and unique divine power couldn't be completely hidden.

After leaving the Hundred Refinement Hall, Li Yue headed towards a mountain god temple.

Many mountain god temples had been built in Qianshan County for the townspeople to offer incense and prayers.

The original temples had become main temples, considered sacred sites, and unauthorized personnel were not allowed to approach.

Therefore, Li Yue went to other mountain god temples.

Watching the old man leave, Qilin frowned slightly and immediately went to the Mountain Tower.

Bai Liang and Du Tiandao were in the tower at that moment, and Qilin recounted the whole story.

"An old man appeared?" Bai Liang frowned upon hearing this, then pressed, "Qilin, even you can't see through that old man?"

Qilin nodded solemnly, "I can't see through him. Facing that old man was like facing an unspeakable being, it made my heart tremble!"

He spoke, still feeling a lingering fear.

When he used his divine sense to investigate, his heart pounded violently, warning him of the extreme danger of his actions, causing him to quickly withdraw his divine sense.

"If that old man is a cultivator, then he is likely a being of the seventh rank or higher," Qilin said.

That intense tremor couldn't possibly be something a Yang God Realm cultivator could give him.

Upon hearing this, Du Tiandao couldn't help but gasp!

A seventh-tier cultivator—this was simply beyond his imagination.

An existence of this level could definitely be called a powerful being.

Currently, Wu Ze, the strongest in Qianshan County, was not even at the fifth-tier Yin God Realm.

"What should we do then? If the opponent is truly a seventh-tier cultivator, wouldn't that be very troublesome?" Du Tiandao asked in a deep voice.

"It won't be that bad. With Lord Mountain God here, even dragons and tigers have to lie low," Bai Liang said.


Chapter 193 The Mountain God Descends to Earth!

“And there’s another possibility: that old man might not be human,” Bai Liang suddenly said.

“Not human!?”

Qilin and Du Tiandao were both startled.

Qilin, his face contorted with rage, said, “Could it be a demon that has infiltrated? I’m going to find the Black Dragon and Fire Phoenix right now!”

“No, I didn’t say it was a demon,” Bai Liang quickly stopped him.

“Not a demon? Then what could it be?” Qilin asked, somewhat puzzled.

Du Tiandao, slow to react, suddenly realized something.

Unfathomable, mysterious, powerful, an old man…

These keywords combined made him think of a certain being.

Du Tiandao gasped, “Hiss… Could it be the Mountain God!?”

Bai Liang, his expression serious, nodded hesitantly and said, “Very likely.”

Qilin, however, was stunned, completely bewildered after realizing what he meant.

What the hell? Could that old man really be the Mountain God?

The thought of the mysterious and unfathomable pressure, the indescribable aura, emanating from the old man filled Qilin with dread.

These feelings were unlike anything a cultivator could evoke.

Thinking this through, there was only one possibility: the old man was a deity!

Having figured this out, Qilin gasped.

He immediately recalled whether he had disrespected the old man, and finding no fault, he breathed a sigh of relief.

However, his heart remained uneasy.

"What do we do now? If that old man is truly the Mountain God, should we go and pay our respects immediately?" Qilin hesitated.

Bai Liang was also hesitant. His heart was in turmoil; he desperately wanted to see the old man.

Even though he had received the Mountain God's blessing, he had never actually seen the Mountain God.

He wanted to see him, but his heart was filled with uncertainty.

If it really is the Mountain God, and He's just been strolling down to earth for a while, and I've abruptly disturbed His refined enjoyment—wouldn't that displease Him?

"Keep it a secret for now, don't tell anyone else, and besides, we're not even sure if it's really the Mountain God," Bai Liang immediately said.

The two Qilin nodded, understanding the importance of this matter.

Although the three of them were eager to pay their respects to the Mountain God, they ultimately suppressed their excitement and dared not disturb Him rashly.

Meanwhile, Li Yue arrived at a newly built Mountain God temple.

Many people had come to offer incense and worship. Although it was a newly built temple, the incense was still incredibly abundant.

In Qianshan County, any Mountain God temple was always bustling with worshippers.

To enter the temple to offer incense, one had to queue.

Li Yue stood at the back of the queue and glanced around. Most of those offering incense and worship had been living in Qianshan County for some time.

Recently, due to the imperial edict, very few people have come to the sacred mountain for refuge.

Of course, this is also because it's difficult for these people to enter Qianshan County.

"Old sir, is this your first time worshipping the mountain god?"

After Li Yue reached the front of the line, a young man in a red robe looked at him curiously and asked.

He was the temple keeper of this mountain god temple, responsible for all matters here.

Li Yue nodded.

Seeing that Li Yue hadn't brought incense or candles, the temple keeper prepared some for him.

Finally, he added, "Old sir, next time you come to worship the Mountain God, remember to bring your own incense and candles. They sell them everywhere in town, very cheaply, only a few coins."

The Mountain God Temple in Qianshan County provides incense and candles for those who forget, but it's not free every day.

Li Yue accepted the incense and candles, indicating he understood.

He hadn't really considered this before; most of it was trivial and not worth knowing.

The Mountain God Temple's furnishings were as simple as ever.

There was no actual mountain statue, but rather a portrait of it.

Li Yue simply glanced at it and didn't offer any worship.

After all, worshipping oneself would be too strange.

After observing the Mountain God Temple, Li Yue quietly left, unnoticed by anyone around, including the temple keeper.

The temple keeper waited at the entrance for a long time, but the old man didn't come out. Feeling something was amiss, he went inside the temple to search for him.

But after searching everywhere, he couldn't find any trace of the old man, which puzzled him greatly.

Could it be that he had overlooked him? Had the old man actually already left?

At this moment, Li Yue appeared in the Spirit Field.

This was a vital area of ​​Qianshan County. Spirit rice was planted in the field, growing vigorously and lushly. A gentle breeze carried the refreshing fragrance of the rice.

Uncle Wu was busy in the rice paddy. He stood up, wiped the sweat from his brow, and looked at the fruits of his labor, a broad smile spreading across his face.

"Grandpa, I caught a fish!"

A child, naked, played in the rice paddy, one hand tightly gripping a rice paddy fish.

"Little Wu, you're so great! Grandpa will grill the fish for you tonight, how about it?"

"Yay!" The child smiled excitedly.

Li Yue then went to the former mulberry picking area, which had now become a textile workshop.

Most of the women from the county town worked there.

The silkworms raised and the cotton grown were all sent to the workshops, continuously woven into cloth, and then sold to cloth merchants in the county town.

The brick kilns, which had been operating previously, had now expanded many times over.

The expansion of the county town meant a severe shortage of building materials; tiles and bricks were extremely scarce.

Er Niu and his group were even using a kiln that was belching black smoke, yet it still wasn't enough.

Therefore, they had to continuously expand the scale and increase production.

Finally, there was the livestock farm in Qianshan County. Originally managed by Er Qing, he was later transferred to the patrol team, so the farm was replaced.

This livestock farm didn't develop as well.

Mainly because eating meat was too extravagant; everyone was already grateful to have enough to eat, let alone dare to buy meat.

Therefore, they mainly sold meat to wealthy farmers and merchants.

Also, some meat was provided to the students of the Hundred Refinements Hall, as cultivation was very demanding, and insufficient nutrition was unacceptable.

Li Yue visited several more places and, after observing them, felt quite moved.

Qianshan County's development was truly rapid.

It had taken only two years to reach its current state; the journey had been far from easy.

However, such rapid development also had its drawbacks, and Li Yue noticed several.

But he didn't dwell on them; they were ultimately minor issues, not requiring Bai Liang's attention. He could let Xiao Nian handle them.

Li Yue arrived at a deserted alley, immediately dispelling his avatar, and his figure vanished into thin air.

Then, his divine sense appeared in the sky, rapidly expanding to a distance of a hundred miles.

He saw the county towns of Tianyue and Taohua.

Taohua County had already become a complete vassal of Qianshan County, and Tianyue County was not far behind.

Qiu Zhen and the Lin brothers had long since followed the imperial court out of Qingzhou, and Tianyue County was now temporarily under the indirect control of Qianshan County.


Chapter 194 Li Xue Breaks Through to the Divine Enlightenment Realm

If it weren't for manpower shortages and the opportune moment, Tianyue County could have already been brought under the control of the Divine Mountain.

Currently, Qianshan County has only stationed a few people in Tianyue County to prevent unrest.

At the same time, they are planning how to spread the Divine Mountain faith.

In Shishu County, Qian Ke and Chen Jianchi had already built the Mountain God Temple.

However, the imperial court's cession of Qingzhou to demons caused panic among the people of Shishu County, and numerous disturbances occurred in the county seat.

Magistrate Wang Lan was also overwhelmed. He was not a particularly upright official, and his poor reputation meant the people lacked faith in him, so few listened to his attempts to appease them.

In the end, only through military suppression could Shishu County be temporarily stabilized.

Wang Lan found Qian Ke and complained, "Brother Qian Ke, I defied the imperial orders and refused to evacuate Qingzhou. Instead, I stayed to face the demons with you. If you return to Qianshan County, you must put in a good word for me."

Qian Ke was pondering how to take advantage of the chaos in Qingzhou to convert all the people of Shishu County into followers of the Mountain God.

Upon hearing Wang Lan's words, Qian Ke glanced at him and said indifferently, "Why didn't Lord Wang evacuate with the imperial court? Wasn't it because you betrayed the General's Office and were afraid of being punished?"

"Uh...you can't say that."

Wang Lan was embarrassed, not expecting Qian Ke to be so blunt.

In the end, he could only leave awkwardly to find Chen Jianchi.

Compared to Qian Ke, Wang Lan felt Chen Jianchi was easier to talk to.

As for why he was so persistent about this matter, it was simply because he desperately wanted to improve.

But when he found Chen Jianchi, the result was the same: he was directly rejected.

Chen Jianchi even turned the tables and lectured Wang Lan.

“Lord Wang, put away your ulterior motives. If you truly want to achieve merit, serve the Mountain God properly, instead of thinking of crooked ways; that's the most useless thing,” Chen Jianchi criticized sternly.

Wang Lan, with a mournful face, said, “Brother, I also want to serve the Mountain God properly, but my abilities are truly limited, and I can't make a single step. I only hope you can put in a good word for me, so I can witness the Mountain God's divine power firsthand.”

“Don't worry, there will be an opportunity. At the Mountain God's sacrificial ceremony in three days, the Mountain God might manifest his divine power,” Chen Jianchi said calmly.

“Is that true?” Wang Lan's eyes lit up.

“Of course.”

“Then, brother, can I go?” Wang Lan asked expectantly.

Since consuming the divine water, his reverence for the Mountain God had been like the endless flow of the Milky Way.

“It should be possible,” Chen Jianchi said after a moment’s thought.

“Then it’s possible! Brother Chen, I’m entrusting this matter to you,” Wang Lan said excitedly.

“I’ll ask around for you, but I can’t guarantee it.”

After saying this, Chen Jianchi wrote a letter and sent it back to Qianshan County.

Shishu County is over a hundred li away from Qianshan County, and the journey would take a considerable amount of time.

Wang Lan was impatient, so he had a fast horse specially prepared for an 800-li express courier service.

Mount Shen, a cave halfway up the mountain.

With a violent fluctuation of spiritual energy, Li Xue successfully broke through to the Enlightenment Realm.

Upon seeing this, Wu Ze and the others smiled and stepped forward to share the good news.

The little girl was only twelve years old and had already reached the Enlightenment Realm; her cultivation speed was simply heaven-defying.

The three members of the Qilin Clan were utterly astonished when they heard that Li Xue was only twelve.

The Black Dragon exclaimed in shock, "How is this possible? A twelve-year-old at the Enlightenment Realm! Even the most talented geniuses in the capital aren't this monstrous!"

"Even starting cultivation from the womb wouldn't be this fast, would it?" the Fire Phoenix couldn't help but ask.

Then Wu Ze chimed in, "Xiao Xue has been cultivating for two years now."

"Two years!? The Enlightenment Realm!?"

The Qilin Clan and the others were utterly shocked. Was this speed even human?

They didn't know that Wu Ze had also reached the Enlightenment Realm in two years; if they did, they would probably question their existence.

After Li Xue broke through to the Enlightenment Realm, the Divine Fate Talisman could be used on cultivators at the seventh-tier Unity Realm.

Becoming a fourth-tier cultivator, Li Xue was also very happy.

She hadn't visited the temple to worship the Mountain God for quite some time, spending most of her time cultivating in the cave.

So she descended the mountain and went to the temple, solemnly kneeling to offer incense.

Over the past two years, she had grown a lot, especially after the incident with Zhao Yihe.

She also understood the principle that with great power comes great responsibility.

Li Yue silently observed with his divine sense, his heart filled with many emotions.

He hadn't expected that the Zhao Yihe incident would make such an innocent little girl so composed.

Keep in mind, Li Xue was only twelve years old, a childlike age.

Of course, people in this world mature early; twelve years old was no longer considered a child, but a teenager.

What affected Li Xue the most was the Divine Fate Talisman.

All the other divine talismans were golden, only the Divine Fate Talisman was red.

Li Yue didn't understand why this was, and had tried to study it, but to no avail.

Ultimately, only one conclusion was reached: the Mountain God's blessing was random, beyond his control.

The last time he immersed himself in comprehending the laws of heaven and earth, he blessed Kong Wang once; that was the last time.

Currently, there are four types of divine talismans: Divine Movement Talisman, Divine Thought Talisman, Divine Fate Talisman, and Divine Concealment Talisman.

The most powerful aspect of the divine talismans isn't their heaven-defying, illogical effects, but rather the granting of the recipient the strongest cultivation talent.

Of the four recipients, Bai Liang is the weakest, at the peak of the Profound Realm.

Because he hasn't cultivated as much as the others, he's wasted considerable time.

Li Yue wondered if he should select a few people to bless now?

In the end, he decided to wait until after the sacrificial ceremony to decide whether to bestow blessings.

...

Outside the capital.

The once-renowned Tianshui Prefecture.

At its peak, Tianshui Prefecture boasted a population exceeding one hundred million, its prosperity rivaling that of Jiangnan Prefecture. As the waterway hub of the Great Chu Dynasty, the Tianhe River traversed it, forcing all merchant ships to pass through Tianshui Prefecture on their way to Jiangnan.

This brought immense wealth to Tianshui Prefecture.

Unfortunately, this once prosperous and thriving region was reduced to ruins after the Battle of Tianshui.

More than one hundred million people perished, and the renowned Tianhe River became a river of blood.

After this battle, the Great Chu Dynasty was no longer capable of resisting the demons.

Several powerful armies were wiped out in the Battle of Tianshui.

And countless human warriors met their end in Tianshui Prefecture.

Today, Tianshui Prefecture is shrouded in dark clouds and smog, the soil a deep purple—the color of dried blood.

After the demons won the Battle of Tianshui and seized control of the prefecture, they ceded it to the Holy Court's 'ally,' the Dragon-Slaying Sect.

Therefore, Tianshui Prefecture is now the Dragon-Slaying Sect's stronghold.

In the heart of Tianshui Prefecture lies a large city, which, from a distance, still retains a semblance of human habitation.


Chapter 195 Unusual Movements of the Dragon-Slaying Cult

This was once the capital of Tianshui Prefecture, but now it's the headquarters of the Dragon-Slaying Cult, Dragon-Slaying City!

All members of the Dragon-Slaying Cult reside here.

The city's inhabitants number a staggering two million, all members of the Dragon-Slaying Cult and their families.

Everything in the city is remarkably similar to the imperial city, even more vibrant!

The people here are filled with fanaticism, almost maniacal fervor.

In the taverns, shouts of killing the emperor, seizing the capital, and establishing a new dynasty frequently erupt.

A veiled woman walks silently down the street, observing everything in Dragon-Slaying City from the side, her thoughts unreadable.

After escaping Qingzhou City with Kong Wang's help, Nangong Hu left Qingzhou immediately.

She first went to Baijiang Prefecture, then received the imperial edict ceding Qingzhou.

She realized this was a momentous event, and the cult leader had also sent a message urging her to return to the cult as soon as possible; there were important matters to discuss.

So she returned.

At the heart of Dragon-Slaying City stood a colossal palace.

The sect leader, the Four Heavenly Kings, and other important members, including the Great Protector, all resided there.

Naturally, she, the Holy Maiden, also lived within the palace.

“Who goes there? Halt! This is the Dragon-Slaying Palace; unauthorized access is prohibited!”

As soon as she approached the Dragon-Slaying Palace, a cultivator appeared and stopped Nangong Hu.

Nangong Hu didn't speak, but simply displayed a token.

Upon seeing this, the man's expression changed, and he quickly bowed his head, his tone becoming respectful: “So it's the Holy Maiden! I was blind and didn't recognize you; I've offended you, sir.”

“Hmm,” Nangong Hu responded indifferently, ignoring the man, and walked straight into the Dragon-Slaying Palace.

Inside the palace, dragons and phoenixes were carved and painted, resplendent in gold and jade, incredibly luxurious.

After walking around the gilded walkway, she arrived at the main hall.

At this moment, a considerable number of people had already gathered in the main hall.

When Nangong Hu appeared, everyone in the hall turned to look at her.

Among these gazes were complex emotions, hostility, and indifference.

"The Holy Maiden has returned," an aged voice rang out, its tone remarkably calm.

"Greetings, Heavenly King Zhang."

Nangong Hu looked at the old man who spoke and nodded slightly.

Heavenly King Zhang nodded faintly, then closed his eyes.

"I heard that the Holy Maiden's trip to Bao Chu this time not only resulted in the deaths of over a dozen elite members of the sect, but she also nearly perished there?"

Another voice rang out, its tone now sharper.

Nangong Hu looked over; it was an old woman with a hunched back.

"Heavenly King Zhao, are you always so harsh?"

Before Nangong Hu could speak, a furious voice rang out.

A burly man, shirtless and covered in strange tattoos, glared angrily at Heavenly King Zhao.

"Heh, compared to the Ancient Heavenly King, my words are rather mild," Zhao Heavenly King chuckled sinisterly.

"Enough! The Sect Leader hasn't even arrived yet, and you're already arguing. Both of you, behave yourselves!"

At this moment, someone turned around, his cold gaze sweeping over the Ancient Heavenly King and Zhao Heavenly King.

The two immediately paled, then dared not utter another word.

Nangong Hu, seeing this, breathed a sigh of relief.

Within the Dragon-Slaying Sect, although she, as the Holy Maiden, was nominally second only to the Sect Leader, she actually held little real power.

At least the Four Heavenly Kings could overshadow her.

After all, strength is the foundation of one's words, and she was ultimately only at the Yin God Realm.

Similarly, the Ancient Heavenly King and the Zhao Heavenly King, both also among the Four Heavenly Kings, were silenced by a single glance from Yang Heavenly King.

The reason was simple: Yang Heavenly King's strength surpassed theirs; he had already touched the threshold of Half-Saint.

If nothing unexpected happened, he would soon become the Vice Sect Leader.

After a short wait, a person suddenly appeared on the high chair in the main hall.

This person was strikingly handsome, almost demonic; though clearly male, he possessed a captivatingly feminine appearance.

"Greetings, Sect Leader!"

Yang Heavenly King was the first to speak, and the others followed suit.

The stunningly beautiful leader of the Dragon-Slaying Sect surveyed the crowd below, her gaze lingering briefly on Nangong Hu before she said calmly, "Gentlemen, no need for formalities. I've summoned you all today because of an important matter I wish to discuss with you."

"Leader, is it about the tyrannical Chu's cession of Qingzhou?"

Zhao Tianwang spoke first, his voice filled with fervor, "The tyrannical Chu is incompetent, eroding the people's resources and committing such a shameful act! This is a tragedy for humanity!"

Many followers echoed her words, launching into a chorus of condemnation against the Great Chu Dynasty.

The leader raised her hand, silencing the murmurs, and said, "It is related to this matter, but it is not the most important."

"Could it be because of the Holy Court's actions?"

Zhang Tianwang asked, frowning.

"You could say that. The Demon Holy Court has decided to attack Qingzhou. Qingyu Ci personally mobilized troops, even sending a seventh-tier demon. They also sent a message to me, requesting our Dragon-Slaying Sect to cooperate with them and head to Qingzhou together," the sect leader said calmly.

"Cooperate with the Holy Court? Why go to such lengths for a small place like Qingzhou?" Ancient Heavenly King was utterly puzzled.

Logically, since Great Chu had abandoned Qingzhou and even withdrawn its cultivators, sending a few demons would have been enough to easily take it over.

"Because of the Divine Mountain!" Yang Heavenly King said, his eyes sharp.

"That's right, because of the Divine Mountain!" The sect leader glanced at Yang Heavenly King and then nodded.

"The Divine Mountain? What Divine Mountain?"

Ancient Heavenly King looked completely bewildered.

Zhang Tianwang, seemingly lost in thought, pondered, "Some time ago, I think I sent someone to Qingzhou to try and subdue a new force, to bring them into the Dragon-Slaying Sect."

"However, there's been no news of the person I sent; they're probably dead. The force's name was something like Qianshan... Manor?"

"It's now Qianshan County," the Dragon-Slaying Sect leader said in a deep voice. "Qianshan County is at the foot of Divine Mountain. According to the message Qingyu Ci relayed, there are cultivators hiding behind this Divine Mountain who killed the sixth-tier Blade Demon of Baijiang Prefecture some time ago; they're very likely seventh-tier cultivators."

These words caused a look of surprise to appear on the faces of those in the hall.

"A rogue cultivator? With such strength, he's no ordinary person. It's a pity his character is flawed. If he could have continued to lie low, he might have even become a half-saint in the future."

"Now that he's killed a sixth-tier demon, he's directly violated the Heavenly Water Pact. The Holy Court will never let him live. It's a pity that countless people in Qingzhou will be buried with him."

"His sins are immense; this act has directly cost tens of millions of lives in Qingzhou. Bao Chu is also incompetent; he didn't even dare to resist and simply abandoned Qingzhou."

The crowd shook their heads, their faces filled with regret.

The leader of the Dragon Slayer Sect looked at the crowd and spoke in a deep voice, "Our Dragon Slayer Sect and the Holy Court are still 'allies.' Now that Qingyu Thorn has requested assistance, we cannot refuse."

"Therefore, I am considering which people to send to Qingzhou."

Upon hearing this, the crowd below was filled with conflicting thoughts.

Going to Qingzhou now would be utterly pointless, and might even be dangerous, so it would be best not to go.


Chapter 196 Nangong Hu's Identity

Going to Qingzhou would bring no benefit, so everyone began to make excuses, claiming they had missions to fulfill and could not go.

Except for the Grand Protector and the Four Heavenly Kings, the rest of the sect members also started to decline.

Yang Tianwang snapped out of his daze, his gaze sweeping over the group of people making excuses, and coldly said, "The Dragon-Slaying Sect does not tolerate idlers. Not going to Qingzhou is fine, but if I discover you don't actually have a mission and are merely making excuses, then don't blame me for being ruthless!"

Upon hearing this, most of them paled.

This was Yang Tianwang, and he meant what he said.

It could be said that the members of the Dragon-Slaying Sect might not fear their leader, but they would definitely fear Yang Tianwang.

Because he was truly ruthless, regardless of who you were!

But the words had already been spoken, and there was no going back now.

So everyone could only think to themselves that they should hurry back and accept some missions, lest they actually get caught.

The leader of the Dragon-Slaying Sect calmly observed the crowd, seemingly unfazed by their reaction.

His gaze softened considerably as he turned to the silent Nangong Hu.

"Holy Maiden, speak your thoughts. You've just returned from Qingzhou, so why don't you tell us what you know?" the leader said slowly.

The noise immediately subsided, and everyone's eyes turned to Nangong Hu, their expressions varied.

"Hmph, fleeing Qingzhou in such a sorry state, what could she possibly know?"

Zhao Tianwang sneered, his eyes filled with venomous resentment as he looked at Nangong Hu, as if she had murdered his entire family.

Nangong Hu ignored the gazes of the crowd, looked up at the leader, and said in a deep voice, "Sect Leader, the situation in Qingzhou is very complicated, and I can't explain it clearly in a short time."

"It's alright, a brief explanation will suffice," the leader said softly.

Nangong Hu nodded, thought for a moment, and then slowly said, "Qingzhou is actually no different from other states. The only variable is the Divine Mountain."

"In Qingzhou City, I encountered someone from the Divine Mountain, a monk. He was only at the fourth level of the Yin God Realm, yet he could see through my disguise at a glance."

"When I chased after him, he wasn't afraid of me. When I tried to capture him, I discovered he possessed a mysterious concealment technique that made him impossible to track."

"This person wasn't particularly strong, but he possessed various secret techniques. In the end, when I was surrounded and attacked by Bao Chu's Yin God Realm cultivators, it was this person who used that mysterious concealment technique to save me."

Nangong Hu told everyone everything he knew, without hiding anything.

After listening, everyone frowned deeply.

Zhao Tianwang was the first to step forward, coldly scoffing, "Nonsense! A mere Enlightenment Realm cultivator, where would he get such secret techniques? How could he possibly fool you?"

"Others may not know what you are, but do you think I don't?"

Zhao Tianwang looked at Nangong Hu and sneered, "A bastard born of a human and a demon, possessing the bloodline and supernatural powers of the Heavenly Azure Fox, a member of the demon royal family? Do you think she can't handle a mere fourth-tier Enlightenment Realm cultivator?"

"Master, in my opinion, this bastard is just like that vixen, blinded by lust. She's taken a liking to that monk from the Divine Mountain. Perhaps the dozen or so elite members of our sect who died were tricked and killed by her!"

Upon hearing this, many people began to discuss, their eyes filled with doubt and disgust as they looked at Nangong Hu.

The Dragon-Slaying Sect Master's eyes narrowed slightly, and his face darkened.

Seeing this, Zhao Tianwang assumed the sect leader was angry with Nangong Hu because he had believed her words, and quickly said, "Sect Leader, we cannot repeat the same mistake. The Vice Sect Leader's body is barely cold, and it's all because of that fox spirit from the demon race. If the Vice Sect Leader hadn't been bewitched by that fox spirit, how could he have ended up like this?"

The old woman spoke with immense grief, a hint of longing flashing in her eyes when she spoke of the Vice Sect Leader, before she glared at Nangong Hu with venomous resentment.

"Enough! Zhao Yu, stop going crazy! Who in the Dragon-Slaying Sect doesn't know you admire Sect Leader Nangong, but your love is unrequited? Sect Leader Nangong has his reasons for not liking you. I wouldn't have any feelings for a crazy old woman either!"

Gu Tianwang sneered.

"Gu Shouyu, say one more word!?" Zhao Yu's face darkened completely, her eyes becoming terrifying.

Sect Leader Nangong, this was her eternal pain.

This has twisted her psyche, especially when she sees the offspring of the Nangong Sect Leader and the fox spirit of the demon race—Nangong Hu—it stirs up her resentment.

“What’s wrong with saying one more word?”

Gu Shouyu remained unfazed.

He was promoted by the Nangong Sect Leader, and now that the Nangong Sect Leader is dead, he naturally has to protect the offspring left behind by the Vice Sect Leader.

“Out of the city, let’s fight!” Zhao Yu said coldly.

“If you want to fight, then let’s fight!” Gu Shouyu declared, ready to oblige.

“Have you had enough?” The leader of the Dragon-Slaying Sect finally spoke, his calm tone instantly silencing both Gu Shouyu and Zhao Yu.

“Gu Shouyu, I told you long ago not to be so impulsive, not to be so impulsive! Have you forgotten?”

“No.” Gu Shouyu lowered his head.

"Zhao Yu, you've gone mad. I remember telling you, 'There are plenty of fish in the sea,' and I've sent you many handsome men, only to have them all tortured to death by inhuman means. They were all pitiful people. If you didn't like them, you could have let them go, or even killed them with a single blow. Why torture them like this?"

"Nangong Yantian is dead. The dead cannot be brought back to life. You must look forward. I will not allow you to take the grudges of the previous generation onto the younger generation. If there is a next time, don't blame me for showing no mercy!"

The leader of the Dragon-Slaying Sect spoke in a deep voice, his words showing no mercy.

Zhao Yu's face was so dark it was almost dripping with water, but he finally nodded heavily.

"Yes, this old man understands."

Finally, the leader looked at Nangong Hu and said, "Since the Holy Maiden understands Qingzhou and has connections with the Divine Mountain, then go to Qingzhou and cooperate with the Holy Court to deal with the Divine Mountain."

Nangong Hu hesitated slightly, but finally nodded in agreement.

It wasn't enough for her to go to Qingzhou alone, so the leader of the Dragon-Slaying Sect allowed her to choose anyone to accompany her.

Anyone below the sixth rank, including those at the sixth-rank Yang God Realm, could be chosen freely!

Before leaving, the leader personally summoned Nangong Hu for a private conversation.

"I treat Yantian like a younger brother. You are practically my niece," the leader said gently.

"I know what King Zhao said today was utter nonsense, but I still want to give you a piece of advice: never fall in love with a human, and don't follow your father's example," he warned.

"I understand," Nangong Hu nodded solemnly.

"Then I'm relieved. You'll return to the Holy Court sooner or later. The Azure Fox bloodline is thin, and very few among them awaken their innate divine abilities. If you return to the Holy Court, you'll definitely receive special training from your maternal uncle."

The Dragon-Slaying Sect Leader looked at Nangong Hu, his gaze extremely complex.

He didn't know whether what he was doing was right or wrong.


Chapter 197 Demons Approach, Gathering in Qingzhou

Nangong Hu selected several skilled individuals, including two at the sixth-tier Yang God realm.

The group of seven, after bidding farewell to acquaintances, set off.

They didn't head directly to Qingzhou, but instead headed towards the Holy Court.

Ultimately, they intended to rendezvous with the vanguard of demons at the Holy Court before entering Qingzhou together.

After traveling for half a day, Nangong Hu's group barely approached the Holy Court when they encountered the demons who were there to meet them.

After exchanging tokens, the demons led them to the vanguard of demons.

This vanguard was practically a demon army.

Nangong Hu looked up; the demons were densely packed, almost stretching to the horizon.

Most were low-level demons, there to fill the ranks and scavenge for scraps.

The blood food that demons of the fifth tier and above wouldn't consider worthwhile belonged to them.

The leader of this group was a seventh-tier dog demon.

Demons of the seventh rank and above can completely solidify their human forms, and their bodies will not display any demonic characteristics. They will appear completely indistinguishable from humans.

Quan Xiaotian's eyes were sharp, and he occasionally sniffed the air, retaining some of his canine demon habits.

"You are the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect?"

Quan Xiaotian's gaze was intense, filled with astonishment as he looked at Nangong Hu.

"You're not human, you're a demon!? No, you're not a demon either, you're half-human, half-demon!" Quan Xiaotian said with surprise.

However, being half-human, half-demon wasn't unusual to him. After all, in the demon's stronghold, many humans were enslaved.

Human women were their objects of amusement.

This resulted in many half-human, half-demon offspring, but these mutants were looked down upon in the Holy Court, their status only slightly better than humans, yet still enslaved.

But Quan Xiaotian didn't show this emotion when facing Nangong Hu; instead, he was filled with doubt and suspicion.

He sensed that the demonic aura emanating from Nangong Hu was that of the royal family of demons—the Azure Fox.

This was the lineage of the Demon Saint, royalty!

But how could someone as noble as the Azure Fox possibly have offspring with a human?

Quan Xiaotian didn't understand, but he didn't want to delve deeper.

It wasn't something he could know. The Azure Fox clan was incredibly small, easily countable, and none of them were people he could afford to offend.

Knowing more wouldn't be a good thing for him.

Nangong Hu knew the status of her demonic bloodline within the Holy Court, so Quan Xiaotian's reaction wasn't unexpected.

Originally, she intended to use the Dragon-Slaying Sect as cannon fodder, but now that she saw the other party possessed the Azure Fox bloodline, she couldn't do that anymore.

Fortunately, the Dragon-Slaying Sect had sent quite a few people; let the others be the cannon fodder then.

Quan Xiaotian sneered inwardly.

........

The Capital City.

In the Golden Hall, Emperor Chu held a rare court assembly.

Civil and military officials rose early and gathered in the hall.

The great Confucian scholar Shangguan Liumo, General Cha Yunpo, and the Imperial Preceptor Yunji stood at the front as usual.

Emperor Chu looked at the crowd below, his eyes slightly narrowed, and said calmly, "The demons have sent out their vanguard and are about to invade Qingzhou. Although I have strategically abandoned Qingzhou, I cannot allow the demons to take it so easily."

"Therefore, I have decided to send the capital's prodigies to Qingzhou to gain experience and, incidentally, to gauge the strength of the demons."

These words caused an uproar throughout the hall!

"Your Majesty, this is absolutely unacceptable! The capital's prodigies are the hope of humanity. If anything were to happen to them, it would be a loss for the court!"

"Your Majesty, please retract your decision. This move will not only cause the prodigies to perish in Qingzhou but will also enrage the demons, bringing nothing but harm!"

"Yes, Your Majesty. If they lack experience, then let us hold a Prodigy Gathering, allowing young prodigies from all walks of life to learn from each other. This way, we can avoid risks and inspire the people, bringing them hope."

"..."

Almost all the officials voiced their opposition, and even those who agreed were drowned out by the chorus of dissent.

The Emperor Chu calmly observed their objections, his expression unchanged.

These opinions were just that—opinions.

He would only listen to the three standing at the very front; the rest he paid no heed to.

"My three ministers, what do you think?" Emperor Chu asked, looking at the three men.

Shangguan Liumo spoke first, his voice deep and resonant: "Your Majesty, I think it's feasible."

"I also think Your Majesty's decision is feasible. The so-called prodigies of the capital have all grown up under our protection. They've only ever seen human blood. Aside from the weak, they haven't encountered any demons above the fifth rank. It's time to let them go out and see some blood."

Cha Yunpo said resolutely.

The Imperial Advisor, Yunji, also spoke in a deep voice: "Prodigies are forged in battle. Therefore, I believe Your Majesty's decision is a divine strategy!"

The three men's surprisingly unified opinion left the other officials dumbfounded.

What the hell? Weren't you always arguing and refusing to concede?

How come you're all on the same side now?

So, the young prodigies of the capital aren't your juniors?

The reason the civil and military officials were so eager to oppose this was naturally because the so-called "prodigies" of the capital were almost all their direct descendants.

Sending their children and grandchildren to Qingzhou for training was simply courting death!

What prodigies? Family members know their own situation. With the limited strength of their descendants, going to Qingzhou would be suicide.

In an instant, the civil and military officials knelt down, repeatedly pleading with Emperor Chu to rescind his order.

"My mind is made up. Jinlong Temple and Xuanqing Temple will also send their prodigies to Qingzhou. How can the court lag behind? Each family must send at least one person. Anyone who dares to disobey or conceal anything will be executed without mercy!"

"Court adjourned!"

Emperor Chu completely ignored them, waving his hand and leaving.

The officials, seeing this, were filled with despair.

The Imperial Preceptor, Yunji, turned to the crowd and said with a faint smile, "Gentlemen, instead of lingering here in sorrow, you should hurry back to prepare magical artifacts, talismans, and various life-saving methods for your families, giving them a better chance of survival."

These words jolted everyone awake.

The officials rose to their feet, not even having time to say goodbye, and rushed out of the Golden Hall to prepare life-saving measures for their descendants.

"His Majesty is starting to force these people to take action," Cha Yunpo said, his tone much more relaxed.

"As for them? You overestimate them. Once they return, they'll surely instruct their families to hide in Qingzhou and wait for the opportune moment to return to the capital. Then they'll be seen as prodigies returning from their Qingzhou training!" the Grand Preceptor sneered.

"Going is better than not going."

Shangguan Liumo said calmly, then added, "I'll be leaving now. My son should already know the news and is probably excited to set off for Qingzhou. I still have something to give him."

“I also have something to give to my son at home.”

Cha Yunpo spoke, then looked at the Imperial Advisor and smiled, “Your disciple is likely to get into big trouble after going to Qingzhou!”


Chapter 198 The Arrogant Prodigies

Meanwhile, at Jinlong Temple.

Several renowned disciples, whose names resounded throughout the capital, received a notification from their elders.

“The Ancestral Master has decreed that you all must immediately proceed to Qingzhou for training. Remember, do not disgrace Jinlong Temple.”

An old monk, his face stern, looked at the disciples before him and spoke in a deep voice.

“Uncle-Master, rest assured, we will not disappoint our elders.”

The leading monk, his eyes sharp and piercing like an eagle's, replied.

“That’s good. Ask for anything you desire. The situation in Qingzhou is extremely chaotic. Not only is there Jinlong Temple, but also demons, the Dragon-Slaying Sect, Xuanqing Temple, the imperial court, and even the mysterious Divine Mountain.”

“His Majesty has decreed that even Yang Shen can leave the capital for Qingzhou. The condition is that if discovered by the demons, they must not admit to being from the Great Chu; they can claim to be from the Divine Mountain.”

The old monk spoke calmly.

The disciples below smiled knowingly upon hearing this.

The Great Chu Dynasty won't take the blame, so let the Divine Mountain stand in its way.

"Uncle-Master, this disciple wishes to take the Maitreya Bell to Qingzhou," someone said.

"The Maitreya Bell, a seventh-tier magic treasure, can you subdue it?" the old monk asked, looking at him.

"I can subdue it!" the man replied firmly.

"Very well, the Maitreya Bell is entrusted to you. I hope you can bring it back safely," the old monk said solemnly.

.............

Xuanqing Temple.

"Amitabha, this is another major situation. Even I, this old Daoist, cannot see whether it is a blessing or a curse."

Tai Xuan frowned, his fingers constantly kneading, but all he could calculate was a hazy mist, impossible to discern.

The waters of Qingzhou are far too deep.

He suddenly felt reluctant to let his disciples go to Qingzhou to wade into this murky water.

But the Chu Emperor had already given the order, and even though he was also a half-saint, he could not disobey the Chu Emperor's command.

“Alas, what is meant to be will be,” Tai Xuan sighed.

“However, great terrors often come with great opportunities. Perhaps this journey will allow some of the younger generation to witness the Great Dao!”

Thinking to himself, Tai Xuan already had a choice in mind.

“Junior Sister, let Wu Hui and Wu Yuan go to Qingzhou.”

Tai Xuan spoke to a screen.

“Senior Brother, you only have these two bloodlines left. Can you bear to send Wu Hui and the others?”

A woman's voice rang out, surprisingly not from behind the screen, but emanating from it.

Through careful observation, one could vaguely see the screen moving slightly, radiating a life force.

“What can I do even if I can't bear to? With great power comes great responsibility. As descendants of my bloodline, we should prepare for this sooner rather than later. If they don't go to Qingzhou, who else can go? Send others to their deaths?”

Tai Xuan said calmly.

Of all the disciples in the sect, only Wu Hui and Wu Yuan possessed the highest talent and the strongest strength.

If the strong aren't sent to face danger, are the weak supposed to take their place?

Then what use are the strong?

"Sigh, since you've said so, Senior Brother, then let Wu Hui and Wu Yuan go."

The woman behind the screen sighed softly.

"Junior Sister, don't be so stubborn. You're turning yourself into something neither human nor ghost. Listen to your senior brother's advice; the Nine-Turn Human Elixir is the right path. Your method won't work."

Tai Xuan looked at the screen, a slight headache on his face.

"Hehe," the screen chuckled, saying, "Senior Brother, you've transformed yourself from an old woman into a child, and now you're a child again. Is this the true path?"

"This is merely a minor setback before attaining the Great Dao," Tai Xuan replied solemnly.

"Then this, too, is a setback before attaining the Great Dao."

The screen echoed the same sentiment.

Tai Xuan and the screen stared at each other, and a sudden silence fell.

The situation in the capital, which had been as calm as a still lake, was now stirred up by the Chu Emperor's sudden outburst.

The city's elites, upon receiving this news, did not share the same fear and anxiety as their elders.

Instead, they were eager to try their luck and couldn't wait to go to Qingzhou.

They had been cooped up in the capital for ages.

They had already tried all sorts of entertainment.

Now they just wanted some excitement.

"This young master considers the demons beyond the Great Wall as nothing more than chickens and dogs. If it weren't for my father's insistence on not leaving the capital, I would have already gone out into the world and terrified them!"

In the taverns, one could frequently hear the arrogant pronouncements of many spoiled young masters.

They had swaggered around the capital, possessed some power, and had never encountered demons before, so in their minds, demons were nothing special.

They even thought that those adults were utterly useless, beaten by demons without daring to fight back, and even signing the Treaty of Tianshui—a humiliating treaty that ceded national sovereignty. It was simply too cowardly!

"Young Master Zhao is right. If you were to leave the capital, demons would surely tremble in fear. After all, who in the world doesn't know how sharp Young Master Zhao's blade is? Calling him the number one blade in the world wouldn't be an exaggeration."

A servant beside him flattered, his face full of obsequiousness.

"No, that's a bit much. Although I possess extraordinary talent, I dare not claim to be the number one blade in the world. I'd say I'm just the number one blade in the Yin God Realm. I must be humble."

Zhao Bufan said proudly.

"That's right, Young Master. You are strong, and yet so humble. You are truly a dragon among men!" the servant continued to flatter.
