Zhao Bufan was very pleased with the flattery and laughed heartily.

Then, as if remembering something, he snorted coldly.

"If it weren't for that lowly commoner causing trouble, which my father found out about, causing me to be punished and delaying my cultivation, I would have already broken through to the Yang God Realm!"

Zhao Bufan's face was filled with malice.

The servant, knowing of this, hurriedly said, "Young Master, that lowly commoner is indeed despicable. He only killed his wife and daughter, yet he dares to sue you? He's simply defied the heavens!"

"If that incident hadn't caused such a stir, I would have killed him right there in the capital. But that lowly commoner isn't much better off; I used my connections to exile him to the borderlands, I think... in Qingzhou."

"Oh? Qingzhou!?" Zhao Bufan's eyes turned cold.

"Yes, Qingzhou. But Qingzhou is in chaos now; he's probably dead by now," the servant said.

"Hmph! He's lucky he died young. Otherwise, if he fell into my hands, I would never let him get away with it!"

Zhao Bufan sneered, a flash of malice crossing his eyes.

Meanwhile, many of the 'prodigies' in the capital were preparing to leave.

Each of them was filled with excitement, believing that once they arrived in Qingzhou, they would surely distinguish themselves!

As for the advice of their elders, they completely disregarded it.

What demons? Just paper tigers.

They'd been in the capital for so many years, and no demons had ever attacked.

People all said demons were incredibly powerful, but that was an exaggeration.

Once they arrived in Qingzhou, they would definitely change the public's perception of demons.

Some 'prodigies' had already formed an alliance, determined to show their true strength.


Chapter 199 Grand Sacrificial Ceremony

Shenshan, Qianshan County.

One day remains until the sacrificial ceremony.

The entire Qianshan County is busy preparing.

The twelve demon corpses have been properly prepared and disposed of.

Obscure parts, hair, and other unsightly items have been removed.

What remains are the finest sacrificial offerings.

In the Mountain Tower, Bai Liang was handling matters related to the sacrificial ceremony when Qilin suddenly reported.

“This is bad. Demon tracks have appeared on the border of Qingzhou. It's possible a demon army is about to attack,” Qilin said gravely.

The demon tracks he discovered were very small, seemingly scouts who had gone ahead to investigate.

“What level are they?” Bai Liang frowned.

“Fifth-tier demons!” Qilin took a deep breath and said.

Bai Liang's expression immediately darkened, and he handed over his work to Xiao Nian.

"Brother Qilin, come with me." With that, he and Qilin went upstairs to the second floor.

Upstairs, Wu Ze was cultivating. They had switched to a shift system, so Bai Liang was no longer handling all the affairs alone.

Otherwise, Bai Liang's cultivation would never catch up, which wouldn't be fair to him.

"Has something happened?" Seeing Bai Liang bring Qilin upstairs, Wu Ze knew something must have happened and stood up.

"The demons have finally made their move," Bai Liang said in a deep voice, then told Wu Ze what Qilin had just said.

Wu Ze frowned after listening.

"It's during the Mountain God's sacrificial ceremony, and the demons have just made their move. This is a bit troublesome," he said.

"Brother Bai Liang, what are your thoughts?" Wu Ze looked up at Bai Liang.

Bai Liang spoke, "The sacrificial ceremony is of utmost importance and cannot be neglected! Let's disregard everything else for now and get it completed!"

"Yes, that's what I think too," Wu Ze nodded.

No matter what, the Mountain God always comes first.

As long as they don't attack us, we don't need to worry about them for now.

"However, we must be cautious. Tomorrow's sacrificial ceremony is of paramount importance and cannot be allowed to fail," Bai Liang said solemnly.

Then he turned to Qilin and said, "Qilin, the three of you go together and keep a close eye on the demons' movements."

"This will make you miss the sacrificial ceremony, but there's no other way. I'll have to make do with it for now."

Hearing Bai Liang's words, Qilin felt a slight pang of disappointment.

After all, the sacrificial ceremony was incredibly sacred in the eyes of these Mountain God's followers.

To be able to participate even once was an immense honor.

But he still said firmly, "Don't worry, we'll keep an eye on the demons, and report any movements back to Qianshan County immediately!"

"Yes, thank you for your hard work," Bai Liang said emphatically.

He knew that the three members of the Qilin Clan were very concerned about the sacrificial ceremony.

But there was nothing he could do; currently, the three of them were the strongest in Qianshan County.

Faced with unexpected situations, their strength allowed for an easy retreat, preventing them from encountering danger.

This time, the three members of the Qilin Clan missed the sacrificial ceremony, but he could only make up for it in the future.

Qilin turned and left, feeling disappointed, but ensuring the smooth running of the sacrificial ceremony was the most important thing.

He found the Black Dragon and the Fire Phoenix, and the three of them left Qianshan County together.

Li Yue's divine sense had been silently watching them the whole time.

Seeing the efforts of the three members of Qilin, I deeply admire them.

"These three men are of good character, loyal and righteous, and worth cultivating."

Li Yue thought to himself.

The only downside was that the three characters' names were too grandiose.

Qilin, Black Dragon, Fire Phoenix.

This reminded him of the primordial world of his past life.

The unusual activity on the Qingzhou border also alerted Li Yue.

The demons had been holding back for so long, but the extent of their latest move remained unknown.

Unfortunately, his divine sense was currently limited to a range of only a hundred miles, making it impossible for him to perceive the situation on the border.

He didn't want to use his incarnation ability, so he could only wait.

He wondered how much his divine sense range would increase if he broke through to the fifth-tier Sacred Mountain.

Looking at the panel again, his merit points were already five million.

[Realm: Fourth-tier Sacred Mountain]

[Merit Points: 5.01 million/10 million]

[Divine Abilities: Mountain God's Blessing, Dharma Body of Heaven and Earth, Rebirth of Incense, Gratitude for the Source of Water]

[Spell: Incarnation]

[Divine Artifacts: Mountain-Suppressing Seal, Heart Mirror, Divine Weapon Furnace, Wind-Stabilizing Pearl]

He wondered if he could break through to the fifth-tier Sacred Mountain in one fell swoop at tomorrow's sacrificial ceremony?

Li Yue suddenly felt a sense of anticipation.

Time passed quickly amidst the tension and anticipation of the people in Qianshan County.

The next day arrived, the most important day in Qianshan County.

Everyone, regardless of status, was given a day off.

Everyone understood that today was the grand ceremony to worship the Mountain God, and they all took it extremely seriously.

They burned incense and bathed early in the morning, donning their finest attire to show the highest respect to the Mountain God.

As key figures in Qianshan County, Bai Liang, Wu Ze, and others prepared with even greater solemnity.

They hadn't slept a wink all night.

In front of the main temple was a vast plaza.

It was now densely packed with people, and in the center was a long row of brand-new tables.

On them were laid out offerings, the most conspicuous being the twelve demon corpses in the center.

The offerings exuded a terrifying aura, the malevolent aura unique to fifth-tier demon generals.

Even in death, it couldn't be concealed.

Those who witnessed this scene were utterly shocked, marveling at the extravagance of Bai Liang and his group.

The Yin God Realm experts from Bai Jiangzhou also arrived at the plaza with utmost solemnity.

Compared to Bai Liang and his group, their preparations were far more grand and formal.

"Finally, this day has arrived! The Mountain God's sacrificial ceremony! Finally, we'll have the chance to witness the Mountain God's manifestation!" Chu Wuliu said excitedly.

Hu Zhengqiang was equally excited, his eyes filled with anticipation.

"What a stroke of luck! Those lads didn't come to Qingzhou; they really missed a great opportunity," he remarked.

The old monk's eyes were fervent, his hands clasped together, his heart filled with impatient anticipation.

No one present was more eager for the sacrificial ceremony than him.

He had already identified the sacred mountain as the holy mountain in Buddhist scriptures.

The Golden Dragon Temple was long forgotten.

"Lord Bai, when does the sacrificial ceremony begin?" Chu Wuliu asked Bai Liang.

Bai Liang looked up at the sky and said, "The time hasn't come yet, let's wait a little longer."

The sun had just risen in the east, and the earth still retained the chill of the night; the temperature hadn't yet risen.

Li Xue, He Liyuan, Du Tiandao, and the others had all arrived, and now they were just waiting for the appointed time to begin the sacrifice.

"Where are Qilin and the others?" Hu Zhengqiang asked, puzzled, not seeing Qilin and the other two.

"They went to the border," Bai Liang replied.

He then explained what had happened at the border.

Hearing that the demons were starting to make moves, everyone frowned.


Chapter 200 Spells: Mastering the Five Elements

"Once the demons arrive, Qingzhou will be in danger!"

Hu Zhengqiang said in a deep voice.

He thought of the devastation that had befallen Baijiangzhou recently. Just two sixth-tier demon generals had slaughtered half of Baijiangzhou's territory, claiming tens of millions of lives; the area remains a wasteland to this day.

And the crisis Qingzhou is about to face is several times greater than that of Baijiangzhou!

"Sixth-tier demons are definitely coming. Are you prepared?" Chu Wuliu asked Bai Liang and the others.

Bai Liang nodded. "We've made all the necessary preparations. We can definitely protect ourselves. But the people of Qingzhou..."

He didn't finish his sentence, but everyone understood what he meant.

Qianshan County is protected by a protective array; no matter how many demons come, they won't pose a threat.

But the people in other places don't have that protection.

"Now, more and more people are flocking to Qianshan County from all directions. Conservatively speaking, there are at least close to two hundred thousand people gathered around Qianshan County!"

Bai Liang said in a deep voice.

Such a large population puts immense pressure on Qianshan County.

These people can't possibly bring enough food, so Qianshan County has to provide grain, although most people bought it with silver.

But given the current situation in Qingzhou, silver is practically useless.

Food is the only real solution!

Fortunately, they experienced hunger initially, which made Bai Liang and his people very particular about food, resulting in a significant stockpile.

It should last for a few months without a problem.

Qianshan County's grain harvest is also approaching, which will provide enough to last for a while longer.

However, the problem is that even more people are arriving on the road, and their numbers are constantly increasing.

Qingzhou has fifty to sixty million people; if even one percent of them believe the rumors about the sacred mountain, the gathered population would still be five to six hundred thousand.

This is terrifying, and Qianshan County is not prepared for this.

Managing such a large influx of people all at once will be extremely difficult.

"What's the big deal? Chu Wuliu is the ruler of a prefecture; managing hundreds of thousands of people is a piece of cake for him. Just ask him for help with any problems!" Hu Zhengqiang said with a smile.

Let alone Chu Wuliu, even for him, this was no problem.

"Indeed," Chu Wuliu said with a faint smile.

Bai Liang's face lit up upon hearing this.

If Chu Wuliu was willing to help, that would be wonderful.

With experienced people assisting in management, various problems would be avoided.

"Then thank you very much, Chu Prefecture. After the sacrificial ceremony, I'll need your guidance again," Bai Liang said.

"It's nothing, no need to thank me," Chu Wuliu waved his hand, indicating it was a small matter.

These were all minor matters for the sacrificial ceremony.

While waiting for the appointed time, people continued to arrive.

Qian Ke and the others who had gone to Shishu County also returned, including Wang Lan.

"Lord Bai, thank you so much for allowing me to participate in the Mountain God's sacrificial ceremony!" Wang Lan said, deeply moved, as soon as she arrived beside Bai Liang.

Chen Jianchi's letter was delivered to the Mountain Pavilion. After reading it, Bai Liang didn't make things difficult and directly invited Wang Lan to come along.

Bai Liang smiled warmly and said, "We're all on the same side, no need for such words."

Although Wang Lan had a history of betrayal, now that she had drunk the divine water, she was one of his own.

Hearing Bai Liang say they were all on his side, Wang Lan was even more moved.

His initial reverence for the Mountain God had deepened, and now his sense of belonging to Qianshan County felt even stronger.

Everyone had arrived, and the sun was in a certain position.

Bai Liang looked up and said in a deep voice, "The time has come. Begin the ceremony!"

As soon as he finished speaking, the arena fell silent. Everyone's expressions were solemn as they looked towards the center of the ceremony.

Wu Ze, as the magistrate of Qianshan County and the first person favored by the gods, stood at the head of the ceremony once again.

Countless residents on the periphery witnessed this grand event with great excitement and anticipation.

Wu Ze took out a eulogy, slowly opened it, his expression extremely solemn and dignified, and spoke in a clear voice:

"Only:"

"On the day of Wushen in the eighth month of the year Guimao in the 489th year of the Great Chu Dynasty, your disciple Wuze offered twelve corpses of demons, along with three sacrificial animals, and burned incense to the Mountain God. Today, the world is in chaos, the court is corrupt, demons wreak havoc, and poison the human realm, causing untold suffering. My Thousand Mountain County, which I established, is fortunate to be protected by the Mountain God, who slayed the demons and warded off evil. Under the Mountain God's jurisdiction, the world is at peace, demons have retreated, the people's hearts are with him, and his grace is boundless, punishing evil and promoting good. I offer this petition in gratitude, and sincerely pray to the Mountain God to enjoy the incense offerings."

"Your humble servant kowtows in reverence!"

After the incantation for the mountain god was recited, Wu Ze immediately knelt down and kowtowed deeply.

Behind him, Bai Liang and the others followed suit, kneeling in unison. The surrounding commoners, without exception, all solemnly kowtowed.

"Disciple thanks the Mountain God!"

They kowtowed nine times in succession before slowly rising.

Then, with a solemn expression, Wu Ze took the sacrificial inscription to a blazing furnace and threw it into the fire.

The inscription was immediately engulfed by the flames, emitting rays of light; the characters seemed to dance, incredibly mystical.

This sacrificial inscription wasn't significantly different from the last one, but the paper itself was completely different.

This was spirit paper purchased at a great expense, and even the ink used was spirit ink, specially prepared for this sacrificial ceremony.

After burning the inscription, everyone began to offer incense.

Just then, a wisp of green smoke drifted down from the sacred mountain, finally settling on the twelve demonic offerings.

Upon seeing this, everyone's expressions changed slightly, a surge of excitement rising within them.

This...this is the Mountain God manifesting?

Before they could react, the green smoke entered the offerings, instantly absorbing all the essence of the demonic corpses.

The demonic corpse instantly transformed into an ordinary body, devoid of any demonic aura.

The green smoke dissipated, replaced by a vast expanse of golden light that enveloped everyone in the plaza.

Feeling the golden light enveloping them, the crowd was filled with horror, sensing wisps of mysterious energy entering their bodies.

Their spiritual power instinctively tried to resist, but upon sensing the aura of that mysterious energy, they turned and fled in terror.

"This...this is divine essence!?"

Chu Wuliu was utterly astonished. Realizing what kind of divine object it was, he was overjoyed. Without thinking, he immediately sat cross-legged, focusing entirely on absorbing the divine essence that had entered his body.

Hu Zhengqiang and the others also realized this, and they too sat cross-legged, beginning to absorb the divine essence.

Bai Liang and the others followed suit, sitting cross-legged and absorbing the divine energy.

Li Yue watched this scene quietly. The golden light was naturally his doing, a small divine gift to these people.

He naturally wouldn't leave out the three Qilins far away on the border; three streaks of golden light flew towards them.
